### 1. [Energy-Based Reasoners: Your Recurrent Reasoner Should Be an Energy-Based Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08977v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08977

**作者：** William Xingxu Chen、Alexi Gladstone、Zeyu Michael Li、Heng Ji、Yilun Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08977v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08977v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Energy-Based Reasoners，在循环块的 latent 推理状态上学习能量函数，把循环更新视为对该能量的梯度下降，使能量及其梯度同时用于状态精修与停止判断，并引入基于探索的训练以发现更多模态、塑造能量地形；在四个算法推理基准上匹配或超过已有循环推理模型，Sudoku-Extreme 达 99.9% 且推理算力大幅减少。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 LVR 的生成与停止问题：用能量为 latent 状态提供进展度量与 STOP 信号，其多解覆盖结果与探索式训练可供核查 latent 迭代的收益成本权衡及轨迹监督方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Energy-Based Reasoners: Your Recurrent Reasoner Should Be an Energy-Based Model

**abstract/A1：** Looping is a powerful inductive bias for reasoning. By repeatedly iterating a recurrent block, looped models can scale their effective depth and enable multi-step reasoning in latent space. However, existing looped models are not inherently guided by an explicit measure of progress, making it difficult to determine when to stop looping and how to improve upon the current state. To enable this, we introduce Energy-Based Reasoners (EBRs), which learn an energy function over latent reasoning states and formulate looped updates as gradient descent on this energy. The energy scores latent states, while its gradient guides refinement and provides a signal for stopping. To train EBRs effectively, we further introduce exploration-based training to discover additional modes and to better shape the energy landscape over a broader set of states. Across four algorithmic reasoning benchmarks, EBRs match or outperform prior recurrent reasoning models, achieving 99.9% accuracy on Sudoku-Extreme while using 345$\\times$ less inference compute than the next-best model and maintaining comparable training compute. On all multi-solution benchmarks, EBRs achieve state-of-the-art coverage of distinct valid solutions while maintaining near-100% single-sample accuracy. These results suggest that energy-based models are a promising approach for strong reasoning performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-2"></a>

### 2. [AtariBench: Evaluating Spatiotemporal Reasoning of Vision-Language Models in Atari Gameplay](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08755v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08755

**作者：** Mingjia Huo、Yao Fu、Bo Chang、Yaqing Wang、Dawei Zhu、Dylan Zhang、Xinyang Yi、Lichan Hong、Ed H. Chi、Hao Zhang、Jiaxi Tang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08755v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08755v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 AtariBench，用 20 款 Atari 2600 游戏的高时序分辨率画面流评测 VLM 的时空推理与序贯决策，并把游戏内动态与推理墙钟速度解耦，报告模型在物体运动方向推断、决策稳定性和依赖文本交互历史方面的缺陷，另提出可分离性分析以筛选可靠分数信号。

**相关性简评：** 该基准直接诊断模型是否保留并实际使用快速变化的视觉状态证据（表现为过度依赖文本历史，类似视觉旁路），可作为 LVR 中状态更新、证据获取与「看/停」决策收益评估的参考测试床。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AtariBench: Evaluating Spatiotemporal Reasoning of Vision-Language Models in Atari Gameplay

**abstract/A1：** Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on static visual understanding benchmarks, yet their ability to act in dynamic environments remains less well understood. We introduce AtariBench, a controlled benchmark that evaluates VLMs across 20 Atari 2600 games, requiring models to make sequential decisions from high-temporal-resolution gameplay streams. By decoupling fast in-game dynamics from wall-clock inference speed, AtariBench focuses on whether models can perceive rapid visual state changes, reason over spatiotemporal dynamics, and ground actions in the current observation. Our experiments reveal substantial limitations in state-of-the-art VLMs: models often fail to infer object motion and direction, exhibit brittle sequential decision-making, and over-rely on textual interaction history despite newly appended visual feedback. We further introduce a separability analysis to identify which in-game scores provide reliable signals of model competence, and use the benchmark to study how memory design and prompting strategies affect performance. Overall, AtariBench exposes a persistent gap between passive visual perception and active decision-making, providing a lightweight and rigorous testbed for developing VLMs that can reason and act in dynamic environments.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 14 figures. Accepted to NeurIPS 2026, Evaluations and Datasets Track

</details>

<a id="paper-3"></a>

### 3. [GeoJeV: Unified Spatial Probability for Training-Free Efficient Evidence Acquisition in Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08879v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08879

**作者：** Xinyi Niu、Lingfeng Chen、Jiehua Zhang、Panhe Hu、Li Liu、Zhen Liu、Yongxiang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08879v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08879v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练方法 GeoJeV，将超高分辨率遥感影像的证据获取建模为空间概率驱动的决策：由冻结的 JeV 模型在每个区域给出候选区域概率分布，用 Region Rank、Sparse Branch 和 Depth Gate 分别控制搜索优先级、分支宽度与细化深度，从而免去 RLHF 生成式中间解码；三个基准上较 Qwen3-VL-8B 平均提升 5.32pp，每题仅增约 1.12 秒，并比近期方法快 7.8 倍。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 LOOK/STOP 的收益与成本、视觉证据筛选和动态计算，可供核查以区域概率分布驱动分支与停止的可靠性；但它不显式维护 latent 推理状态，也未检验所选证据是否真正影响答案，其内部机制仍需读原文。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GeoJeV: Unified Spatial Probability for Training-Free Efficient Evidence Acquisition in Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing

**abstract/A1：** Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing imagery provides rich spatial details but poses challenges for accurate and efficient multimodal understanding. Existing evidence acquisition methods selectively explore relevant image regions, yet largely rely on generative multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for intermediate search control. However, reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) primarily aligns these models with human-preferred responses rather than explicitly optimizing region-level relevance judgments, while repeated autoregressive decoding introduces additional computational overhead. To address these limitations, we propose GeoJeV, a training-free method that formulates evidence acquisition as spatial probability-driven decision making. At each evaluated region, a frozen JeV model produces a probability distribution over candidate regions. Specifically, Region Rank prioritizes candidate regions, Sparse Branch adaptively determines branching width through sparse probability support, and Depth Gate controls refinement depth based on branch sparsity and distribution concentration. By coordinating search priority, branching width, and refinement depth through a shared distribution, GeoJeV reduces reliance on RLHF-aligned generation and eliminates intermediate decoding. Experiments on LRS-GRO, MME-RealWorld-RS, and LRS-VQA achieve accuracies of 60.59%, 52.79%, and 34.32%, respectively. Across the three benchmarks, GeoJeV improves the average accuracy of Qwen3-VL-8B by +5.32pp with only 1.12 additional seconds per question, while achieving a 7.8-fold inference speedup over the recent method.

</details>

<a id="paper-4"></a>

### 4. [Did I See It or Think It? Probing Reality Monitoring in Vision-Language Models with Concept Injection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09014v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09014

**作者：** Tina Khezresmaeilzadeh、Seyedarmin Azizi、Mehdi Kamal、Massoud Pedram

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09014v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09014v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出概念注入范式，独立操纵图像与注入概念，要求六个 VLM 报告所见内容、是否出现被注入的「想法」及其来源，以考察其现实监控能力。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及 VLM 对内部生成内容与视觉证据的来源区分，可为 LVR 中 latent 内容是否真正被使用、视觉旁路及答案归因提供诊断线索；其注入方式与源判断指标能否迁移仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Did I See It or Think It? Probing Reality Monitoring in Vision-Language Models with Concept Injection

**abstract/A1：** Recent work tests whether language models can introspect by injecting a concept direction into their activations and asking whether they notice it and can name it. These studies are text-only. Vision-language models (VLMs) face an additional problem, known in cognitive psychology as reality monitoring: they must keep internally generated content separate from what they actually see. We therefore ask: can VLMs distinguish injected concepts from image content? We introduce a paradigm that varies the image and the injected concept independently. On each trial, the model reports what it sees, whether a thought was injected, and what that thought is. This design scores both whether the model identifies the concept and which source it assigns to it. Across six VLMs, only Gemma-3-27B reliably identifies injected concepts (35% vs. 3% for steering alone), with almost no false alarms. Even then, the source it reports is fragile. Strong or visual-pathway injections are reported as seen, and a visible object nearly eliminates correct thought reports. The same failure appears in ordinary captioning, where injected objects appear in up to 87% of captions of real photographs. Evaluating multimodal self-reports therefore requires checking both their content and their reported source.

</details>

<a id="paper-5"></a>

### 5. [AdaAlloc: Adaptive Visual Token Allocation for Long-Video Question Answering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09092v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09092

**作者：** Joungbin An、Kristen Grauman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09092v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09092v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究长视频问答在固定视觉 token 预算下如何权衡全局覆盖与局部细节，提出免训练推理框架 AdaAlloc：先规划全局/局部预算比例，再用时序定位找出候选片段并精炼为紧凑时序记忆，按策略组合稀疏低分辨率全局帧与密集高分辨率局部帧供最终作答。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应「视觉证据筛选」与预算分配问题，可作为固定 token 预算下全局/局部取舍的对照基线；其分配只作用于输入 token 而非内部 latent 状态，因此可供核查 latent 方法能否以更低成本取得同类证据针对性收益。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AdaAlloc: Adaptive Visual Token Allocation for Long-Video Question Answering

**abstract/A1：** Long-video question answering forces multimodal large language models to reason under strict visual-token budgets, creating a tradeoff between broad temporal coverage and fine-grained spatial detail. Uniform sampling preserves coverage but often obscures decisive local cues, while caption-based or frame-similarity retrieval operates over lossy proxies that can miss motion, state changes, and before-after relations. We recast long-video reasoning as a visual-token allocation problem: given a fixed budget, decide how much to spend on global context versus high-resolution local evidence, and where in the video to draw that local evidence from. We introduce AdaAlloc, a training-free inference framework that adaptively allocates visual tokens between a global video overview and localized visual detail. AdaAlloc first plans a global/local allocation policy, then uses temporal grounding to locate candidate evidence segments and refines them into a compact temporal memory. The selected policy controls both frame sampling density and resolution, combining sparse, lower-resolution global frames with denser, higher-resolution local frames drawn from the refined memory. The final answerer reasons over the allocated global context and refined local evidence. Across LVBench, MLVU, VideoMME, and LongVideoBench, AdaAlloc improves over budget-matched uniform sampling by up to 10.5 percentage points and outperforms the compared frame-selection and agentic methods on their reported benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026. Project page: https://vision.cs.utexas.edu/projects/adaalloc

</details>

<a id="paper-6"></a>

### 6. [Learning When to Think: Dual-Reference Offline Optimization for Adaptive VLM Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09154v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09154

**作者：** Hongbin Lin、Sizhe Zou、Juangui Xu、Xinyue Xu、Zhuoli Ouyang、Stanislav Kolchin、Chengwei Qin、Zhongxiang Dai、Yao Shu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09154v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09154v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 VLM 的自适应快/慢思考，提出双参考离线优化方法 DR-BOLT：把期望的快慢混合参考分布与采样分布区分开，在奖励加权训练中修正二者错配，使模型对简单输入直接作答、必要时才展开长推理。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应「何时思考」的停止/路由决策与推理成本权衡，但其核心是离线训练目标与贡献分配而非 latent 状态生成；其快慢划分竞争与跨基准 slow ratio 可供核查停止决策的行为证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning When to Think: Dual-Reference Offline Optimization for Adaptive VLM Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Reasoning traces improve vision-language models on complex multimodal tasks, but forcing every input to follow a long reasoning process leads to overthinking and unnecessary inference cost. We study adaptive fast/slow thinking for VLMs, where one model answers directly for simple inputs and reasons step by step when needed. Fast answers and slow traces often come from distinct behavior policies, making a single-reference offline objective misweight one mode. We propose DUAL-REFERENCE BOLT (DR-BOLT), which distinguishes the desired fast/slow mixture reference from the sampling distribution and corrects their mismatch in reward-weighted training. Fast/slow partition competition explains routing in the optimal target; an efficient source-wise correction approximates the exact objective when response formats are well separated. Matched Qwen3-VL comparisons use identical candidate banks to isolate the objective from slow-teacher quality. DR-BOLT improves score-length trade-offs over the evaluated offline and online baselines on ScienceQA and three held-out transfer benchmarks, with a 24.1% cross-benchmark slow ratio and a 2.90-fold end-to-end training speedup over reproduced GRPO. These results support adaptive reasoning in the studied two-reference setting, while broader robustness remains open. Our code is available at https://github.com/HongbinLin3589/DR-BOLT.

</details>

<a id="paper-7"></a>

### 7. [V-STORM: A Structure-Aware Grounded Chain of Thought Dataset for Faithful and Fine-Grained Spatio-Temporal Video Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09511v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09511

**作者：** Dian Jiao、Yuqian Yuan、Jiaqi Zhu、Mingjian Gao、Siwen Tan、Yunqi Cao、Jinhui Li、Sijing Li、Zhenxuan Fan、Haoyang Wang、Wenqiao Zhang、Siliang Tang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09511v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09511v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 V-STORM 框架，将视频转换为时空场景图并从验证过的证据路径导出 grounded chain of thought，据此生成 260K 训练集与 3K 人工核验基准，用于细粒度时空视频推理的训练与评测。

**相关性简评：** 它把每个推理步锚定到可验证的时空证据，其「能定位证据却难以组合成多步推理」的分离诊断与视觉证据筛选、推理链监督及贡献分配直接相关，可供核查；但方法仍是显式文本 CoT，不涉及 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** V-STORM: A Structure-Aware Grounded Chain of Thought Dataset for Faithful and Fine-Grained Spatio-Temporal Video Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** While MLLMs can often identify what happens in a video, they still struggle with fine-grained spatio-temporal reasoning, which requires precise grounding when and where the supporting evidence appears and composing such evidence into a coherent reasoning chain. This challenge is exacerbated by the lack of scalable data construction and evaluation pipelines that provide granular, verifiable supervision linking reasoning steps to explicit spatio-temporal evidence.To address this gap, we present V-STORM, a structure-aware framework that transforms videos into Spatio-Temporal Scene Graphs (STSGs) and derives Grounded Chain of Thought (GCoT) from validated evidence paths, making each reasoning step traceable to structured and verifiable spatio-temporal evidence.Under this framework, we construct a 260K-sample training set and a 3K-sample human-verified benchmark, both featuring GCoT annotations that link reasoning to explicit spatio-temporal evidence.Experiments reveal a clear separation between evidence localization and evidence composition: current MLLMs can often identify relevant spatio-temporal cues, yet still fail to integrate them into correct multi-step reasoning, exposing a bottleneck that answer-only evaluation largely obscures. Moreover, fine-tuning with V-STORM substantially improves both answer accuracy and grounding faithfulness, validating its effectiveness as supervision for faithful and evidence-grounded video reasoning. Our dataset, benchmark, and source code will be publicly released.

**comment/C1：** 9 Pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-8"></a>

### 8. [BEV3SR: Evidence-Aware 3D Spatial Reasoning from Video](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09816v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09816

**作者：** Fan Zhu、Rui Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09816v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09816v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BEV3SR，一个 training-free、backbone-agnostic 框架：将 RGB-D 视频与相机参数转为可查询的度量 BEV 场景记忆，并把每个查询形式化为含参考系、空间约束、目标与证据需求的空间任务；需要几何证据的查询由几何推理器确定性求解，其余则按 BEV 索引检索对象一致的支持视图交给 VLM grounding，在多个 3D 场景 QA 基准上提升不同 VLM 骨干。

**相关性简评：** 核心在「证据需求决定推理路径」与「按 BEV 索引筛选支持视图」两点，与视觉证据筛选、证据获取和计算分配的研究兴趣直接相关；但其状态是显式度量记忆而非 latent，可供核查的是证据路由与视图选取策略，以及固定 16 帧预算下的收益—成本权衡能否迁移到 LVR。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BEV3SR: Evidence-Aware 3D Spatial Reasoning from Video

**abstract/A1：** 3D spatial reasoning from video remains challenging for VLMs because videos provide sequential, view-local observations rather than persistent metric scene records. Many questions must be answered in a query-specified perspective rather than the observed camera viewpoint, and prompting with a fixed set of RGB views may miss sparse or occluded visual cues. To address these gaps, we introduce BEV3SR, a training-free and backbone-agnostic framework for RGB-D videos with camera parameters. BEV3SR turns video observations into queryable metric bird's-eye-view (BEV) scene memory and formalizes each query into an evidence-aware spatial task. The Spatial Task Formalizer specifies the query's reference frame, spatial constraints, objective, and evidence requirement. The evidence requirement determines the appropriate reasoning pathway. A query treated as requiring geometric evidence is handled by the Geometric Reasoner, which deterministically computes the answer using the metric BEV records in the query-specified reference frame. Otherwise, a query treated as requiring visual evidence is passed to the Visual Evidence Aligner, which uses BEV-indexed object records and their associated supporting views to retrieve object-consistent and scene-context RGB frames for VLM grounding. Within a standard 16-frame budget, BEV3SR consistently improves different VLM backbones across multiple 3D scene QA benchmarks, delivering large gains on spatially intensive questions and significant improvements on open-ended scene QA. These results demonstrate that BEV3SR offers an effective, efficient, and interpretable framework for explicit 3D spatial reasoning from video.

**comment/C1：** WACV camera ready

</details>

<a id="paper-9"></a>

### 9. [AlterNet: Adaptive-Depth Mixture-of-Experts with Learned Halting for Elastic Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08741v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08741

**作者：** Alexander Kastius

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08741v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08741v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种把浅层专家 MoE 与动态深度结合的架构，通过迭代计算、序列级深度路由、专家内 halting 机制与深度编码，使前向计算量随任务自适应，并可在训练后按执行环境缩放而不重训。

**相关性简评：** 其可学习停止、深度编码与“按需分配计算”的正则化，可供核查 LVR 中 THINK/STOP 的停止决策、循环状态更新与计算预算—收益权衡；但论文为纯文本架构且仅给初步基准，迁移到多模态 latent 推理仍属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AlterNet: Adaptive-Depth Mixture-of-Experts with Learned Halting for Elastic Inference

**abstract/A1：** Most LLM architectures have fixed compute demands determined at training time. We propose a novel architecture that combines the Mixture-of-Experts architecture with shallow experts and a novel dynamic-depth system to make the extent of a forward pass dynamically adjustable. Our approach lets the model automatically determine the compute needed for a given task, paired with a system to scale it down after training without retraining when the execution environment demands faster or cheaper inference. To achieve this, we introduce an iterative computation mechanism, sequence-level depth-based routing steered by a halting mechanism within the experts, a depth encoding that lets the model track its progress in the chain, and a regularization mechanism that incentivizes the model to use allocated compute efficiently. We present initial benchmarks and discuss future research directions.

</details>

<a id="paper-10"></a>

### 10. [LEF-JEPA: Denoising-Trained Latent Energy Fields for Spatial Representation Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08989v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08989

**作者：** Zhiyuan Liu、Zhihao Xu、Sicheng Liu、Huanhuan Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08989v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08989v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LEF-JEPA：在 JEPA 框架下用「目标引导的 latent 恢复」配合相对能量约束学习空间结构，并将监督转移到视觉编码器；能量模块仅用于预训练、不增加推理开销，还用条件 Ising 哈密顿量把低能量态搜索交给专用求解器以降低预训练成本。

**相关性简评：** 论文关注的「既要保留空间结构又不过度编码纹理颜色等低层细节」与 LVR 中视觉细节保留和信息坍缩的权衡直接相关，其仅训练期使用的外部约束可为 latent 状态监督与贡献分配提供可核查的借鉴线索，但本身不涉及多模态推理或推理期 latent 生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LEF-JEPA: Denoising-Trained Latent Energy Fields for Spatial Representation Learning

**abstract/A1：** A joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) that applies prediction supervision only at masked positions struggles to clearly capture object shapes and boundaries in its latent representations. We further observe that some existing methods improve spatial structure while also retaining rich variations in texture and color. However, preserving more low-level visual information does not necessarily make representations better suited to prediction and action. This raises a key question: how can we strengthen the spatial structure in latent representations that supports prediction and action without over-encoding these details? We introduce LEF-JEPA, which learns spatial structure through target-guided latent recovery and transfers this supervision to the visual encoder through a relative-energy constraint; the energy module is used only during pretraining and adds no inference overhead. To reduce the cost of energy optimization during pretraining, we further construct a conditional Ising Hamiltonian that allows low-energy-state search to be performed by specialized Ising solvers. On physical hardware, we validate the feasibility of achieving lower forward latency with fewer local updates. Under a unified CIFAR-10 pretraining and frozen-encoder evaluation protocol, both realizations outperform V-JEPA2.1 in image classification, foreground segmentation, and visual planning.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 7 figures, 7 tables. Includes supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-11"></a>

### 11. [Definitions, Label Names, or Demonstrations? Auditing What Governs LLM Annotation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09457v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09457

**作者：** Viraj Mishra、Shubham Gupta、Dhruv Gupta、Yuehong Cassandra Tai、Qunhua Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09457v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09457v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一种反事实审计：把类别定义在输出标签间重新分配，从而区分 LLM 标注时是遵循研究者给定的定义，还是沿用数据集的原有标签映射，并在三个政治文本数据集及检索增强设定下检验定义、标签名与示例各自的作用。

**相关性简评：** 其「替换所提供的证据、观察输出是否随之改变」的干预设计可供核查并借鉴到 VLM，用于判断推理答案究竟依赖视觉证据还是语言先验与标签关联，即视觉旁路与证据使用问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Definitions, Label Names, or Demonstrations? Auditing What Governs LLM Annotation

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to annotate social-science texts, but high accuracy does not establish that they apply researcher-supplied category definitions: predictions may instead reflect prior associations with category labels or labeled demonstrations. We introduce a counterfactual audit that reassigns definitions among output labels, distinguishing adherence to supplied definitions from preservation of a dataset's original mapping. Across three political-text datasets, models rarely follow reassigned definitions, and standard fine-tuning strengthens the original mapping without reliably increasing definition adherence. We then study retrieval-augmented annotation. Retrieving the nearest training examples generally outperforms retrieving examples separately within each category despite using fewer demonstrations. On the two tasks with substantial gains, direct \\(k\\)-nearest-neighbor classification recovers 74--93\\% of the improvement, indicating that neighborhood labels provide much of the predictive signal. Relabeling fixed demonstrations redirects predictions, whereas aligning demonstrations with reassigned definitions substantially increases adherence to the new mapping. Thus, accuracy-improving interventions need not increase codebook adherence; definitions, category labels, demonstrations, retrieval, and training data should be aligned and validated jointly.

</details>

<a id="paper-12"></a>

### 12. [Maya a1: Do the Options Read the Model, or Does Their Position? Order Sensitivity in a Non-Autoregressive Decision Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09803v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09803

**作者：** Mohammed Nayeem Khan、Logesh L

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09803v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09803v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文重新工程化非自回归决策模型 Laya（421M，一次前向在每个选项的 \[MASK\] 位置读取隐状态打分），在 951 道题的全部循环置换下检验答案是否随选项位置改变：25.8% 的题答案翻转，gold 在首位与末位时准确率差 10.1 点，翻转集中在低置信度题；用打乱选项的 2818 行微调把翻转率降到 7.2%，而置信度加权多顺序投票以 4.1 倍算力仅从 69.1% 提到 69.7%。

**相关性简评：** 这是对「latent 读出究竟读内容还是读位置」的直接诊断，与 LVR 中 latent 打分的位置混淆、并行多 marker 的注意力结构及顺序不变训练相关，可供核查；但材料为纯文本设定，迁移到视觉多模态读出需研究者自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Maya a1: Do the Options Read the Model, or Does Their Position? Order Sensitivity in a Non-Autoregressive Decision Model

**abstract/A1：** Laya answers typed multiple-choice questions in one forward pass by scoring a hidden state at one \[MASK\] marker per option. We re-engineer the checkpoint (421M parameters; a 2-layer option head with no positional tensor; a documented but unused parallel layout) and test whether the answer depends on where an option is listed. Presenting 951 Qwen-verified questions under every cyclic rotation of their options (or six random permutations for 12 options), the released checkpoint changes its answer for 25.8% \[22.9-28.7\] of questions (19.7% on the 605 English-language items alone); accuracy with the gold option first versus last differs by 10.1 points, and the flips concentrate where the model's own confidence is low (54% versus 8% across confidence terciles). Fine-tuning with Laya's trainer on 2,818 teacher-verified rows with option shuffling cuts the flip rate to 7.2%; the identical rows without shuffling give 11.4%, so shuffling is responsible for 4.2 points of the improvement. A confidence-weighted vote over orderings gives 69.7% against 69.1% for a single base pass at 4.1x the compute. Labels are LLM-made and the test sets are teacher-filtered. We also fine-tune with the authors' parallel layout on the same rows: flip rate 0.5%.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 13 figures, 23 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-13"></a>

### 13. [A Tight Hierarchy for Chain of Thought](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09759v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09759

**作者：** Thanh Le、A. Pavan、N. V. Vinodchandran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09759v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09759v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在 transformer 加 CoT 的计算复杂性框架下证明首个层级定理：对任意 r(n)≥n 与 ε&gt;0，CoT(r(n)) 严格包含于 CoT(r(n)^{1+ε})，并借助与图灵机的双向粗粒度模拟及改造的 padding 技术完成证明。

**相关性简评：** 其将 CoT 步数刻画为严格递增的计算资源，可为 LVR 的动态计算预算、THINK/STOP 决策与长度泛化提供理论参照；但该工作为纯文本且不涉及 latent 状态，具体可迁移性仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Tight Hierarchy for Chain of Thought

**abstract/A1：** Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for extending the computational capabilities of transformer-based models. A growing body of recent work has begun to uncover the fine-grained computational power of transformers augmented with CoT, as well as their connections to classical complexity theory. In this work, we advance this line of research by establishing the first hierarchy theorem for CoT-based computation. Our main result shows that for any reasonable resource bound $r(n) \\geq n$ and any $\\varepsilon &gt; 0$, \\\[ \\mathrm{CoT}(r(n)) \\subsetneq \\mathrm{CoT}(r(n)^{1+\\varepsilon}), \\\] where $\\mathrm{CoT}(r(n))$ denotes the class of decision problems solvable by transformers using $O(r(n))$ CoT reasoning steps on inputs of length $n$. Our result shows that increasing the number of CoT reasoning steps provably yields strictly greater computational power, establishing CoT steps as a fundamental resource in transformer-based computation. Unlike classical hierarchy theorems, which are typically proved via diagonalization, our approach leverages known coarse-grained simulations between CoT-augmented transformers and Turing machines in both directions. Building on these connections, we adapt the classical padding technique from complexity theory to this setting to obtain a tight hierarchy. A key technical contribution of our work is the implementation of padding within the transformer-based CoT model.

</details>

<a id="paper-14"></a>

### 14. [Embedding Consistent Recurrent Neural Networks in Finite Element Simulations for Path-Dependent Damage Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08494v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08494

**作者：** Muhammed Adil Yatkın、Mihkel Kõrgesaar、Hasan Kurban

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08494v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08494v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将训练好的 RNN 损伤准则用 Fortran 重写并嵌入 Abaqus/Explicit 的 VUMAT，作为逐步更新的断裂准则运行，比较 SimpleRNN 与所提 ConsRNN；后者通过改造状态转移函数使预测随应变增量细分而收敛，但固定分辨率精度明显下降。

**相关性简评：** ConsRNN 对增量步数细化的收敛性设计可为 latent 循环推理在循环次数、计算步长变化下的状态稳定性和长度泛化提供核查线索，但其对象是有限元材料本构而非视觉多模态推理，迁移性需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Embedding Consistent Recurrent Neural Networks in Finite Element Simulations for Path-Dependent Damage Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Capturing sheet-metal localisation physics with a trained surrogate inside a finite element (FEM) solver requires the surrogate to deliver consistent predictions irrespective of the solver's strain-increment count, which is difficult to control in non-linear explicit codes. We address this by re-implementing a trained recurrent neural network (RNN) damage criterion in Fortran and embed it as a live, increment-by-increment fracture criterion inside an Abaqus/Explicit user material subroutine (VUMAT), advancing the network state alongside the solver. Two architectures are compared: a SimpleRNN and the proposed Consistent RNN (ConsRNN), whose transition function is designed so that predictions converge as the strain path is refined rather than drifting with the increment count. Both are trained on bilinear strain paths and evaluated under varying temporal discretisations and nonlinear histories. SimpleRNN predictions drift as the increment count increases, which disqualifies it for embedding; ConsRNN converges under refinement, at a substantial fixed-resolution accuracy cost relative to SimpleRNN on multilinear paths. Deployed at structural scale, the embedded ConsRNN surrogate governs element deletion in a clamped steel plate and reaches the same peak force and peak displacement as an established two-parameter fracture criterion. This pre-peak agreement is expected by construction, since no element is deleted before the peak and the two runs therefore share an identical constitutive path; the responses diverge only on the post-peak softening branch. The results establish discretisation consistency as the decisive property for embedding recurrent surrogates in explicit FEM solvers, and report the first embedding of a customised, non-standard-transition recurrent cell at structural scale.

**comment/C1：** Journal paper. 22 pages, 16 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-15"></a>

### 15. [Subliminal Learning in Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08538v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08538

**作者：** Arman Hatami、Romina Aalishah、Ilya E. Monosov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08538v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08538v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在 2B–7B 的 VLM 中微调 teacher 使其偏好某种动物或面部属性，让其在与该偏好无关的几何图形任务上生成数字，再用这些数字微调同源 student，检验隐藏偏好是否迁移，并比较 student 解释用词与 biased teacher 的重合度。

**相关性简评：** 「控制教师 + 无关任务输出」的对照设计可供核查 LVR 中用教师轨迹或推理数据训练时，监督信号是否夹带与任务无关的隐藏属性或被 latent 状态吸收；其对答案选择与解释文本的分层评测也提示需区分表述变化与内部状态的实际改变。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Subliminal Learning in Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Subliminal learning is a phenomenon in large language models (LLMs) where a teacher passes a hidden preference to a student through training data unrelated to that preference. We study whether subliminal learning also occurs in vision-language models (VLMs). We finetune a teacher VLM to prefer a specific animal or facial attribute and use a control teacher from the same base model. Both teachers generate numbers for an unrelated visual task involving geometric shapes. Two students initialized from the same base VLM are finetuned on the same images, using numbers generated by either the biased or control teacher. Across VLMs ranging from 2B to 7B parameters, we find that both animal and facial preferences can transfer through this unrelated task. Students trained on biased-teacher outputs select the target more often than control students, with a difference of up to $\\sim$10 percentage points in the animal preference task. Transfer can become stronger with more training data and varies with the target preference, model, and how the numbers are generated. Beyond preferences, we show that the biased student's explanations use more of the same words and phrases as the biased teacher's explanations than those of the control student, even when all three models select the same image. These results matter for training VLMs and show that training on unrelated data may change both a VLM's image preferences and how it explains its choices, and that transfer can become stronger with more training samples.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-16"></a>

### 16. [How Small Can We Go? Calibrating Activation-Cache Compression for SAE Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08551v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08551

**作者：** Abraham Yeung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08551v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08551v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察激活缓存压缩（fp16、int4、PCA、随机投影等）对 SAE 特征保留的影响，用「冻结 SAE」与「重训 SAE」两种测试衡量，并以同字节重训为上限、换随机种子为重下界来校准各站点的比较基准。

**相关性简评：** 其「上限/下界校准」思路与缓存精度对内部特征保真度的影响，或可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否保留任务必要信息时的度量选择与种子/精度混杂；但论文不含视觉推理任务，迁移需自行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Small Can We Go? Calibrating Activation-Cache Compression for SAE Training

**abstract/A1：** To train a sparse autoencoder (SAE), you need a massive cache of model activations. We look into the effects that compression has on an SAE in two ways: we keep the original fp32-trained SAE and pass the compressed cache into the same SAE to see how many feature activations stay the same (the frozen-SAE test), and we retrain an SAE on the compressed cache and see how many of the original features still exist (the retraining test). We found that in the retraining test, there is no clear scale for retained features. On a GPT-2 cache, rounding everything to fp16 keeps 60.6% of features, but changing one value in a million by the smallest step fp32 allows (a change eight orders of magnitude smaller) keeps only 70.5%. Meanwhile, changing the random seed keeps 23.0%. For the frozen-SAE test, the frozen SAE's correlation with the original increases in order of how precise the codecs are, but plain $L\_2$ distance gives nearly the same ordering. We also see the same frozen reading correspond to different percentages retained at different sites. This leaves the question of the right measurement. We build one by calibrating: retraining on identical bytes sets the ceiling, and a reseed sets the floor. We then measure the effect of each compressed codec against its own site's floor. With TopK SAEs on GPT-2 small and Pythia-1.4B at research-scale budgets, naïve per-token int4 loses more features than a reseed everywhere we tested, as do PCA and random projection on GPT-2. Standardizing each channel before quantizing brings int4 back above the floor in terms of retained features, at the same raw file size. At our smallest budget, a bf16 forward pass, which some pipelines use to save memory, loses slightly more features than rotated 8-bit storage. Calibrating a site costs four training runs and two evaluation passes.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Interpretability as a Science (InterpScience), non-archival. 27 pages, 5 figures, 30 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-17"></a>

### 17. [BEVCast: Path-Adaptive BEV for Efficient End-to-End Autonomous Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08631v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08631

**作者：** Qiren Wang、Mingyu Guo、Zixuan Wang、Weisong Shi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08631v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08631v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BEVCast 两阶段 BEV 表示：先用 4.096 m 粗网格编码全场景，再由按记录轨迹训练的 heat head 在固定预算下挑出高热度单元细化为 1.024 m，轨迹头读取混合分辨率 BEV，从而在减少 BEV query 与编码时间的同时保持或改善规划误差。

**相关性简评：** 其「任务头打分＋固定预算」决定哪些区域获得高分辨率计算，与 LVR 中按任务必要性筛选视觉证据、动态分配计算量的思路可能相通，可供核查该选择机制能否迁移到 latent 视觉状态的分辨率或 token 预算分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BEVCast: Path-Adaptive BEV for Efficient End-to-End Autonomous Driving

**abstract/A1：** Camera-based driving models build a dense bird's-eye-view (BEV) grid and spend the same computation on every cell of it, although the vehicle crosses only a few of those cells within the planning horizon. We present BEVCast, a two-stage BEV whose resolution adapts to the predicted driving path. A coarse pass encodes the whole scene on a grid of 4.096 m cells. A heat head trained on the recorded path assigns a heat to every coarse cell and the cells with the highest heat are refined into 1.024 m cells under a fixed budget. The remaining cells keep their coarse resolution, so the trajectory head reads a mixed-resolution BEV that still covers the entire scene and needs neither an HD map nor a navigation command. Against a full grid of 1.024 m cells with the same architecture and training recipe, BEVCast at a 10% refinement budget encodes 28% of the BEV queries and needs 66% of the BEV encoder time at batch size 1. On nuScenes its mean displacement error is 1.078 m against 1.149 m and its drivable area violation rate is 1.46% against 2.29%. On NAVSIM navtest its mean displacement error is 0.577 m against 0.598 m and its extended predictive driver model score is 82.51 against 81.28. Next to published planners BEVCast reaches a comparable error on both benchmarks without a navigation command. The resolution of a driving BEV can therefore follow the task instead of the grid.

</details>

<a id="paper-18"></a>

### 18. [Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments for Test-Time Selection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08640v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08640

**作者：** Hannah Guan、Yilun Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08640v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08640v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments（APT）：在 test-time 让多个现成方面验证器按自然语言指定的属性对候选解做两两比较，再以单败淘汰赛选出最终答案，并在多领域和多生成模型上验证其优于多种基线。

**相关性简评：** 该工作不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，但其按方面分解的两两验证与淘汰选择机制，可能为 LVR 中候选推理轨迹的筛选、停止决策及验证器使用提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments for Test-Time Selection

**abstract/A1：** Scaling test-time compute has proven highly effective at improving LLM reasoning. A popular method is best-of-n sampling, where a model generates many candidate solutions and one is selected. This motivates the question: how can we select the best candidate? When humans make choices, we find it more natural to compare two options than judge each in isolation. Additionally, when the options become complex, we like to judge the options one aspect at a time, such as comparing two job candidates on experience, communication, etc. In the test-time setting, candidate solutions should be judged in the same way. Here we introduce Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments (APT), which verifies by comparison and compares by aspect. Each matchup of a single elimination tournament is decided by a vote over multiple off-the-shelf aspect verifiers, each expressing a pairwise preference along its own aspect. Across five domains and three generator LLMs, APT improves over binary multi-aspect approval, self-consistency and reward model verification, with the largest gains on open-ended tasks. We find that both components matter; neither binary approval over the same aspects nor a generic pairwise judge at a matched budget matches APT. Because aspects are specified in natural language, APT also lets users steer selection toward properties they care about, better aligning with user preferences without needing to regenerate solutions. No component is trained, so APT transfers to new domains by simply rewriting prompts.

</details>

<a id="paper-19"></a>

### 19. [VoluCompose: Constructing Compact Volumetric Visual Representations for Medical Image Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08649v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08649

**作者：** Zanting Ye、Yunrui Lin、Shengyuan Liu、Jing Hao、Chen Hu、Yefeng Zheng、Lijun Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08649v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08649v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VoluCompose，一个无需训练、模型无关的医学体数据视觉 token 压缩框架：将「保留哪些内容」与「分配多少 token」解耦，用双曲空间 Lorentz 距离把连续切片组合为有序支撑单元并指导 token 分配与锚点选择，同时依据视觉编码器注意力熵在注意力分散时分配更多 token，在约 22.78% token 预算下较好保持输出质量。

**相关性简评：** 其内容选择与预算分配分离、以注意力熵驱动 token 容量的做法，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与动态计算分配提供可核查线索，但不涉及 latent 推理状态的生成或使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VoluCompose: Constructing Compact Volumetric Visual Representations for Medical Image Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Medical vision--language models can interpret volumetric images, but processing full volumes is costly because hundreds of slices produce long, redundant visual-token sequences. Existing compression methods often couple content selection with budget determination. We observe that neighboring slices share visual features and that attention concentration varies across visual encoders. We propose VoluCompose, a training-free, model-agnostic framework that separates what visual content to retain from how many tokens to allocate. Its hyperbolic token composition module groups contiguous slices into ordered support units and uses the Lorentz distance to account for feature magnitude and direction during token allocation, anchor selection, and refinement. Its capacity control module uses visual-encoder attention entropy to allocate more tokens when attention is more dispersed. We evaluate VoluCompose on two tasks across three datasets and four backbones. Across nine calibrated settings, VoluCompose attains the highest mean quality--retention hypervolume among the compared methods. It achieves a mean relative quality of 121.68\\% against the uncompressed references while retaining 22.78% of visual tokens on average. Compression remains robust even at a 10% token budget. Across settings, the lowest ROUGE-L ratio to the uncompressed reference is 96.4\\% for VoluCompose. The corresponding minima for the compression baselines range from 15.0% to 70.5%. Code is available at https://github.com/yezanting/VoluCompose.

</details>

<a id="paper-20"></a>

### 20. [Turkish Multimodal Video Fact-Checking: A New Dataset and a Comparison of Zero-Shot Agentic and Fine-Tuned Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08651v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08651

**作者：** Azra Açıl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08651v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08651v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建首个土耳其语多模态视频事实核查数据集 TeViD，并比较两种共享同一开源基座的验证流水线：多角色 agentic 的 3MFact-TR（含语义不一致检查、5W1H 问题管理、双语证据检索）与基于 LoRA 的闭卷微调模型。

**相关性简评：** 其闭卷微调仅用主张与视频文本描述产出判定，可作为核查「视觉旁路／视觉证据是否真正被使用」的对照线索；agentic 流水线的证据检索与问题管理也可能为证据获取与停止决策提供参照。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Turkish Multimodal Video Fact-Checking: A New Dataset and a Comparison of Zero-Shot Agentic and Fine-Tuned Systems

**abstract/A1：** Video-based misinformation spreads rapidly on social media, but almost all existing fact-checking systems focus exclusively on English text. Tools for low-resource languages such as Turkish are severely limited. In this work, we present TeViD (Turkish Video Fact-checking Dataset), to the best of our knowledge the first multimodal video fact-checking dataset for Turkish. We compiled the dataset from articles published by Teyit.org, an IFCN-certified organisation, and enriched it with translated Snopes articles and synthetic examples to reduce class imbalance. Building on TeViD, we develop two verification pipelines that share the same open-source base model. First, in 3MFact-TR (Pipeline 1), we adapt the multi-role 3MFact framework to Turkish with the Qwen2.5-7B large language model and the Qwen2-VL-7B vision-language model, and add three modules of our own: a semantic inconsistency checker, a 5W1H question manager, and a bilingual evidence retriever that excludes fact-checking sites to prevent leakage. Second, as our Fine-Tuned Model (Pipeline 2), we fine-tune a single Qwen2.5-7B with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to produce closed-book verdicts from the claim and the video's textual description. We report accuracy, macro F1, True class F1, and True class recall for both systems, together with ablation studies.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables. MSc dissertation, Queen Mary University of London, 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-21"></a>

### 21. [Higher Order Component Attribution via Learned Surrogates in High Energy Physics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08656v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08656

**作者：** Lauri Laatu、Aritra Bal、Benedikt Maier、Markus Klute、Michael Spannowsky

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、hep-ex、hep-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08656v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08656v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在高能物理喷注识别中，用 KAN 学习代理模型，把 Particle Transformer 内部组件（如多头注意力）的贡献归因到物理描述量，以检验这些机制是否学到了可判别特征。

**相关性简评：** 其基于代理模型的组件级归因思路可能可供核查，用于判断 LVR 中注意力或内部组件是否真正使用视觉证据，以及推理贡献如何在模块间分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Higher Order Component Attribution via Learned Surrogates in High Energy Physics

**abstract/A1：** Experimental High Energy Physics (HEP) has adopted the transformer architecture for identification of jets, particularly the state-of-the-art Particle Transformer (ParT) which injects explicit information about particle correlations into the attention mechanism. While able to achieve state-of-the-art results in accuracy, interpretability to its effectiveness has been limited. In this paper we propose component attribution methods using Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) that directly tie back to the physical descriptors of jets, to probe whether mechanisms such as Multi-Head Attention (MHA), or the ParT architecture, are able to learn these as discriminating features.

</details>

<a id="paper-22"></a>

### 22. [JEPAhead: From Inpainting to Causal Forecasting for Urban Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08657v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08657

**作者：** Daniel G. P. Petrini、Davi Pincinato、Fabio Grassiotto、Pedro L. F. Prado、Alexandre F. S. Osorio、Raphael V. Ramos、Felipe Pamboukian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08657v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08657v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文冻结 V-JEPA 编码器，将预训练预测器由双向 latent inpainting 微调为基于连续过去观测的因果预测，一次前向输出对应 16 帧的 8 组未来 token，并用候选检索、语义线性探针与像素指标评估预测质量。

**相关性简评：** 其「检索命中率＋固定线性探针」衡量 latent 是否携带任务必要信息，可供核查 LVR 中预测状态的内容保真度，group-level 因果注意力与 horizon 对齐目标也可能对预测式工作区训练有线索；但任务是驾驶视频预测，与 VLM 推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** JEPAhead: From Inpainting to Causal Forecasting for Urban Driving

**abstract/A1：** We fine-tune a pre-trained V-JEPA1 predictor from bidirectional latent inpainting to action-free causal forecasting while keeping the encoder frozen. Using contiguous past observations, horizon-aligned targets, and group-level causal attention, the model forecasts eight future token groups, corresponding to sixteen frames, in one forward pass. On CARLA Town12, it retrieves the correct future among 591 candidates in 69.0% of cases, compared with 55.3% for persistence, with greater gains at longer horizons and on tokens exhibiting greater change. It also outperforms a compact predictor trained from scratch with six times fewer parameters. Semantic analysis indicates driving-relevant information in predicted features: a fixed linear probe trained on real encoder tokens achieves 0.16 mIoU on patches undergoing semantic-class changes, versus 0.13 for persistence. Vehicle-conditioned retrieval improves over persistence by 15 percentage points at the longest horizon. Pixel-space evaluation shows modest L1 gains but little improvement under LPIPS, motivating direct evaluation through latent and semantic measures alongside an oracle reconstruction reference. These findings support further investigation of latent forecasting for driving perception, while downstream utility remains unverified.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-23"></a>

### 23. [ChainFlow: Few-Step Latent Flow Matching for Speculative Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08669v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08669

**作者：** Selim Aktas、Yusuf Erdem Erdem、Ahmed Zeer、Himmet Toprak Kesgin、Muzaffer Kaan Yuce、Mehmet Fatih Amasyali

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08669v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08669v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ChainFlow 将 speculative decoding 的草稿生成视为隐状态空间上的短程 flow matching 传输，仅用一到两步即可从目标模型隐状态产生候选 token，并用 VAE 压缩隐状态、轻量反馈头恢复 token 间依赖，配合树式草稿提升接受率。

**相关性简评：** 该文以少步 flow 在隐状态上做并行状态更新并用反馈头补回序列依赖，可为 LVR 中「如何并行生成或更新 latent 推理状态、并以少步传输替代多步去噪」提供方法线索，但其研究对象是纯文本 LLM 的草稿加速、无视觉证据筛选，需核查隐状态传输是否保留任务必要信息。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ChainFlow: Few-Step Latent Flow Matching for Speculative Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Speculative decoding has become a standard way to accelerate LLM inference: a lightweight drafter proposes tokens that the target verifies in one parallel pass. Existing drafters face trade-offs that limits their speedups: autoregressive drafters generate tokens one at a time, so drafting cost grows with draft length, while diffusion drafters generate tokens in parallel but predict each position independently, ignoring inter-token dependencies. We introduce ChainFlow, a new speculative decoding paradigm and the first flow-matching drafter conditioned on the target's hidden states. ChainFlow treats drafting as a short transport over hidden states, needing one or two flow steps instead of the many denoising steps of generation from noise. A variational autoencoder compresses these states so drafting cost barely grows with target size, lightweight feedback heads restore inter-token dependencies, and tree drafting raises acceptance. On Qwen3.5 models, at matched drafter size and training data, ChainFlow outperforms EAGLE-3 and DFlash in vLLM by 39% and 11%, respectively. These gains further widen as drafters are adapted to new languages sequentially. Our ablations cover drafter design and concurrent serving, and we open-source our code and drafter models.

</details>

<a id="paper-24"></a>

### 24. [Dual-Stream LLM-Guided Reward Design for Reject-Inference-Aware Reinforcement Learning in E-Commerce Payment Fraud Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08706v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08706

**作者：** Bo Qu、Zhurong Wang、Frank Zahradnik、Ming Ouyang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08706v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08706v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将 LLM 引导的奖励设计用于电商支付欺诈检测中的 reject-inference 问题：单一 LLM 以可审计的代码形式写出双流奖励，一流对已标注交易施加金额加权精度目标，另一流借助新模型与旧评分系统的对比为被旧系统拦截的无标注交易打分。

**相关性简评：** 以 LLM 搜索可读奖励代码、并用代理信号为不可观测结果补监督，与 LVR 中 latent 步骤的贡献分配和验证器/奖励设计可能有共通之处，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dual-Stream LLM-Guided Reward Design for Reject-Inference-Aware Reinforcement Learning in E-Commerce Payment Fraud Detection

**abstract/A1：** Production fraud-detection models learn from a censored slice of their own traffic: transactions a legacy system blocked never receive fraud labels, so models train on a biased, approved-only distribution. We extend LLM-guided reward design, the framework of Qu et al. (2025) in which an LLM evolves the reward of a two-step REINFORCE fraud-blocking policy, to attack this reject-inference problem inside the reward. A single LLM designs a dual-stream reward: stream 1 applies the standard dollar-weighted precision objective to labeled transactions; stream 2 scores legacy-blocked, unlabeled transactions by contrasting the newest gradient-boosted risk signal against the legacy scores that drove the original block. Rather than prescribe which signals matter, we expose a superset of evidence and let the LLM decide for itself which to use, guided by evaluation feedback alone; because it writes the reward as code, that choice is directly auditable. On a held-out four-month out-of-time test, the dual-stream agent beats the prior labeled-only agent at every target recall, by +4.7, +2.4 and +3.0 points of dollar-weighted precision at 80%, 85% and 90% recall, and the production baseline by +6.8 points on average. The resulting policy is architecturally identical to that of Qu et al. (2025) and adds no inference cost.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 1 figure, 3 tables. Extends Qu et al., ACL 2025 Industry Track: https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-industry.9/

</details>

<a id="paper-25"></a>

### 25. [An Educated Guess: Deriving Statistically Aligned Gradient Estimates for Zeroth-Order Optimisation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08712v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08712

**作者：** Justus Huebotter、Serge Thill、Marcel van Gerven、Nasir Ahmad

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08712v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08712v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Spectral Zeroth-Order 方法族 SZO-κ，用前向传播中可得的激活几何构造零阶优化的搜索方向，替代盲随机扰动，并在多层与卷积网络上分析其梯度对齐、偏差-方差结构和训练稳定性。

**相关性简评：** 其「用内部激活几何引导梯度估计」的思路，可能为 latent 推理多步轨迹中的贡献分配与免反传训练监督提供线索，值得核查该方向构造能否用于 latent 状态的更新信号，但原文并不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An Educated Guess: Deriving Statistically Aligned Gradient Estimates for Zeroth-Order Optimisation

**abstract/A1：** As novel hardware architectures are explored for the purposes of machine learning, including neuromorphic chips, asynchronous systems, and ASICs, effective learning algorithms are highly desirable. While backpropagation is the standard for differentiation on standard hardware, its requirement for global synchrony and exact differentiability limits the exploration of exotic algorithms on non-traditional substrates and devices. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimisation methods, such as SPSA and weight perturbation, offer a compelling alternative by estimating gradients through inference-only passes; however, these methods have historically suffered due to the \`curse of dimensionality', where random perturbations become increasingly misaligned with the true gradient in high-dimensional spaces. In this work, we move beyond blind random noise by deriving zeroth-order search directions from activation geometry available during the forward pass. This leads to a family of Spectral Zeroth-Order methods, SZO-$κ$, whose directions solve local response-based gradient-guessing objectives under explicit stochastic assumptions. These methods explore feature-supported directions that are more likely to produce informative loss responses. Using multilayer and convolutional networks, we analyse gradient alignment, bias-variance structure, and training stability of these methods relative to standard perturbation baselines and backpropagation.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 15 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-26"></a>

### 26. [Patching the Blind Spots: Reversible LLM Rule Overlay for Frozen Fraud Detectors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08714v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08714

**作者：** Bo Qu、Zhurong Wang、Frank Zahradnik、Ming Ouyang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08714v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08714v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文不改动已部署的树模型欺诈检测器，让 LLM 针对 SHAP 显示被低估的特征提出规则，经经验支持筛选与校准后形成可加、可逆的 log-lift 覆盖层，并用三个骨干等权 logit 平均压低单模型方差。

**相关性简评：** 「冻结主干＋外部提议→筛选→校准→可逆覆盖」以及多提议者平均换取有界最差情况的思路，可能为 LVR 中验证并利用 latent 内容、按贡献分配外部修正提供可供核查的线索；但任务为表格欺诈检测，不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Patching the Blind Spots: Reversible LLM Rule Overlay for Frozen Fraud Detectors

**abstract/A1：** In industrial tabular fraud classification, gradient-boosted trees remain state-of-the-art, but even a well-trained model has blind spots, and retraining the deployed model is not always safe. We propose a framework that patches such a model without modifying it: LLMs propose rules anchored on features the model underweights by SHAP, and the resulting noisy pool is screened for empirical support and calibrated into an additive, reversible log-lift overlay that runs at the production cut-off. Single-LLM benefit is high-variance and inverts between datasets, so we pool three diverse backbones by equal-weight logit averaging, trading peak gain for a bounded worst case. On a real-world e-commerce fraud detector trained on selection-biased data, continued training regresses even when carefully tuned, whereas the overlay gains on average +2.26% recall at equal precision ($Δ$R@P) and +1.60% precision at equal recall ($Δ$P@R) over production cut-offs spanning 30-57% precision and 23-63% recall. On a public benchmark of seven designed bias-shift variants it raises $Δ$P@R on every variant and keeps $Δ$R@P near neutral. Each amendment is a readable predicate naming the fraud pattern it covers.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 1 figure, 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-27"></a>

### 27. [AttentionSwitch: Jailbreak Defense for LLMs via Joint Learning of Sparse Attention-Head Selection and Scaling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08752v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08752

**作者：** Doniyorkhon Obidov、Honggang Yu、Xiaolong Guo、Kaichen Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08752v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08752v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 AttentionSwitch，在冻结 LLM 上联合学习一组稀疏注意力头及其 query 条件化的乘性缩放因子，对选中头的输出做衰减或放大而不改变表示方向，用于越狱防御，并在数据稀缺时表现较好。

**相关性简评：** 其联合进行稀疏头定位与强度缩放的做法，可能为 LVR 中定位承载视觉证据的头部并做定向调制或贡献分配提供方法线索，但论文面向安全防御、无多模态推理实验，需核查其选择机制能否迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AttentionSwitch: Jailbreak Defense for LLMs via Joint Learning of Sparse Attention-Head Selection and Scaling

**abstract/A1：** Large language models have achieved remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks, yet they remain susceptible to jailbreak attacks that circumvent safety mechanisms and elicit harmful or unintended outputs. Although safety can be improved through model fine-tuning, such approaches can be computationally expensive and may degrade previously acquired capabilities through catastrophic forgetting. Recent jailbreak defenses have therefore explored targeted interventions on specific model components associated with unsafe behavior. However, existing approaches typically decouple two inherently related decisions: where to intervene and how to modify the selected components. Such sequential optimization may be suboptimal because the optimal intervention location can depend on the intervention applied there, and vice versa. In this work, we introduce AttentionSwitch, the first jailbreak defense to jointly optimize intervention sites and strengths within a frozen LLM. Specifically, AttentionSwitch learns a sparse set of attention heads together with query-conditioned multiplicative scaling factors, allowing the outputs of the selected heads to be either attenuated or amplified without altering the directions of their representations. Across popular language models and jailbreak attacks, AttentionSwitch outperforms existing defenses, with particularly strong results when training data is scarce.

</details>

<a id="paper-28"></a>

### 28. [Debate to Deceive: Poisoning LLM's Fact-Checking via Game-Theoretic Multi-Agent Debate](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08756v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08756

**作者：** Liting Huang、Huizhi Liang、Yi Lei、Xingjun Ma、Varun Ojha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08756v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08756v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Debate-to-Deceive，按人类辩论结构组织多智能体辩论并以演化博弈更新修辞策略，生成语义连贯且抗确认偏误的对抗段落来操纵 LLM 事实核查判断，在四个事实核查基准上优于现有攻击并规避多数防御。

**相关性简评：** 其将行为解耦为「紧凑策略层的博弈式选择＋LLM 生成层」，可能为 LVR 中离散潜状态选择、多假设竞争与验证提供结构线索；纯文本对抗设定能否迁移到视觉证据核查需进一步评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Debate to Deceive: Poisoning LLM's Fact-Checking via Game-Theoretic Multi-Agent Debate

**abstract/A1：** Knowledge attacks on Large Language Models, such as manipulating fact-checking judgments, pose significant risks to AI reliability. Existing approaches -- adversarial suffix/template injection and single-agent self-reflection -- lack robustness across diverse defenses and are prone to confirmation bias and costly trial-and-error, respectively. We propose Debate-to-Deceive (D2D), a multi-agent debate framework grounded in human debate structure and rhetorical style, enabling agents to generate adversarial passages that are semantically coherent, rhetorically effective, and resistant to early confirmation bias. D2D formalizes the adversarial process as an evolutionary game in which agents adaptively evolve their rhetorical strategies (Logos, Pathos, Ethos) through competition. To manage the intractably large LLM output space, we decouple agent behavior into a strategic layer, which selects a rhetorical action from a compact strategy space via evolutionary-inspired learning dynamics, and a generation layer, where the LLM produces a concrete passage conditioned on that strategy and the debate context. On four fact-checking benchmarks (FEVER, HealthVer, CLIMATE-FEVER, and SciFact), D2D substantially outperforms existing attacks and evades most defenses.

</details>

<a id="paper-29"></a>

### 29. [MEDALLION: Manifold Embedding Distillation and Attributions for Low-dimensional Latent InterpretatiON](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08767v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08767

**作者：** Vicky Jung、Claire M. He、Irene Chang、Genevera I. Allen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08767v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08767v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 MEDALLION 框架：用神经编码器蒸馏任意低维嵌入（如 t-SNE、UMAP），再借助现有神经特征归因方法解释是哪些原始特征塑造了该低维表示，并在 MNIST 与单细胞转录组数据上验证。

**相关性简评：** 「蒸馏嵌入 + 特征归因」的流程可供核查是否能用于定位哪些视觉证据或输入维度写入 VLM 的 latent 推理状态，但原文面向降维可视化而非多模态推理，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MEDALLION: Manifold Embedding Distillation and Attributions for Low-dimensional Latent InterpretatiON

**abstract/A1：** Dimensionality reduction is widely used to visualize high-dimensional data and guide scientific discovery, yet interpreting which original features contribute to low-dimensional representations remains challenging, particularly for black-box methods such as t-SNE and UMAP. We introduce \\textbf{MEDALLION} (Manifold Embedding Distillation and Attributions for Low-dimensional Latent InterpretatiON), a novel framework for interpreting low-dimensional embeddings through knowledge distillation and neural feature attribution. MEDALLION distills any given embedding with a neural encoder which we explain via a broad range of existing neural feature attribution methods. Through experiments on MNIST and applications in single-cell transcriptomics, we show MEDALLION provides informative feature attributions that cast additional insights into the underlying embedding and compare its performance with existing interpretability approaches.

</details>

### 30. [Jacobian-Causal Memory Control for Persistent Memory in Language Model Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08779v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08779

**作者：** Marvin Danig

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08779v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08779v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Jacobian-causal memory control（JCMC）：以共享行为测量空间中的预测影响签名刻画每次被授权的记忆干预，按目标效应与副作用预算选择干预，并在保留或回滚前比对预测与观测效应；同时给出局部二次近似、可达效应集合、选择遗憾及非可加交互的理论界，但报告无基准实测。

**相关性简评：** 「影响签名—效应预算—预测与观测校验—回滚」这一干预式框架，可能为 LVR 中 latent 更新的因果贡献分配与 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益成本权衡提供可核查线索；但该工作为纯文本记忆控制的纯理论稿，能否迁移到视觉推理状态尚待验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Jacobian-Causal Memory Control for Persistent Memory in Language Model Systems

**abstract/A1：** Persistent-memory systems choose not only which records are retrieved but which prior information can alter current inference. We propose Jacobian-causal memory control (JCMC), which represents each authorized memory intervention by a predicted influence signature in a shared behavioral measurement space, selects interventions against a target effect and side-effect budget, and compares predicted with observed effects before retaining or rolling back the intervention. Under explicit smoothness and bounded-estimation assumptions, we prove a quadratic local-approximation bound, characterize the target effects reachable by a set of signatures, bound selection regret under signature error, and bound non-additive interactions between simultaneous memories. The paper reports no benchmark measurements; it defines a paired counterfactual estimator and an evaluation protocol comparing JCMC with relevance, graph, and policy-filtered retrieval baselines. The central claim is narrowed or falsified if those simpler baselines match effect targeting, side-effect control, and rollback utility under equal model, memory, policy, and cost budgets.

**comment/C1：** Architecture and formal analysis preprint. Four conditional theorems; no benchmark measurements. Companion research essay: https://achiral.ai/research/jacobian-causal-memory-control

</details>

<a id="paper-31"></a>

### 31. [Not All Latents Are Equal: Importance-Weighted Latent Graph Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08788v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08788

**作者：** Biraaj Rout、Jay Revolinsky、Han Liu、Harry Shomer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08788v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08788v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对潜空间图生成性能落后于离散扩散的问题，发现少数潜维度主导图重建，提出以 decoder Jacobian 衡量的维度重要性对训练损失加权（LIR），方法模型无关且不增加参数或超参数。

**相关性简评：** 其用 Jacobian 相对影响度量并加权各 latent 单元的监督，可能为 LVR 中判断哪些 latent 状态真正承载任务必要信息、如何分配贡献与监督提供可供核查的度量线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Not All Latents Are Equal: Importance-Weighted Latent Graph Generation

**abstract/A1：** Graph generation has shown tremendous effectiveness in modeling complex scientific tasks ranging from molecule generation to optimized circuit design. Most recent advancements have developed models that learn to denoise noisy graphs using either discrete diffusion or flow matching. However, discrete graph diffusion scales quadratically with the number of nodes, making it difficult to use such models on larger graphs. On the other hand, latent graph generation instead seeks to denoise noisy graph latents, which scale better to more nodes due to their lower dimension. However, in practice, latent graph generation models lag behind their discrete counterparts in performance. We study this problem, finding that it is caused by the unequal distribution of that different latent dimensions have on graph reconstruction. the impact. We observe that the vast majority of impact is contained in only a smaller number of latent dimensions. Since equal emphasis is placed on reconstructing the entire latent in the loss function, existing models are optimized to reconstruct information irrelevant to the downstream task. To tackle this problem, we propose Latent Importance Reweighting ({\\bf LIR)} which weights each latent dimensions via it's importance to final graph reconstruction. Importance is calculated as the relative influence of a single latent unit on graph reconstruction, which is represented via the Jacobian of the decoder relative to the latent. Our method is model-agnostic and requires no additional model parameters or hyperparameters. Experiments show that LIR can boost performance and lead to faster training convergence when applied to two different autoencoders on various benchmark datasets. Code: https://github.com/biraaj/GraphGenLatentReweight

</details>

<a id="paper-32"></a>

### 32. [TopoSHAP: Shapley for Explainable and Efficient Topological Deep Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08789v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08789

**作者：** Mathilde Papillon、Nina Miolane

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08789v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08789v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TopoSHAP：用 Shapley 值给拓扑深度学习中的数据单元（如分子环、社交群体）分配贡献并抽取紧凑解释，另以 TopoSHAP-neighbor 评估邻域组件对性能的贡献，并据此做反向消除式架构搜索，在 GraphXAI 类基准上更快且解释恢复更好。

**相关性简评：** 其按单元/组件做 Shapley 贡献归因与基于归因的反向消除剪枝，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 步骤间的贡献分配提供可核查的度量思路，但原文并不涉及多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TopoSHAP: Shapley for Explainable and Efficient Topological Deep Learning

**abstract/A1：** Topological deep learning (TDL) extends learning on graphs, such as molecules or social networks, to higher-order structures, such as rings in a molecule or groups in a social network. Explaining which of these structures drive a model's predictions, and which architectural components matter most for its performance, remains challenging. We introduce TopoSHAP, an efficient and general explanation framework that addresses both questions across topological domains and model architectures. TopoSHAP-cell assigns Shapley values to topological data cells and extracts compact explanations. These explanations match or outperform graph-based counterparts on GraphXAI benchmark data while running up to 112$\\times$ faster. They also reveal advantageous topological feature design, leading to measured improvements in explanation recovery and predictive accuracy. On the architecture side, TopoSHAP-neighbor explains how neighborhoods contribute to model performance, and turns these evaluations into a backward-elimination search that identifies smaller, competitive architectures early in training. This is up to $11.3\\times$ cheaper than traditional TDL training sweeps.

</details>

<a id="paper-33"></a>

### 33. [MotionBench-XAI: Benchmarking What Shapes Shapley Explanations in Spatiotemporal Machine Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08797v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08797

**作者：** Mathilde Papillon、Louisa Cornelis、Nina Miolane

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08797v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08797v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 MotionBench-XAI 基准，利用五个具有精确 Shapley 真值的合成时空数据集和三个真实数据集（临床步态、ECG、音频），在三种 player 构造下比较十种归因方法与三种训练架构，分析哪些设计选择影响解释质量。

**相关性简评：** 其关于「player 在空间/时间上如何分组比填补器选择更关键」以及真实数据上代理指标相互矛盾的发现，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据贡献分配与忠实度评价的构造提供核查线索，但对象是时空信号而非 VLM，迁移需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MotionBench-XAI: Benchmarking What Shapes Shapley Explanations in Spatiotemporal Machine Learning

**abstract/A1：** Predicting outcomes from spatiotemporal data, such as assessing Parkinson's severity from human motion, often requires explainability in addition to accuracy. Practitioners need to know what drove the prediction, whether it be spatial (a body joint), temporal (a specific window), or both. Shapley values are attributions computed by treating input features as players in a cooperative game and measuring how the model output changes as coalitions of players are removed. Computing them involves choosing what counts as players, how absent players are imputed, and how the game is evaluated. How these choices affect explanation quality is poorly understood for spatiotemporal data, and real data carries no ground-truth attributions to check against. We present MotionBench-XAI, a benchmark built on i) five synthetic spatiotemporal datasets with exact ground-truth Shapley values, ii) three real datasets across clinical gait, electrocardiogram, and audio, iii) ten attribution methods across three player sets, and iv) three trained architectures. We show that grouping features into players, across time, space, or both, matters more than the choice of imputer. Generative imputers improve explanations only when their conditional models are accurate. On real data, the standard proxy metrics select contradictory best methods. We distill these findings into practical insights and provide an open-source pipeline.

</details>

### 34. [On the Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models: A Few Gradient Signs Are All You Need](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08800v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08800

**作者：** Nairouz Mrabah、Ismail Ben Ayed、Mahdi S. Hosseini、Eric Granger

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08800v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08800v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对视觉-语言模型少样本适配中梯度估计噪声大的问题，发现梯度幅值严重失准但少数大幅值分量的符号仍有信息量，据此提出 SignSparseSGD 与 DynSignSparseSGD：仅用幅值选取更新位置、用固定幅值符号决定更新方向，不保留优化器动量状态，并在 11 个数据集、3 个 CLIP 骨干上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「低监督下梯度噪声大、仅信任稀疏符号方向、免优化器状态」的训练线索，可能可供核查于 latent 推理轨迹训练中的噪声归因与显存受限场景；但论文本身面向分类适配，与视觉证据使用和推理期状态生成无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models: A Few Gradient Signs Are All You Need

**abstract/A1：** The limited supervision available for the few-shot adaptation of vision-language models makes gradient estimation particularly noisy. We find that the gradient magnitudes are severely miscalibrated, while the signs of a small top-magnitude subset remain informative. This motivates SignSparseSGD and DynSignSparseSGD, two state-free optimizers that use gradient magnitudes only to select where to update and fixed-magnitude signs to decide how to update. The static variant fixes the support after the first minibatch. The dynamic variant refreshes it at every step. Across 11 datasets, three CLIP backbones, and five shot levels, the proposed methods are competitive with or outperform parameter-efficient and optimization-based baselines on average, while exhibiting markedly lower sensitivity to long training schedules. They introduce no task-specific modules, maintain no first- or second-moment optimizer states, and use one shared configuration across datasets, backbones, and shot levels. Our code is included in the supplementary materials and will be released upon publication.

**comment/C1：** 98 pages, including supplementary material

</details>
