### 35. [Impact of color in computer and human-based object recognition under occlusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08808v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08808

**作者：** Courtney M. King、Elissa Aminoff、Daniel D. Leeds

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08808v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08808v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察物体色与场景色两种伪装式部分遮挡如何影响人类以及 CNN、Transformer 的物体识别，并提出 NOP、NOR、AIO 三项指标，度量模型激活与遮挡区域及无遮挡情形的对齐程度。

**相关性简评：** 其遮挡下激活一致性与遮挡区域激活占比的度量思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据是否被保留并真正使用，但论文本身不涉及多模态推理或 latent 状态生成，仅有分析工具层面的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Impact of color in computer and human-based object recognition under occlusion

**abstract/A1：** Partial occlusions challenge object recognition in both human and computer vision (CV), but the impact is largely dependent on the occlusion and model design. Properties relating to color are particularly important in biological recognition and frequently leveraged for camouflage, however there are still open questions about what makes them effective and how they translate to CV. This work studies partial occlusions that create camouflage, including those that blend in with target objects or with scenes, which alter object representations in distinct ways. Experiments evaluate recognition performance on both human participants and models ranging from convolutional neural networks (CNNs) to transformers, and additional analysis is performed on models to uncover trends in processing and object representations under multiple occlusion types. Novel metrics offer explainability of model behavior under occlusion: non-occlusion precision (NOP) and non-occlusion recall (NOR) measure alignment of model activations with and without occlusion, and activation in occlusion (AIO) measures the percentage of activations that occur in occluded regions. Findings show that human recognition, and most transformers, are negatively impacted by generated camouflaging occluders, with scene-based camouflage performing worse than object-based. Classical CNNs show a strong opposite trend, performing the worst on neutral-colored occlusions, suggesting that contextually-related colors, particularly scene-based, benefit object recognition rather than disrupting shape or boundary cues. Additionally, generated camouflaging occluders for training augmentation improves robustness to unseen camouflage for selected CNN model, and occlusion metrics suggest trends about learned mechanisms. Findings have interesting implications for alignment in human and computer vision, model vulnerabilities, and explainability.

</details>

<a id="paper-36"></a>

### 36. [Solver-Admitted Metamorphic Testing of Learned Network Optimization Surrogates](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08837v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08837

**作者：** Vineet Jagadeesan Nair、Stephen Peng、Arun Ravindran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08837v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08837v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为学习型优化求解器替代模型构造蜕变测试，用参考求解器一致性与独立约束检查筛选样本，并在 AC-OPF、TSP、CVRP、MIS 上量化等价编码下预测不一致的程度及各类修补手段的效果。

**相关性简评：** 其「等价输入变换下输出应保持一致」的审计协议可能可供核查 LVR 内部状态是否保留任务必要信息，并把一致性修补与准确性、可行性评估分开，但需迁移到视觉推理场景。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Solver-Admitted Metamorphic Testing of Learned Network Optimization Surrogates

**abstract/A1：** Equivalent encodings of an optimization problem can produce different learned predictions. Interpreting these differences requires accounting for numerical error and multiple optima. We construct metamorphic tests for optimization solver surrogates with explicit eligibility conditions, output alignment, and numerical thresholds. Reference-solver agreement and independent constraint checks determine which sampled pairs enter the audit. We study alternating-current optimal power flow (AC-OPF), the traveling salesman problem (TSP), the capacitated vehicle routing problem (CVRP), and maximum independent set (MIS). Across five grids, GridSFM-Open violates each of 13 relations on at least one grid, and 12 by more than twice the threshold. Randomly initialized copies of the grid models fail nearly the same relations, so the failures do not require training. Source patches and input canonicalization remove selected discrepancies, but no intervention establishes preserved accuracy and feasibility within the declared margin on every grid. In routing, Policy Optimization with Multiple Optima (POMO) has a single-pass rotation/reflection violation rate that rises from 12.7% to 89.5% between 20 and 100 cities. Graph-Based Diffusion Solvers for Combinatorial Optimization (DIFUSCO) violates the MIS disjoint-union relation on 35.6% of draws. Post hoc normalization within connected components removes these violations on the tested graphs. These findings support reporting metamorphic consistency alongside optimality, feasibility, and accuracy, and validating repair quality separately from consistency.

</details>

<a id="paper-37"></a>

### 37. [Mind the Divergence: Divergence-Guided Reliability-Aware Fusion and Adaptive LLM Prompting for Conversational Emotion Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08860v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08860

**作者：** Jingwen Wang、Jiao Li、Yuhang Qian、Yuyin Wang、Min Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SD

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08860v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08860v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MiD-ERC，用于对话情绪识别：以单模态情绪分布间的归一化散度衡量模态间预测分歧，并据此估计样本级模态可靠性，协调跨模态门控、可靠性加权与散度条件下的自适应 LLM 提示。

**相关性简评：** 其按样本估计模态可靠性并按分歧调节融合与提示的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据可信度筛选、模态贡献分配及何时依赖视觉的决策提供可核查线索；但任务为情绪分类，无 latent 推理状态，联系需自行迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Mind the Divergence: Divergence-Guided Reliability-Aware Fusion and Adaptive LLM Prompting for Conversational Emotion Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal emotion recognition in conversations (MERC) integrates textual, acoustic, and visual cues to identify utterance-level emotions. However, existing approaches generally do not explicitly adapt multimodal processing to the degree of inter-modal predictive disagreement. In real conversations, semantic incongruity, concealed emotion, noisy observations, and ambiguity can affect modality-specific emotional evidence differently, while the reliability of individual modalities varies across samples. Rather than identifying each source of inconsistency separately, we model their common consequence for emotion recognition as predictive disagreement among unimodal emotion distributions over the same emotion-label set. Such disagreement makes indiscriminate fusion prone to propagating unreliable or competing evidence. Inspired by the Brunswik Lens Model, we propose MiD-ERC, a divergence-centric framework that quantifies inter-modal predictive disagreement using a normalized divergence score and estimates sample-specific modality reliability. MiD-ERC uses these signals to coordinate divergence-guided cross-modal gating, divergence-modulated reliability weighting, and divergence-conditioned adaptive LLM prompting. The framework is trained end-to-end using only standard emotion annotations, without requiring additional conflict labels, auxiliary data construction, or external retrieval modules. Experiments on IEMOCAP and MELD demonstrate that MiD-ERC achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly pronounced improvements on high-divergence samples.

</details>

<a id="paper-38"></a>

### 38. [Rethinking Agent Trajectory Compression: A Workflow-Aware Perspective](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08865v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08865

**作者：** Qijia Zhuang、Rui Liu、Yuxiang Ren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08865v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08865v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究长程工具型 agent 的执行轨迹上下文膨胀问题，提出 WAAC 框架：从历史成功轨迹中抽取可复用的 workflow 压缩经验，并结合在线反向依赖分析动态确定各步信息需保留的要求，据此为不同历史信息选择压缩策略。

**相关性简评：** 其「反向依赖决定信息保留」的思路可能为 LVR 中哪些中间证据需留在 latent 状态、如何筛选视觉/中间信息提供可核查线索，但对象是纯文本 agent 轨迹，需确认能否迁移到多模态推理状态维护。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rethinking Agent Trajectory Compression: A Workflow-Aware Perspective

**abstract/A1：** As long-horizon agents increasingly rely on multi-step tool interactions to accomplish complex tasks, their execution trajectories continuously accumulate structured outputs and intermediate artifacts, leading to growing context overhead. Existing compression methods primarily rely on text importance or fixed truncation rules, often overlooking cross-step dependencies within workflows and potentially discarding critical information required for subsequent task execution. To address this challenge, we propose WAAC, a workflow-aware framework for agent trajectory compression. WAAC structurally models successful historical trajectories to extract reusable workflow compression experience and incorporates online backward dependency analysis to dynamically identify information retention requirements. By combining current dependency constraints with historical experience, WAAC selects appropriate compression strategies for different types of historical information. Experiments on PinchBench and Odysseys demonstrate that WAAC achieves an overall score of 87.6 and a perfect completion rate of 19.0%, respectively, while reducing execution costs by 19.7% and 29.1% compared with the strongest baseline methods. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of workflow-aware trajectory compression in improving task performance and cost efficiency for long-horizon agents. Code and resources are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/waac-6527/.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-39"></a>

### 39. [Learning to Restart: Differentiable Restart Policies for Optimization Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08883v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08883

**作者：** Yiman Fong、Zhonglin Xie、Zaiwen Wen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08883v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08883v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对加速一阶优化器中的自适应重启策略难以学习的问题，借助连续时间极限为离散重启事件构造可高效计算的替代梯度，并在 L2O 设置中训练重启策略，在二次规划和线性规划上优于手工准则，且可从合成二次问题零样本迁移到真实数据上的逻辑回归。

**相关性简评：** 将离散的重启/状态重置事件可微化的替代梯度思路，可能可供核查用于学习 latent 推理循环中的辅助状态重置与停止决策，但其属纯优化领域、与视觉多模态推理无直接联系，迁移需较多工作。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Restart: Differentiable Restart Policies for Optimization Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** Adaptive restart mechanisms, which reset the momentum or other auxiliary state of an iterative optimizer, play a central role in the empirical performance of accelerated first-order methods, yet existing restart rules remain almost entirely hand-designed. The obstruction to learning restart policies is that, unlike the typical Learning to Optimize (L2O) setting, the discrete restart events make the resulting trajectory non-differentiable in the algorithm parameters and thus block gradient-based optimization of the restart policy. We resolve this obstruction by developing a differentiable framework for restart policies through their continuous-time limit. We introduce surrogate gradients for the restart events and optimization trajectories that are efficient to compute and provably converge to their continuous-time counterparts. We validate the effectiveness of our surrogate gradients in L2O settings, training restart policies that improve upon standard handcrafted adaptive restart criteria on quadratic and linear programs, and that transfer zero-shot from synthetic quadratics to logistic regression on real datasets.

</details>

<a id="paper-40"></a>

### 40. [MotionCore: A Latent Neural Simulator for Video Generation with Rigid-Body Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08904v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08904

**作者：** Zheng Wang、Zhengwei Gong、Yuanzhi Liang、Chi Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08904v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08904v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MotionCore，用可学习 PDE 求解器构建 latent 神经模拟器，从观测运动预测物体级物理状态的演化，再将其转为空间对齐的 latent 控制信号驱动视频扩散生成，并以 diffusion-matching 目标配合对物体身份与位置的低容量可解码性约束。

**相关性简评：** 该工作并非 VLM 推理研究，但其把动力学状态与外观分离、并用低容量可解码性约束保留位置与身份信息的做法，可能为 LVR 中潜在状态是否保留几何与任务必要信息、如何抑制信息坍缩提供可供核查的训练线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MotionCore: A Latent Neural Simulator for Video Generation with Rigid-Body Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** Video generation models can produce highly realistic videos while generating physically inconsistent object motion. This limitation suggests that visual modeling alone may not provide a transferable representation of physical dynamics. We introduce MotionCore, a framework for learning physical dynamics via a learnable partial differential equation solver and focuses on rigid-body motion. We introduce a latent neural simulator learning to predict the evolution of object-level physical states from observed motion. Its predictions are then converted into spatially aligned latent controls for a controllable video diffusion model. The generator serves as a visual interface: it provides appearance and scene detail, while the learned dynamics model specifies the physical trajectory. To preserve this trajectory through the interface, MotionCore combines a diffusion-matching objective with a low-capacity decodability constraint on object identity and position. We evaluate MotionCore on public physical-consistency benchmarks and on paired data with known trajectories. Results indicate that MotionCore improves physical consistency and trajectory obedience in the evaluated rigid-body settings, while maintaining comparable visual quality. A controlled ablation protocol compares learned dynamics with an analytic prior and studies the choice of latent evolution processor.

</details>

<a id="paper-41"></a>

### 41. [Glyph: An Interpretability Engine for AI-Driven Scientific Discovery in Clinical Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08922v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08922

**作者：** Robbie Holland、Ashwin Kumar、Mateus A. Esmeraldo、David Le、Andrea Fisher、Jiazhen Pan、Louisa Fay、Magdalini Paschali、Vasiliki Sideri-Lampretsa、Ates Fettahoglu、Stefania Moroianu、Philipp Wesp、Ivan Lopez、Tom Nuno Wolf、McKenzie S. White、Eduardo Pontes Reis、Fatima Rodriguez、Curtis P. Langlotz、Vasilis Syrgkanis、Imon Banerjee、Akshay S. Chaudhari、Sergios Gatidis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08922v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08922v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出 Glyph 多阶段框架，用基础模型结合稀疏自编码器从腹部 CT、放射报告与化验数据中提取隐含特征，将其解读为可编码的临床概念，并在 4.1 万余名患者上验证这些概念对临床科学发现的预测价值。

**相关性简评：** 其「用稀疏自编码器把多模态基础模型 embedding 拆解为可命名概念」的做法，可能为核查 LVR 内部状态是否保留任务必要的视觉与实体信息提供可借鉴的探针工具，但原文并非推理期 latent 生成研究，需核查其概念语义是否可靠。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Glyph: An Interpretability Engine for AI-Driven Scientific Discovery in Clinical Data

**abstract/A1：** Automated scientific discovery has the potential to advance understanding of disease mechanisms and accelerate identification of early disease markers. Manual data annotation is infeasible at population scale, motivating methods that automatically extract pre-defined phenotypes in multimodal and unstructured clinical data. Such pre-defined feature sets determine and limit which associations these approaches can discover. This limitation extends to agentic AI systems that use these tools to query population-scale data. In contrast, foundation models (FMs) trained on large volumes of clinical data can perform tasks beyond their training objectives. This suggests their latent embeddings may represent novel or unexplored features that could yield insights into disease mechanisms, yet such latent features remain difficult to interpret. To address this challenge, we introduce Glyph, an automated multistage framework that extracts features from multimodal and unstructured clinical data using FMs and sparse autoencoders, interprets them as coded clinical concepts, and evaluates their value for clinical scientific discovery. We applied Glyph to abdominal CT volumes, CT radiology reports and laboratory measurements from 41,628 patients across different institutions. We identified 397 predictive concepts supported by 4,463 Bonferroni-significant associations. Moreover, a majority of discovered associations were automatically reproducible in held-out cohorts across different sites. Glyph concepts provided complementary associations to literature-derived markers, leading to gains in predictive power. We envision that interpretability can extend beyond a mechanistic understanding of neural networks to connecting implicitly learned features with clinical knowledge and establishing their significance for patient outcomes. We release Glyph at https://github.com/StanfordMIMI/Glyph.

</details>

<a id="paper-42"></a>

### 42. [MESAM: Enhancing SAM2 for Few-Shot Segmentation through Semantic-Visual Memory Generation and Refinement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08943v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08943

**作者：** Tianyu Zou、Shengwu Xiong、Rui Wu、Ruilin Yao、Yi Rong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08943v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08943v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向少样本分割，针对支持集与查询图像的外观差异导致 SAM2 记忆匹配失效的问题，提出构造语义记忆、查询前景视觉记忆与支持自适应视觉记忆，并用交互式语义记忆细化模块注入细粒度视觉细节、抑制误激活背景。

**相关性简评：** 其「语义记忆+视觉细节细化」机制可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态该保留何种内容、如何筛选视觉证据并提供核查线索，但该工作仅做分割、无推理生成与停止决策，迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MESAM: Enhancing SAM2 for Few-Shot Segmentation through Semantic-Visual Memory Generation and Refinement

**abstract/A1：** This paper investigates how to leverage the powerful capabilities of the Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) to tackle few-shot segmentation (FSS) tasks. One major challenge lies in the substantial appearance differences between the support and query images, which may diminish the effectiveness of the memory-matching process in SAM2. Through systematic analysis, we find that the memory generated based on the categorical semantic information is robust to this appearance divergence and is more compatible with SAM2. Based on these observations, we propose a new Memory-Enhanced SAM2 (MESAM) method, which first introduces a Semantic-Visual Memory Generation (SVMG) module to separately construct a query semantic memory, a query foreground visual memory, and a support adaptive visual memory. Then, in our designed Interactive Semantic Memory Refinement (ISMR) module, we further enrich the obtained semantic memory with fine-grained visual details, and suppress its misatcivated background responses. Extensive experiments on the PASCAL-$5^i$, COCO-$20^i$, and LVIS-$92^i$ datasets demonstrate that MESAM consistently outperforms existing FSS methods, achieving new state-of-the-art segmentation performance. Our source code is available at: \\href{https://github.com/tianyu-zou/MESAM}{https://github.com/tianyu-zou/MESAM}.

**comment/C1：** 17pages, 12figures

</details>

### 43. [Accuracy Is Not Value: Choosing Weak Supervisors by What the Student Inherits](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08959v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08959

**作者：** Romano Li、Xunfan Cai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08959v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08959v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究弱监督者的价值应如何评估，证明在平方损失与共享线性学习器下，监督者价值取决于其误差经学生学习算子过滤后的形态，并分析用未标注数据和少量干净标签恢复该学生相对价值的加权与风险界。

**相关性简评：** 其「监督价值须相对学生而非监督者自身误差衡量」的视角，可供核查 LVR 中多条推理轨迹或弱监督信号的加权与贡献分配问题；但结论限于线性学习和平方损失的理论设定，迁移到 latent 监督前需验证适用性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Accuracy Is Not Value: Choosing Weak Supervisors by What the Student Inherits

**abstract/A1：** Weak supervisors are usually judged by their own errors, but what matters is what a student learns from them. For squared loss and any linear learner shared by all supervisors, we show that a supervisor's value is determined by its errors after they pass through the student's learning operator. Consequently, the inner products of the supervisors' raw errors can be compatible with every feasible value of the student's risk, and weighting supervisors by them can be arbitrarily worse than the best weighting in relative excess risk. This student-relative value is not identifiable from unlabeled data, but what is missing is small: one finite-dimensional projection of the target decides whether training on a weak aggregate helps, another decides how to weight the supervisors. Known teacher training rules, or a label-model condition stated in student-relative form, shrink it further. Clean labels close the remaining gap at a cost set by the geometry of the unresolved directions rather than by the dimension of the student's representation, with matching worst-case lower bounds. In Gaussian designs we also quantify when exact teacher structure beats ordinary stacking and when weak supervisors, combined with clean labels, beat every procedure that uses the clean labels alone. The guarantees extend to fitted ridge students and finite unlabeled data.

</details>

<a id="paper-44"></a>

### 44. [PhysioWave-T: Token-Efficient Wavelet Foundation Models for Biosignals](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08969v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08969

**作者：** Yanlong Chen、Simone Benatti、Luca Benini、Cuntai Guan、Yawei Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08969v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08969v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PhysioWave-T 生物信号（EEG/ECG/sEMG）小波基础模型族，通过动态尺度折叠在 token 化前合并多级子带压缩序列长度，并用可靠性感知的 latent 融合（RALF）以样本自适应权重整合多个独立预训练编码器的摘要 token。

**相关性简评：** RALF 的样本自适应、按可靠性加权融合多个摘要表示，可能可供核查 LVR 中多种 latent 假设或视觉证据与推理状态的融合与筛选；尺度折叠带来的 token 长度—延迟/内存权衡也可对照 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的成本分析，但论文为生理信号模态，迁移性未经视觉推理验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PhysioWave-T: Token-Efficient Wavelet Foundation Models for Biosignals

**abstract/A1：** We present PhysioWave-T, a token-efficient wavelet foundation model family for EEG, ECG, and sEMG. Dynamic scale folding recombines J+1 wavelet subbands prior to tokenization, compressing sequence length from (J+1)CS to CS tokens for J levels, C channels, and S temporal patches. Route-specific front ends and learned channel-name embeddings allow one Transformer per modality to handle diverse montages and sampling rates, while unseen setups reuse the backbone via lightweight adapters. We pre-train a 30M-parameter EEG model on over 15M windows (19--128 channels), alongside ECG (30M) and sEMG (12M) encoders. Across four EEG routes, scale folding cuts Transformer latency by 4.0--7.2x and peak memory by 6.8--12.7x over scale expansion without accuracy loss. Our EEG encoder leads 8 of 9 metrics against BENDR, BIOT, LaBraM, and EEGPT, and surpasses LUNA-L on wearable seizure detection with fewer parameters. Reliability-aware latent fusion (RALF) further integrates summary tokens from independently pre-trained encoders via sample-adaptive weights. On SeizeIT2, RALF raises event sensitivity at 11 false alarms/h from 61.4% to 74.4% and window AUPRC from 0.321 to 0.399 over the best unimodal baseline, outperforming weighted-logit and concatenation fusion across all variants. PhysioWave-T also achieves state-of-the-art sEMG accuracies and top macro-F1 on PTB-XL Super and Chapman-Shaoxing. Code and models: https://github.com/ForeverBlue816/PhysioWave.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 11 figures, 5 tables. Code: https://github.com/ForeverBlue816/PhysioWave , Models &amp; Weights: https://huggingface.co/collections/ForeverBlue/physiowave-t

</details>

<a id="paper-45"></a>

### 45. [KnowEvo: Knowledge Evolution for Protein Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09013v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09013

**作者：** Zijie Xing、Xingyue Liu、Runze Wang、Luoming Hu、Yanming Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09013v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09013v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 KnowEvo，把可执行「知识块」本身作为优化对象，用策略变异树组织组合式探索，并以 Thompson 式前向预算分配配合沙盒代码智能体的后向修订，迭代积累蛋白质稳定性与溶解度优化经验。

**相关性简评：** 其「多机制耦合使改进无法归因到单个组件」的问题设定与用 rollout 证据做局部程序更新的做法，可能与 LVR 中 latent 步骤的贡献分配、有限评估预算下的搜索与验证线索相通，可核查迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** KnowEvo: Knowledge Evolution for Protein Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in scientific discovery, yet systematically accumulating and refining reusable scientific knowledge remains challenging. Existing approaches primarily improve LLM-based problem solving through parameter optimization or feedback-driven refinement of prompts, programs, and solution trajectories, leaving acquired expertise either embedded in model weights or scattered across iterative problem-solving processes. As a result,knowledge is difficult to systematically inspect, evaluate, and refine. We propose KnowEvo, a framework that treats externalized knowledge itself as the optimization target. Our key insight is that scientific knowledge is inherently modular: multiple interacting mechanisms jointly determine the design outcomes. This makes knowledge evolution fundamentally challenging, as improvements cannot be attributed to individual components in isolation. To address this, KnowEvo represents knowledge as executable expertise blocks and organizes their refinements into strategy mutation trees, enabling structured exploration over a combinatorial knowledge space. The framework combines a Thompson-style forward allocation strategy that adaptively distributes evaluation budgets across strategy variants with a backward revision process, in which a code agent operates in an isolated sandbox to translate rollout evidence into localized program updates. This design enables persistent accumulation, evaluation, and refinement of mechanistic expertise across iterative experimentation. We evaluate KnowEvo on protein optimization tasks including stability and solubility. Results show that knowledge evolution consistently improves over initial strategy blocks and outperforms monolithic program evolution and prior LLM-guided evolution baselines across metrics such as pass@k, MRR, MAP,and NDCG@5. Code is available at https://github.com/recynie/KnowEvo.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-46"></a>

### 46. [Scout Before You Route: Predicting Task Success and Token Count from a Small LLM Prefill](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09028v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09028

**作者：** Aristides Milios、Christopher Pal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09028v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09028v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出用单个小型冻结 LLM 的 prefill 激活作「scout」，同时预测各候选模型的成功概率与输出长度，以便按查询而非固定成本为模型路由定价，并比较不同探测器与训练样本量下的节省效果。

**相关性简评：** 从冻结模型内部状态预判生成长度与成功率，可能为 LVR 中 STOP 决策和思考预算估计提供线索；但任务是文本路由、无视觉证据，需核查该探针能否用于视觉推理状态及其长度泛化。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scout Before You Route: Predicting Task Success and Token Count from a Small LLM Prefill

**abstract/A1：** Routers typically choose a model per query by trading predicted correctness against cost. Most price each model at a fixed cost, as if every query used the same number of output tokens, but the output length of reasoning models varies by an order of magnitude across queries. When is per-query cost worth predicting? What factors drive cost? How cheaply can predictions be made? We predict both the success probability and the output length of candidate models from the prefill activations of a single small frozen LLM, the scout. With reasoning candidates, perfect per-query cost knowledge would save 29-61% of spending on eight of nine datasets, and much less with non-reasoning candidates on the same problems. On those eight datasets, our predicted costs save 9-33% at matched accuracy relative to median-length pricing, up to 69% of what perfect knowledge would save. They are significantly cheaper than each estimator we reimplemented or adapted from published work, including ZeroRouter and a MixLLM-style predictor, on a majority of datasets. Output length depends mostly on the problem. On most math and code datasets, harder problems are longer, so predicted success alone prices cost nearly as well. On knowledge and mixed-format datasets they are not, and predicted success falls well behind a probe trained on length. The probe keeps most of its saving with 20 labelled problems and transfers without labels to four of seven new benchmarks. On a mixture of all nine benchmarks, it saves 22% beyond pricing each benchmark at its own median length. Savings stop growing at about a 2B scout, so above 4B a larger scout costs more than it adds.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 4 figures, 17 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-47"></a>

### 47. [When Can You Trust Neural Critics? Auditing Mutual Information Estimates of Higher-Order Epistasis in Protein Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09046v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09046

**作者：** Maryam Rahimimovassagh、Waldemar Karwowski、Niloofar Yousefi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09046v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09046v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文审计 ESM-2 上高阶上位性的神经互信息估计，比较多种估计器在随机种子和配对方案下的稳定性，并用已知互信息的合成校准及特权方向对照检验其可靠性，最后给出可复用的诊断流程。

**相关性简评：** 文中互信息探针的方差、评估配对敏感性和特权信息对照，可供核查 LVR 中「内部状态是否保留视觉信息」的表示探针结论；但研究对象为蛋白质序列而非多模态推理，迁移线索仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Can You Trust Neural Critics? Auditing Mutual Information Estimates of Higher-Order Epistasis in Protein Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Mutual information (MI) estimation is used to probe what neural representations encode, including in protein language models, but variational MI estimators have variance that scales at least exponentially in the true MI. We audit a prior neural-MI study of order-resolved epistasis in ESM-2 on the four-site GB1 landscape. Run-to-run differences show no systematic bias and a magnitude comparable to ordinary cross-seed retraining variability, and the evaluation-pairing scheme shifts scores substantially. At the original dimensionality, two of three alternative estimator families stabilize the estimate across seeds and one does not. In a synthetic calibration at the representation dimensionalities of two other protein language models (a Gaussian channel with known MI; no model representations), all three families recover materially less signal than required, despite correct signal ordering and stable seeds. A control critic given the true signal direction, which also reduces its input to one dimension, recovers 93.3% and 98.7% of the analytic MI at gain 1.5 (at 5,120 and 4,096 dimensions), so the signal is recoverable with privileged information, but the control does not identify why blind estimation fails. We package the checks into a reusable diagnostic protocol and conclude that even a stabilized estimator is not currently sufficient for order-resolved dependence claims made under blind search at this dimensionality and sample size.

</details>

<a id="paper-48"></a>

### 48. [Grounding-Aware Judge Reward Reinforcement Learning for Out-of-Domain Face Anti-Spoofing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09047v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09047

**作者：** Xiaoyong Yu、Xinge Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09047v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09047v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对人脸反欺骗（FAS）任务，在 CoT 监督微调后用 32B 视觉语言模型作为 judge，对推理链的图像证据锚定、逻辑连贯与领域知识打分，并将该分数作为 GRPO 奖励，以改善域外泛化并抑制仅凭答案的捷径。

**相关性简评：** 其「以图像为验证信号」的 judge 式过程监督，可能为核查 LVR 中视觉证据是否真正参与推理、以及贡献分配与视觉旁路诊断提供线索，但论文本身不涉及 latent 状态或推理期计算控制，需进一步核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Grounding-Aware Judge Reward Reinforcement Learning for Out-of-Domain Face Anti-Spoofing

**abstract/A1：** Face Anti-Spoofing (FAS) must distinguish genuine faces from presentation attacks. A unified Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) is an attractive solution: it detects physical attacks (print, replay, mask) in one model and produces interpretable forensic evidence via Chain-of-Thought (CoT). A central obstacle to post-training such a model with reinforcement learning is that FAS reasoning has no rule-verifiable ground truth: only the binary verdict is checkable, while the reasoning chain cannot be verified by any hand-crafted rule. Optimizing the verdict alone is therefore a sparse, hackable signal. The model can reach the correct answer through a shortcut such as a false-rejection bias, which does not generalize. We address this with a grounding-aware judge reward. A 32B vision-language judge scores each reasoning chain against the image on evidence anchoring, logical coherence, and domain knowledge, turning the image itself into the verification signal. The reward is applied in a Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) stage after CoT supervised fine-tuning. It is the judge, rather than the RL objective, that supplies the process supervision a verdict-only reward cannot. Our study starts from a model that already performs well In-Domain (ID) after CoT-based supervised fine-tuning and asks how to improve its Out-of-Domain (OOD) generalization. We find that grounding-aware rewards are an effective means to that end. We validate the method on OOD generalization, where the reward reduces ACER on two physical benchmarks (LCC-FASD 0.288 to 0.273, MSU-MFSD 0.431 to 0.409) while preserving ID accuracy (CelebA-Spoof 0.033 to 0.035). Against ResNet-50/101 and CLIP-ViT-B/16 baselines, the MLLM dominates ID and matches or surpasses the strongest baseline OOD. To our knowledge this is the first use of LLM-as-judge process supervision for FAS.

</details>

<a id="paper-49"></a>

### 49. [StreamTab: Training-Free Temporal Link Prediction with Tabular Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09052v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09052

**作者：** Mingqi Yang、Renqi Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DB、cs.SI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09052v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09052v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 StreamTab，用预训练且冻结的表格基础模型做免训练时序链接预测：把候选对的交互历史统计量连同近期样本和负例作为上下文示例输入模型打分，并按候选采样方式调整负例构成（regime conditioning）。

**相关性简评：** 「让示例负例与测试候选分布对齐」这一设计可能为 LVR 中判断答案是否真正使用 latent 视觉证据提供对照性核查线索：构造与待判定差异匹配的负例，而非随机负例，值得人工核查其思路可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StreamTab: Training-Free Temporal Link Prediction with Tabular Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Temporal link prediction uses past interactions to predict whether two nodes will interact in the future. Temporal graph neural networks achieve strong results on this task, but they are trained separately for each graph. How a model should use interaction history also depends on the candidates it is compared with. A past interaction can distinguish a recurring link from a random pair that has never interacted, but when the negative candidates have also interacted before, other signals are needed. We propose StreamTab, a training-free framework built on a frozen tabular foundation model pretrained only on synthetic tables. StreamTab describes each candidate pair by statistics of its past interactions and shows the model recent observed interactions and sampled negative pairs as labeled examples, from which the model scores new candidates. Its central design, regime conditioning, samples the negative examples according to how the candidates are sampled, so the examples show the distinction the model must make. As new interactions arrive, StreamTab updates the statistics and refreshes the examples while the model parameters stay fixed. On thirteen datasets against 21 temporal graph baselines, StreamTab achieves the highest average precision on twelve datasets under historical sampling and eleven under inductive sampling, and ranks first or stays within one point of the best on seven datasets under random sampling. With the same features and model, regime conditioning raises average precision over examples with random negatives by a median of 12.6 points under historical sampling.

</details>

<a id="paper-50"></a>

### 50. [ProtoCouncil: Multi-Encoder Anatomical Prototypes for Prostate Cancer Detection in Micro-Ultrasound](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09058v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09058

**作者：** Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi、Nooshin Maghsoodi、Armin Saadat、Obed Dzikunu、Zhuoxin Guo、Hannes Cash、Ferdinand Luger、Adam Kinnaird、Brian Wodlinger、Parvin Mousavi、Purang Abolmaesumi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09058v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09058v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ProtoCouncil，用 MedSAM、DINOv3、V-JEPA 2.1 三个基础编码器在腺体整体、穿刺针道、腺区分区三个解剖层级上学习病例原型，并将类别 logit 分解为偏置加原型相似度的精确求和，编码器分歧时触发选择性预测，用于微超声前列腺癌检测。

**相关性简评：** 其「logit 可精确拆为原型相似度贡献」与「编码器分歧即弃权」的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 内容的贡献分配与 STOP 决策提供可核查的方法线索，但任务本身并非多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ProtoCouncil: Multi-Encoder Anatomical Prototypes for Prostate Cancer Detection in Micro-Ultrasound

**abstract/A1：** Purpose: Micro-ultrasound ($μ$US) is a promising modality for prostate cancer (PCa) detection, but identifying suspicious tissue depends heavily on clinical experience. Models trained on sparse, noisy core-level pathology offer little insight into their decisions and can fail under domain shift. Clinical use needs a traceable decision process and a flag for uncertain predictions. Methods: We introduce ProtoCouncil, in which three foundation encoders (MedSAM, DINOv3 and V-JEPA 2.1) learn case-based prototypes at three anatomical levels: whole gland, needle track and gland sectors. Prototypes are projected onto real training-core regions, and label-free fusion keeps every class logit as a bias plus an exact sum of prototype similarity contributions. Encoder disagreement triggers selective prediction. Results: In five-fold patient-level cross-validation (6,588 cores, 693 patients), ProtoCouncil reaches 82.9% AUROC for clinically significant PCa (csPCa, Grade Group $\\geq$3), 4.3 points above the best previously published method. In zero-shot evaluation on two external cohorts, ProtoCouncil achieves higher all-cancer and csPCa AUROC than all previous methods. Nearest same-class exemplars match test-core tumor involvement better than random same-class cases. Externally, csPCa accuracy falls from 79% when encoders agree to 27% when two oppose the fused call. Abstaining on disagreement raises balanced accuracy by 7 and 15 points, while retaining 58% and 54% of the cores. Conclusion: ProtoCouncil grounds core-level predictions in exact similarity-based evidence from real, anatomically indexed training cases, which makes its reasoning interpretable. It achieves the highest csPCa AUROC across three cohorts and identifies unreliable predictions through encoder disagreement.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 3 figures, Under Review

</details>

<a id="paper-51"></a>

### 51. [WearableBridge: Task-Guided Latent Transport for Free-Living Activity Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09067v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09067

**作者：** Jingjing Zou、Yixin Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09067v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09067v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 WearableBridge：冻结参考编码器与分类器，仅训练一个任务引导的传输映射（Mapper 回归参考特征、Distillation 匹配类别概率、MeanFlow 单步流），把腕部加速度特征搬到分类器认定的活动区域，以少量参考数据提升自由生活活动识别。

**相关性简评：** “冻结读取端＋任务损失引导的 latent 传输＋特权参考模态蒸馏”可供核查 LVR 中如何把视觉/参考证据对齐进内部状态、以及仅靠分类损失搬运特征带来的旁路与表征坍缩风险，但与 VLM 多步推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WearableBridge: Task-Guided Latent Transport for Free-Living Activity Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Wrist-worn accelerometers make activity monitoring feasible at scale, but incidental arm motion, device orientation, and shifting context blur the signal that separates activities. Calibration studies supply higher-quality references such as concurrent thigh recordings or supervised laboratory wrist sessions, but they are expensive, cover few participants, and are rarely available at deployment. How can limited reference data improve recognition for participants who wear only a wrist device? We propose WearableBridge, which holds the reference encoder and classifier fixed and learns a task-guided transport map, the bridge, that carries each wrist feature into the region the classifier associates with its activity. Reference features or the classifier's predictions indicate the destination, and a classification loss penalizes wrist features that land in the region of another activity. We instantiate WearableBridge with three bridges: Mapper regresses onto reference features, Distillation matches the classifier's class probabilities, and MeanFlow learns a one-step flow from wrist toward reference features. On 40 held-out free-living PAAWS participants, all three bridges improve macro-F1 in every training run, and the strongest, MeanFlow, raises mean macro-F1 from 35.1% to 56.0% with concurrent thigh references (Thigh2Wrist), and from 54.0% to 63.5% with group-matched laboratory wrist references (Lab2Freeliving). Fitted only on PAAWS, MeanFlow also raises macro-F1 on the CAPTURE-24 cohort from 42.8% to 54.7%. The gains replicate on selfBACK, hold across four reference encoders, and grow with temporal context. A study can therefore collect reference recordings for a calibration subset and improve wrist-only recognition for every other participant, training only a bridge of 0.13 to 2.44 million parameters while the reference encoder and classifier stay unchanged.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 5 figures, 11 tables. Code and interactive demo: https://github.com/jingjingz/WearableBridge

</details>

<a id="paper-52"></a>

### 52. [GlobalPuzzle: Globally Informed Genetic Search for Eroded Jigsaw Puzzle Reassembly](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09077v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09077

**作者：** Lunyan Wang、Chuanzhi Xu、Ziyuan Tao、Weidong Cai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09077v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09077v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GlobalPuzzle 框架，将全局画布级评估、动作引导与遗传搜索结合，用于边界被侵蚀的拼图重组，以缓解仅靠成对边界匹配难以判断的模糊邻接关系。

**相关性简评：** 用全局评估器对候选整体组装打分并指引搜索与迭代修改（替换、整组移动），可能为 LVR 中推理状态的全局评价、搜索验证与回退操作提供借鉴线索；但任务属纯视觉拼图，评分器能否迁移到 latent 状态评价尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GlobalPuzzle: Globally Informed Genetic Search for Eroded Jigsaw Puzzle Reassembly

**abstract/A1：** Jigsaw puzzle reassembly remains a highly challenging task, particularly when piece boundaries are irregularly eroded to simulate damage to cultural artifacts. On the GAP benchmark, existing methods achieve a perfect assembly accuracy of only 0.3% on $5 \\times 5$ puzzles, whereas humans can solve jigsaw puzzles with remarkably high accuracy. Humans typically approach puzzle solving by identifying potentially adjacent pieces, evaluating the current assembly, and iteratively performing complex operations such as adding pieces, replacing incorrectly placed pieces, and moving entire connected groups of pieces. However, existing methods face a fundamental limitation: approaches that leverage global visual information typically predict piece arrangements directly, making it difficult to explicitly perform such complex iterative operations, while search based methods can rearrange pieces but primarily rely on pairwise boundary compatibility, lacking global visual information to evaluate and guide the search process. To bridge this gap, we propose GlobalPuzzle, a jigsaw puzzle reassembly framework that integrates global canvas level evaluation, action guidance, and genetic search. By incorporating global visual information into the evaluation and optimization of candidate assemblies, GlobalPuzzle aims to resolve ambiguous adjacency relationships that are difficult to determine from irregularly eroded piece boundaries alone, enabling flexible, globally informed reconstruction.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages (10 pages main paper and 12 pages supplementary material), 16 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-53"></a>

### 53. [Training-Free Active Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09084v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09084

**作者：** Jihwan Bang、Sumyeong Ahn、Hwanjun Song、Jae-Gil Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09084v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09084v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究视觉语言模型部署期的「主动测试期适应」：在当前样本预测必须先于其标签给出的约束下，提出免训练即插模块 ACTOR，用一致性-可靠性检查把不确定样本路由到基础预测、标签查询或基于已查询标签的记忆修正，并在十个跨域基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「何时花费外部证据预算、并用记忆证据纠正」的路由判据，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的证据获取与停止决策提供借鉴线索；但论文不涉及 latent 推理状态，判据能否迁移需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Training-Free Active Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Test-time adaptation (TTA) for vision-language models (VLMs) is usually studied in a fully unsupervised regime, where adaptation relies on pseudo-labels, confidence scores, or entropy computed by the model itself. This is a severe restriction on hard shifted samples: the model is asked to repair its own uncertain predictions without any external evidence. At the other extreme, supervised online adaptation assumes labels can directly train or correct the model. We study the intermediate deployment regime between these two extremes. In Active Test-time Adaptation for VLMs (Active VLM-TTA), a pretrained VLM may spend an explicit labeling budget on selected test samples, but the prediction for the current sample must be emitted before its label can be used; queried labels are therefore costed, delayed evidence for future samples rather than free correction for the present one. Under this future-only constraint, the central question is how to reuse sparse labels conservatively. We instantiate this setting with ACTive VLM-TTA for Online Retrieval (ACTOR), a training-free plug-in module that routes uncertain samples among base prediction, label query, and memory-based correction using a consistency-and-reliability check over previously queried labels. Across ten cross-domain benchmarks and a domain-transfer benchmark based on ImageNet, ACTOR consistently improves strong VLM-TTA baselines on ResNet and ViT backbones with negligible inference overhead. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/ACTOR.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-54"></a>

### 54. [Trajectory Forcing: Exploiting Diffusion Trajectories for Autoregressive Long Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09096v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09096

**作者：** Bohan Wang、Shuo Chen、Yunzhi Li、Zhaozheng Chen、Junzhe Zhang、Hanwang Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09096v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09096v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Trajectory Forcing（TraF）：在自回归视频扩散中，不只以上一个 chunk 的最终结果为上下文，而是把此前 chunk 的去噪轨迹也作为条件，并用高斯插值伪轨迹与教师模型真实 rollout 两阶段训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「中间预测被丢弃、应保留生成历史作为上下文」的论点，与 LVR 中 latent 状态留存及跨步状态更新问题相通，其轨迹条件与自 rollout 监督可供核查是否可迁移；但论文属视频生成，未涉及推理正确性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Trajectory Forcing: Exploiting Diffusion Trajectories for Autoregressive Long Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive (AR) modeling is arguably the only way to extend a fixed-length video diffusion model to longer or even streaming videos. However, existing AR diffusion methods use only the previously predicted chunk as context for the next, discarding the intermediate predictions that constitute the chunk's generation history. In contrast, conventional AR models such as LLMs condition on the entire history of preceding tokens. To this end, we propose Trajectory Forcing (TraF), an AR diffusion method that exploits the denoising trajectories of previous video chunks as context. TraF operates under two training regimes: (1) with ground-truth videos, where pseudo-trajectories are constructed from Gaussian interpolations and re-predicted by the model, and (2) with a teacher model, where real trajectories are obtained from the student's own denoising rollout. The first regime naturally provides a strong initialization for the second. On the 5-second VBench benchmark, TraF achieves a Total score of 85.15, outperforming Self Forcing (+0.84) and Causal Forcing (+1.11), with Quality and Semantic improving simultaneously. On 30-second generation---6$\\times$ the training horizon---TraF achieves the highest overall quality among all compared methods.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 55. [PathXAI: Evidence-guided structured pathology reporting by transferable tissue segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09117v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09117

**作者：** Zhaorui Tan、Jun Aun Tan、Weixiang Zhong、Xiaoxi Wang、Tan Pan、Xingdong Xue、Li Liu、Jing Zhang、Jing Zhang、Weimiao Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09117v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09117v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PathXAI，将分割得到的组织成分信息与 H&amp;E 图像、可选领域知识一起输入自托管预训练 VLM（不更新权重），引导诊断推理、针对性复检与结构化病理报告生成，并在内部与外部数据上评估分割 Dice 及报告与参考诊断、形态描述的一致性。

**相关性简评：** 把显式组织成分测量作为可核查的证据通道注入冻结 VLM，并生成证据链接的报告，可供核查视觉证据筛选与「证据—结论」对应关系；但论文不涉及 latent 推理状态生成或动态计算分配，迁移价值需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PathXAI: Evidence-guided structured pathology reporting by transferable tissue segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Pathology reporting requires translating tissue observations across regions and slides into specific diagnoses and supporting morphological descriptions. However, the use of explicit tissue-composition measurements to guide H&amp;E-based report generation remains underexplored. Thus, we developed PathXAI, which combines segmentation-derived tissue information with H&amp;E images and optional domain knowledge to guide diagnostic reasoning, targeted reinspection and structured report writing. Its segmentation component exceeded baselines in mean Dice on internal and external tests. In automated report evaluation on 1,200 public and 151 private cases, PathXAI achieved higher agreement with reference diagnoses and documented morphology than the H&amp;E-based comparators. On the public evaluation cohort, PathXAI-Report exceeded Qwen + H&amp;E in cancer family match rate by 4.4 percentage points and in finding F1 by 5.7 percentage points. PathXAI generates evidence-linked reports with a self-hosted pretrained vision-language model, without updating its weights.

**comment/C1：** More experimental results will be added in the future version

</details>

### 56. [CoMET: Tracking the Shared Pose Error of Roadside Messages for Asynchronous Vehicle-Infrastructure Cooperative 3D Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09121v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09121

**作者：** Shuwei Sun、Jian Huang、Hui Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09121v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09121v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究异步车路协同3D检测中路边消息的整体位姿平移误差，提出无学习参数的 CoMET：在各消息到达时匹配路侧与自车检测、用互为最近的点对估计偏移，并以 Kalman 滤波按发送方持续跟踪，无匹配时沿用先前估计来校正全部路边框；同时报告基准中零偏移占位与偏移搜索触边的标签问题。

**相关性简评：** 其「用互见证据估计并随时间维护一个共享隐变量、证据缺失时携带状态前行」的机制，可供核查是否能类比 LVR 中潜在状态的维护、视觉证据筛选与缺失证据处理；关于基准占位偏移影响纯路侧目标标签的分析，也可能启发对证据与评价可靠性的检查，但领域相距较远，迁移价值需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoMET: Tracking the Shared Pose Error of Roadside Messages for Asynchronous Vehicle-Infrastructure Cooperative 3D Detection

**abstract/A1：** A roadside sensor can detect vehicles hidden from the ego vehicle, but a pose error shifts every detection in the same message. We study asynchronous vehicle-infrastructure cooperative 3D detection on V2X-Seq using only information available at decision time, without ground truth, future frames, or per-pair calibration offsets at inference. Objects seen by both sensors reveal the shared translation, but even at zero added delay, 38% of validation messages have no matched pair. We propose CoMET (Common-Mode Error Tracking), which estimates this translation over time. At each message arrival, CoMET matches vehicle and roadside detections, estimates the offset from mutually nearest pairs, and updates a Kalman filter for that sender. When no pair matches, the filter carries its estimate forward. The correction applies to all roadside boxes, including those unseen by the ego vehicle. The estimator has no learned parameters; a separate frozen head ranks the merged detections. Across four delays from 0 to 400 ms on 21 validation sequences, CoMET averages 66.63 bird's-eye-view AP at IoU 0.7, compared with 63.18 for the same late fusion using uncorrected online poses and 62.96 for the best intermediate-fusion baseline. Across five detectors from three architectures, CoMET gains 2.2 to 3.5 AP points over online poses and 0.3 to 0.6 over estimating the offset from the current message alone. We find that the benchmark uses a zero offset as a placeholder in 28% of validation messages and that its offset search reaches the boundary in 45% of the rest. These offsets affect the labels for objects seen only from the roadside. Under two alternative label references, CoMET improves on uncorrected online poses in every evaluated population. Retraining intermediate fusion with CoMET poses and corrected labels improves detection on all three architectures tested, with statistically significant gains on two.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 4 figures. Code and experimental results archived at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23999418

</details>

<a id="paper-57"></a>

### 57. [Look Closer, Align Better: Explicit Visual Attributes for Robust EEG-to-Image Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09123v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09123

**作者：** Ke Xiao、Banghua Yang、Wenbin Guo、Fenqi Rong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09123v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09123v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究EEG到图像检索，先验证显式视觉属性对EEG的可解码性与互补信息，再提出AttrEcho：用区域对齐的属性融合（可学习符号残差门）把EEG相关局部属性注入空间对应的预训练特征，并以特征token聚合做对比对齐，在THINGS-EEG上报告检索结果。

**相关性简评：** 其「预训练视觉表示缺乏细粒度细节、需按空间对齐补充显式属性」的思路，可能为LVR中视觉细节如何进入内部表示、以及属性级证据筛选提供可供核查的方法线索，但EEG检索任务与VLM推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Look Closer, Align Better: Explicit Visual Attributes for Robust EEG-to-Image Decoding

**abstract/A1：** EEG-to-image retrieval commonly aligns neural responses with representations from pretrained vision encoders. However, these representations may underrepresent fine-grained visual details needed to distinguish semantically similar images. To address this limitation, we first investigate the EEG decodability and complementary predictive value of explicit visual attributes, identifying visual cues that capture EEG-relevant information beyond pretrained representations. Building on these findings, we propose AttrEcho, a framework that enriches pretrained visual representations with complementary, spatially aligned visual attributes. Specifically, Region-Aligned Visual Attribute Fusion (RAVF) integrates EEG-relevant local visual attributes with spatially corresponding pretrained features through a learnable signed residual gate. We further employ Feature Token Aggregation (FTA), which learns dimension-specific weights across regions to aggregate refined visual features for contrastive alignment with EEG representations. Experiments on THINGS-EEG demonstrate that AttrEcho achieves 94.8% Top-1 and 99.7% Top-5 accuracy in intra-subject 200-way retrieval, while maintaining 82.1% Top-1 accuracy as the gallery expands to 1,600 images with same-concept distractors. AttrEcho also achieves 25.3% Top-1 and 53.0% Top-5 accuracy in inter-subject retrieval. These results highlight the importance of EEG-relevant visual attributes and spatially aligned fusion for fine-grained EEG-to-image retrieval.

</details>

<a id="paper-58"></a>

### 58. [TouchAnnotator: Multi-Source Tactile Supervision with Privileged-View Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09129v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09129

**作者：** Ao Li、Zhihao Yuan、Chubin Zhang、Kunrui Huang、Sheng Xu、Zengjue Chen、Junjie Wang、Jiawei Li、Yansong Tang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09129v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09129v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TouchAnnotator，用统一手部场整合手套压力、几何接触与机器人指尖形变等多源触觉监督，并以 EMA 教师利用同步的自视角与腕视角，将手部局部表示与触觉预测蒸馏进仅用自视角视频和估计位姿的学生模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「特权视角教师蒸馏 + 源感知掩码 + 紧凑历史片段记忆」可能为 LVR 的训练期视觉旁路监督与推理状态的时间上下文维护提供可核查线索；但任务本体是触觉标注而非多模态推理，属外围方法借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TouchAnnotator: Multi-Source Tactile Supervision with Privileged-View Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Egocentric video captures human-object interaction at scale but rarely includes tactile labels for hidden contact and pressure. We introduce TouchAnnotator, an ego-only bimanual tactile annotator trained with multi-source tactile supervision and privileged-view distillation. A canonical hand field unifies glove pressure, geometry-derived human contact, and robot fingertip deformation from six datasets and three embodiments, while source-aware masks preserve valid targets. During training, an EMA teacher observes synchronized ego and wrist views and distills hand-local representations and tactile predictions into a student that uses only ego video and estimated pose at inference. Compact past-clip memory supplies temporal context. We evaluate on EgoTouch, zero-shot FPHA transfer, and 860 synchronized glove recordings. The model transfers to bare-hand contact and produces qualitatively coherent tactile responses across bare-hand, gloved-hand, and robot embodiments. On measured recordings, it improves temporal accuracy by 5.38 points and Volumetric IoU by 5.02 points under a shared protocol. These results show that heterogeneous human--robot supervision and privileged training views can turn egocentric video into dense tactile annotations.

</details>

<a id="paper-59"></a>

### 59. [CARGO: Causal Attribution and Responsibility-Guided Preventive Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09139v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09139

**作者：** Jingjia Teng、Yang Li、Wenjie Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09139v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09139v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CARGO，一个用于混合交通匝道合流的协作式多智能体强化学习框架：以共享 latent 上下文后验和动作条件响应模型估计有向行为影响，并将归因与责任引导的预防性安全优化、条件扩散风险估计结合。

**相关性简评：** 其「有向影响 + 边际团队效用」的因果归因与责任分配机制，可能为 LVR 中推理轨迹或潜步的贡献分配与监督提供可核查线索；但场景为驾驶控制，与视觉推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CARGO: Causal Attribution and Responsibility-Guided Preventive Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving

**abstract/A1：** This study presents Causal Attribution and Responsibility-Guided Optimization (CARGO), a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for cooperative on-ramp merging in mixed traffic. A shared latent-context posterior and an action-conditioned response model estimate directed behavioral influence, with posterior uncertainty propagated into interaction-edge selection. Complementary attribution mechanisms couple signed influence with marginal team utility for cooperative reward shaping and assign responsibility from positive excess tail risk relative to a safe reference policy. Conditional diffusion generates future responses for action-dependent risk estimation. Responsibility-guided preventive optimization calibrates agent-specific risk-reduction requirements to local capacity within a policy trust region, using reserved safety margins, adaptive requirement scaling, and joint feasibility verification. Theoretical analysis establishes risk monotonicity and sufficient local conditions for non-decreasing environmental return under explicit assumptions. Experiments in highway-env across three traffic densities, together with controlled graph-recovery and attribution studies, support the effectiveness of the framework. Under high-density traffic, CARGO increases the success rate by 66.04\\% and reduces the crash rate by 56.23\\% relative to the best evaluated baseline for each metric.

**comment/C1：** Abridged version containing the main results; the full version is under peer review and will be updated here upon acceptance

</details>
