### 60. [Learning Generative Dynamics for 3D Molecule Generation via Sequential Neuro-Symbolic Constraints](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09142v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09142

**作者：** Zhuoran Li、Xu Sun、Chang Wen Chen、Wanyu Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09142v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09142v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SensDiff，把符号化的物理化学规则按「先宏观几何、后微观化学」的顺序、以自适应权重注入 3D 分子扩散模型的训练，使生成过程逐步被约束到有效分子结构上。

**相关性简评：** 该工作并非视觉或多模态推理研究，但其依据扩散轨迹从粗到细演化来分阶段施加约束与权重调度，可能为 diffusion 式 latent 推理状态的分阶段监督和约束设计提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Generative Dynamics for 3D Molecule Generation via Sequential Neuro-Symbolic Constraints

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion models have recently emerged as a powerful paradigm for generating 3D molecules. Despite showing remarkable promise, they learn atomic distributions implicitly from data, without explicitly encoding physicochemical rules into their generative dynamics. Consequently, generated samples can be statistically plausible yet chemically invalid. To address this problem, we propose Sequential Neuro-Symbolic Constrained Diffusion (SensDiff), a framework that integrates symbolic rules into generative modeling, enabling the model to internalize scientific priors during training. Central to SensDiff is the observation that the diffusion trajectory of 3D molecule generation exhibits a coarse-to-fine evolution: valid molecules arise by first mitigating macroscopic geometric conflicts and then refining structures toward microscopic chemical consistency through iterative denoising. Accordingly, SensDiff mirrors this dynamic by sequentially enforcing constraints with adaptive weighting during training, progressively steering generation toward valid molecular geometries. Moreover, SensDiff translates domain knowledge into interpretable generative controls, grounding opaque neural denoising in scientific principles. Experiments on molecular benchmarks and real-world constrained generation tasks confirm that SensDiff consistently improves chemical validity while adhering to physicochemical rules and user-specified targets. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/WanyuGroup/NeurIPS2026\_SensDiff.

</details>

<a id="paper-61"></a>

### 61. [Beyond the Grid: Continuous Dictionary Pursuit for Interpretable Signal Decomposition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09163v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09163

**作者：** Nick Janssen、Melanie Schaller、Bodo Rosenhahn

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09163v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09163v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Continuous Dictionary Pursuit，在由周期、趋势、瞬态和不连续结构组成的异构库上，以数据驱动先验和梯度优化贪心搜索可微连续原子，并按原子预算与残差收敛停止，再将提取原子作为序列模型训练的结构化课程目标。

**相关性简评：** 其残差驱动的贪心原子选择与预算/收敛停止准则，可供核查 LVR 中逐步获取视觉证据与停止决策的设计；把分解原子用作中间监督目标，也可能与 latent 轨迹的分步监督相参照，但跨域迁移尚需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond the Grid: Continuous Dictionary Pursuit for Interpretable Signal Decomposition

**abstract/A1：** Deep learning models achieve high accuracy in signal processing but remain difficult to interpret. Classical matching pursuit and sparse coding offer better transparency but suffer from spectral leakage and grid mismatch when signal frequencies do not align with discrete dictionary atoms. We introduce Continuous Dictionary Pursuit (CDP), a framework for interpretable signal decomposition that learns the parameters of closed-form functions directly from data. CDP optimizes over a continuous manifold of differentiable atoms and, unlike prior continuous pursuit methods that operate on a single analytic family, searches a heterogeneous library spanning periodic, trend, transient, and discontinuous structures. The algorithm employs a greedy iterative strategy using data-driven priors and gradient-based optimization to isolate individual signal components, with discontinuous waveforms made differentiable through spectral sharpness annealing. To ensure a sparse atom set for reconstruction, the framework utilizes a stopping criterion based on a predefined atom budget and residual error convergence. Evaluations on synthetic and real-world datasets show that CDP removes the grid discretization error for atom families contained in its library, recovers generative parameters to within $1\\%$ on stationary synthetic signals expressible in that library, and achieves the best sparsity-accuracy trade-off among classical, continuous-pursuit, and neural baselines in the sparse regime. Beyond decomposition, we demonstrate that the extracted atoms can serve as structured curriculum targets for training sequence models, improving forecasting accuracy over standard end-to-end training on most evaluated datasets. The method offers a glass-box alternative for signal decomposition by providing high reconstruction fidelity alongside direct physical interpretability.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages main paper, 3 pages references, 22 pages appendix, to be published in NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-62"></a>

### 62. [TOFS: Feature Selection with Swap Supervision for Semantic Communication over Fading Channels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09173v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09173

**作者：** Zhiyi Zhu、Eiji Takimoto、Patrick Finnerty、Chikara Ohta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09173v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09173v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TOFS 框架，用接收端分类损失评估特征替换前后的损失差，为选择器网络构造成对排序监督，使其一次前向即预测特征优先级，用于衰落信道下的语义通信图像分类。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 推理无直接关系，但「按替换后任务损失差构造特征贡献监督」可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与贡献分配提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TOFS: Feature Selection with Swap Supervision for Semantic Communication over Fading Channels

**abstract/A1：** Task-oriented semantic communication transmits task-relevant features to support receiver inference under limited communication resources and adverse channel conditions. For image classification, the usefulness of a feature depends on the other transmitted features and the distortion introduced by the wireless channel. Effective feature prioritization should therefore account for classification performance after transmission of the selected features. This paper proposes a Task-Oriented Feature Selection (\\textit{TOFS}) framework and method, which evaluates feature replacements using receiver classification loss. Loss differences confirmed under independent transmission samples provide pairwise ranking supervision for a selector network. The trained selector predicts priorities in a single forward pass. CIFAR-10 simulations demonstrate improved classification accuracy over an importance-based feature selection baseline at the same transmitted feature count and average transmit power. The proposed \\textit{TOFS} framework shows 84\\% accuracy performance under Rayleigh fading and Gaussian noise, compared with the 68\\% performance of a SOTA method.

</details>

<a id="paper-63"></a>

### 63. [Cross-Layer TopK: Dynamically Allocating Sparsity Across Layers in Transcoders](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09233v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09233

**作者：** Anyuan Zhuo、Shaohua Zhang、Tianle Li、Yuan Lin、Diandian Gu、Jun Wang、Pinyan Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09233v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09233v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Cross-Layer TopK：为整个模型设定单一 L0 预算并在训练中跨层动态分配，以训练 Cross-Layer Transcoders 分解 LLM 激活、追踪跨层电路；在 Qwen3-8B-Base 上以更少激活 latent 获得更低 FVU 与更完整电路，固定 per-token 预算还便于稀疏解码加速。

**相关性简评：** 跨层动态分配稀疏预算、固定每 token 开销以换取计算节省的思路，可能可供核查作为 LVR 中跨层或跨步骤 latent 容量与计算分配的借鉴；但该工作为纯文本可解释性工具，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，迁移性需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cross-Layer TopK: Dynamically Allocating Sparsity Across Layers in Transcoders

**abstract/A1：** Cross-Layer Transcoders (CLTs) decompose the activations of large language models into human-interpretable features and trace how these features interact across layers to form circuits, making them a key tool for mechanistic interpretability. However, training CLTs effectively and efficiently is hard. Beyond the usual trade-off between sparsity and reconstruction quality, a CLT has to model all layers at once, which adds a further challenge. The common JumpReLU recipe controls sparsity only indirectly through a penalty, while the direct extension of TopK selects a fixed number of latents in every layer. Yet reconstruction difficulty differs widely across layers, and intermediate layers are much harder than others, so giving every layer the same budget is suboptimal. We propose Cross-Layer TopK, which sets a single L0 budget for the whole model and dynamically allocates it across layers during training. In a comparison with JumpReLU and other TopK variants on Qwen3-8B-Base, this gives lower FVU while using 20% fewer active latents than JumpReLU. The better reconstruction carries over to circuit tracing, yielding more complete circuits with higher replacement and completeness scores. The fixed per-token budget of Cross-Layer TopK also makes it easy to pair with sparse decoding, which cuts both computation and communication and gives a 2.9x speedup per training step. We further show that Cross-Layer TopK follows scaling laws in both latent size and sparsity. We have open-sourced the weights of our CLT at https://huggingface.co/ByteDance-Seed/Cross-Layer-TopK-CLT.

</details>

<a id="paper-64"></a>

### 64. [Controllable Molecule Generation via Sparse Representation Editing: An Interpretability-Driven Perspective](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09235v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09235

**作者：** Zhuoran Li、Xu Sun、Chang Wen Chen、Wanyu Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09235v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09235v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SpaRE，在 LLM 表示上学习过完备稀疏特征空间，把表示解耦为对应化学概念的 latent 特征，再直接编辑这些特征实现指定位置原子/官能团的局部控制与整体性质的全局控制，用于可控分子生成。

**相关性简评：** 其「稀疏过完备特征解耦 + latent 直接干预」的思路可供核查能否用于定位和编辑 VLM 内部承载视觉证据或任务必要信息的 latent，但论文对象是分子生成，与 LVR 推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Controllable Molecule Generation via Sparse Representation Editing: An Interpretability-Driven Perspective

**abstract/A1：** Controllable molecule generation is crucial for diverse scientific applications, such as drug discovery and materials design. While large language models (LLMs) show great promise, their dense and entangled representations impede precise control over the generation of molecules with bespoke substructures or properties. To address this, we propose Sparse Representation Editing (SpaRE), an interpretability-driven framework for fine-grained and precise control in LLM-based molecule generation. The crux of SpaRE is to learn an overcomplete sparse feature space that disentangles LLM representations into a compact set of latent features corresponding to chemically meaningful concepts. Within this space, we can directly manipulate these concept-aligned latent features to achieve (1) local control, by generating target atoms and functional groups at specified positions; and (2) global control, by customizing the overall structural and physicochemical properties within defined ranges. In this way, our framework advances interpretability from post-hoc analysis to actionable generative control. Experiments show that SpaRE can generate chemically desirable molecules under complex constraints in real-world scenarios, while offering mechanistic insights for quantitative structure-property analysis. The code and demo are available at https://github.com/WanyuGroup/ICML2026\_SpaRE.

</details>

<a id="paper-65"></a>

### 65. [Zero-Query Transferable Pretend Benign Attacks with Amortized Online Generation for Cooperative Perception](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09256v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09256

**作者：** Hongwei Lin、Xun Huang、Chenglu Wen、Cheng Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09256v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09256v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究协同感知中的「伪装良性」迁移攻击：用异构替代模型集成把区域选择与多目标扰动策略变为与受害者无关的离线攻击，并提出 RT-TrPB 用区域预测器和可复用扰动字典摊销每条消息的生成成本，同时给出基于时空一致性与身份信任的防御 STTrust。

**相关性简评：** 其「把逐样本优化摊销为预测器 + 可复用字典」的在线生成思路，可能为 LVR 中低成本产生并复用 latent 推理状态提供方法线索；攻击侧重「局部可验证性弱的区域」也可供核查，但距离视觉推理主线较远，需较多核实。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Zero-Query Transferable Pretend Benign Attacks with Amortized Online Generation for Cooperative Perception

**abstract/A1：** Cooperative perception enables an ego agent to acquire information from collaborators beyond its own sensors' reach. Yet, the most beneficial messages are often the hardest to verify from local observations. Pretend Benign (PB) exploits this asymmetry by targeting regions with weak local verifiability and applying multi-objective perturbations, making harmful feature changes appear to be benign assistance. However, PB is victim-dependent: selecting regions and optimizing perturbations require the victim model's architecture, parameters, and gradients, limiting transfer to unseen victims. A transferable attack must capture properties shared across cooperative detection models rather than those specific to one network. We address this by building a victim-agnostic attack from a heterogeneous ensemble of surrogate models and encoding its capabilities in a reusable dictionary to reduce per-message generation cost. Specifically, Transferable Pretend Benign (TrPB) transfers PB's region selection criterion and multi-objective perturbation strategy across heterogeneous surrogates to construct an offline attack. We further propose RT-TrPB, which encodes transferable regions and perturbation patterns into a region predictor and reusable perturbation dictionary, amortizing per-message attack generation. Experiments on widely used cooperative perception benchmarks show that RT-TrPB bypasses the consensus-based defenses ROBOSAC and FLD with success rates approaching 100% and generates each perturbation in under 56 ms. To counter PB-style attacks masquerading as benign collaboration, we also propose STTrust, which verifies received messages using spatial and temporal consistency and incorporates identity-level trust. STTrust detects over 90% of PB-style attacks and restores perception performance to near the attack-free level.

</details>

<a id="paper-66"></a>

### 66. [AutoRefine: Auto-Calibrating Code Judges from Workflow Outcomes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09291v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09291

**作者：** Shervin Shahidi、James Poon、Hang Xie、Kenneth O. Xu、Robin Håkanson、Vikram Nair、Jens Pillgram-Larsen、Nachiappan Nagappan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09291v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09291v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AutoRefine：用一个同时看到判官输入与工作流结果标签的教师模型改写可部署判官的 prompt，并在结果无法由判官可见输入解释时弃权，从而用免费但嘈杂的结果标签自动校准代码判官。

**相关性简评：** 其「用下游结果信号做监督、并对输入无法解释的样本弃权」的做法，可能为 LVR 中轨迹训练的结果式监督与判别内部状态是否含必要证据提供可供核查的线索，但任务本身与视觉多模态推理无关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AutoRefine: Auto-Calibrating Code Judges from Workflow Outcomes

**abstract/A1：** LLM judges increasingly serve as quality gates for code changes that have no test oracle, but a judge prompt must be calibrated for its task and recalibrated as the system it judges evolves, while hand labels for the calibration are resource intensive and go stale. Development workflows emit outcome labels for free (tests pass or fail, changes land or are abandoned), but these labels are noisy. We present AutoRefine, a loop that rewrites a deployable judge's prompt to reproduce outcome labels, with an informed teacher that sees the judge's input plus the outcome, explains each label it keeps, and abstains when the outcome cannot be explained from what the judge sees. On a held-out dataset with an execution oracle, calibration raised chance-corrected agreement (Scott's $π$) from 0.36 to 0.60. On a deployed agent's outcome labels, with 25\\% flipped at random to introduce noise, the teacher set aside flipped \\textsc{fail} labels at more than twice the rate of genuine ones, cutting overall label noise from 25\\% to 20\\%. Recalibrating that agent's judge using AutoRefine raised its agreement with merge/abandon outcomes on 17 independently held-out diffs from the current workflow ($π$ 0.16 to 0.51); on 16 diffs from older workflows, the previous judge stayed ahead, illustrating the need for recalibration as the agent evolves. By abstaining, the teacher not only removes noisy labels but also flags changes whose outcome the judge's input cannot explain, telling a team when a judge needs better inputs rather than a better prompt.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 3 figures, prepared for submission to CAIN 2027 industrial track

</details>

<a id="paper-67"></a>

### 67. [A Stochastic Latent Variable Generalization of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09302v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09302

**作者：** Nikos Giakoumoglou、Paschalis Giakoumoglou、Tania Stathaki

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09302v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09302v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 S-JEPA，在掩码图像预测的 JEPA 框架中把原本固定的条件 token z 改为由学习到的后验与先验建模的随机潜变量，在不重建像素的情况下产生预测不确定性和多样补全。

**相关性简评：** 其随机潜变量与多假设采样可能为 LVR 中多模态 latent 状态、候选视觉补全以及基于不确定性的停止与验证提供方法线索，但论文仍属视觉自监督预训练，向多模态推理迁移的可行性需要核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Stochastic Latent Variable Generalization of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures

**abstract/A1：** Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) predict the latent embedding of a target signal from a context signal, with an auxiliary variable z conditioning the predictor. Despite its prominent role in the original JEPA blueprint, the content of z has been carried by a fixed deterministic token in every prior instantiation, collapsing the genuinely multi-modal distribution over plausible completions to a point estimate. We present S-JEPA, which realises z as a proper stochastic latent variable for masked image prediction: a learned posterior infers z from context and target during training, while a learned context-conditioned prior approximates the distribution of plausible completions at inference, with no decoders and no reconstruction. In its deterministic limit the objective reduces to the I-JEPA loss in functional form, generalising that objective rather than strictly containing the I-JEPA model. Our central finding is that stochasticity adds capabilities, error-correlated predictive uncertainty and diverse completions, at no cost to representation quality: under matched architectures and schedules S-JEPA improves over the deterministic I-JEPA baseline and exceeds LeJEPA at matched architecture, while additionally producing spatially structured uncertainty maps and semantically diverse completions, neither of which the deterministic predictor produces.

**comment/C1：** WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-68"></a>

### 68. [DiffusionRef-IMU: Guide-Anchored Residual Diffusion for Cross-Location Inertial Measurement Unit Translation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09323v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09323

**作者：** M. M. Golam Hafiz、Safia Akter Simi、Md. Rakibul Islam、M. F. Mridha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09323v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09323v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出两阶段跨部位 IMU 信号翻译框架 DiffusionRef-IMU：先用确定性 Direct 网络估计目标位置信号，再用引导锚定的残差扩散模型只修正剩余差异，并在受试者不交叉的多折留出、容量匹配基线与真零样本设定下评测。

**相关性简评：** 「确定性估计＋残差扩散精修」且只对残差建模的两阶段方案，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的迭代细化、锚定防漂移与生成步数—成本权衡提供可核查线索；但任务是连续信号回归，无视觉或推理成分。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DiffusionRef-IMU: Guide-Anchored Residual Diffusion for Cross-Location Inertial Measurement Unit Translation

**abstract/A1：** Cross-location variation in wearable inertial measurement unit (IMU) signals complicates learning when measurements from a desired target body location are unavailable. This study proposes DiffusionRef-IMU, a two-stage cross-location translation framework in which a deterministic Direct network first estimates the target signal and a guide-anchored residual diffusion model refines only the remaining target discrepancy. The evaluation comprises five subject-disjoint activity-location composition-held-out folds, capacity-matched deterministic and flow-based residual baselines, source-only activity conditioning, computational ablation, and true zero-shot evaluation on two independent datasets. DiffusionRef-IMU with 16-step deterministic DDIM achieved a mean nRMSE of 0.087538 +/- 0.004922, compared with 0.090262 for Direct, 0.088847 for deterministic residual refinement, and 0.088417 for eight-step CompFlow, and outperformed each baseline in all five folds. Four-step DDIM retained a mean nRMSE of 0.087820 at 47.62 ms per window, providing a favourable quality-computation trade-off. Replacing ground-truth activity conditioning with a source-only activity predictor (macro-F1 0.8473) increased 16-step DiffusionRef nRMSE only to 0.088190. True zero-shot evaluation produced mixed results: DiffusionRef was marginally favourable on Grouvel2025 but remained worse than Direct on Saldaña2025. Overall, guide-anchored residual diffusion improves controlled cross-location reconstruction while revealing clear limits in zero-shot external transfer.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 5 figures, 6 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-69"></a>

### 69. [RegNC-Mamba3: Shared Structural Feature Learning Using Non-Causal State Space Principles for Multi-Modal Image Registration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09336v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09336

**作者：** Zhikang Li、Yan Wu、Xin Hu、Yi Dai、Ming Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09336v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09336v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RegNC-Mamba3，一种粗到细的多模态图像配准方法：以非因果状态空间原理（NC-SSP）对所有循环扫描路径做相对权重归一化聚合，构建不依赖扫描顺序、无长程衰减的全局隐状态，并用不同方向的可学习 Log-Gabor 核构造复值序列以增强跨模态共享结构特征。

**相关性简评：** NC-SSP 对多路径隐状态做全局聚合以摆脱扫描路径依赖、缓解长程信息衰减，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的并行/多路径更新与信息坍缩问题提供可核查的方法线索；但该工作面向像素级配准而非推理，状态是否可用于推理期生成需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RegNC-Mamba3: Shared Structural Feature Learning Using Non-Causal State Space Principles for Multi-Modal Image Registration

**abstract/A1：** The main challenge of multi-modal image registration is shared structural feature extraction with higher accuracy and lower computational complexity. Compared with a State Space Model (SSM), the State Space Principles (SSP) formulation of Mamba-3 uses a more accurate discretization with lower truncation error while maintaining linear complexity. However, vanilla SSP is designed for natural language processing (NLP) and is not directly suitable for images. To address this issue, we propose a novel non-causal SSP (NC-SSP) with shared structure awareness and develop a coarse-to-fine registration method named RegNC-Mamba3. First, NC-SSP is designed to eliminate the dependence of the integral computation on the scanning path. By combining relative-weight normalization with aggregation over all circular scan paths, NC-SSP constructs a globally hidden state without long-range decay, which provides a wider receptive field and higher efficiency than the original Mamba-3. Second, NC-SSP is modified to strengthen the extraction of cross-modal structural features. When computing hidden states and feature interactions, we construct complex-valued sequences in NC-SSP by applying learnable Log-Gabor kernels, with different orientations to each head for frequency-domain modulation. This improves the perception of shared structural information across modalities and increases both registration accuracy and quality. Experiments on VIS-SAR and VIS-IR demonstrate that RegNC-Mamba3 outperforms the compared detector-based and semi-dense multi-modal registration methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-70"></a>

### 70. [KineCouple: Substrate-conditioned residual readout for enzyme kinetic prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09377v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09377

**作者：** Chun Kang、Yunbo Zhang、Fanhu Zeng、Jian Liu、Siwei Deng、Lei Sha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09377v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09377v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向酶动力学参数预测，用底物作 query、残基特征作 key/value 做交叉注意力，实现底物条件下（而非均匀）的读出，并以门控残差在保留全局蛋白表示的同时加入条件读出与均匀读出之差；在 CataPro 基准上相对复现的 CataPro 仅有极小提升。

**相关性简评：** 「query 条件下聚合 + 门控残差保留全局表示」可能为 LVR 中按问题筛选视觉证据、避免条件化读出造成信息坍缩提供一个可核查的读出模块线索，但领域相距较远且收益微小，借鉴价值有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** KineCouple: Substrate-conditioned residual readout for enzyme kinetic prediction

**abstract/A1：** Accurate prediction of enzyme kinetic parameters can support enzyme engineering and metabolic modeling by complementing experimental characterization. Existing prediction methods often pool residue-level protein features before combining them with substrate features. Such substrate-independent pooling cannot adapt residue weighting to the substrate during aggregation, potentially diluting information relevant to a specific enzyme-substrate pair. Since the contributions of individual residues to catalysis can depend on the substrate, substrate-conditioned aggregation offers a way to emphasize residue features relevant to the prediction. We introduce KineCouple, which uses substrate queries and residue-level keys and values in cross-attention, allowing the substrate to guide residue selection before aggregation. The model preserves the global protein representation and adds a gated correction defined as the difference between the conditioned readout and a uniform readout. ProtT5 and MolT5 remain frozen, and separate models predict kcat and Km on a logarithmic scale. In pooled out-of-fold predictions on the CataPro benchmarks, KineCouple reduces kcat RMSE from 1.333 to 1.330 and increases PCC from 0.492 to 0.496 relative to reproduced CataPro. For Km, RMSE decreases from 0.998 to 0.997 and PCC increases from 0.633 to 0.634. Spearman correlation also improves for both tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-71"></a>

### 71. [Exploring Weight-Space Programming: Capabilities, Generalization, and Limitations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09382v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09382

**作者：** Junru Hu、Cheng Tang、Wei Li、Hongze Zhu、Chun-Mei Feng、Qi Zhang、Yirong Chen、Ming Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09382v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09382v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统考察 Weight-Space Programming：让推理 agent 直接检查并编辑模型权重，在 7 个基准、两个模型规模上执行 16 条编辑轨迹，发现仅改动 1023 个参数即可带来最高 29.45 点的提升，但跨任务迁移不均衡、结果波动大，且 6 条轨迹因改进无法可靠验证而保留原权重。

**相关性简评：** 其「稀疏参数干预 ＋ 验证失败则回退」的协议可能可供核查，用于定位承载任务必要信息的内部计算位置、评估状态更新的贡献分配与可靠性；但对象是权重而非推理期 latent 状态，也无视觉证据筛选，迁移到 LVR 需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Exploring Weight-Space Programming: Capabilities, Generalization, and Limitations

**abstract/A1：** Weight-Space Programming (WSP) enables reasoning agents to directly inspect and modify neural network parameters, offering an alternative to gradient-based model adaptation. However, its capabilities, cross-task generalization, and practical limitations remain insufficiently understood. We systematically investigate WSP through 16 editing trajectories across seven benchmarks and two model scales (Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-9B). Our findings reveal three key insights. First, WSP can achieve substantial performance gains through highly localized interventions. On full held-out evaluations, accuracy improves by up to 29.45, 4.42, 36.72, and 17.11 percentage points on MATH-500, GPQA Diamond, IFEval, and SciBench, respectively. Notably, modifying only 1,023 parameters yields a 29.45-point improvement. Second, WSP exhibits promising but uneven cross-task generalization. A Math-edited checkpoint improves GPQA Diamond, IFEval, and SciBench by 3.03, 12.18, and 20.10 points, respectively, without further editing, while leaving SciCode performance unchanged. Third, effective editing remains challenging and inconsistent. Outcomes vary substantially across trajectories, and six of the 16 trajectories retain the original weights when candidate improvements cannot be reliably validated. Together, these results highlight WSP's potential for highly localized model adaptation while revealing important challenges in intervention discovery, reliability, and cross-task transfer. Homepage: https://wsp-scope.github.io/ GitHub: https://github.com/WSP-Scope/WSP-Scope-Project

</details>

<a id="paper-72"></a>

### 72. [A Bridge from Paste to Place: Physics-Guided One-Step Image Composition via Object-Scene Interaction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09387v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09387

**作者：** Hongzhen Li、Youwei Pan、Yingfang Zhu、Sen Zhu、Fengjie Zhu、Leilei Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09387v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09387v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Paste2Place，一个基于 flow 的端到端模型，借助 albedo-shading-residual 的显式分解与重组，学习从粘贴输入到真实合成结果的一步 latent 桥接，并用 Blender 渲染管线构建约 8.6 万训练对与 1500 例基准。

**相关性简评：** 其一步 latent 桥接与显式外观分解可供核查「如何在融合时把视觉细节保留在内部状态中」，但论文目标是图像合成而非多模态推理，联系较弱，仅作方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Bridge from Paste to Place: Physics-Guided One-Step Image Composition via Object-Scene Interaction

**abstract/A1：** Realistic image composition must make a pasted object appear naturally present in a scene while preserving the fine details of both foreground and background. Reference-based insertion methods can change object pose and appearance, while foreground harmonization methods and shadow-specific synthesis methods each address only part of this task. An efficient, unified solution to this task remains an open challenge. We introduce Paste2Place, an end-to-end, physics-guided flow-based model that learns a direct, one-step latent bridge from pasted inputs to realistic composites, guided by explicit albedo-shading-residual decomposition and recomposition within the model. To support model training and evaluation, we develop a Blender rendering and validation pipeline to generate paired composites with physical annotations, and construct real-scene pairs from open-source object-removal data. This effort yields 86,273 training pairs and a 1,500-example benchmark. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments demonstrate that Paste2Place achieves substantial advantages in image composition quality and inference speed. Overall, our work provides a clear task definition and an effective solution for image composition, and fills a gap in large-scale, high-quality datasets and benchmarks for training and evaluating image composition.

</details>

<a id="paper-73"></a>

### 73. [Causal graph fusion across incomplete cohorts and causal steering of tabular foundation models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09400v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09400

**作者：** Yanke Li、Dhiyaa Al Jorf、Ayoube Ighissou、Anke Scheel-Sailer、Robert Riener、Diego Paez-Granados

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.AP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09400v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09400v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出跨不完整队列的因果图融合，使缺失值处理嵌入因果发现过程，并在推理阶段把学到的图编译进冻结表格基础模型的注意力，实现因果注意力引导。

**相关性简评：** 其「不重训即把外部因果图注入冻结模型注意力」的推理期引导方式，或可为 LVR 中定向写入视觉或因果证据到 latent 状态提供可核查的接线思路，但对象为表格临床数据，迁移性需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Causal graph fusion across incomplete cohorts and causal steering of tabular foundation models

**abstract/A1：** Clinical data are split across incomplete cohorts with partially overlapping variables. Causal discovery under missing data and multi-source integration have developed separately, and tabular foundation models cannot take a causal graph without retraining. We make two contributions. First, causal graph fusion learns a partial ancestral graph on the shared variables, fuses it into each cohort's graph under temporal background knowledge and lets Markov blankets guide re-imputation, so missing data are handled inside discovery. Second, causal attention steering compiles the learned graph into the attention of a frozen tabular foundation model at inference. Against known ground truth, fusion dominates pooled and per-cohort baselines in every missingness and heterogeneity condition tested (p&lt;0.001), and steering improves discrimination under distribution shift (p&lt;10^{-5}), a gain that a random graph does not produce. In two spinal cord injury cohorts, fusion orients age, albumin and hemoglobin as ancestors of length of stay, a set no single cohort orients in full, and yields compact, temporally ordered predictor sets that match or exceed automatic feature selection on four of five outcomes.

</details>

<a id="paper-74"></a>

### 74. [PhyCAD: Physics-Aware Text-to-CAD Generation via Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09401v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09401

**作者：** Yuxin Cui、Weijie Zheng、Zhengyang Li、Han Wang、Wenjie Feng、Qiyu Kang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09401v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09401v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** PhyCAD 研究文本到参数化 CAD 程序的生成，构建含几何与物理约束的数据集及执行—几何检查—有限元分析的自动验证器，并提出按验证状态分层的 GRPO 分层平衡优势，以优先满足关键约束并保留部分进展反馈。

**相关性简评：** 其「分层分组优势 + 组内同伴排序」为异质、多级验证奖励下的信用分配提供了具体线索，可能可供核查 LVR 中轨迹训练与视觉/物理约束监督的优先级设计，但论文本身不涉及 latent 视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PhyCAD: Physics-Aware Text-to-CAD Generation via Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Language-driven computer-aided design (CAD) generation enables the creation of editable parametric models from text, yet geometric correctness alone does not ensure physical feasibility. We present PhyCAD, a framework for physics-aware Text-to-CAD generation that trains language models to produce executable CadQuery programs under geometric and physical constraints. To support this setting, we construct a Text-to-CAD dataset that links parametric CAD models with geometric specifications, loading conditions, and physical constraints. We further develop an automated CAD--CAE verifier that combines program execution, geometric checks, and finite element analysis to provide feedback for model training. A key learning challenge is to prioritize joint satisfaction of geometric and physical constraints while retaining informative feedback on partial progress. A flat aggregation of heterogeneous rewards can obscure this priority, allowing improvements in individual criteria to compensate for unmet design requirements. We therefore introduce a Hierarchical Balanced Advantage for Group Relative Policy Optimization, grouping candidates into levels according to their verification status and combining between-level progress with objective-wise peer ranking within each level. This formulation preserves cross-level priority in the training signal while providing fine-grained feedback for satisfying constraints and improving feasible designs. Experiments across multiple language models show that PhyCAD consistently improves joint geometric and physical constraint satisfaction. These results demonstrate the value of verifiable engineering feedback for advancing language-driven CAD generation beyond geometric reconstruction toward physics-aware design.

</details>

<a id="paper-75"></a>

### 75. [MIRA: Mitigating Pole Geometry Bias in Recurrent Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09412v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09412

**作者：** Róisín Luo、Ihsan Ullah、Christian Gagné、Dong Li、Karyn Morrissey

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09412v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09412v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文将选择性 SSM、RWKV、RetNet 等 Transformer 循环后继统一刻画为 IIR 系统，指出其参数化导致极点被限制在实轴，并借鉴 lattice–ladder 结构提出可同时表示实极点和复共轭极点的线性时间循环架构 Mira，预训练 0.14B 模型验证困惑度与上下文长度泛化。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本架构工作，未涉及视觉推理；但其对循环状态极点和时域模态的刻画，可能为分析 LVR 中循环/多步推理状态的表达力与长度泛化提供可核查的工具视角。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MIRA: Mitigating Pole Geometry Bias in Recurrent Language Models

**abstract/A1：** We show that the Transformer's recurrent successors --- such as selective state-space models (SSMs), Receptance Weighted Key Value (RWKV), and Retentive Network (RetNet) --- can be characterized as infinite-impulse-response (IIR) systems. The zero--pole geometries of these IIR systems characterize the temporal modes they can represent. However, to trade parallelism, the algebraic structures of the parameterizations of these recurrent systems often give rise to poles constrained to the real axis, thereby inducing an architectural bias in their recurrent dynamics. To unify these recurrent architectures and eliminate their pole geometry bias, inspired by the lattice--ladder realization of general IIR systems, we present \\textsc{Mira} (\\textbf{M}odal \\textbf{I}IR \\textbf{R}ecurrent \\textbf{A}rchitecture), a unified family of linear-time recurrent architectures for building large language models (LLMs) with a highly parallel algebraic structure. Each \\textsc{Mira} block realizes a higher-order IIR system that represents temporal modes from token streams through a lattice--ladder structure, followed by a feed-forward network (FFN). This realization enables the model to represent the temporal modes associated with both real and complex-conjugate poles. We pretrain \\textsc{Mira-S}, a 12-layer small-scale LLM with 0.14B parameters composed entirely of \\textsc{Mira} blocks. Throughout the pretraining, \\textsc{Mira-S} achieves lower perplexity than strong recurrent baselines, exhibits stronger context-window generalization beyond the training length, and attains higher throughput with a dedicated CUDA kernel.

</details>

<a id="paper-76"></a>

### 76. [Future-Informed Actor-Critic](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09417v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09417

**作者：** Samy Mokeddem、Gaspard Lambrechts、Adrien Bolland、Damien Ernst

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09417v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09417v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「未来知情价值函数」，使 critic 可同时以未来状态、动作与奖励的外生和内生特征为条件，并据此导出未来知情策略梯度与相应 actor-critic 算法，在噪声与稀疏奖励环境中优于标准 actor-critic。

**相关性简评：** 其用未来信息（尤其依赖动作的内生特征）改善贡献分配的做法，可能为 latent 推理轨迹训练中的信用分配与中间状态监督提供线索，可供核查如何用后续状态的 latent 特征作为训练信号。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Future-Informed Actor-Critic

**abstract/A1：** Actor-critic methods optimize a parametrized policy using a critic to estimate their policy gradient. Recent work has shown that conditioning the critic on features of future states, actions, or rewards can improve sample efficiency and even final return. However, existing methods are restricted to exogenous features (independent of actions), overlooking endogenous features (dependent on actions) that may be crucial for credit assignment. We introduce the future-informed value function, which can be conditioned on both exogenous and endogenous features of future states, actions, and rewards. Then, we express the policy gradient using this value function, yielding the future-informed policy gradient. Building on that, we propose the future-informed actor-critic algorithm. Empirical results show that this algorithm leveraging endogenous features of the future outperforms standard actor-critic algorithms in environments with noisy and sparse rewards.

**comment/C1：** 4 pages workshop-paper

</details>

<a id="paper-77"></a>

### 77. [The Role Of Recurrent Refinement In Medical Image Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09436v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09436

**作者：** Joao D. S. Marques、David Calhas、Arlindo L. Oliveira

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09436v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09436v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在分割骨干网络后附加 CRF、现代 Hopfield 网络和卷积 GRU 等轻量循环精修模块，并在 Kvasir-SEG 与 ARCADE 上考察测试时迭代次数增加（包括超出训练迭代范围）时的性能与稳健性。

**相关性简评：** 其「循环精修 + 测试时迭代外推」可供核查 LVR 中迭代式 latent 状态更新、动态计算量与超出训练长度后泛化的关系，但任务是医学图像分割，缺少语言推理与多模态证据使用层面的验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Role Of Recurrent Refinement In Medical Image Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** While feed-forward segmentation networks perform well on large cohorts, they degrade in constrained clinical settings with few training samples and imaging artifacts. Human vision, by contrast, remains robust under these conditions, a property partly attributed to the recurrent and feedback connectivity of the visual cortex, which refines visual information over time. Inspired by this, we append lightweight recurrent refinement blocks to segmentation backbones, namely Conditional Random Fields (CRFs), modern Hopfield networks and convolutional GRUs, and study their behaviour as the number of iterations increases at test time (including beyond the training horizon). Evaluated on two open-source datasets, Kvasir- SEG and ARCADE, our recurrent blocks add less than 1.5% parameters and yield gains on clean data (up to 1.4 pp) and under noise (up to 17 pp) while reducing HD95. Code is available at https://github.com/joaodsmarques/recurrencehelpsegmentation .

</details>

<a id="paper-78"></a>

### 78. [Not All Entropy Is Equal: Reward-Conditioned Entropy Allocation in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09448v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09448

**作者：** Song Yu、Li Li、Zhenming Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09448v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09448v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RCEA 分层诊断框架，将完整轨迹熵与平均 token 熵分离，并按验证器结果与过程有效性条件化，分析六种 RLVR 目标下熵变化、正确解模式保留及局部协方差更新与推理准确率的关系。

**相关性简评：** 其轨迹级、结果条件化的熵分解与正确解模式审计，可能为 LVR 训练中 latent 轨迹的探索、贡献分配和多样性坍塌诊断提供分析线索，但尚需核查能否迁移到视觉证据与 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Not All Entropy Is Equal: Reward-Conditioned Entropy Allocation in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards

**abstract/A1：** Policy entropy is widely used to diagnose exploration and collapse in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), yet its magnitude does not consistently track reasoning performance across optimization objectives. We argue that this ambiguity arises because global token entropy conflates uncertainty in successful and failed trajectories, diversity across correct solution strategies, and variation among realizations of the same strategy. We introduce \\textbf{Reward-Conditioned Entropy Allocation (RCEA)}, a hierarchical diagnostic framework that separates complete-trajectory entropy from average token entropy, conditions uncertainty on verifier outcome and process validity, measures correct-solution-mode retention, and connects these population-level quantities to local covariance-driven updates. In controlled sweeps over six RLVR objectives at two model scales, entropy trajectories do not induce a stable ordering of reasoning accuracy. Token entropy is higher for verifier-failed than successful trajectories under both broad separately aggregated estimates and a common-question paired analysis, whose confidence intervals exclude equality at 10 of 12 displayed endpoints. A local covariance approximation predicts the direction of adjacent-checkpoint entropy change in 95.5\\% of nonfailed transitions; a reward-stratified endpoint proxy further shows that positive chosen-token covariance is more tail-concentrated within failed prefixes. Every reportable trained endpoint exhibits some contraction in independently audited correct-solution-mode breadth, whose positive association with large-\\(k\\) coverage remains exploratory. These results show that entropy collapse should be diagnosed by which uncertainty is removed, rather than by global entropy alone.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-79"></a>

### 79. [From LoRA to Language: Recovering Skill Documents from Adapters](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09454v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09454

**作者：** Saranya Dintakurthy Swadha、Pavan C Shekar、Arpit Jain、Aswanth Krishnan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09454v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09454v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究能否从 LoRA 适配器及其冻结基座模型中反写回原始「技能文档」，比较了直接读权重、权重到文本的学习映射、以及在适配模型上训练小 writer，并用关键指令覆盖率与任务成功率评分；对仅学到行为的适配器，则用基座与适配模型的行为差异来生成技能。

**相关性简评：** 其「行为差分探测适配器隐含内容」与「权重到文本的可解码性」线索，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否保留任务必要信息、以及从生成行为反推内部表示内容的可行性，但纯文本设定且不涉及视觉推理，联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From LoRA to Language: Recovering Skill Documents from Adapters

**abstract/A1：** Agent skills are short documents that teach a language model how to handle a class of tasks. To save context, these documents are increasingly compiled into low-rank adapters (LoRA), which keep the behaviour but lose the text: an adapter is hard to read, edit, audit or move to another model. We ask whether the skill document can be written back from the adapter and its frozen base model, and we score each recovered document by the share of the original's key instructions it states and by the tasks it solves. Reading the weights alone recovered little with the methods we tried; a learned map from weights to text passed on about one bit of the document. Running the adapter worked much better. The Executing Writer, a small writer trained on top of the adapted model, recovers 39-44% of the key instructions of held-out skills, against 1.3-1.5% with another skill's adapter, and on adapters fitted to 41 external skills its documents restore 85-91% of the original's task gain. These strongest results are on adapters whose training stored the document; adapters trained on behaviour alone are much harder for trained writers (1-2% of instructions). For these adapters we test an agent that runs the base and adapted models and writes a skill from where they differ. On ALFWorld its skills recover 58-71% of a LoRA's gain and carry over to models that cannot load the LoRA, lifting Llama-3.1-8B from 5% to 55%. Whether such a skill comes from the adapter depends on what the adapter learned: when its behaviour cannot be guessed, the adapter is the source; when it can, a reward loop without the adapter does as well.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 5 figures, 18 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-80"></a>

### 80. [Left-Right-Consistent Iterative Stereo Matching via Cross-Injection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09458v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09458

**作者：** Manuel Knott、Diogo Luvizon、Daniel Scharstein、Elias Vansteenkiste

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09458v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09458v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对立体匹配只预测左视图视差的问题，提出 cross-injection（CI）：在迭代精化模型的每一步中，把显式的左右不一致量（带符号）作为额外输入注入，使左右两次预测相互耦合。实验显示该方法在多个基准上显著降低左右不一致，并基本保持物体边界锐利，同时也能降低单视图视差误差。

**相关性简评：** 其“把显式的不一致/校验信号逐步回注到迭代状态更新中”的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与循环推理状态的误差反馈提供可核查线索，但该论文面向立体匹配，未涉及 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Left-Right-Consistent Iterative Stereo Matching via Cross-Injection

**abstract/A1：** State-of-the-art stereo matching models predominantly predict the disparity of only the left (reference) view. However, applications such as per-eye AR/VR rendering, novel view synthesis, or two-view depth fusion require disparity for both views, and need those two maps to agree. The usual workaround of running the network a second time on the horizontally flipped and swapped pair gives two independent predictions that can be inconsistent, especially on transparent surfaces, in textureless regions, and at occlusion boundaries. We introduce cross-injection (CI), a lightweight extension for iterative (refinement-based) models that can be added to any pretrained left-only iterative matcher. It couples the two passes by injecting the explicit, signed left-right inconsistency as an additional input cue at every step of the refinement stage. We empirically show that CI substantially reduces left-right disagreement on every benchmark (more than halving it on Booster for FoundationStereo), while largely preserving sharp object boundaries. Furthermore, using two state-of-the-art stereo matching models, we show that CI also reduces the single-view disparity error, making it a worthwhile extension even if only left disparity is needed.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-81"></a>

### 81. [ENGRAM: From Agent Experience to Weights through Verified Consolidation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09472v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09472

**作者：** Ruiyi Yang、Zechen Li、Lihuan Li、Du Yin、Hao Xue、Flora D. Salim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09472v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09472v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ENGRAM 框架，将「是否纳入训练」与「何时释放外部记忆」分开：用示范感知门控挑选经验、以 Engram Trace Graph 提供上下文，再用验证门控比较「无检索的新模型」与「有检索的基模型」来决定外部记录能否退出检索。

**相关性简评：** 其「内部已内化即撤除外部证据」的验证式释放门控，可为核查 LVR 中何时停止依赖外部视觉证据（STOP）及外部旁路与内部状态的贡献分配提供方法线索，需人工判断迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ENGRAM: From Agent Experience to Weights through Verified Consolidation

**abstract/A1：** Language-model agents use experience through external retrieval and parameter learning. Consolidating stored experience into model weights requires deciding both which experience to train on and when its external records can leave retrieval. Admission based solely on student mastery can exclude useful teacher demonstrations, while removal immediately after training can discard guidance that the model still needs. We introduce ENGRAM, a framework that separates training admission from memory release. A demonstration-aware gate admits skills based on student mastery or sufficient successful teacher demonstrations. The student learns from selected action traces with context from an Engram Trace Graph (ETG). A verification gate then compares the updated model without episodic retrieval against the base model with retrieval on reserved probes. Release requires valid training participation and scores meeting both relative and absolute criteria; records for skills that fail verification remain retrievable. Experiments across interactive and reasoning benchmarks show consistent gains over frozen-weight ETG controls, with ablations supporting demonstration-aware admission and selective adapter activation. A controlled held-out comparison shows that verified release substantially reduces the retrievable footprint while remaining close to retain-all performance and outperforming immediate and budget-matched random release. ENGRAM also improves task performance while reducing input-token usage and model calls. Skill-level verification further reduces retrievable experience through selective archival, retaining external guidance for skills that do not meet the release criteria. Code is available in anonymous.4open.science/r/engram-anonymous.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-82"></a>

### 82. [LEMMO: A Large Electromagnetic Model leveraging Pretrained LLMs for Million-Sample Signal Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09476v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09476

**作者：** Mingkai Li、Jiajun Wu、Yihan Mao、Zilin Sang、Taosong Yu、Yuan Ma、Minghe Liu、Qianqian Yang、Zhiguo Shi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SD

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09476v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09476v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LEMMO，用线性注意力电磁编码器加抗混叠时间重采样处理最长八百万复数样本的原始 IQ 记录，并通过指令条件下的可学习查询检索信号证据，支撑识别与结构化详细描述，并发布大规模 EM 语料。

**相关性简评：** 指令条件查询从超长原始信号中筛选证据、以及重采样压缩下的细节保留，可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选与长序列表示压缩提供可供核查的线索，但研究对象是电磁信号而非视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LEMMO: A Large Electromagnetic Model leveraging Pretrained LLMs for Million-Sample Signal Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Large language models have expanded beyond text to vision, audio, and other modalities, but understanding raw electromagnetic (EM) signals remains challenging. Wideband recordings can contain millions of samples, whereas existing waveform-based EM models are primarily evaluated on short in-phase and quadrature (IQ) segments, leaving long-recording understanding and detailed description insufficiently explored. We introduce LEMMO, a large EM model for instruction-driven recognition and description of long raw IQ recordings. LEMMO combines a linear-attention EM encoder with anti-aliased temporal resampling to process up to eight million complex samples. Its EM-text interaction module conditions learnable queries on instructions before retrieving signal evidence. Compact queries support recognition, while structured queries with auxiliary factual supervision organize scene-level properties, signal attributes, and cross-signal relationships for detailed description. We also construct and release a unified 1.38-TB corpus of real-world and synthetic EM signals, including LEMMO-ShortQA and LEMMO-LongDesc. Compared with MERLIN-LoRA, LEMMO improves mean EM-Bench perception accuracy by 15.77 %, mean strategy-generation ROUGE-L F1 by 4.38 points, and mean LEMMO-ShortQA accuracy by 14.31 %. It also improves categorical and numerical fact F1 on both LEMMO-LongDesc tasks while preserving its language backbone's MMLU performance. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/N1rv4n4/LEMMO and https://huggingface.co/datasets/DaftJun/RFLAS, respectively.

</details>

<a id="paper-83"></a>

### 83. [FusionCLIP: Multimodal Contrastive Pretraining for Fusion Plasma Diagnostics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09493v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09493

**作者：** Qiang Chen、Xiao Wang、Qingquan Yang、Hao Si、Zikang Yan、Guosheng Xu、Jin Tang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09493v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09493v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FusionCLIP 多模态对比预训练框架，用于托卡马克放电诊断：用「时空稳定性选择」筛选视频中有信息的视觉 token，并用按观测时间戳建模的 Temporal Query Encoder 融合异步诊断信号，再以对比学习对齐两种模态，并在自建 EAST-MMD 数据集的扰动预测、ELM 分类与 H98 回归任务上评估。

**相关性简评：** 其视觉 token 筛选与异步时间戳编码可供核查是否能迁移为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与多源证据对齐机制，但论文本身不做推理或 latent 状态生成，且对比对齐是否压缩视觉细节尚不明确。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FusionCLIP: Multimodal Contrastive Pretraining for Fusion Plasma Diagnostics

**abstract/A1：** Visible-light videos and multivariate diagnostic signals provide complementary observations of tokamak discharges. However, existing fusion pretraining methods, primarily learn representations by reconstructing or predicting observations, leaving the correspondence between these modalities underexplored. Representation learning is further challenged by the fact that plasma-related regions occupy only part of each video frame and diagnostic variables have substantially different sampling rates. We propose FusionCLIP, a multimodal contrastive pretraining framework. Spatiotemporal Stability Selection retains informative visual tokens, while a Temporal Query Encoder integrates asynchronous diagnostic signals using their observation timestamps. Contrastive learning then aligns the two modality representations. We construct EAST Multimodal Diagnostics (EAST-MMD), comprising 1307 EAST discharges, and evaluate the model on three independently fine-tuned tasks: disruption prediction, edge-localized mode (ELM) classification, and $H\_{98}$ regression. FusionCLIP achieves an aggregate score of 89.07\\%, exceeding the best general-purpose pretraining configuration and an in-domain contrastive pretraining configuration without Spatiotemporal Stability Selection by 6.27 and 2.12 percentage points, respectively. Ablations and attention visualizations further support the contributions of its key components.

</details>

<a id="paper-84"></a>

### 84. [TimeOmni-Live: Continuous &amp; Live Time Series Understanding with Long-Term Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09502v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09502

**作者：** Tong Guan、Alexander Chemeris、Yiqing Guo、Warren Jin、Zhao Li、Shirui Pan、Ming Jin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09502v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09502v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向开放数据流提出「连续实时时序理解」范式，构建含 18 项任务、2.2B 时间步的 LiveTS-Suite 数据与评测协议，并设计带混合时序编码、流式持久混合记忆与弹性推理同步的 TimeOmni-Live 模型。

**相关性简评：** 虽属时序而非视觉模态，但其「精确历史证据 + 长期任务状态」的混合记忆与延迟—精度联合评测，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据保留、状态更新与 THINK/LOOK/STOP 成本权衡提供方法线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TimeOmni-Live: Continuous &amp; Live Time Series Understanding with Long-Term Memory

**abstract/A1：** Existing time series language models predominantly follow an offline, one-shot paradigm: they process fixed-length inputs independently and do not retain past states. This static approach fundamentally misaligns with the continuous nature of real-world streaming applications and fails to maintain the historical context necessary for cross-window reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce Continuous &amp; Live Time Series Understanding (CL-TSU), a novel paradigm that shifts the focus toward maintaining a persistent, stateful understanding over open-ended data streams. To systematically evaluate this paradigm, we present LiveTS-Suite, the first comprehensive data suite tailored for CL-TSU, supporting data generation, model training, and systematic evaluation across 18 tasks spanning six core capabilities. It comprises over 5K streams totaling 2.2B timesteps, annotated with 15K specific response events. We further design an evaluation protocol that jointly assesses response timeliness, content accuracy, and computational efficiency. Building on this foundation, we propose TimeOmni-Live, the first live time series understanding model. It integrates a hybrid time series encoding for natively temporal input, a stream-persistent hybrid memory mechanism to effectively preserve exact historical evidence and long-term task states, and an elastic stream-inference synchronization strategy to reduce processing lag. Evaluations show that TimeOmni-Live establishes a strong baseline for CL-TSU, outperforming frontier LLMs (90.0 vs. 79.3 for Opus 5) with only 4B parameters and achieving 206$\\times$ lower p95 latency than its backbone. Deployment on a Jetson edge device further shows its ability to support real-time continuous temporal understanding locally at the edge.

</details>

<a id="paper-85"></a>

### 85. [Embedding Predictive Adaptation and Refinement for Multi-Modal Industrial Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09521v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09521

**作者：** Jaeho Cho、Jinsik Bang、Taehwan Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09521v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09521v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向多模态工业异常检测，提出 EPAR：通过预测被遮挡区域的 embedding 并加入 patch 级 VIC 正则来适配编码器，再用关系差异学习型噪声抑制网络细化 patch 级异常图。

**相关性简评：** 「预测遮挡区域 embedding」与基于关系差异而非外观的异常信号，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节保留、latent 状态训练监督与证据筛选提供可供核查的线索，但该文面向缺陷定位，不涉及推理生成与停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Embedding Predictive Adaptation and Refinement for Multi-Modal Industrial Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) has evolved from 2D image-based approaches to multi-modal methods that leverage the complementary nature of RGB images and 3D point clouds. Among these, memory bank-based methods, which identify anomalies by the discrepancy between each query patch feature and its nearest normal prototype, have proven particularly effective. However, two limitations remain: (i) employing frozen pretrained encoders yields insufficient discrimination between normal and anomalous features due to the domain gap, while existing contrastive learning-based adaptation struggles to capture the spatial relationships required for precise localization; and (ii) pixel-level anomaly maps interpolated from patch-level distance scores remain coarse, lacking fine-grained detail. To address these limitations, we propose EPAR (Embedding Predictive Adaptation and Refinement). We adapt encoders by predicting the embeddings of masked regions to capture spatial relationships, together with patch-level Variance-Invariance-Covariance (VIC) regularization, resolving the poor discriminability between normal and anomalous features caused by the domain gap. We further propose a Noise Suppression Network that refines coarse maps by learning the relational discrepancy between each patch feature and its nearest normal prototype, rather than the anomaly's appearance. This relational signal remains valid for unseen real defects, despite being learned from synthetic anomalies. EPAR demonstrates strong practical applicability, excelling in few-shot settings and improving AUPRO@1% on MVTec 3D-AD and Eyecandies by +1.8% and +1.9%, respectively.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-86"></a>

### 86. [RadarDINO: Distilling a Visual Foundation Model into mmWave Radar for Human Mesh Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09524v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09524

**作者：** Furkan Mert Algan、Zhifan Ni、Eckehard Steinbach

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09524v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09524v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RadarDINO：借助标定的 RGB-雷达几何，把 DINOv3 视觉描述子迁移到雷达体素上，提供体素级与身体部位级的语义监督，并叠加顶点与关节损失直接约束几何，以提升毫米波雷达人体网格重建。

**相关性简评：** 把视觉基础模型的部位级语义蒸馏进另一模态的内部表征作为辅助监督，可能可供核查是否有助于缓解 LVR 中视觉细节在 latent 状态里坍缩的问题；但该工作面向雷达 HMR，不涉及推理期状态生成，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RadarDINO: Distilling a Visual Foundation Model into mmWave Radar for Human Mesh Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar offers a privacy-preserving and illumination-robust alternative for human mesh reconstruction (HMR). However, high-fidelity radar HMR remains challenging. Sparse point cloud-based methods suffer from information loss and missed detections, while raw radar tensor methods like RT-Mesh lack fine-grained semantics and explicit local body modeling. In this paper, we propose RadarDINO, a radar HMR framework that distills visual semantics from a DINOv3 teacher into 3D radar representations and introduces explicit geometric supervision. DINOv3 descriptors are transferred to radar voxels via calibrated RGB-radar geometry, providing voxel- and body-part-level supervision for part-aware radar representations. We further incorporate vertex- and joint-losses to directly constrain reconstructed geometry. On the M4Human dataset, our method outperforms the SOTA baseline across all three evaluation splits.

</details>

<a id="paper-87"></a>

### 87. [ExQuery: Explicit Structured Query Priors for Temporal 3D Object Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09527v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09527

**作者：** Honglin Sun、Yifei Zhang、Junyang Zhang、Kenji Hashimoto

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09527v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09527v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ExQuery，将每个解码 query 拆成共享语义切片与中心、尺寸、朝向、速度等物理状态切片，并在回归分支与由预测状态、分类软先验重建的时序 query 中保持该结构，用门控仿射调制与已存 latent 实例特征融合，在 nuScenes 上验证检测性能。

**相关性简评：** 其「结构化切片 latent + 门控调制融合重建先验」的做法，可能与 LVR 中 latent 状态如何分块承载任务必要信息、以及新证据与缓存 latent 的筛选融合问题相通，可供核查；但该文为纯 3D 检测、无语言推理，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ExQuery: Explicit Structured Query Priors for Temporal 3D Object Detection

**abstract/A1：** Sparse query-based 3D detectors commonly encode heterogeneous semantic, geometric, and motion information into unified latent queries, including when explicit 3D anchors are used. We introduce ExQuery, an explicit structured query framework for temporal 3D object detection. Each decoded query is organized into a shared semantic context slice and physical-state-specific slices for center, dimensions, orientation, and velocity. This structure is preserved from prior encoding to branch-specific regression and is reused to reconstruct temporal queries: predicted physical states are transformed by ego motion, classification scores form soft semantic priors, and the reconstructed query is fused with the stored latent instance feature through gated affine modulation. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that our method achieves competitive detection performance, and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our proposed modules.

**comment/C1：** BMVC 2026

</details>
