### 88. [Alignment by Confident Debate: Preference Learning from Multi-Agent Debate](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09568v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09568

**作者：** Nikhil Verma、Dingxian Wang、Mohammad Reza Rezaei、Andrew Rabinovich、Zhao Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09568v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09568v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 AbCD，将多智能体辩论轨迹按任务级语义等价聚类，结合置信度加权分歧构造无金标偏好数据，再用于推理任务的后训练。

**相关性简评：** 不含视觉或 latent 状态，但其「无金标偏好监督 + 按置信度聚合分歧答案」的做法，可能为 LVR 中多 latent 假设的筛选、轨迹监督与贡献分配提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Alignment by Confident Debate: Preference Learning from Multi-Agent Debate

**abstract/A1：** Post-training language models for reasoning typically rely on gold answers, task-specific verifiers, or learned reward models, limiting scalability when such signals are scarce. Multi-agent debate offers a self-supervised alternative, but converting debate into reliable training supervision remains challenging: majority voting weights agreeing responses equally, lacks a principled tie-breaking rule, and is ill-defined for open-ended tasks without a canonical answer; likelihood-based scoring can conflate fluency with correctness; and homogeneous committees may reinforce correlated errors. We introduce Alignment by Confident Debate (AbCD), which converts debate traces into preference data without gold answer labels. A sampling-diverse committee debates each problem, after which final answers are clustered by task-level semantic equivalence. We use verifiable tasks (mathematical reasoning, multiple-choice quantitative QA, and executable code-generation) as a controlled test bed: their answer keys or executable checkers let us evaluate the quality of debate-derived supervision against ground truth, even though the oracle itself never uses gold labels. Post-training on the resulting preferences outperforms inference-only ensembling and debate, as well as SFT on the same traces, across five model families spanning 1B to 8B parameters and four datasets, improving consensus accuracy, with a maximum gain of $21.5\_{pp}$. Ablations show that the approach transfers to a larger model and to code generation, while also revealing limitations of heterogeneous committees and agreement-based weighting. Overall, the results suggest that debate traces can yield useful preference supervision when answer equivalence is combined with confidence-weighted disagreement.

**comment/C1：** 53 pages, 9 Figures, 31 Tables

</details>

<a id="paper-89"></a>

### 89. [MSR-ABC: Complementary Feature Fusion for Clear Cell Renal Cell Carcinoma Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09583v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09583

**作者：** Yuan Liang、Sourav Bhattacharjee、Abraham Campbell

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09583v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09583v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 MSR-ABC，一个融合 MedVAE 重建特征、MedSigLIP 图文嵌入与影像组学描述符的全局—局部框架，用于 CT 上透明细胞肾细胞癌分类；通过跨表示注意力、影像组学辅助监督与门控残差整合局部与全局信息，并在 396 例内部与 111 例外部队列上报告 AUROC 与消融结果。

**相关性简评：** 其「用辅助监督迫使池化后的局部表示保留定量影像属性」以及跨表示注意力、门控融合的做法，可能为核查 LVR 中视觉细节与任务必要信息能否留存于内部表示、以及多源视觉证据如何整合提供线索，但该文属纯判别式分类，不含 latent 推理状态或生成过程。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MSR-ABC: Complementary Feature Fusion for Clear Cell Renal Cell Carcinoma Classification

**abstract/A1：** Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (ccRCC) classification from computed tomography (CT) may benefit from representations that capture different aspects of tumour phenotype. However, simply concatenating reconstruction-derived features, image--text embeddings, and radiomic descriptors may not fully exploit their complementary information. We propose MSR-ABC, a global--local framework integrating MedVAE, MedSigLIP, and radiomics. Spatially matched local image descriptors interact through cross-representation attention, while radiomic auxiliary supervision encourages the pooled local representation to retain quantitative tumour attributes. A gated residual mechanism integrates local information with global summaries. Area-aware pooling and visual and soft-text prompting complete the framework. We evaluate discrimination using a patient-level split of 396 KiTS23 cases and an external cohort of 111 TCGA/TCIA cases, reporting mean AUROC over three training seeds with 95% patient-bootstrap confidence intervals. MSR-ABC achieves mean AUROCs of 0.806 internally and 0.879 externally. Internally, it exceeds global MedVAE--radiomics and three-source concatenation by 3.35 and 3.61 AUROC percentage points, respectively. Removing any source lowers mean AUROC in both cohorts, with the largest decreases following radiomics removal. Individual removal of cross-representation attention, auxiliary supervision, or gated fusion also lowers internal mean AUROC. These findings are consistent with complementary contributions within the integrated architecture and support further investigation of structured representation fusion for non-invasive renal tumour characterisation.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to IEEE ISBI 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-90"></a>

### 90. [Transformer-FSQ: Discrete Latents for Extreme KV-Cache Compression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09598v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09598

**作者：** Andras Szecsenyi、Thomas L. Lee、Vaisakh Shaj、Elliot J. Crowley、Amos Storkey

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09598v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09598v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Transformer-FSQ，用有限标量量化瓶颈替换标准 K/V 投影，使 KV cache 以每 token 的离散整数码存储且无需逐 token 量化元数据，并在 0.6B–8B 模型上以长上下文检索（RULER）和解码吞吐评估极端压缩比下的表现。

**相关性简评：** 对缓存状态的极端离散压缩可能决定视觉细节等任务必要信息能否保留在内部状态中，其量化粒度与信息坍缩、下游可用性的关系可供核查；但该文为纯文本效率研究，未涉及多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Transformer-FSQ: Discrete Latents for Extreme KV-Cache Compression

**abstract/A1：** Private, personalised language models must run on local consumer devices, where large, frontier models cannot fit, and retaining long contexts is non-trivial. Sequential memory---the key-value (KV) cache---grows linearly with context length, quickly dwarfing the model itself: for Qwen3-1.7B-Base, 200,000 retained entries require 21 GiB, 6.6 times more than the model weights. Yet the KV-cache compression methods we measured fail beyond moderate compression ratios. We introduce Transformer-FSQ, addressing extreme KV-cache compression by replacing standard key and value projections with a finite scalar quantisation bottleneck. Our cache stores a packed vector of discrete integer codes per token, with no per-token quantisation metadata. Converting a pretrained transformer trains only these projections on 0.25 tokens per original parameter. We evaluate Transformer-FSQ against five baseline families on models from 0.6B to 8B parameters. On long-context retrieval (RULER), from 12$\\times$ to 97.5$\\times$ Transformer-FSQ is the most accurate method we evaluate, across scales. A fused Triton kernel decodes directly from the integer codes; with baselines given their authors' kernels where available, it decodes at $1.01\\times$ (95% interval $0.98$--$1.05$) the throughput of native SDPA attention on the uncompressed Qwen3-8B-Base at $32\\times$ and $1.54\\times$ at $97.5\\times$. Extreme KV-cache compression is therefore a practical way to extend how much context a fixed device can hold.

</details>

<a id="paper-91"></a>

### 91. [Dual-Region Slice Aggregation for CT-Based Renal Tumor Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09599v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09599

**作者：** Yuan Liang、Sourav Bhattacharjee、Abraham Campbell

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09599v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09599v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作面向 CT 肾肿瘤 ccRCC 分类，在共享的冻结 DINOv3 特征图上分别池化肿瘤与 0–10 mm 瘤周区域，经位置感知切片聚合器与仅训练期 PCA 得到表示，再用两个标量门控与影像组学特征融合，并在 KiTS23 与外部病例上评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「肿瘤+瘤周」双区域独立读出与共享切片聚合、冻结视觉特征的轻量适配，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据区域筛选与跨切片 token 汇聚的线索，但无推理状态生成或答案使用分析，与主旨联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dual-Region Slice Aggregation for CT-Based Renal Tumor Classification

**abstract/A1：** Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (ccRCC) is the most common kidney cancer in adults. We present a region-aware architecture for CT-based ccRCC classification. Tumor and 0--10 mm peritumoral context are pooled separately from shared frozen DINOv3 feature maps. A shared position-aware slice aggregator precedes fixed training-only principal component analysis (PCA). The resulting representations are fused with radiomics using two scalar feature gates. Evaluation on KiTS23 and 111 external cases yields internal test and external AUCs of 0.8078\\pm0.0154 and 0.9094\\pm0.0015. Compared with matched single-tumor readout, mean AUC increases by 1.50 and 2.34 percentage points, respectively. Early-PCA and reconstruction controls yield lower internal mean AUCs, while external AUC is similar for early and late PCA. These findings highlight the joint role of regional readout and slice aggregation in adapting frozen visual features to renal CT.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to IEEE ISBI 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-92"></a>

### 92. [Not Your Diary, Just Your Topic: Grading the Wrong-User Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09609v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09609

**作者：** Karina Romanova、Igor Kiselev、Vladimir Platonov、Viliana Devbunova、Gregory Leleytner、Adeliya Leleytner、Lina Veltman、Aleksandr Nikolich

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09609v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09609v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文以冻结语言模型在 LaMP-2 新闻分类上检验「换用他人历史」的个性化评测，用相似用户历史及标签与文本相似度匹配的合成历史作对照，发现类别标签即可复现大部分准确率差距。

**相关性简评：** 其控制内容混淆、把增益归因到用户特定信息的对照设计，可能为核查 LVR 中 latent 视觉内容是否真被答案使用、而非来自内容捷径或视觉旁路提供借鉴，值得核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Not Your Diary, Just Your Topic: Grading the Wrong-User Control

**abstract/A1：** A common test of personalization replaces a user's history with someone else's and measures the drop in accuracy. But this swap also changes the topics in the history, so the drop may reflect topic mismatch. We examine this problem with a frozen language model on LaMP-2 news categorization, using histories from similar users and synthetic histories assembled from other users' articles. The user's own history improves accuracy by 2.3 percentage points over a model trained without history. Removing history only at evaluation makes this gain appear larger, at 4.8 points. Category labels alone reproduce 86% of the accuracy gap between the user's own history and a random user's history. With synthetic histories matched on labels and text similarity, the gap falls to 0.9 points. Across two user splits and three training seeds, the advantage over a similar user's history is 3.2 points for users seen during training and 0.65 points for unseen users, where the evidence for an advantage remains inconclusive. A small movie-tagging experiment with one training seed also finds no advantage over other users' histories. These findings show why personalization should be evaluated on unseen users, with histories of similar content and a model trained without history as comparisons. We also find that adding gated cross-attention improves on a prefix-only model under our training setup; whether this holds more broadly remains open.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages. NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Personalized, Aligned, Long-Term Memory for AI Systems (PALM)

</details>

<a id="paper-93"></a>

### 93. [CRTrack-Cap: A New Benchmark for Cross-View Dense Video Object Captioning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09617v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09617

**作者：** Xiangqun Zhang、Likai Wang、Zekun Qian、Rui Zhou、Ruize Han、Wei Feng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09617v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09617v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出跨视角密集视频目标描述任务并构建 CRTrack-Cap 基准，把多摄像头的局部轨迹关联为场景级全局轨迹、为每条轨迹生成统一描述，并用 CV-CHOTA 联合评测跟踪与描述质量。

**相关性简评：** 其 Conflict-Evidence Gating 按跨视角描述证据的可靠性与分歧加权融合，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与冲突消解提供可核查线索；但论文本身聚焦视频跟踪与描述，不涉及 latent 推理状态或其生成与使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CRTrack-Cap: A New Benchmark for Cross-View Dense Video Object Captioning

**abstract/A1：** Dense video object captioning (DVOC) localizes, tracks, and describes object trajectories within a video. Existing methods process each camera view independently, producing separate camera-local tracklets and captions for the same person across cameras. These outputs lack a unified description and leave complementary appearance evidence across views unused. We therefore formulate cross-view DVOC (CVDVOC), which associates these tracklets into scene-level global trajectories and generates one caption for each trajectory from synchronized views. To support this task, we construct CRTrack-Cap from CRTrack by auditing text-identity links and converting usable referring expressions into canonical captions. It retains 425 global trajectories and 838 local tracklets across 13 scenes, with canonical captions for 335 identities. We adapt CHOTA to the cross-view setting as CV-CHOTA to jointly evaluate tracking and caption quality. Our reference framework combines View-Exclusive Cross-Camera Association (VECA) for grouping tracklets with Conflict-Evidence Gating (CEG) for integrating caption evidence using its reliability and disagreement across views. Against three single-view methods adapted with MvMHAT for cross-view association, our framework achieves CV-CHOTA scores of 83.43 and 33.48 on the scene-disjoint in-domain and cross-domain tests, respectively. Together, CRTrack-Cap and our framework provide a benchmark and a strong baseline for describing the same person consistently across camera views.

</details>

<a id="paper-94"></a>

### 94. [Encoding Memory in World Models with Test-Time Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09625v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09625

**作者：** Maxime Burchi、Fayaz Ali Dharejo、Radu Timofte

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09625v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09625v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TT-MBRL：在测试时用梯度下降把每个 episode 的观测记忆写入世界模型参数，从而为每个 episode 得到单独的世界模型；在自建 Memory Maze 2D 与 3D Memory Maze 上验证效果，并在 DeepMind Control、Atari 100k 等非记忆基准上检查是否保持性能。

**相关性简评：** 以测试时参数更新而非上下文作为长程记忆载体，为 LVR 中「视觉证据应存入 latent 上下文还是模型参数」的取舍及长序列记忆保持提供可核查的方法线索，但该文面向 RL 世界模型而非 VLM 推理，迁移尚属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Encoding Memory in World Models with Test-Time Model-Based Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) approaches have demonstrated strong performance across diverse domains in recent years, learning accurate world models from agent trajectories. However, despite this progress, current approaches still struggle to surpass human performance on tasks that require long-term memory and planning. In this work, we address the problem of long-term memory in MBRL by moving beyond the commonly studied paradigm of architecture design. We introduce Test-Time Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (TT-MBRL), using gradient descent at test time to encode the memory of episode observations into world model parameters. This enables the agent to learn a distinct world model for each episode, improving memory capabilities compared to traditional approaches that solely rely on contextual memory. We first establish a proof of concept on a simplified two-dimensional variant of the Memory Maze environment, which we refer to as Memory Maze 2D, showing consistent improvements across different world model architectures. We then demonstrate the effectiveness of TT-MBRL in the 3D domain, achieving new state-of-the-art results on the Memory Maze benchmark. Finally, we show that TT-MBRL maintains strong performance on non-memory benchmarks such as DeepMind Control and Atari 100k.

</details>

<a id="paper-95"></a>

### 95. [Verifier Noise Is a Temperature: Testing Tilt Predictions in Finite-Budget LLM-Guided Search](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09699v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09699

**作者：** Xiaomeng Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09699v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09699v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把LLM引导搜索中的验证器噪声建模为温度，在可逆变异与logistic分数差下推导出由exp(beta f)倾斜的平稳分布，并用预注册实验检验程序搜索和prompt优化中的倾斜预测。

**相关性简评：** 其关于minibatch接受门、验证器噪声、分数缓存偏差与平稳分布的分析，可能为LVR中候选latent假设的搜索、验证和停止决策提供可核查的方法线索，但未涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verifier Noise Is a Temperature: Testing Tilt Predictions in Finite-Budget LLM-Guided Search

**abstract/A1：** LLM-guided search loops propose a modified candidate, score it, and keep it if it scores better. When both candidates are re-scored at every comparison, as in the minibatch acceptance gates of prompt optimizers, keep-if-better is a reversible Markov chain if the score difference is logistic and the mutation operator is reversible, and its stationary law is the mutation equilibrium tilted by exp(beta f). For any continuous symmetric noise difference the same tilt is the stationary law of a small-step diffusion limit, with beta = 4F'(0); for a minibatch gate beta grows with the square root of the minibatch size. Caching the incumbent's score instead inflates the recorded score. We test the tilt as a prediction for LLM-guided program search, committing each prediction from calibration data before the search runs. The level after 150 steps rises with the minibatch size in six of seven settings, but its predicted value fails: on bin packing, search attains 62-88% of the predicted gain when the proposer edits the parent and 109-119% when it writes new heuristics. The attained fraction falls with the parent-child quality correlation of unselected mutation; a registered test on two new settings confirmed the direction, but not the quantitative agreement after a correction. Further registered tests found that runs started from a good program kept losing quality over 300 steps, more so from starts that search had selected, by a margin that passed its registered rule narrowly. Runs from different starts had not reached a common level, so whether the tilt describes the stationary law of LLM editing remains open. A registered test in prompt optimisation on frozen task-model scores failed: search exceeded the tilt, and the bin-packing relation did not transfer.

**comment/C1：** 34 pages, 5 figures. Pre-registered predictions, code and data checksums are described in Appendix C

</details>

<a id="paper-96"></a>

### 96. [Attend at the Right Resolution: Head-Adaptive Sparse Attention via Query-Key Interaction Structure](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09700v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09700

**作者：** Wajih Hassan Raza、Ahsan Bilal、Yidan Shen、Renjie Hu、Xin Fu、Yu Wen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09700v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09700v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练的头部自适应稀疏注意力 HASA：用每个预训练头 query-key 算子的奇异谱确定特征维上的有效秩，得到各头专属的紧凑响应空间，并依据 token 响应在该空间中的方向一致性渐进合并相邻区域，最后对选中区域用原始全维 KV 计算精确注意力。

**相关性简评：** 其「按头结构决定选择分辨率、再用层级合并筛选 KV 区域」的思路，可能为长上下文 VLM 推理中的视觉证据筛选与缓存压缩提供可核查线索，但需确认该头部特异性是否同样存在于跨模态注意力与 latent 状态读取中。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Attend at the Right Resolution: Head-Adaptive Sparse Attention via Query-Key Interaction Structure

**abstract/A1：** Attention is a major scalability bottleneck in large language models (LLMs), with its computational cost and key-value (KV) memory traffic becoming increasingly prohibitive as context length grows. Sparse attention addresses this bottleneck by restricting each query to a subset of the context, but its effectiveness hinges on identifying relevant regions both cheaply and reliably. Existing training-free methods primarily rely on runtime relevance estimates, with selection dimensionality and granularity typically governed by fixed heuristics rather than the intrinsic interaction structure of pretrained attention heads. We take a different perspective: the query-key operator of each pretrained head defines a characteristic response structure that determines the resolution needed for sparse selection, both across feature directions and across context tokens. Building on this insight, we propose Head-Adaptive Sparse Attention (HASA), a training-free sparse attention framework that jointly adapts selection resolution along these two dimensions. Along the feature dimension, the singular spectrum of each head's query-key operator determines an effective rank, yielding a compact, head-specific response space. Along the sequence dimension, neighboring regions are progressively merged according to the directional coherence of their token responses in this same space, while heterogeneous regions remain separate. Exact attention is then computed using the original full-dimensional KV states of the selected regions. HASA captures 88.1% of dense-attention mass and, compared with UNIQUE, a strong recent sparse-attention baseline, improves accuracy by up to 3.47 and 2.57 points on RULER and LongBench-Pro, respectively, and accelerates self-attention by up to 1.32x, while achieving up to 14.07x speedup over dense attention.

</details>

<a id="paper-97"></a>

### 97. [RidgeFill: Training-Free Late-Layer Cache Fill for Faster Prefill](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09709v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09709

**作者：** Dylan Johnston、Robert P. Smith

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09709v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09709v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** RidgeFill 提出免训练的 prefill 加速：只在浅层精确计算到某一分割层，用闭式岭回归拟合的逐层映射预测后续每层输入并由各层写入自己的 KV cache，配合填充区域检索与 splice 检验定位误差来源。

**相关性简评：** 其「由浅层状态预测后续层状态」的闭式映射与填充区内容是否被真正使用的诊断思路，或可供核查 LVR 中状态预测、视觉证据筛选与 prefill 成本分配问题，但原文为纯文本效率工作，与多模态推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RidgeFill: Training-Free Late-Layer Cache Fill for Faster Prefill

**abstract/A1：** RidgeFill shortens prefill by not computing most of a model's upper layers for the prompt. The lower layers run exactly up to a split; from the split layer's state a per-layer map predicts every later layer's input, and each late layer writes its cache from that prediction. The design follows YOCO and DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, where the projection is trained into the model. RidgeFill retrofits it onto models that were never trained for it: the map is a ridge regression fitted once in closed form from one pass over calibration text, the layer's own projections run unchanged, and a chosen set of rows, the last few thousand tokens, and every decoding step run the full model. Nothing is trained and no token is dropped. Most of the work was in measurement, finding a repeatable methodology. Loss averaged over all tokens hides RidgeFill's error. Needle retrieval in the filled region, loss on tokens that depend on distant context, and splice tests that swap filled keys or values for exact ones show which component carries it: the keys on Llama-3.1-8B, half of it in their scale, and the values and the recurrent state on Qwen3.5-9B. On Qwen3.8-27B in a llama.cpp fork on an A100, time to first token is 1.25 to 1.31x faster at 16K to 120K tokens with the maps in VRAM; RULER changes by -1.2 and -1.4 points and LongBench v2 by -1.4; the 95 % intervals include zero but do not rule out losses of a few points. On Qwen3.8-Flash-Next in vLLM it is 1.50 to 1.60x faster than exact at 9K to 32K tokens, with RULER's needle tasks at 99.8 against 100.0 and perplexity on novel tokens 2.65 % higher \[-0.02 %, +4.93 %\]. On three 8 to 9B reference models, at the same split and tail as the identity fill, the fitted map retrieves 49.1 and 49.6 more filled-region RULER points on the two dense models (at that split Llama's loss is worse), and its long-document loss is +/-0.03 nats of the released SwiftKV checkpoint's.

**comment/C1：** 50 pages, 15 figures, 38 tables. Code and data: https://github.com/Approximate-Science/ridgefill-data

</details>

### 98. [ICare-DT: a digital twin for continuous forecasting of intensive care patients](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09712v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09712

**作者：** Yanke Li、Yahang Qi、Yumi Kim、Diego Paez-Granados

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09712v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09712v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ICare-DT：用 Transformer 依据常规 ICU 记录每小时更新隐患者状态、无需重训即可预测生命体征越界，并构建注入已知大小治疗效应的基准来检验反应预测。

**相关性简评：** 时序 latent 状态的逐时更新，以及以扣留或替换新时段数据来归因增益来源的对照设计，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态如何吸收新证据及其贡献分配提供可供核查的评测线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ICare-DT: a digital twin for continuous forecasting of intensive care patients

**abstract/A1：** Digital twins of intensive care patients should update forecasts from new data and predict treatment responses, but few studies test whether routine records support either. We propose ICare-DT, a Transformer-based twin that updates a latent patient state hourly from new measurements without retraining. We trained it on 140,473 eICU-CRD stays to forecast vital signs crossing their training-set means. On test data, later forecasts beat the twin's own hour-6 forecasts of the same windows in all 12 settings (+0.073 to +0.153 AUROC). On validation data, controls that withheld or swapped the new hours traced the gain to the patient's own measurements. Without refitting, the model kept its gain on a second database (8 of 9 settings, +0.025 to +0.114 AUROC) in a prespecified evaluation of the previously analyzed MIMIC-IV test split. Refitted within MIMIC-IV, a twin state fine-tuned on clinical endpoints gave gradient boosting a significant gain for acute kidney injury (+0.0035 AUROC; hypotension +0.0010, not significant). To test response prediction, we built a benchmark that replaces the treatments in real records with simulated doses and injects responses of known size, visible only at measurement times. Ridge models supervised with injected effects recovered them from real records only through frequently measured variables (heart rate) read at their measurement times. We asked standard learners to predict these effects for a dosing regime no patient had received. No standard learner outperformed predicting zero effect at an out-of-support query. When told how the simulated responses were generated, a separate learner recovered the effects from the same records without seeing their true values (R2 0.30 to 0.40 at one effect size). Routine records support forecasts that improve with each patient's new data, and our benchmark is a testbed for models that learn how responses are generated.

</details>

<a id="paper-99"></a>

### 99. [Fixed-point acceleration on Neural Wasserstein manifolds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09723v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09723

**作者：** Vitalii Aksenov、Sebastian Gutierrez Hernandez

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09723v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09723v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出在神经 Wasserstein 流形上用 Riemannian Anderson mixing 加速自然梯度下降，把优化视为流形上的不动点迭代，并利用历史残差近似二阶信息；在采样、PDE 反问题、聚合动力学、贝叶斯回归与生成建模上检验其减少迭代次数的效果。

**相关性简评：** 其用历史残差加速不动点迭代、并在参数流形上简化收缩与向量传输的思路，可能可供核查于循环式 latent 推理中迭代状态更新的收敛控制与计算预算分配；但原文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，联系仅为方法层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fixed-point acceleration on Neural Wasserstein manifolds

**abstract/A1：** We propose a fixed-point acceleration method for optimization over probability measures, applicable to a variety of machine learning problems. We represent probability measures as pushforwards of a reference distribution through a neural network map and equip the parameter space with the induced pullback Wasserstein metric. This geometric structure allows natural gradient descent to be interpreted as a fixed-point iteration on a Riemannian manifold. We accelerate this iteration using Riemannian Anderson mixing method, which exploits historical residuals to capture approximate second-order information without forming Hessians. In the present parametric setting, the manifold is represented by Euclidean coordinates endowed with a point-dependent metric, simplifying retraction and vector transport. We evaluate the method on multimodal sampling, PDE-based inverse problems, aggregation dynamics, Bayesian logistic regression, and generative modeling. Across the tested tasks, Anderson mixing reduces the number of natural-gradient iterations and achieves competitive performance relative to SGD and Adam in terms of both loss-gradient evaluations and wall-clock time. These results suggest that fixed-point acceleration is an efficient tool for improving the computational efficiency of training on parametric Wasserstein methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-100"></a>

### 100. [TriNetra: Thermal-Reliability-Guided RGB-Thermal Fusion and Tracking for Surveillance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09725v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09725

**作者：** Sadique Khatib、Aditya Ahirrao、Vedant Sonawane、Disha Kapse、Y. D. Bhise

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09725v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09725v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TriNetra 监控框架：用五个免标注图像线索计算帧级「热红外可靠性系数」TRC，据此对双分支 YOLOv8m 的热特征做门控并传给 ByteTrack 与规则行为分析，在 M3FD 上验证了损坏热流时的鲁棒性，并报告跟踪端收益有限。

**相关性简评：** 其免监督可靠性打分与特征门控可作为「视觉证据何时不可信、何时该降权某模态」的具体方法线索，供核查是否可迁移到 LVR 的证据筛选与 LOOK 决策；但论文本身不涉及 latent 状态或推理，联系仍较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TriNetra: Thermal-Reliability-Guided RGB-Thermal Fusion and Tracking for Surveillance

**abstract/A1：** Visible cameras fail at night and thermal sensors fail under saturation, blur or loss of contrast, yet most RGB-thermal (RGB-T) detectors mix the two streams with fixed weights and cannot tell when one modality has become unreliable. TriNetra is a reliability-aware surveillance framework built around this problem. The proposed solution computes a frame-level Thermal Reliability Coefficient (TRC) from five label-free image cues, uses it to gate the thermal features of a dual-branch YOLOv8m detector whose visible branch, neck and head are frozen and COCO-pretrained, and passes the same score to a ByteTrack-based tracker that feeds a rule-based behaviour analyser. On the M3FD benchmark (2,940/630/630 split) the detector reaches 87.6% precision, 79.4% recall, 70.0% mAP@50 and 47.8% mAP@50:95 on the test split, with 17.0 M of its 42.9 M parameters trained. TRC stays near 0.78 on clean frames (standard deviation 0.012), decreases with the severity of thermal saturation and blur, reaching 0.53 and 0.69 at full severity, and is insensitive to RGB-thermal misalignment. With identical weights, the adaptive gate matches a fixed gate on clean data (70.0% versus 69.8% mAP@50) but keeps 43.6% mAP@50 when the thermal stream is corrupted, against 38.4%, 29.2% and 25.0% for a fixed gate, full thermal admission and a closed gate; the 5.1-point gain over the fixed gate has a 95% bootstrap confidence interval of +3.6 to +6.5 points. In tracking, the TRC-gated association performs on par with ByteTrack on a synthetic benchmark with exact ground truth and on 14 M3FD clips, and is slightly less stable under thermal saturation, so its measured benefit is confined to the detector. The limitations of the current evaluation are reported together with the results.

</details>

<a id="paper-101"></a>

### 101. [Assessing the Impact of Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Algorithmic Problem-Solving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09791v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09791

**作者：** Gabriel Souza Baggio、Gabriel Machado Lunardi、Guilherme Medeiros Machado、Subhankar Maity、Faiza Belbachir、José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09791v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09791v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建面向竞赛编程的检索增强系统：用 embedding 检索相似题目并以动态阈值抑制检索噪声，在 CodeContests 上对比原始描述、随机样例与检索样例三种条件，并以 Min-K 成员推断区分真实泛化与训练数据记忆。

**相关性简评：** 相似度检索加动态阈值过滤噪声的做法，可供核查 LVR 中「何时引入外部证据、如何筛选证据」的阈值式控制；成员推断区分记忆与泛化的思路，也可能借用于检验答案是否真正依赖检索或 latent 内容而非先验记忆。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Assessing the Impact of Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Algorithmic Problem-Solving

**abstract/A1：** Competitive programming problems pose distinctive challenges for Large Language Models, as they demand not only proficient code generation but also advanced algorithmic reasoning. This study presents a Retrieval-Augmented Generation approach specifically designed to enhance the capacity of Large Language Models to address such problems. Our approach integrates an embedding-based retrieval mechanism and a similarity search algorithm to identify analogous problems within a knowledge database, employing a dynamically adjusted threshold to mitigate retrieval noise. We conducted an evaluation using the CodeContests dataset, focusing on the test split problems that exhibit similarity to instances from the training split. The experimental framework considered three conditions: (i) problems provided solely with their original descriptions, (ii) problems supplemented with randomly selected examples from the training split, and (iii) problems augmented with similar examples retrieved through the proposed method. In addition, we incorporated a Min-K membership inference analysis to examine whether the generated code solutions were influenced by potential memorization of training data within the evaluated Large Language Models. This complementary perspective enables a deeper understanding of model behavior by distinguishing genuine generalization through retrieval from implicit recall of pretraining samples. Performance was assessed using both pass rates and the CodeForces Elo rating of the problems. The results provide empirical evidence that similarity-based retrieval substantially enhances Large Language Models performance in competitive programming scenarios, offering an scalable framework for improving code generation in algorithmic problem-solving contexts.

</details>

<a id="paper-102"></a>

### 102. [AR-Edit: Training-Free Autoregressive Video Editing without Inversion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09795v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09795

**作者：** Hovhannes Margaryan、Quentin Bammey、Christian Sandor、Vicky Kalogeiton

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09795v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09795v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文分析 DMD 蒸馏的自回归视频扩散模型的自注意力动态，发现去噪过程中 query/key 稳定、value 发散并承载内容，据此提出无需训练、无需 inversion 的 AR-Edit：缓存一次源视频的 value 特征并在去噪中按需注入，实现实时流式视频编辑。

**相关性简评：** 其「Q/K 稳定而 V 承载内容」的注意力分量分工及跨去噪步缓存复用的观察，可能为 LVR 中视觉内容驻留位置、latent 缓存与相似度分析的解读提供核查线索，但论文本身属视频编辑，未涉及推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AR-Edit: Training-Free Autoregressive Video Editing without Inversion

**abstract/A1：** Editing a video stream in real time, without access to future frames, is essential for interactive applications. Existing training-free video editing methods, however, assume full offline access to the entire sequence, while streaming approaches either rely on additional training or are limited to specific editing techniques. We show that this limitation can be removed by analyzing the self-attention dynamics of DMD-distilled autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models. We uncover a key asymmetry: queries and keys remain stable and aligned with their source counterparts across denoising timesteps, while values diverge and encode the evolving content. Inherited from the non-distilled bidirectional teacher, this asymmetry is strongest at high noise, where few-step streaming models operate. The resulting mechanism is simple: a single forward pass suffices to capture source identity in the value features, which can be reused throughout generation. Starting from pure Gaussian noise and injecting only these source values reconstructs the input with fidelity matching or surpassing inversion-based methods, effectively eliminating inversion. Based on this insight, we introduce AR-Edit, a training-free, inversion-free method that caches source values once and selectively injects them during denoising. Across multiple AR backbones, AR-Edit enables real-time streaming editing with superior source preservation and tonal fidelity at minimal overhead.

</details>

<a id="paper-103"></a>

### 103. [Test-Time Compute Scales Better with Parallel Agent Teams](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09798v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09798

**作者：** Junlin Wang、Haozhe Chen、Ang Li、Rahul Chalamala、Zhuang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09798v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09798v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 parallel agent teams：在给定验证器可对每次尝试打分的条件下，并行采样多个小型协作团队并保留最佳团队结果，在 12 个长程任务（GPU kernel、形式化证明、漏洞利用开发）上对比 Best-of-N 与单个大团队，并用统计模型解释均值与方差收益的权衡。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本长程 agent 任务，但其「多个小规模采样单元并行 + 验证器筛选 + 均值与方差权衡」的思路可供核查是否适用于 LVR 中多条 latent 推理轨迹的采样、验证与计算预算分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Test-Time Compute Scales Better with Parallel Agent Teams

**abstract/A1：** Test-time scaling improves the performance of a fixed model by spending more computation at inference. There are two common ways to spend it on agents when a verifier can score every attempt. Best-of-N sampling runs many independent agents and keeps the best result but saturates quickly. One large team of collaborating agents can score higher but stops improving as it grows. Can we get the best of both? Our method, parallel agent teams, does so by sampling many small teams in parallel and keeping the best team's result. We test it on 12 long-horizon tasks from GPU kernels and formal proofs to exploit development. With a 27B model and a budget of 100 agents, parallel teams of ten beat parallel agents on 10 of 12 tasks and beat one team of 100 on all four tasks where we ran such large teams. At equal dollar cost the same parallel teams beat a frontier model on half the tasks. A simple statistical model shows that individual agents lose because their many samples have a low mean and that one large team loses because a single sample gets no gain from variance. Sampling many small teams in parallel balances a high mean with the gain from variance. Parallel agent teams thus offer a more effective way to scale test-time compute.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 18 figures, 4 tables. Code: https://github.com/modal-projects/parallel-agent-teams-scaling

</details>

<a id="paper-104"></a>

### 104. [Generative Latent Priors for Interpreting and Steering Protein Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09818v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09818

**作者：** Daniel Hinjos、Lucía Urcelay

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09818v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09818v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文把扩散式「生成潜先验」（GLP）迁移到 ESM-2 激活上，既用作探测离散概念与连续属性的解释透镜，也用作 on-manifold 校正与 DiffMean steering 的编辑先验，并发现多轮解码后序列结构合理性会劣化。

**相关性简评：** 对冻结模型激活分布建模并做流形内校正与状态编辑，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的干预、真实性与「编辑后能否解码回有效证据」提供可核查的方法线索，但对象是蛋白质序列，与视觉推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generative Latent Priors for Interpreting and Steering Protein Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Dominant methods for interpreting large models impose strong structural assumptions. Generative Latent Priors (GLP) are a complementary approach: a diffusion model that learns the distribution of a frozen model's activations directly, imposing no assumptions. We port GLP to ESM-2 activations (UniRef50) and evaluate it at two host scales (ESM-2-8M and -650M), both as a lens for protein representations and as a prior for editing them. Generation quality scales smoothly with depth and compute. As a lens, single GLP "meta-neurons" are the strongest probes of discrete Swiss-Prot concepts at both hosts, beating InterPLM's released SAE features, and lead on continuous properties at 8M though not at 650M; falsification tests show this is mostly concentration, not new information. As an editing prior, the on-manifold correction makes DiffMean steering of three biochemical properties effective and restores on-manifoldness, but only in isolation: re-embedding the sequences actually produced after several rounds of decoding, the structural plausibility is compromised. This is mainly reproduced in additional SAE-based clamping structural steering experiments, where only Helix motif succeeds. The naturalness failure is traced to the rewriting coverage and the decoder rather than the prior, highlighting what the method must clear before it can serve for both discovery-by-editing and discovery-by-exploration. Trained GLPs are released on huggingface.co/collections/HPAI-BSC/glprot.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the Interpretability for Discovery (Interp4Discovery) Workshop at the 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-105"></a>

### 105. [BitDecoding-FP4: Native FP4 KV Cache Decoding via Algorithm-System Co-Design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09825v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09825

**作者：** Dayou Du、Woon Yee Ng、Guangming Sheng、Nick Lau、Jianyi Cheng、Luo Mai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.AR、cs.CL、cs.PF

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09825v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09825v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BitDecoding-FP4，用双残差查询量化、概率下溢感知缩放和离群感知 key 量化抑制精度损失，并配合转置 softmax 等系统优化，实现接近 BF16 精度的原生 FP4 KV cache 解码与 2.4–3.0 倍服务吞吐提升。

**相关性简评：** 其误差源分解（query 量化误差、长上下文概率下溢、key 离群）可供核查低比特 KV 缓存是否会削弱视觉证据与 latent 内容的保真度，但论文本身不涉及多模态推理或 latent 状态使用，借鉴性需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BitDecoding-FP4: Native FP4 KV Cache Decoding via Algorithm-System Co-Design

**abstract/A1：** The key-value (KV) cache dominates memory capacity and bandwidth in long-context LLM serving. While 4-bit microscaling formats (NVFP4, MXFP4) shrink the cache and run attention on FP4 Tensor Cores, a fundamental gap remains between algorithmic quantization and system-aware acceleration: existing FP4 KV systems use vanilla quantization that causes severe accuracy drops, whereas accuracy-oriented 4-bit algorithms rely on vector codebooks whose lookup overhead degrades decoding throughput. We present BitDecoding-FP4, an algorithm--system co-design for accurate and efficient native FP4 KV decoding. We mitigate three error sources: Dual-Residual Query Quantization splits queries into two FP4 tensors, cutting query logit error from 60% to 1%; Underflow-Aware Probability Scaling prevents E4M3 scales from rounding long-context probabilities to zero; and Outlier-Aware Key Quantization combines static smoothing, Hadamard rotation, and scale search to suppress key outliers under token-wise scaling. At the system level, we remove the softmax bottleneck via transposed softmax with fused probability quantization and stacked query MMA on paged KV caches. Across dense and MoE models with GQA and MLA, BitDecoding-FP4 stays within 0.5 points of BF16 KV accuracy with NVFP4 (1 point with MXFP4), and tracks BF16 KV in RL training rollouts. In end-to-end serving on NVIDIA B300 with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B, it delivers 2.4-3.0$\\times$ higher peak serving throughput than BF16 KV. The implementation is available at https://github.com/OpenBitSys/BitDecoding-FP4.

</details>

<a id="paper-106"></a>

### 106. [Designing Effective Monitor-Based Interventions for Mitigating Reward Hacking During RL](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09828v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09828

**作者：** Aria Wong、Joshua Engels、Neel Nanda

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09828v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09828v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文开源三个 Qwen3-4B 会出现 reward hacking 的环境（覆写测试的代码环境、谄媚的医疗对话、幻觉的传记生成），研究 RL 训练中基于 monitor 的惩罚干预应如何设计，并分析监控器被规避、学习信号泄漏与训练题难度等因素的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其「模型可学会规避高准确率探针与 LLM 判官」「泄漏更多学习信号的监控更能抑制 hacking 但更易被规避」等发现，可能为 latent 推理轨迹的过程监督与验证器设计提供核查线索，尤其是监督强度与规避行为之间的权衡。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Designing Effective Monitor-Based Interventions for Mitigating Reward Hacking During RL

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) rewards are notoriously difficult to design and control, often leading to the model learning unintended behaviors such as reward hacking. One potential solution is to monitor for reward hacking and penalize it when detected; however, training against a monitor could lead to evasive behavior, and our general understanding of how to apply monitors effectively during training is limited. To study how best to use monitors to mitigate reward hacking, we introduce and open source three realistic environments where Qwen3-4B reward hacks: a coding environment hackable via test overwriting, a medical chat environment hackable via sycophancy, and a biography generation environment hackable via hallucination. We first focus on the coding environment, where we find that: (1) models can learn to evade highly accurate monitors by exploiting systemic flaws in probes and LLM judges; (2) monitors that leak more learning signal during RL suppress reward hacking but are more often evaded; and (3) including easier problems in training can decrease reward hacking. We apply our findings to build better reward hacking monitors for the medical chat and biography generation environments that improve upon naive baselines to reduce reward hacking rates across seeds to before-training levels in some interventions. Our results demonstrate that our takeaways translate to new settings and that better monitor intervention designs are possible.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-107"></a>

### 107. [Can Agentic Systems Close the Gap in Radiology with Production AI Tools?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09836v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09836

**作者：** Aubin Charley、Julien Khlaut、Charles Corbière、Pierre Manceron、Corentin Dancette、Léo Machado

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09836v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09836v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出一个放射科 agentic 框架，规范化 orchestrator 调用模块化工具生成结构化报告，并在大规模多模态报告语料上模拟报告生成，比较「已获批设备发现类目录」与「无限制目录」、以及由临床指征还是目标报告驱动规划时的发现召回差异。

**相关性简评：** 其方法线索在于把漏检逐项归因为「工具未提供」还是「已提供却未被调用」，并用模拟工具输出隔离工具误差，可供核查如何评估 LVR 中证据获取与支持组合的失败来源，以及 latent 内容是否真被用于回答；但对象是放射报告流程，与视觉推理内部状态无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Agentic Systems Close the Gap in Radiology with Production AI Tools?

**abstract/A1：** Radiology AI has developed along two paths: specialized models that perform well on narrow tasks but cover few clinical findings, and generalist models that address a broader range of tasks but remain prone to hallucination and inaccurate measurements. Combining the two through agentic systems offers a route towards comprehensive radiology reporting, but published systems are tied to one modality or body part. We describe a radiology agentic framework that standardizes how an orchestrator calls modular tools to produce a structured report, and use it to measure what such a system can do with the tools from FDA-cleared radiology AI devices. Report generation is simulated on a large multi-modality report corpus, with the orchestrator given either a catalog of finding classes consolidated from the cleared devices, each available on the modalities it is cleared for, or an unlimited catalog in which any class can be requested, and planning either from the clinical indication or from the target report. Tool outputs are simulated from the reference report, so the comparison isolates tool availability and orchestration from tool error. With the cleared catalog and the indication alone, the orchestrator recovers under a third of the clinically actionable findings; recovery improves substantially with the unlimited catalog, and further when the plan is written from the target report. Attributing every missed finding to a cause shows that most are classes no cleared device offers for the study's modality, and that a third are classes or tools that were offered and not called. Adding the most frequently missing tools closes most of the gap to the unlimited catalog on held-out reports, whereas a smaller orchestrator with the unlimited catalog gives up the whole gain. Tool availability and effective orchestration are thus complementary constraints on comprehensive AI-assisted radiology reporting.

</details>

<a id="paper-108"></a>

### 108. [Flow-Guided Target-Space Alignment via Path Consistency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09863v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09863

**作者：** Ruizhi Yuan、Zeqiu Yu、Wei Gao、Wei Chen、Xiwei Tang、Lu Tang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09863v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09863v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FlowAlign，将条件 flow matching 复用为训练期对齐信号而非推理期传输模型，用学到的流场引导源模态表示对齐到固定的目标模态空间，兼顾实例级配对与全局分布结构，并在合成数据、单细胞多组学和图文对齐上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其训练期流场与路径几何约束可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉信息在共享 latent 工作区中的保留、配准与坍缩问题，但论文不涉及推理期 latent 状态的生成或视觉证据筛选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Flow-Guided Target-Space Alignment via Path Consistency

**abstract/A1：** Many multimodal alignment problems require mapping a source modality into a fixed space defined by a target modality, where the target space is not merely an intermediate embedding but is used directly for downstream analysis. In this setting, successful alignment should preserve both the global structure of the target distribution and the instance-level correspondence observed in paired source--target training data. Existing objectives typically address only part of this goal: contrastive losses encourage relative similarity, distribution-matching losses improve marginal agreement, and pointwise regression fits each source sample to its paired target without explicitly modeling target-space path geometry. We propose FlowAlign, a flow-guided target-space alignment method that repurposes conditional flow matching as a training-time alignment signal rather than an inference-time transport model. FlowAlign uses the learned flow field to guide the alignment of source-side representations with the fixed target space, encouraging them to preserve paired correspondence while remaining compatible with the global target-space structure. We further provide theoretical analysis showing how FlowAlign helps improve paired correspondence by controlling endpoint accuracy and capturing local target-space geometry. Experiments on synthetic data, paired single-cell multiome benchmarks, and image--text alignment demonstrate that FlowAlign improves instance level correspondence while preserving target-space structure across different modality settings.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-109"></a>

### 109. [GRAC: Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Captioning for Surgical Scene Description](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09866v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09866

**作者：** Rochak Dhakal、Chen Zhao、Soup Cobb、Xin Qian、Xiaofeng Yang、Weihua Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09866v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09866v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GRAC，将手术场景表示为包含器械、动作与解剖结构关系的场景图，从训练集检索相似图-文本对，并用器械-组织交互的比较特征条件化检索到的 caption 表示来生成手术场景描述。

**相关性简评：** 其「检索相似证据—比较差异—条件化生成」流程可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与外部检索信息如何写入推理状态提供核查线索，但该工作面向文字描述生成，未涉及 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GRAC: Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Captioning for Surgical Scene Description

**abstract/A1：** Purpose: Surgical operative reports support procedural documentation, surgical training, performance assessment, and workflow review. However, extracting meaningful information often requires extensive manual review and clinical expertise. Automated surgical scene descriptions could reduce this burden by leveraging recurring surgical activities and prior knowledge. Methods: We propose GRAC (Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Captioning), a framework that represents surgical scenes as graphs capturing relationships among instruments, actions, and anatomical structures. Given an input scene graph, GRAC learns to retrieve relevant graph-caption pairs from training data. It compares instrument-tissue interactions between retrieved examples and the input scene, using comparison features to condition retrieved-caption representations during generation. Results: On the EndoVis-18 benchmark, GRAC achieved a CIDEr-D of 5.88, compared with 4.23 for SGT and 3.36 for the strongest conventional Transformer baseline. Exact instrument-action-target triplet-set accuracy reached 52.6%, compared with 32.7% for SGT and 26.2% for the strongest conventional baseline. These results demonstrate improvements in language quality and factual agreement. On TORS, GRAC achieved a CIDEr-D of 5.06, compared with 3.80 for the strongest baseline. Conclusion: GRAC demonstrates that combining structured surgical scene representations with retrieved examples improves surgical scene captioning and produces more contextually meaningful descriptions.

**comment/C1：** 20 Pages and 4 Figures

</details>

<a id="paper-110"></a>

### 110. [On the Reliability of Majority-Vote Pseudo-Labels in Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09872v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09872

**作者：** Haoyu Wang、Ru Wang、Yuanyuan Wang、Jiaxian Guo、Mingming Gong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09872v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09872v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文分析测试时强化学习中多数投票伪标签的可靠性，将误差分解为总体多数偏差与有限采样波动，并提出按投票可靠性加权的鲁棒 DPO 及缓存机制以缓解在线适配中的遗忘。

**相关性简评：** 其基于投票可靠性的条件重加权与缓存式在线训练，可能为 LVR 中自生成推理轨迹的噪声监督筛选和更新稳定性提供可核查的方法线索，但未涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Reliability of Majority-Vote Pseudo-Labels in Test-Time Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) adapts pretrained language models to unlabeled target-domain data using self-generated pseudo-labels, whose reliability remains poorly understood. We analyze majority-vote supervision for TTRL with online Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and decompose its error into population-majority bias and prompt-dependent finite-rollout variation. Because population-majority bias is generally unidentifiable without external supervision, we focus on mitigating the downstream effects of finite-rollout variation. We propose \\emph{Prompt-Conditional Distributionally Robust DPO (PC-Dr.DPO)}, which conditions within-prompt robust reweighting on observed vote reliability to make updates from sampling-sensitive prompts more conservative. We further introduce a cache-based mechanism to reduce self-supervised forgetting during online adaptation. Across six mathematical and knowledge-intensive reasoning benchmarks and five model backbones, PC-Dr.DPO matches or outperforms the strongest TTRL baseline in 24 of 30 settings.

</details>

<a id="paper-111"></a>

### 111. [FlowMoP: Stochastic Multi-Person Motion Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09882v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09882

**作者：** Aadya Agrawal、Ho Kei Cheng、Alexander Schwing

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09882v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09882v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出基于条件 flow matching 的多人 3D 运动预测框架，联合生成多样化未来轨迹与根相对姿态，并用 B-spline 重参数化压缩生成状态空间、以双流 transformer 建模邻居交互条件。

**相关性简评：** 其 flow matching 多假设生成、B-spline 状态空间压缩与交互条件化或可为 LVR 中并行 latent 推理状态的生成与压缩提供方法线索，但对象为人体运动而非视觉推理，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FlowMoP: Stochastic Multi-Person Motion Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Predicting the future motion of multiple people is inherently stochastic: the same observed history admits many plausible continuations depending on intent, coordination, and social context. Yet the dominant paradigm in multi-person 3D motion prediction remains deterministic, with existing methods producing a fixed number of outputs per agent. In contrast, we present a conditional flow matching framework that jointly generates diverse future motions for multiple agents, modeling both global trajectories and root-relative poses. A B-spline reparameterization compresses the generative state space while enforcing temporal smoothness, and a dual-stream transformer conditions each agent's predictions on observed neighbor motion for interaction-aware forecasting. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on CMU-Mocap (UMPM) and 3DPW, outperforming deterministic baselines on joint position error, pose error, and final displacement error despite producing a full predictive distribution. We additionally establish the first motion prediction baseline on WorldPose, a large-scale professional soccer dataset, where social conditioning demonstrably concentrates predictive uncertainty along interaction-constrained directions.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 22 pages, including references and appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-112"></a>

### 112. [CPU-ARC: Agentic Reasoning Caching for on-CPU Reasoning Reuse](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09891v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09891

**作者：** Peter Belcak、Greg Heinrich、Saurav Muralidharan、Shizhe Diao、Pavlo Molchanov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09891v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09891v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 agentic reasoning caching：把 LLM 的推理轨迹转成可在 CPU 上执行的智能体推理缓存（ARC），在新问题实例上复用已推得的解法，在数学与科学任务上取得与前沿模型相当的表现且调用成本低 16–291 倍。

**相关性简评：** 其「推理轨迹编译为可复用、可解释执行体」的思路，可供核查 LVR 中的轨迹监督、推理状态复用与推理成本/停止决策评价之间的对应关系；但该工作为纯文本且无视觉证据通路，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CPU-ARC: Agentic Reasoning Caching for on-CPU Reasoning Reuse

**abstract/A1：** Large language models reinvent the wheel all the time. They conflate task-solving with the execution of the solutions, which forces them to solve every problem from scratch. We show that once a task has been reasoned through, the application of its solution to a new problem instance can be done very efficiently and with little involvement from language models. We introduce agentic reasoning caching, a method for constructing robust CPU-executable agentic reasoning caches (ARCs) from LLM reasoning traces. ARCs show on-par performance with frontier LLMs across a range of difficult mathematical and scientific tasks, are fully explainable, continually trainable, robust to input perturbations, and 16-291x cheaper to invoke than the source model.

**comment/C1：** Tech report. 28 pages, 9 figures, 11 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-113"></a>

### 113. [FasterRW: An Efficient Prior-Assisted Random Walk Solver for Thermal Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08490v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08490

**作者：** Zixiao Wang、Tianshu Hou、Chenghan Wang、Zhen Zhuang、Leilei Jin、Tsung-Yi Ho、Farzan Farnia、Bei Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.NA、stat.CO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08490v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08490v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向 IC 热仿真，为 Feynman–Kac 随机游走提出“延续值”加速框架：证明截断尾期望等于当前衰减权重乘以停止点温度，用廉价先验替代未知延续值，得到误差可控的停止阈值规则，并用 kriging 融合多点查询。

**相关性简评：** 其停止时间残差恒等式与先验校正的延续值估计，可能为 LVR 中「何时停止 latent 推理」及截断/提前终止的偏差量化提供可核查的形式化线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FasterRW: An Efficient Prior-Assisted Random Walk Solver for Thermal Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Thermal simulation is becoming increasingly critical in modern IC design and manufacturing. Feynman--Kac random walk methods can estimate localized temperatures without solving the global field, but in practical settings without Dirichlet boundaries they require very long paths to control the truncation residual. This paper presents FasterRW, a continuation-value framework for accelerating Feynman--Kac thermal random walks. We prove a stopping-time residual identity showing that the expected tail after truncation equals the current attenuation weight multiplied by the temperature at the stopping location. This identity enables a prior-corrected single-point estimator: a cheap, noisy prior replaces the unknown continuation value, making the truncation bias scale with prior error rather than absolute temperature and yielding an error-controlled threshold-selection rule. For multi-point queries, FasterRW further converts cross-query pass-through tails into pseudo-samples and refines the resulting observations in prior-error coordinates through kriging. Compared with prior state-of-the-art methods, FasterRW achieves up to $28\\times$ acceleration while maintaining accuracy. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/ShiningSord/FastRW.

</details>

<a id="paper-114"></a>

### 114. [Generative Reconstruction of Full-Field Sensor States for Safety-Critical Digital Twins](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08499v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08499

**作者：** Umme Mahbuba Nabila、Linyu Lin、Majdi I. Radaideh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08499v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08499v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究针对高温试验设施中热电偶漂移或失效造成的部分可观测，用 GNN-GRU 空间编码配合扩散模型、VAE、GAN 及确定性 RNN、岭回归，在实验内与跨实验协议下重建稀疏传感器全场状态并比较性能。

**相关性简评：** 其「部分观测下条件生成补全缺失状态」及已知传感器数量减少时的稳健性评估，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、内部状态是否保留任务必要信息的诊断形成概念类比；但对象为非视觉传感器时序，迁移线索需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generative Reconstruction of Full-Field Sensor States for Safety-Critical Digital Twins

**abstract/A1：** Accurate reconstruction of incomplete sensor fields is critical for state awareness in safety-critical digital twins (DTs). Thermocouples in high-temperature and radiation environments may drift, fail, or become intermittently unavailable, causing partial observability. This study develops a sparse-sensor reconstruction framework for Oregon State University's High Temperature Test Facility (HTTF) and evaluates six methods under intra- and inter-experiment protocols: three conditional generative models (i.e., a diffusion model \[DM\], variational autoencoder \[VAE\], and generative adversarial network \[GAN\]), two deterministic recurrent neural-network models, and ridge regression. For intra-experiment reconstruction within the PG{-}29 depressurized conduction cooldown (DCC) test, the models combine graph-based spatial encoding with gated recurrent units. The graph neural network (GNN)--gated recurrent unit (GRU) VAE achieves the lowest error, with a mean absolute error (MAE) of $11.73,^\\circ$C, a root mean square error (RMSE) of $20.54,^\\circ$C, and an $R^2$ of 0.98. The deterministic GNN--GRU and plain GRU obtain RMSE values of $25.26,^\\circ$C and $35.22,^\\circ$C, respectively, demonstrating the benefit of spatial modeling. All three generative models reduce RMSE by more than half compared with ridge regression. The DM maintains an $R^2$ of 0.94 when known sensors decrease from 91 to 45. For inter-experiment reconstruction, models trained on PG{-}27 pressurized conduction cooldown (PCC) are evaluated on PG{-}29 using section-context conditioning. The section-context DM achieves the best transfer performance, with an RMSE of $42.84,^\\circ$C and an $R^2$ of 0.91. These results demonstrate the value of spatiotemporal generative modeling for missing-sensor reconstruction in safety-critical DT applications.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 8 figures, and 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-115"></a>

### 115. [Interpretable Multifidelity Learning with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Spatiotemporal Nuclear Reactor Flow Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08512v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08512

**作者：** Meredith Eaheart、Majdi I. Radaideh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08512v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08512v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在压水堆堆芯流动的时空预测任务上比较多保真度前馈网络与可解释的 Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络（MFKAN），并用 TabDDPM 合成数据扩充稀缺的高保真瞬态，发现预测精度受保真度层级间固有相关性限制。

**相关性简评：** 其「低成本数据与高成本数据融合、且效果受跨保真度相关性上限约束」的设定，或可类比 LVR 中粗粒度与高分辨率视觉证据的组合及信息瓶颈问题，供核查；但本文不涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpretable Multifidelity Learning with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Spatiotemporal Nuclear Reactor Flow Modeling

**abstract/A1：** High-fidelity computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations of nuclear reactor systems are computationally expensive, motivating machine learning methods that reduce cost while preserving accuracy. Multifidelity surrogate modeling addresses this by combining inexpensive low-fidelity data with limited high-fidelity data. This work investigates whether the high-fidelity data required to predict the spatiotemporal mass flow rate across all 193 fuel assemblies of a pressurized water reactor (PWR) core can be reduced, comparing black-box multifidelity feedforward neural networks (MFNNs) with interpretable multifidelity Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (MFKANs). Cross-fidelity correlation across the core is weak, making this a challenging multifidelity problem, and neither architecture predicts the transient particularly well in absolute terms. MFKAN nonetheless performs comparably to MFNN for one-time-step-forward prediction while also providing closed-form symbolic equations unavailable with MFNN. To address the scarcity of CFD transients, with only one available per fidelity level, TabDDPM-generated synthetic data was used to augment cross-fidelity forecasting. KAN and FNN achieve comparable self-evaluation accuracy (Pearson correlation of roughly 0.73--0.81), but both perform poorly at true cross-fidelity forecasting (correlations of 0.03--0.06). Assembly-level analysis shows that each assembly's forecast correlation tracks its real cross-fidelity correlation for both architectures ($r=0.840$--$0.896$ for KAN, $r=0.804$--$0.903$ for FNN), confirming that forecasting accuracy is fundamentally limited by how correlated the fidelity levels already are. Overall, KAN-based multifidelity modeling achieves accuracy consistently comparable to MFNN across all settings, while additionally providing symbolic equations that match or exceed the spline representation's accuracy.

**comment/C1：** 33 pages, 15 figures,9 tables, and a preprint under peer review

</details>

<a id="paper-116"></a>

### 116. [What Are LLM Agents Actually Learning on Low-Budget Molecular Optimization Benchmarks?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08522v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08522

**作者：** Jiayu Qin、Zhengquan Luo、Changyou Chen、Zhiqiang Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08522v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08522v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在低预算分子优化基准上拆解 LLM agent 的增益来源：通过隐藏任务名与参考化合物、identity-blind 条件、无 LLM 的检索基线及 2x2 消融，发现大部分收益来自可检索的任务信息而非在线优化，并据此提出检索安全的 prompt 与随机游走下界两项检验。

**相关性简评：** 虽非视觉任务，但其“信息检索贡献 vs 在线计算贡献”的拆解思路与遮蔽、下界检验设计，可能为核查 LVR 中 latent 推理是否真正贡献、而非依赖捷径或任务先验提供评价框架线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Are LLM Agents Actually Learning on Low-Budget Molecular Optimization Benchmarks?

**abstract/A1：** Recent work reports large gains for LLM agents on low-budget molecular optimization. We measure the contributions of online optimization, retrievable task information, and permissive oracle landscapes. On PMO at 50 oracle calls, a GPT-4o mini agent's sum AUC falls from 14.44 to 4.85 after task names and curated reference compounds are hidden. An identity-blinded condition that states only the oracle form (e.g., similarity to a hidden reference) scores 5.39, while a deterministic retrieval-only baseline with no LLM reaches 12.16 (84% of the full agent). A full 23-task 2x2 ablation and a three-seed replication on eight tasks support this interpretation. On a protein-targeted benchmark with an AutoDock Vina composite oracle, a single-edit random walk is a strong lower bound; across three seeds it obtains composite 0.636 +/- 0.008 and docking improvement -1.11 +/- 0.04 kcal/mol. As a positive control, a retrieval-free population agent exceeds REINVENT at a matched 50-call AUC budget, with its strongest result at 100 calls. These results motivate two inexpensive sanity checks: retrieval-safe PMO prompts and a random-walk floor for docking-based optimization.

</details>

<a id="paper-117"></a>

### 117. [Evolutionary Adaptive Metric Learning: A Multi-Objective Framework for Learning Local Distance Functions in Nearest Neighbor Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08549v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08549

**作者：** Isaac Touza

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08549v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08549v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EAML：以锚点间 softmax 插值的对角缩放叠加全局低秩相关分量，构造实例相关的正定距离函数，用 elitist 遗传算法优化，并给出正定性、光滑性、泛化与收敛性分析；多个公开数据集上局部度量仅在部分数据有效，整体未显著优于全局度量。

**相关性简评：** 该文的局部度量场、光滑性与正定约束，以及“噪声/冗余特征下全局度量反而更优”的负结果，可能为 latent 相似度度量的几何正则化与评估设计提供可核查的类比线索，但与 VLM 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evolutionary Adaptive Metric Learning: A Multi-Objective Framework for Learning Local Distance Functions in Nearest Neighbor Classification

**abstract/A1：** Nearest neighbour classifiers inherit the geometry of their distance function. Fixed distances and globally learned metrics apply the same geometry throughout the input space, although relevant feature directions may vary across regions. We propose EAML (Evolutionary Adaptive Metric Learning), a framework that learns instance-dependent positive-definite metrics through a low-dimensional parameterization. Each local metric combines an input-dependent diagonal scaling, generated by softmax interpolation between anchor points, with a global low-rank correlation component. A real-coded elitist genetic algorithm optimizes the parameters using a scalar objective combining leave-one-out prediction error, robustness, metric smoothness, and regularization. We establish positive definiteness, metric equivalence, existence of an optimal solution, and Lipschitz continuity of the metric field. We further investigate prediction stability, generalization bounds, and evolutionary convergence under explicit assumptions. Experiments on synthetic data with region-dependent feature relevance show that EAML can recover local metric variations and outperform global metrics. Evaluation on thirteen public datasets using nested 5-fold cross-validation reveals a more nuanced picture: EAML improves performance on selected datasets but does not significantly outperform global metric baselines overall. Additional experiments show that global diagonal metrics can be preferable under nuisance features and test-time noise. These findings identify the conditions under which evolutionary local metric learning is beneficial and highlight its practical limitations.

</details>

<a id="paper-118"></a>

### 118. [Minimum-Sufficient Knowledge and Computation for Wind Energy Predictive Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08558v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08558

**作者：** Purbak Sengupta、Aparna S. varde、Emil Jørgensen Njor

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08558v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08558v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「最小充分知识与计算」（MSKC）原则，在风电场功率爬坡事件预测中实例化自适应知识路由：以状态感知门控在本地、关系与物理先验三条路线间按需升级，并给出可行性保持、成本占优与期望节省的条件及真实数据验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「按状态选择最低成本路径、不足才升级」的门控与成本占优分析，可能为 LVR 的动态计算分配与停止决策（THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益成本权衡）提供问题表述参考，但无视觉或 latent 状态内容，跨域可迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Minimum-Sufficient Knowledge and Computation for Wind Energy Predictive Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Resource-constrained cyber-physical decision systems often apply fixed sensing, knowledge, and computational pipelines despite state-dependent information needs. We introduce Minimum-Sufficient Knowledge and Computation (MSKC), a resource-aware computing principle: select the least-costly route meeting decision-risk and deployment constraints, escalating only when cheaper routes are insufficient. As proof of concept, we isolate adaptive knowledge routing, the decision underlying Knowledge-Guided Machine Learning (KGML), which conventionally injects external knowledge unconditionally rather than only where needed. We instantiate this for wind ramp-event predictive analysis, an edge-oriented decision-support task, evaluating local, relational, and physics-informed routes with a state-aware gate on publicly available real data from two wind farms. We establish conditions for feasibility preservation, cost dominance, and strict expected savings over always-on routing. At a 35.9% relational-knowledge injection rate, adaptive routing is statistically indistinguishable from always-on injection on the reported metrics while reducing knowledge calls by 64%, supporting sustainable, Green-IT-aligned deployment. These results support MSKC's premise that uniformly maximal intelligence is unnecessary. This offers intelligent data analysis for big data streams: the analysis itself decides, per state, how much knowledge and computation the data warrant.

**comment/C1：** To be published in IEEE BigData 2026 Conference

</details>

<a id="paper-119"></a>

### 119. [PINNeval: A Comprehensive Evaluation Standard for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08559v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08559

**作者：** Kevin von Bargen、Hamid Ebrahimy、Tim Römer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08559v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08559v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一套 PINN 综合评测协议，把域重要性自适应、损失平衡、网络架构与优化方案四个组件视为整体系统来考察，要求非研究对象采用当前有竞争力的实现，并发布含 18 种方法与 9 个基准问题的 JAX 包 PINNpipe。

**相关性简评：** 其「整体评测＋强基线＋可追溯记录」的协议思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等模块的贡献分配与消融设计，避免以弱默认实现得出错误归因；但领域较远，需人工判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PINNeval: A Comprehensive Evaluation Standard for Physics-Informed Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** The Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) literature has produced a rapidly expanding catalogue of methods. We divide a PINN training pipeline systematically into four components and observe that many existing benchmarks study these components in isolation. These components are domain-importance adaptation, loss balancing, neural network architecture, and optimization scheme. Moreover, the studies set the surrounding and not considered components of the pipeline to a minimal default that is no longer representative of current best practice approaches. Conclusions drawn under such conditions may not transfer to competitive pipelines. In this work, we propose a new evaluation protocol that (1) treats the four components as an integrated system; (2) crucially requires competitive implementations of the components not under investigation; (3) specifies the metrics and reporting format; and (4) is accompanied by structured documentation that ensures the traceability of evaluations across studies. Based on this comprehensive protocol, we release PINNpipe, an innovative JAX package containing 18 different methods from the current literature as well as nine efficiently implemented benchmark problems. Using PINNpipe, we illustrate our evaluation protocol in relation to established benchmark studies and report results that challenge common assumptions.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026), Track on Evaluations and Datasets. 54 pages. Code: https://github.com/KevinvonBargen/pinnpipe

</details>

<a id="paper-120"></a>

### 120. [NormGym: Evaluating Normative Competence through Social Interaction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08613v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08613

**作者：** Binze Li、Zihuan Zhang、Seokhyun Baek、Gillian K. Hadfield、Tianmin Shu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08613v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08613v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** NormGym 提出一个交互式基准：新加入的 LLM agent 在完成自身任务的同时，需从多智能体社区的行为与惩罚中推断隐藏的任意规范，并迁移到新情境；评测显示最强模型联合任务与合规成功率仅 55.3%，且难以利用他人的行为与所受惩罚。

**相关性简评：** 隐藏规范可视为需从间接证据推断并更新的潜在假设，其「可能」与「允许」的区分及证据筛选失败分析，可能为 LVR 的 latent 假设推断、视觉证据利用与停止决策提供概念参照，但无视觉或内部状态机制，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NormGym: Evaluating Normative Competence through Social Interaction

**abstract/A1：** Normative competence, the ability to infer socially enforced expectations from interaction and adjust behavior accordingly, is essential for agents operating in unfamiliar human communities. Yet existing evaluations largely test whether agents can recognize or apply norms they may already know, rather than whether they can acquire previously unknown norms from social interaction. We introduce NormGym, an interactive benchmark for normative competence in which a newcomer must infer a hidden, arbitrary norm while pursuing its own task in a multi-agent community. Success requires distinguishing what is possible from what is permitted, inferring the norm from observed behavior and sanctions, and applying it in new situations. NormGym contains diverse norms and distinct environments, allowing systematic evaluation of current agents, including large language model (LLM) agents. We find that normative competence remains challenging: the strongest evaluated LLM achieves a joint task-and-compliance success rate of 55.3%. Our analysis reveals that models can sometimes revise their behavior following sanctions for their own actions, but struggle to use other agents' behavior and the sanctions they receive to infer the underlying norm. We further find that fine-tuning models may be insufficient to improve normative competence. These results suggest that current models lack a robust, transferable capability for acquiring normative knowledge through social interaction.

</details>

<a id="paper-121"></a>

### 121. [IWIBench: Evaluating Gradient-Free Model Driven Intra-weight Injection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08647v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08647

**作者：** Chris von Csefalvay、Pranav Doma、Achyuthan Unni Krishnan、Tamás Földi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08647v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08647v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 IWIBench，评测大模型能否在无梯度条件下直接改写小目标模型（如 Gemma 3 4B IT）的权重，以完成十类文本编辑目标并尽量保留其他能力，并给出受限读写工具、rollout 预算与独立验证环境下的结果。

**相关性简评：** 其「受限干预工具＋固定预算＋独立验证器对照未修改模型」的协议可能可供核查，用于设计对内部状态（如 latent 表示）施加改写并检验其因果作用的评测；但论文干预对象是权重而非推理期视觉状态，与 LVR 主线联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** IWIBench: Evaluating Gradient-Free Model Driven Intra-weight Injection

**abstract/A1：** Intra-weight injection lets one model change another model's behaviour by editing its weights directly, without gradients. Such edits can range from altering lexical associations to enforcing output formats or updating factual knowledge. Despite growing interest in these approaches, there has been no systematic way to evaluate how well models perform them.
