We introduce IWIBench, which measures a large editor model's ability to modify the weights of a smaller target model (typically Gemma 3 4B IT) to achieve one of ten classical text-editing objectives while preserving its other capabilities. Editors are limited to four tools for reading and writing weights, with bounded tensor access and a fixed budget for generating and inspecting rollouts. A validator applies each proposed patch in a clean environment and compares the edited model against the unmodified target. Evaluating frontier closed- and open-weight models reveals sharply diverging capabilities, both across models and across tasks. A small number of models, most notably Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol, perform far ahead of the rest. The recency of this ability (little of it is evident in GPT-5.6 Sol, for instance), together with qualitative analysis, suggests it has been acquired through recent training. Tasks also vary widely: structured response resisted every attempt, and answer-length control yielded only one successful solution, whereas alias acquisition and recoding proved most amenable. Success in one task family correlated poorly with success in others, indicating an early and uneven capability frontier. We discuss strategies that editors repeatedly adopted and conclude with an outlook on intra-weight injection as an emerging but already potent approach to gradient-free behaviour modification.

</details>

<a id="paper-122"></a>

### 122. [Scoring, Not Learning: Decomposing Catalog-Consolidation Gains in Web-Scraped Retail Product Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08655v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08655

**作者：** Kaden Leis、Xiang Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08655v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08655v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 16 万余张网络抓取的奶酪图像上考察商品目录整合（去包装图、去近重复、把细粒度条目合并为基类）对识别准确率的影响，发现 +4 点左右的收益主要来自把预测概率按合并组求和即可复现的评测标签空间变化，而非重训练带来的模型改进。

**相关性简评：** 该文没有状态、证据获取或推理轨迹，与 LVR 主线无直接关系；但其「收益来自正确性定义改变而非模型能力」的拆分思路，可能供核查 latent 推理增益是否也把评测口径或标签合并误算为内部状态使用的贡献。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scoring, Not Learning: Decomposing Catalog-Consolidation Gains in Web-Scraped Retail Product Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Retail product catalogs assembled from web image search are cheap to build and pervasively noisy: queries return packaging shots instead of products, and entries proliferate under names that cannot be distinguished visually. Consolidating such a catalog can look like an alternative to scaling the model. We show this is not a comparison between two ways of improving a recognizer, but between improving a recognizer and changing what counts as correct. We study a public archive of 165,769 cheese images across 1,824 query-named entries. A packaging filter and near-duplicate removal reduce it to a 998-class recognition set on one fixed, group-aware split. Canonicalizing entries to their base type (844 classes) raises top-1 accuracy by 4.0 points. Scoring the unchanged model against the merged label space, by summing its predicted probability within each canonical group, recovers +4.27 points with no retraining (+4.25 over six seeds). Retraining on the consolidated catalog then adds -0.10$\\pm$0.53, +0.08$\\pm$0.65 and -0.10$\\pm$0.65 points at 28, 50 and 88 M parameters, indistinguishable from zero at every capacity ($\\pm$ is the across-seed standard deviation). That component is specific, not mechanical: random merges with the identical size profile yield only +0.68 points, and the three backbones agree to within 0.1 points. But it is overwhelmingly category coarsening, not alias correction: across the 37 merged groups, type coarsenings supply 94% of the gain and alias collapses only 7%. We also detect no interaction between consolidation and backbone size: tripling backbone parameters is worth +1.50$\\pm$0.57 points on the raw catalog and +1.42$\\pm$00.44 on the canonical one, a within-seed interaction of -0.08 \[-0.79,+0.63\]. On this archive, the reported consolidation gain primarily reflects the evaluation label space, and separating that out costs one evaluation

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 4 figures, was accepted by a computer vision conference workshop

</details>

<a id="paper-123"></a>

### 123. [Pdb or Not to Be: Comparing Debugging Interfaces With Pass$\_{X}$@$k$](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08666v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08666

**作者：** Ryan Magnuson、Ellie Kitanidis、Ariel Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08666v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08666v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 SWE-bench Verified 上固定其余 scaffold，比较仅用交互式调试器与仅用 bash 的编码智能体，并引入跨智能体 pass\_X@k 估计量，以区分接口带来的专长与重复采样带来的覆盖扩张。

**相关性简评：** 其「两种配置解出集合部分不相交却多半源于运行方差」的检验框架，可能可供核查多种 latent 假设或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 组合的增益是否真为互补而非采样噪声。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pdb or Not to Be: Comparing Debugging Interfaces With Pass$\_{X}$@$k$

**abstract/A1：** Interactive debuggers are indispensable to human software development, yet coding agents overwhelmingly rely on edit-execute-observe loops, using common bash tools, test scripts, and print statements to debug via repeated shell commands. Stepping through a debugger provides live access to runtime state, which may enable different problem-solving capabilities. However, prior work typically introduces the debugger as an additional tool or through a new subagent, making its individual contribution difficult to isolate from the associated scaffold changes. To address this, we compare a debugger-only agent against a bash-only agent on all 500 SWE-bench Verified instances, holding the rest of the scaffold fixed. We evaluate both architectures on two models, GPT-OSS-120B and Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B, with three independent runs per architecture $\\times$ model configuration. Crucially, to distinguish interface specialization from expanded coverage under repeated sampling, we introduce pass$\_X$@$k$, a cross-agent pass@$k$ that provides an unbiased estimator of the probability that at least one of $k$ agents is successful. Although the bash and debugger agents solve partially disjoint subsets of the benchmark in any single pair of runs, our pass$\_X$@$2$ estimator reveals that this is largely due to run-to-run variance rather than tool complementarity. Across both models, we find that two bash rollouts outperform the combination of one bash and one debugger rollout. Our findings highlight the importance of accounting for stochastic variance in evaluating agent and tool diversity, and we hope that pass$\_X$@$k$ will serve as a useful framework for the community.

**comment/C1：** Accepted, NeurIPS 2026 workshop "Who Verifies the Agents? Toward Reliable Agent Development"

</details>

<a id="paper-124"></a>

### 124. [Transporting the Past: An Optimal Transport View of Backtracking Counterfactuals](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08682v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08682

**作者：** Zhangyi Liu、Pouria Fatemi、Suvrit Sra

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08682v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08682v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文从最优传输视角研究结构因果模型中的回溯反事实推断，提出两种把事实观测诱导的外生变量后验分布传输到满足反事实查询的约束集上的框架，并给出边际、惩罚与硬约束求解器。

**相关性简评：** 其把反事实视为对后验状态分布的约束传输与独立性保持，可能为 LVR 中「干预 latent 推理状态并评估其因果作用」及 latent 变量独立性/可解释性约束提供形式化线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Transporting the Past: An Optimal Transport View of Backtracking Counterfactuals

**abstract/A1：** We study backtracking counterfactual inference for Structural Causal Models (SCMs) through the lens of optimal transport. In SCMs, a factual observation generally induces a posterior distribution over exogenous variables, making counterfactual inference inherently distributional. We introduce two complementary frameworks that transport this posterior onto the set of exogenous configurations satisfying a desired counterfactual query. The first constructs a natural pointwise projection and is suitable when the transported exogenous distribution can be used directly. The second enforces mutual independence among counterfactual exogenous variables, so that the transported variables remain interpretable as genuine exogenous noises of the same SCM. We show that both frameworks are consistent with the original backtracking counterfactual principle, and we propose marginal, penalty-based, and hard-constrained solvers for finite-posterior settings. Synthetic experiments on non-bijective SCMs illustrate the geometry of the problem and reveal trade-offs among transport cost, counterfactual constraint satisfaction, and preservation of exogenous independence.

</details>

<a id="paper-125"></a>

### 125. [Does a Trading Policy Need to Know What It Trades? Microstructure Invariance and Zero-Shot Transfer of Execution Policies](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08701v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08701

**作者：** Hieu Tran、Hong Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE、q-fin.TR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08701v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08701v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文检验微观结构不变性归一化能否让「排队等待或跨价差」的执行策略跨资产零样本迁移：在 Nasdaq 逐笔回放上训练，测试未见股票、加密资产与另一交易所，并比较不变单位与原始单位、z-score 等表示。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接联系，但其尺度不变的表示归一化，以及「用迁移差距判断表示是否正确可能失效」的结论，可能为 latent 表示的评价与跨分布泛化指标提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Does a Trading Policy Need to Know What It Trades? Microstructure Invariance and Zero-Shot Transfer of Execution Policies

**abstract/A1：** Execution algorithms are calibrated asset by asset, because the queues, trade rates and bid-ask spreads they face differ by orders of magnitude across assets. For reinforcement learning this is zero-shot generalisation across contexts of very different scales, and microstructure invariance proposes a normalisation fixed by theory: measured in each asset's own units of time, size and price, markets look alike. In an exact order-by-order replay of public Nasdaq data, we train policies once, on 954 stocks in 2019, to choose between waiting in the queue and crossing the spread, and evaluate them unchanged on 1,964 unseen stocks in 2020-21, 2,629 stocks in 2025-26, crypto pairs and a second exchange. First, a policy that sees a stock only through its recent trade rate, trade size, spread and price increment (tick) costs within 0.015 spreads per share (a fifth of what learning saves) of a policy whose training data included the stock, and the stock's own history does not help it; it comes within a similar margin of policies trained on the second exchange when every episode counts equally, and within a wider one on crypto. Second, in mean cost before fees, invariant units beat raw units, z-scores and asset descriptors, but by under a basis point: most of their advantage is information and a common cost scale that a learner can also take as plain inputs, and none of the representations tested extrapolates to unseen spread regimes. Third, a near-zero transfer gap to a pooled comparator is weak evidence that a representation is right: we show that such a comparator detects only a small share of what a poor representation loses, and confirm it in synthetic markets and on pendulums.

**comment/C1：** 33 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-126"></a>

### 126. [Random Symbolic Boosting: A Geometric Approach to Symbolic Regression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08753v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08753

**作者：** José R. Ortegaa、José Manuel Muñoz Contreras、Leonardo Trujillo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08753v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08753v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Random Symbolic Boosting，用 boosting 在随机生成的语法树种群上直接构建可解释的加性符号模型以替代世代进化，并引入基于预测向量与目标向量夹角、与问题尺度无关的自适应几何早停机制；在符号回归基准与 200 个表格分类任务上评估。

**相关性简评：** 其尺度无关、跨任务免调参的角度式早停准则，可能为 LVR 中动态计算与 STOP 决策的停止判据提供可核查线索，但研究对象是符号模型拟合而非视觉 latent 推理状态，联系仅为概念层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Random Symbolic Boosting: A Geometric Approach to Symbolic Regression

**abstract/A1：** Symbolic models offer interpretability, but their construction through generational evolution can be computationally expensive. This article presents Random Symbolic Boosting (RSB), a method that constructs explicit additive symbolic models via boosting over populations of randomly generated syntax trees, eliminating the need for generational evolution. The idea combines two observations: high-dimensional spaces can be spanned by enough linearly independent vectors, and the semantics of syntax trees can be directly manipulated as such vectors. It is shown that, for Ordinary Least Squares, the boosting process guarantees a monotonic reduction of the training error for both regression and classification. Furthermore, an adaptive geometric early stopping mechanism is introduced. It is based on the angle between the prediction and target vectors, is independent of the problems scale, and requires no adjustments across tasks. Evaluated on a standard benchmark for symbolic regression, the method is located at the Pareto-optimal point between predictive accuracy and training time, ranking first on the real-world datasets. Additionally, it was tested on a large tabular classification benchmark with 200 problems, achieving competitive results.

</details>

<a id="paper-127"></a>

### 127. [On Lipschitz Explosion in Deep Neural Networks with Normalization: Consequences for Optimization and Adversarial Robustness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08775v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08775

**作者：** Ashkan Soleymani、Reyhaneh Hosseinpourkhoshkbari、Hadi Daneshmand、Patrick Jaillet

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、math.OC、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08775v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08775v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文证明含归一化层（如 BatchNorm）的深层网络即使在逐层范数受控、甚至线性激活下，输入与参数 Lipschitz 常数仍可随深度指数增长，使优化与鲁棒性证书失效，并提出「秩分离」机制解释对抗脆弱性，用 MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10 实验验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「引入新奇异方向的扰动被放大远强于同子空间扰动」的结论，可能为诊断 latent 视觉状态对细节扰动的稳定性、敏感性度量与视觉证据筛选提供参照，可供核查是否能用于 latent 推理轨迹的逐层不稳定分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On Lipschitz Explosion in Deep Neural Networks with Normalization: Consequences for Optimization and Adversarial Robustness

**abstract/A1：** Can a network look stable at every layer and still be exponentially unstable as a whole? The Lipschitz constant of a neural network is a basic stability quantity appearing in optimization guarantees, generalization bounds, and robustness certificates. Deep networks naturally admit two such notions, input Lipschitzness, measuring sensitivity to data perturbations, and parameter Lipschitzness, measuring sensitivity to weight perturbations. We prove that, for deep networks with normalization layers, with batch normalization as the canonical example, both Lipschitz constants can grow exponentially with depth and hence with parameter dimension, even under strong per-layer norm control such as $\\\|W\_\\ell\\\|\_2\\le 1$, and already for linear activations. Thus, a mechanism widely used to stabilize training can create severe worst-case instabilities that are invisible locally, i.e., from layerwise norm bounds alone. Parameter-Lipschitz explosion turns Lipschitz-dependent nonsmooth optimization guarantees into exponential-in-dimension bounds for deep normalized networks, while input-Lipschitz explosion makes worst-case Lipschitz-based generalization and robustness certificates vacuous. The latter also yields a concrete rank-separation mechanism for adversarial vulnerability. The theory predicts that perturbations introducing new singular directions should be amplified much more strongly than perturbations at the same noise level that remain within the input's original singular subspace. Experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 support this prediction, showing that rank-creating perturbations cause substantially sharper drops in accuracy, confidence, and margins than same-subspace perturbations.

**comment/C1：** The Fortieth Annual Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS) 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-128"></a>

### 128. [Aligning, Fast and Slow: A Control-Theoretic Oversight Framework for Long-Horizon Trajectories and Maintained Deployments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08781v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08781

**作者：** Stephen Peng、Vineet Jagadeesan Nair、Noah Broestl、Anne Friederike Kleppe、Steven Mills、Adi Zolotov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08781v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08781v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个控制论式监督框架：快速回路审查每步提出的动作，慢速回路定期复核持久性改动（记忆、代码）并在模型或工具更新后重评配置，并给出部署层面的偏差界与审查预算分配规则，用编码与交易压力测试验证组合控制的效果。

**相关性简评：** 其「快回路/慢回路」分工与固定审查预算如何在两者间切分，可能与 LVR 中 THINK 步数、视觉证据复核与停止决策之间的计算分配问题形成类比，可供核查；但该文不涉及多模态内部表示，联系仅为概念层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Aligning, Fast and Slow: A Control-Theoretic Oversight Framework for Long-Horizon Trajectories and Maintained Deployments

**abstract/A1：** AI agents can pass checks on individual tasks while retaining memory or code changes that alter their later behavior. We introduce a control-theoretic framework that couples a fast loop controlling proposed actions with a slow loop reviewing persistent changes and reassessing configurations after model or tool updates. When measurement errors and changes between checks are bounded and controllers can enforce their decisions, we derive a deployment-level bound and a rule for dividing review effort. In simulation, dividing a fixed review budget between both loops reduces mean maximum departure from intended behavior roughly twelvefold compared with review confined to individual runs. In coding stress tests with authored memories and five matched seeds, combined controls reduce mean severity on a 0-3 scale from 0.325 without gates to 0.117, resolving 33 of 120 issues versus 35 without gates; in trading stress tests, combined controls reduce mean shortfall in low-emission purchases from 0.892 without gates to 0.107. The overall framework helps developers and auditors compare and enforce oversight designs, while the agent studies test component controls rather than the full deployment-level guarantee.

</details>

<a id="paper-129"></a>

### 129. [Extrapolation Matters: Sparse-Only Depth Completion Beyond LiDAR Coverage](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08782v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08782

**作者：** Maximilian Jansen、Gasser Elazab、Praveen Kulkarni

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08782v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08782v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究超出 LiDAR 覆盖范围的深度补全：用冻结的单目深度基础模型提供先验，经轻量逐帧对齐到稀疏 LiDAR 几何后由细化模块融合稀疏锚点输出稠密度量深度，并引入高频法向一致性损失与区分「LiDAR 支持区」和「覆盖外区」的评测协议。

**相关性简评：** 其把「有证据支撑区」与「证据外推区」分开评测的做法，可能为 LVR 中区分由视觉证据支撑的 latent 内容与纯外推内容、以及 LOOK/STOP 的收益核算提供评价思路，但任务本身不涉及推理状态生成，仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Extrapolation Matters: Sparse-Only Depth Completion Beyond LiDAR Coverage

**abstract/A1：** Extrapolation beyond LiDAR coverage is an important yet under-evaluated challenge of depth completion. In real camera--LiDAR systems, partial sensor overlap, occlusions, and limited field coverage often leave large image regions weakly observed or completely unobserved by LiDAR. Thus, we investigate depth completion in this beyond-coverage regime using only sparse supervision from raw single-sweep LiDAR, without dense depth labels, LiDAR accumulation, or pseudo-LiDAR generation. Our approach leverages a frozen monocular depth foundation model, lightweight per-frame alignment to sparse LiDAR geometry, and a refinement module that fuses the aligned prior with sparse LiDAR anchors to predict dense metric depth. To regularize learning under raw sparse supervision, we introduce a high-frequency normal-consistency loss defined as the image-space gradient of the per-pixel normal cosine discrepancy. This term penalizes spatial variation in scalar angular error; it does not directly match changes in neighboring normal vectors. We further introduce an evaluation protocol that explicitly separates LiDAR-supported and beyond-coverage regions, revealing extrapolative behavior that conventional depth-completion metrics can obscure. Our method achieves strong depth accuracy beyond LiDAR coverage while remaining competitive in LiDAR-covered regions, providing a practical way to deploy monocular foundation priors in multi-sensor systems.

</details>

<a id="paper-130"></a>

### 130. [Training Stability under Adaptive Curricula](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08787v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08787

**作者：** Johan Obando-Ceron、Walter Mayor、Michael Dennis、Aaron Courville、Pablo Samuel Castro

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08787v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08787v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在无监督环境设计（UED）下考察学习者侧的训练稳定性，跨多种课程机制与复杂度发现参数增长、价值—结果排序退化、表示多样性丧失等病理，并通过干预验证稳定化学习者可改善长程表现与零样本泛化。

**相关性简评：** 其「表示多样性丧失／信息坍缩」与「学习者优化状态反过来决定未来训练分布」的分析，可能为 LVR 中自生成推理轨迹的监督分配与 latent 状态多样性维持提供类比线索，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Training Stability under Adaptive Curricula

**abstract/A1：** Unsupervised Environment Design (UED) approaches train reinforcement learning (RL) agents on diverse curricula that may evolve with their capabilities. While prior work has largely focused on how these curricula are constructed, we study whether their students remain trainable under the resulting distributions. Across multiple UED mechanisms, environment complexities, and extended training horizons, we identify recurring learner-side pathologies, including parameter growth, degraded value--outcome ordering, and loss of representational diversity, that accompany stagnation and performance degradation. These findings connect limitations in UED to optimization failures previously identified in conventional deep RL, while showing how adaptive curricula couple the learner's optimization state to its future training distribution. Controlled interventions show that mitigating selected pathologies improves learning. Stabilizing the learner improves long-horizon performance and zero-shot generalization without changing the curriculum rule or increasing environment interaction. Our results establish learner trainability as a complementary design principle for UED. Effective curricula require students that remain capable of learning from the distributions they produce.

</details>

<a id="paper-131"></a>

### 131. [The Matthews Correlation Coefficient: Fifty years](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08799v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08799

**作者：** Jesus S. Aguilar-Ruiz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08799v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08799v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文综述 Matthews 相关系数（MCC）五十年来的历史、数学基础与实践含义，澄清常见误解，形式化其与 Pearson 相关及 2×2 表 Chi2 的等价性，并用导数与等高线框架分析其在类别不平衡下的偏差、鲁棒性与敏感性。

**相关性简评：** 与 latent visual reasoning 无直接联系，但其对不平衡条件下二分类指标偏差与敏感性的分析，可能可供核查用于评价「继续思考/继续看/停止」等二元决策及证据筛选效果的指标选择。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Matthews Correlation Coefficient: Fifty years

**abstract/A1：** Fifty years after its introduction, the Matthews Correlation Coefficient (MCC) remains central -and debated- in binary classification. This survey offers a comprehensive retrospective and an in-depth analysis of MCC, covering its historical development, mathematical foundations, and implications for practice. We clarify long-standing misconceptions, formalize its equivalences (to Pearson's correlation and to Chi2 in 2x2 tables), and examine its behavior under class imbalance. Beyond MCC, we develop analytical (derivatives) and graphical (contour plots) frameworks that generalize to any confusion-matrix-based metric. These frameworks expose structural properties -bias, robustness, sensitivity- that scalar summaries conceal, and they provide actionable guidance for selecting and interpreting performance measures in applications where reliability and fairness matter. Together, our results position MCC within a broader toolkit while clarifying when it is informative -and when alternatives may be preferable.

</details>

<a id="paper-132"></a>

### 132. [Scale as a Modality: Multi-Scale Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08802v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08802

**作者：** Ruibo Chen、Kaishen Wang、Tianyi Xiong、Chenxi Liu、Yanshuo Chen、Heng Huang、Jian Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08802v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08802v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MS-pDiT，把空间尺度当作一种模态：对同一含噪图像按多种 patch 大小分词，各尺度在独立 transformer 流中处理，并通过压缩的 MM-DiT 式联合注意力双向交换信息，用于像素空间扩散图像生成。

**相关性简评：** 该工作面向图像生成而非推理，但「多尺度流 + 跨尺度双向注意力」及「较晚引入细粒度流更优」的观察，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据的粒度组织与细节流引入时机提供概念线索，需核查其代价是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scale as a Modality: Multi-Scale Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Latent diffusion models rely on pretrained autoencoders that introduce lossy compression and constrain spatial granularity. Pixel-space diffusion avoids this bottleneck and enables flexible tokenization at multiple spatial scales, yet existing approaches largely adopt two-level coarse-to-fine architectures with limited cross-scale interaction. We introduce MS-pDiT, a multi-scale pixel-space diffusion transformer that treats spatial scale as a modality. MS-pDiT tokenizes the same noisy image at multiple patch sizes, processes each scale in a dedicated transformer stream, and enables bidirectional information exchange through compacted MM-DiT-style joint attention, with attention cost independent of the finest patch size. This design establishes the number of scales as a new architectural scaling dimension. On ImageNet $256\\times256$, increasing the number of scales from two to five consistently improves FID from 4.39 to 2.31, 2.26, and 2.19, while adding an intermediate scale substantially outperforms increasing depth under comparable parameter budgets. Further analysis highlights the importance of bidirectional cross-scale interaction and late introduction of fine-grained streams. MS-pDiT achieves FID scores of 1.43 and 1.44 on ImageNet $256\\times256$ and $512\\times512$, respectively, and naturally extends to text-to-image generation, achieving 0.86 on GenEval and 83.9 on DPG-Bench.

</details>

<a id="paper-133"></a>

### 133. [I Think, Therefore I Attack: Evaluating Thought Injection Attacks on Reasoning Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08811v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08811

**作者：** Parth Illendula、Garik Livingston、Zain Shamsi、Ravi Srinivasan、Alex Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08811v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08811v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究针对大型推理模型的「思维注入」攻击：先测试静态注入提示在不同风格及有无 thinking tag 条件下的效果，再在容器化智能体靶场中用模仿目标模型自身推理痕迹的间接注入，取得最高 50% 的攻击成功率。

**相关性简评：** 论文显示模型会把伪装成自身思考的文本当作可信内部状态、且依赖标签的区分并不可靠，这可能可供核查 LVR 中内部推理状态与外部证据的边界、思考标记作为状态隔离手段的有效性及状态来源可信度，但材料本身无视觉或多模态推理内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** I Think, Therefore I Attack: Evaluating Thought Injection Attacks on Reasoning Agents

**abstract/A1：** Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are one of the key components of current agentic systems. LRMs augment their output with an explicit chain-of-thought reasoning stage, introducing a new attack surface for thought injection, in which untrusted text resembling the model's own deliberation is fed back to the model. We present a two-part study of this attack, performed against the prevailing large, production class open-weight models (e.g., GLM-5.3, MiniMax-M2.5, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next). We first evaluate the effectiveness of static thought-injection prompts under different styles as well as tagged and untagged conditions and find that the effects of thinking tags and payload styles are all properties of the specific target model. In the second study, we exploit this model-specificity in a containerized agentic cyber range, using indirect injection prompts styled in the form of the target model's own reasoning traces to achieve attack success rates (ASR) up to 50% on operational objectives such as reverse-shell exfiltration and repository backdooring. We also find that the same malicious objective in standard prompt injection user voice never succeeds, and thinking tags suppress the agent's tendency to disclose the injection. Our results show that imitating a reasoning model's own thought style is effective for this style of attack, and that tag stripping is an unreliable defense.

</details>

<a id="paper-134"></a>

### 134. [The APL Doesn't Fall Far From The Tree: Unsupervised Artefact Prototype Learning in Histopathology](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08838v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08838

**作者：** Emilia Agasi、Charlie Gourley、C. Simon Herrington、Robert L. Hollis、T. Ian Simpson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08838v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08838v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 APL，在冻结的病理基础模型嵌入空间中用 out-of-distribution patch 原型无监督地区分技术伪影与组织，无需标签或微调，单中心数据上优于 GrandQC 与 HistoART，但组织褶皱和血液识别较弱。

**相关性简评：** 其利用预训练嵌入几何做原型式 OOD 判别，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与无效 patch 剔除提供线索，但属医学影像应用，与 latent 推理状态生成无直接关系，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The APL Doesn't Fall Far From The Tree: Unsupervised Artefact Prototype Learning in Histopathology

**abstract/A1：** Histopathology foundation models provide rich representations of tissue morphology, but technical artefacts in whole slide images introduce noise that may impact diagnostic accuracy. We introduce Artefact Prototype Learning (APL), a novel approach to detecting these artefacts that exploits the geometry of pre-trained embedding spaces without manual labels or fine-tuning. Rather than treating artefacts as isolated outliers, APL computes prototypes of out-of-distribution patches and uses them to distinguish artefact-associated patterns from tissue. Evaluation on a single-centre dataset shows that APL outperforms prior methods GrandQC and HistoART in detecting artefacts such as blur, dark spots, slide edges and pen marks, but struggles with tissue folds and blood. These results show that frozen pre-trained embeddings already carry information that allows artefact detection without expensive labels or vast computational resources.

</details>

<a id="paper-135"></a>

### 135. [What Matters for LLM Persona Simulation: Signal Form in Cold-Start E-Commerce Advertising](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08840v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08840

**作者：** Jiwoo Choi、In Ik Lee、Woo Sung Seo、Minho Lee、Yun Young Choi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08840v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08840v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用真实 Google Ads 冷启动电商广告数据检验 LLM 人格化模拟中哪些设计选择影响效果，发现极简人口统计锚点、相对的定性信号以及提示措辞是三项关键因素，并用消融与基线比较给出误差改善。其中“更丰富的人格属性反而误差更高、相对定性线索优于定量锚点”这一观察，与 LVR 中多模态条件信号的粒度、相对与绝对形式如何影响模型推理，可能存在形式上的可比性，可供核查其是否在视觉属性或几何线索注入时复现。

**相关性简评：** 领域较远，存在具体借鉴线索，即“属性越丰富未必越好”的信号形式消融，或可启发对 LVR 条件信号粒度问题的假设。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Matters for LLM Persona Simulation: Signal Form in Cold-Start E-Commerce Advertising

**abstract/A1：** Estimating advertising performance in cold-start settings is challenging because historical logs are unavailable before launch. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential alternative by simulating persona-conditioned user responses, but it remains unclear which design choices actually drive LLM-based persona simulation. Using real Google Ads e-commerce campaign data across five demographic segments, we identify three signal-form dimensions that affect simulation quality: (i) a demographic anchor without noisy rich attributes, (ii) a relative qualitative banner-blindness signal rather than quantitative anchors, and (iii) prompt phrasing. On this campaign, our method attains an MAE of 24.9 and a MAPE of 26.1%, a 51.3% lower MAE than the best tested non-LLM baseline and 77.3% lower than an LLM configuration using explicit CTR anchors. Systematic ablations show that minimal demographic descriptions achieve lower mean error than richer persona configurations, while relative qualitative cues substantially outperform the tested quantitative anchors. We additionally compare these configurations with direct-prior and rule-based baselines and with alternative LLM configurations.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at GenAIECommerce'26: The Third Workshop on Agentic and Generative AI for E-Commerce, co-located with RecSys 2026. 13 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-136"></a>

### 136. [Learning Whom to Trust About the Unseen: Source Credibility and Reliability Transfer in Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08855v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08855

**作者：** Ruijia Guan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08855v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08855v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用三项行为实验考察六个语言模型能否把信息源已证实的可靠性迁移到无法核验的新主张上，发现迁移存在领域边界：可靠性主要作用于自然类主张，几乎不迁移到灵性类主张，且源对自身可靠性来源的解释会进一步收窄其影响范围。

**相关性简评：** 虽无视觉模态与 latent 状态，但其「可靠性作用范围随主张类型与来源解释而变」的发现，可为 LVR 中视觉证据与文本线索的加权、证据筛选提供概念性参照，是否涉及模型的内部证据使用尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Whom to Trust About the Unseen: Source Credibility and Reliability Transfer in Language Models

**abstract/A1：** A source who has been right before deserves attention, but about what? We ask how far a demonstrated record of reliability travels in language models when the new claim cannot be checked, and whether the answer depends on the kind of claim. Three behavioral studies across six models approach the question from three angles. Study 1 adapts the Einstein-effect paradigm and finds that models, unlike the human sample, favor a spiritual leader over a scientist for the same obscure statement, and that the preference largely reverses when the statement is reworded in the vocabulary of physics. Study 2 adapts a paradigm in which a trusted expert calls a scientific or religious explanation unknown or a mystery; models reproduce the human domain asymmetry in exaggerated form. Study 3, the central test, gives two fictional informants different records on checkable river forecasts and then has them endorse matched claims about hidden natural and spiritual causes of the same village events. Most models carry the record to natural causes and barely at all to spiritual ones; one model transfers it weakly to either. Telling the model that the reliable informant forecasts by spiritual guidance rather than by measurement lowers that informant's influence on natural claims in every model and produces no comparable gain on spiritual claims in any. Demonstrated reliability thus has a scope in these models, and the account a source gives of how they came to be reliable can narrow it. Whether this reflects a judgment about relevance or the low prior credence of spiritual claims is the question the design leaves open, and we outline the experiment that would settle it.

</details>

<a id="paper-137"></a>

### 137. [Align3D: Image-Structure Co-Aligned 3D Scene Generation with Observability-Aware Fidelity-Preserving Guidance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08861v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08861

**作者：** Chenliang Zhou、Yifan Wang、Andrzej Banburski-Fahey

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08861v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08861v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Align3D，一个以单张照片为条件的扩散模型，生成既贴合图像又可外推到未观测视角的 3D 室内布局，依靠置信度校准的场景图、模态置信加权融合与可观测性感知的保真引导，在 3D-FRONT 上提升图像对齐与场景质量指标，推理为单次前馈。

**相关性简评：** 其用对象级置信度掩码区分已观测与欠观测自由度、并让先验只填补欠观测部分而不破坏图像-场景图匹配的做法，可能为 LVR 中「视觉证据不足时先验如何补位、又如何避免信息坍缩」提供线索，但该机制作用于布局输出空间而非 latent 推理状态，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Align3D: Image-Structure Co-Aligned 3D Scene Generation with Observability-Aware Fidelity-Preserving Guidance

**abstract/A1：** We present Align3D, an image-conditioned diffusion model that generates 3D indoor layouts that are both faithful to a single input photograph and physically plausible beyond the observed view. From the photograph we infer a confidence-calibrated scene graph and train a set-diffusion layout denoiser with image-structure co-alignment where confidence-weighted modality fusion balances image and scene-graph alignment using predicted confidences, while observability-aware fidelity-preserving guidance projects conflicting scene-quality gradients and reweighs them with an object-level confidence mask so under-observed degrees of freedom absorb physical priors without undoing the image-graph match. On 3D-FRONT, Align3D improves image-alignment and scene-quality metrics over recent baselines; once trained, it maps previously unseen photographs to layouts in a single feed-forward pass.

</details>

### 138. [Decoding spatial and semantic information from large-scale visual cortical activity for natural-movie reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08887v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08887

**作者：** Yukai Huang、Haoyu Albert Wang、Jiawei Zhang、Jialun Ma、Jiayao Ding、Yiran Lin、Xingyu Chen、Ziqi Gao、Yuguo Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08887v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08887v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出 Ca2REAL，用小鼠 V1 钙成像群体活动分别驱动预训练视频扩散模型的布局与语义控制通路，重建自然电影片段，并通过扰动与消融分析考察分布式群体结构及各通路的贡献。

**相关性简评：** 其「布局—语义」分离通路与扰动/消融归因，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节与实体关系是否保留在内部状态、以及各因素的因果贡献如何拆分提供一种可借用的分析视角，需核查其指标能否迁移到模型 latent 评价。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decoding spatial and semantic information from large-scale visual cortical activity for natural-movie reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing natural visual experience from cortical population activity is a central challenge in neural decoding, particularly for calcium imaging, where signals are noisy, temporally filtered, and paired neural-video data are limited. Here we present Ca2REAL (Calcium Activity to Reconstruct Events, Appearance, and Layout), a neural-to-video framework that conditions a pretrained video diffusion model through separate layout and semantic controls derived from calcium activity. Using measured responses from 7,863 V1 neurons in one mouse, Ca2REAL reconstructed held-out natural-movie clips with 81.4% all-pairs stimulus-identification accuracy, with its clearest gains over deterministic baselines in perceptual and semantic measures. We further evaluated the framework in a stimulus-aligned virtual population of 116,705 neurons assembled from encoding-model-predicted responses across 11 independently recorded datasets. Perturbation and ablation analyses showed that reconstruction depends on distributed, stimulus-specific population structure, with layout and semantic pathways providing complementary spatial and identity information. Temporal analyses further showed improved preservation of semantic dynamics across reconstructed clips. These results show that large-scale cortical population activity constrains the spatial, semantic, and temporal structure of natural visual experience, providing a generative framework for high-fidelity neural reconstruction of dynamic visual scenes.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 6 figures, 2 tables; 11-page supplementary information included as ancillary file

</details>

### 139. [Composable Causality: A Toolkit for Systematic Time-Series Causal Discovery and Treatment-Effect Benchmarking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08918v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08918

**作者：** Muhammad Hasan Ferdous、Md Osman Gani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08918v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08918v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 DTCD，一个把拓扑、机制、噪声、观测掩码与干预策略拆为可独立组合组件的多变量时间序列数据生成库，并附带因果发现与处理效应估计的基准评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「单属性受控变化」的生成思路与成对反事实孪生（缓存同一随机实现）可能可供核查：用于构造只改变某一视觉证据或缺失机制的配对样本，从而检验 latent 内容对答案的因果贡献与视觉证据筛选的稳健性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Composable Causality: A Toolkit for Systematic Time-Series Causal Discovery and Treatment-Effect Benchmarking

**abstract/A1：** Real-world multivariate time series rarely come with verified ground-truth causal structure, so research on time-series causal discovery and treatment-effect estimation depends on synthetic data. Existing tools are either fixed datasets that cannot be modified after release or script-based generators in which each script fixes one combination of mechanism, confounding, and missingness. Neither supports controlled experiments that vary a single property, such as sparsity, noise distribution, or missingness mechanism, while holding the others fixed. Counterfactual evaluation has the same gap, since existing simulators are built per paper with hardcoded mechanisms or produce observational data only. We introduce DTCD (Datasets and Toolkit for Causal Discovery), a data generation library that produces multivariate time series with ground-truth contemporaneous and lagged adjacency matrices. Topology, mechanism, noise, observational mask, and intervention policy are independent components that compose through configuration alone. Paired counterfactual twins, produced by caching the stochastic realization during the factual rollout and re-injecting it during the interventional rollout, recover individual treatment effects to numerical precision. A reference benchmark of eight discovery algorithms on eighty-one configurations and four treatment-effect estimators on eighteen configurations exposes failure modes that aggregate metrics hide. These include the inability of standard discovery methods to handle missing values, regardless of whether the missingness mechanism is informative, and a marginal-variance shortcut (varsortability) that we measure and remove by standardization. Code and data: https://github.com/hferdous/Composable\_Causality

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 10 main text pages plus references and appendices, with figures and tables. Accepted to the NeurIPS 2026 Evaluations and Datasets Track. Code and data at https://github.com/hferdous/Composable\_Causality

</details>

<a id="paper-140"></a>

### 140. [Frame: Form-Restricted Authoring with Model-Based Evaluation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08921v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08921

**作者：** Nan Jiang、Mohammed Mohsin Hussain、Onkar Tiwari、Ankit Jain、Thong Q. Nguyen、Abhishek Agarwal、Vishal Chowdhary

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08921v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08921v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FRAME 基准，将「沿用样例报告结构、按新 grounding 文档更新内容」形式化为 form-restricted report generation，包含 120 个金融与学术实例，并用结构相似度、信息单元覆盖率和 claim 级 grounding 验证三项指标评测五个 LLM。

**相关性简评：** 其 claim 级忠实度验证与信息单元召回指标，可能可供核查 LVR 中答案是否真正使用 latent 中的视觉证据、以及任务必要细节与结构保留的评价设计；但该工作为纯文本长文档生成基准，与多模态内部状态无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Frame: Form-Restricted Authoring with Model-Based Evaluation

**abstract/A1：** Many professional reports reuse the structure of prior editions while updating the content. We formalize this setting as form-restricted report generation: given a sample report and new grounding documents, generate a report that preserves the sample's form while accurately reflecting the new evidence. We introduce FRAME, a benchmark of 120 EDGAR- and arXiv-derived instances across Financial Quarterly, Financial Annual, Academic Simple, and Academic Complex categories, built with a temporal three-snapshot pipeline. We evaluate outputs with three human-validated metrics: structural similarity via semantic tree-edit distance, coverage via information unit recall, and faithfulness via claim-level grounding verification. Across five LLMs, open-source models nearly match proprietary models on structure but lag on coverage and faithfulness; all models perform better on financial than academic reports, and performance declines with longer outputs and more complex grounding.

</details>

<a id="paper-141"></a>

### 141. [FANO: Anchored Forward-Noise Optimization for Diffusion Inverse Problems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08923v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08923

**作者：** Shilong Han、Hongxia Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08923v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08923v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究扩散模型求解图像逆问题时，退火解耦方法直接优化去噪器输入 x\_t 会导致噪声分量范数的径向漂移，提出 FANO：以前一层去噪估计为锚点，只优化分布已知的前向噪声 e，并在极坐标下用软势约束其半径。

**相关性简评：** 「对 latent 反复优化会使其范数偏离典型值、把模型推离高性能区」这一观察，可能可供核查 latent 推理中多步状态更新的分布漂移与状态范数约束；但论文面向图像重建，与 VLM 推理本身无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FANO: Anchored Forward-Noise Optimization for Diffusion Inverse Problems

**abstract/A1：** Pre-trained diffusion models have become a widely adopted zero-shot prior for image inverse problems, among which annealed decoupling methods achieve leading reconstruction performance on nonlinear tasks. We find, however, that annealed methods that directly optimize the denoiser input $x\_t$ exhibit radial drift in later layers: within-layer optimization drives the noise-component norm of $x\_t$ away from its typical value, pushing the denoiser out of its high-performance regime and degrading reconstruction quality. To address this, we propose FANO (Anchored Forward-Noise Optimization). At each annealing layer, FANO fixes the previous denoiser estimate as an anchor and optimizes only the forward noise $e$, whose distribution is known, instead of $x\_t$, thereby preserving the forward-diffusion structure of the denoiser input. The standard Gaussian prior on $e$ concentrates its norm near the typical value, and deviations degrade denoiser performance. We accordingly decompose $e$ in polar coordinates into a direction and a radial magnitude, and apply a soft potential to regulate the radius, keeping $x\_t$ in the high-density region of the forward distribution. On multiple linear and nonlinear inverse problems, FANO matches the strongest current baselines on linear tasks and achieves consistent improvements on nonlinear tasks.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 9 figures, 8 tables. Preprint, under review

</details>

<a id="paper-142"></a>

### 142. [Enforcing Symmetries on Pre-trained ML Models for Physics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08992v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08992

**作者：** Wenjie Zhou、Valentino F. Foit、David W. Hogg、Soledad Villar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.comp-ph、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08992v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08992v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究在已训练好的物理仿真模型上事后施加群对称性：对有限与紧群用群平均（Reynolds 算子）投影到不变/等变预测器，对非紧群改用 Weyl 技巧、数据依赖正交投影和 frame averaging 三种替代，并在单位缩放群与洛伦兹群上做理论与数值验证，结论是后验对称化可延伸到非紧群，但不稳定优于直接训练对称模型。

**相关性简评：** 把预测器投影到不变/等变子空间以及用轨道代表集合做聚合，可能与 LVR 中 latent 表示的对称性、几何结构和多假设聚合有概念联系，可供核查；但论文无视觉推理或 latent 证据使用实验，属较远领域的思路借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Enforcing Symmetries on Pre-trained ML Models for Physics

**abstract/A1：** Post-hoc group averaging (or test-time augmentation) is a simple way to impose symmetry on a trained predictor without changing its architecture or retraining it. For finite and compact groups, averaging over a normalized Haar measure on the group gives the Reynolds operator: a canonical projection onto invariant or equivariant predictors with known non-increasing risk guarantees under certain assumptions. This paper provides numerical experiments on physics emulation models that demonstrate the effectiveness of the approach in this setting. We then extend this framework to non-compact group symmetries. For non-compact groups, the Haar measure cannot be normalized, so group averaging cannot be defined in the same way. We replace it with three alternatives: the Weyl unitary trick (which requires complexifying the space and extending the predictor), a data-dependent orthogonal projection (which involves a non-trivial optimization on the space of invariant or equivariant functions), and a frame averaging approach (which involves mapping each point to a set of orbit representatives). We illustrate the constructions theoretically and empirically on the unit-rescaling group and the Lorentz group. Our results show that in the non-compact case, the Weyl trick and frame averaging do not provide a consistent improvement over the baseline, except where the test data is out-of-distribution (an orders-of-magnitude improvement in this case) and, for the Weyl trick, in low-data regimes. The orthogonal projection can provide improvements in-distribution and out-of-distribution. We also observe that models trained directly on invariant features matched or beat the projection of an unconstrained one. We conclude that post-hoc symmetrization extends to non-compact groups, but it does not consistently outperform training a symmetric model.

**comment/C1：** 32 pages, 5 figures, 9 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-143"></a>

### 143. [When2What: A Timing-Conditioned Framework for Long-Horizon Forecasting of Continuous Cardiovascular Waveforms](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08993v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08993

**作者：** Chaoqi Zhang、Shujie Yan、Zhao Zhang、Yanhua Sun、Yu Wang、Li Li、Lingzhong Meng、Xiaoyi Chen、Haixu Tang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08993v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08993v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 When2What 框架，把准周期心血管波形（ECG、ABP、PPG）的长程预测分解为「何时」与「何形」：先预测密集节拍时序图并锐化为离散高斯脉冲事件，再以这些事件和历史为条件生成波形。

**相关性简评：** 其「全局时序快速衰减而局部形态长期稳定」的不对称分析与显式离散事件承诺，可能为 LVR 中内容与位置/时序因素的解耦、以及中间状态提交提供可核查的概念线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When2What: A Timing-Conditioned Framework for Long-Horizon Forecasting of Continuous Cardiovascular Waveforms

**abstract/A1：** Long-horizon forecasting of quasi-periodic cardiovascular waveforms, such as the electrocardiogram (ECG), arterial blood pressure (ABP), and photoplethysmogram (PPG), requires predicting both when future cardiac events will occur (beat timing) and what the local structural pattern looks like around each event (beat morphology). However, standard sequence forecasters trained with pointwise mean squared error (MSE) average across uncertain future heartbeat phases, flattening sharp R peaks and pulse upstrokes into featureless baselines. Our analysis of intraoperative ECG reveals a clear temporal asymmetry: beat-time predictability from history decays within seconds due to stochastic heart-rate variability, whereas local beat morphology aligned at true beat times remains stable over several minutes. Guided by this insight, we introduce When2What, a timing-conditioned framework comprising three stages: Predict, Commit, and Generate. Given a 120-s history, When2What predicts a dense 60-s timing map, selects and sharpens discrete beat events into unit-height Gaussian impulses, and conditions a neural waveform generator on both these committed events and the observed history. Across intraoperative ECG, When2What substantially outperforms existing time-series and physiological forecasters, achieving a near-term waveform event skill of 0.644 and reproducing 95.62% of its predicted heartbeats in the generated waveform where direct long-horizon forecasters collapse into flat baselines. Furthermore, When2What transfers zero-shot to external hospital and atrial fibrillation cohorts, extends effectively to ABP and PPG pulse waveforms, and is supported by ablations and synthetic analyses that establish explicit timing conditioning as an effective paradigm for quasi-periodic long-horizon waveform forecasting.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages (18 pages main text, 13 pages supplementary material), 7 figures, 31 tables. Code available at https://github.com/prncoprs/when2what-release

</details>

<a id="paper-144"></a>

### 144. [Calibrating your confidence requires thinking of all the ways you could be wrong](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08997v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08997

**作者：** Marco Miani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08997v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08997v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该博士论文提出一种低开销的贝叶斯后验：协方差取与 Fisher 信息零空间对齐的投影矩阵的退化高斯，并给出后验采样与预测置信度直接计算的数值算法，借助信息几何中参数扰动在正交子空间上的分裂来解释其合理性，并在大模型上实现置信度校准与 OOD 检测的领先结果。

**相关性简评：** 其置信度校准与不确定性量化可能与 LVR 的 STOP 决策（何时停止思考或继续获取视觉证据）相关；零空间/正交子空间分裂思路或可为区分 latent 状态中的信息性与无效方向提供分析视角，但需核查是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Calibrating your confidence requires thinking of all the ways you could be wrong

**abstract/A1：** A primary limitation of modern deep learning is the miscalibration of model confidence; traditional architectures fundamentally lack a mechanism to quantify their own uncertainty. Larger models and larger datasets mitigate this issue, but they do not resolve the underlying structural deficiency. Bayesian methods offer a principled uncertainty-aware learning framework through stochastic parameters. Yet, despite the established benefits, the inherent computational overhead often compromises either scalability or quality. This trade-off currently prevents the widespread adoption of Bayesian methods. This Thesis bridges the gap between high-quality and low-complexity by introducing a novel distribution on parameter space: a degenerate Gaussian whose covariance is a projection matrix aligned with the null space of the Fisher information matrix. We demonstrate the scalability of this Bayesian posterior through two numerically efficient algorithms: one for posterior sampling and another for the direct computation of predictive confidence. Empirically, we show State-Of-The-Art confidence calibration and out-of-distribution detection for large models. We provide theoretical grounding for this approach. We study the degeneracies inherent in Amari's information geometry and highlight its most relevant component: the split of parameter's perturbations into two orthogonal subspaces. From this perspective, our posterior naturally emerges as the simplest choice that captures this split, assigning unitary variance to one subspace and zero variance to the other. Moreover, through the tools of differential geometry, we utilize this split to identify and rectify a critical misalignment issue of the non-linearized Laplace Approximation. Finally, with a focus on PAC-Bayes generalization bounds, we pave the way for a deeper understanding of the learning capabilities of overparametrized models.

**comment/C1：** PhD Thesis, Technical University of Denmark (DTU), 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-145"></a>

### 145. [Function-Space Certification for Neural Operators](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09038v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09038

**作者：** Ryan Hsiang、Robert Joseph George、Anima Anandkumar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09038v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09038v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向 neural operator 的函数空间认证框架：把 IBP 与 CROWN 推广到函数空间，借助严格数值积分逐层传播上下界函数，并扩展到高阶导数以给出 PDE 残差与解误差的界。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但其沿层传播严格上下界、并约束高阶量误差的做法，可能为界定 latent 视觉证据对输出的影响范围或验证答案是否真正依赖某类证据提供可核查的工具线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Function-Space Certification for Neural Operators

**abstract/A1：** Certifying the robustness and physical consistency of scientific machine-learning models ensures that their predictions remain reliable beyond the test set. For neural operators, which learn PDE solution operators by mapping infinite-dimensional function spaces, certification means establishing rigorous, domain-wide guarantees for their output functions. However, standard neural network (NN) verification methods are fundamentally designed for vector-to-vector mappings, and they can only certify neural operators at fixed grid resolutions and provide no guarantees over the entire domain or across another resolution. We close this gap by introducing a function-space certification framework that verifies neural operators across the entire continuous domain. We formulate interval bound propagation (IBP) and CROWN in function space, propagating upper and lower bound functions through each layer using rigorous numerical integration. In addition, we extend bound propagation to higher-order derivatives to certify PDE residuals and solution error bounds. Experiments across several neural operator architectures and scientific problems, including the 572.5-million-parameter spherical Fourier neural operator for global weather forecasting, show that our method produces tight, domain-wide guarantees efficiently, providing a foundation for trustworthy operator learning.

**comment/C1：** 45 pages, 6 figures

</details>

### 146. [LeanDojo-v2: A Comprehensive Library for AI-Assisted Theorem Proving in Lean](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09048v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09048

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LeanDojo-v2: A Comprehensive Library for AI-Assisted Theorem Proving in Lean

**abstract/A1：** Building on recent progress in machine learning for formal theorem proving in Lean, we introduce LeanDojo-v2: an end-to-end framework that unifies repository data extraction, LLM fine-tuning, proof search, AI autocompletion, and LLM-assisted theorem proving in a single software library. In addition, we provide Lean4Code, a lightweight IDE that integrates the various features of LeanDojo-v2 through a simple, mathematician-friendly UI. These tools will enable mathematicians and researchers to easily fine-tune language models on domain-specific Lean codebases and experiment with LLM-assisted theorem proving without a complex setup.

### 147. [BrainCoT: A Multi-Task Zero-Shot Brain Signal Foundation Model with Neurometric-Anchored Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09082v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09082

**作者：** Yiyu Gui、Mingzhi Chen、Guibo Luo、Yuchao Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09082v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09082v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 BrainCoT，将多类脑信号任务统一为指令驱动框架，实现无需下游适配的多任务零样本脑信号基础模型，并引入以神经度量证据为锚的思维链推理与「决策一致性理由训练」来对齐推理过程与正确预测。

**相关性简评：** 虽非视觉模态，但其「以可核验证据锚定推理链」和让中间理由与最终预测保持一致的训练约束，可能为 LVR 中视觉旁路、证据筛选与过程监督的贡献分配提供概念参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BrainCoT: A Multi-Task Zero-Shot Brain Signal Foundation Model with Neurometric-Anchored Chain-of-Thought Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Brain signals exhibit substantial multi-center heterogeneity, strong inter-subject variability, and highly diverse task settings, which makes it difficult to build models that generalize beyond a single task. While recent brain signal foundation models leverage large-scale pretraining to learn generalizable representations, they often fall short in practice because they still require target-data-specific supervised adaptation, lack multi-task zero-shot capability, and rarely provide verifiable evidence to support their predictions. In this work, we present BrainCoT, a multi-task zero-shot Brain signal foundation model with neurometric-anchored Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. BrainCoT unifies heterogeneous tasks into an instruction-driven framework so that, after pretraining, a single model can be directly applied to multiple tasks in zero-shot settings without additional adaptation. It further introduces neurometric-anchored chain-of-thought grounded in neurometric evidence for verifiable reasoning, and adopts Decision-Consistent Rationale Training to keep process learning aligned with correct predictions. Across extensive evaluations, BrainCoT achieves an average ACC of 67.41% and AUC of 74.33% in zero-shot classification without downstream training data, outperforming the strongest linear-probing baseline trained with 1% labeled downstream data by 3.85% in ACC and 5.97% in AUC.

**comment/C1：** 37 pages. Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-148"></a>

### 148. [BayesCrop: Real-Time Probabilistic Multi-Crop Video Reframing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09100v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09100

**作者：** Cheng-Han Lee、Rajesh Sureddi、Neil Birkbeck、Yilin Wang、Balu Adsumilli、Alan Bovik

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09100v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09100v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BayesCrop 实时概率多裁剪视频重构框架：用双分量空间各向同性高斯混合建模裁剪位置与尺度的偏好及局部不确定性，并仅在稀疏锚帧做神经预测、借压缩码流运动矢量传播轨迹以支持双视图与额外候选采样。

**相关性简评：** 把连续视觉状态表示为带不确定性的混合分布并可采样多假设，加上「稀疏神经计算＋廉价传播」的成本分配，可能为 LVR 的多假设 latent 状态与动态计算预算提供问题表述线索，但任务本身与多模态推理无关，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BayesCrop: Real-Time Probabilistic Multi-Crop Video Reframing

**abstract/A1：** Automatic video reframing is increasingly important for adapting landscape content to heterogeneous display formats. Existing methods typically predict a single deterministic crop, despite the subjective nature of reframing and the presence of multiple plausible regions of interest. We present \\textbf{BayesCrop}, a real-time probabilistic framework for multi-crop video reframing. BayesCrop represents crop preferences using a two-component spatially isotropic Gaussian mixture over crop position and scale. Each component defines a representative reframing mode together with its local uncertainty, enabling both dual-view prediction and sampling of additional plausible crop candidates. For efficient video processing, we introduce an anchor-and-propagate strategy that performs neural prediction only on sparse anchor frames and propagates crop trajectories using motion vectors available from the compressed video bitstream. The resulting trajectories can be combined into a single output using a lightweight periodic view-switching strategy. Experiments on LIVE-YT-MC, LIVE-YT-VC++, and a multi-target long-video benchmark demonstrate competitive single-output localization, high multi-crop reference coverage, diverse yet coherent crop hypotheses, and stable perceptual quality. BayesCrop achieves real-time throughput of 112 FPS at the default operating point, while sampling from the learned distributions further improves reference coverage beyond the two representative crop means.

**comment/C1：** This paper is accepted by WACV 2027. We plan to publicly release the source code at https://github.com/steven413d/BayesCrop

</details>

### 149. [Finite-sample Calibration Guarantees for Binary Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09120v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09120

**作者：** Bruno Viti、Martin Holler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09120v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09120v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种二值分割的事后校准方法，从集中不等式推导出显式考虑分割输出空间依赖的校准误差界，并实验验证该方法满足该保证且优于现有事后校准方法。

**相关性简评：** 空间相关性下的有限样本校准保证或可作为量化视觉证据可靠性的工具，为 LVR 中的证据筛选与停止决策提供置信度依据，但如何迁移到 latent 推理状态仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Finite-sample Calibration Guarantees for Binary Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Machine learning based segmentation models are frequently miscalibrated, producing for each pixel a label prediction whose associated confidence does not accurately reflect the true underlying probability. This limitation is particularly critical in high-stakes applications such as medical image segmentation. Although several calibration methods have been proposed, only a small subset of them provides theoretical guarantees on the calibration error, i.e., they do not typically ensure that, with high probability, the model exhibits a low calibration error. In this work, we develop a post-hoc calibration method for which formal calibration guarantees can be established. To achieve this, we derive from standard concentration inequalities an improved theoretical bound that explicitly accounts for the spatial dependence inherent in binary segmentation outputs. We empirically demonstrate that the proposed calibrator satisfies the derived guarantee and outperforms existing post-hoc calibration approaches.

**comment/C1：** Published in Transactions on Machine Learning Research 10/2026

</details>
