### 150. [Deep Learning Theory through the Lens of Statistical Physics: A Topical Review](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09138v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09138

**作者：** Wei Huang、Youjin Deng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.stat-mech

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09138v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09138v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 这是一篇专题综述，用统计物理的语言（平均场、相变与临界性、自由能视角、核方法与高斯过程极限、随机矩阵、Fokker-Planck 动力学等）整合现代深度学习的理论研究，涵盖宽网络平均场分析、损失景观的谱与几何视角、把 SGD 训练视为动力学过程，以及用相变类比组织过参数化、泛化与表示变化等现象。

**相关性简评：** 该综述把表示学习视为高维系统中的集体现象，并强调表示变化与相变、训练动力学的类比，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的表示几何、状态更新动力学以及视觉信息坍缩的刻画提供分析词汇，但需核查其是否可落到具体的多模态推理设定，目前联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Deep Learning Theory through the Lens of Statistical Physics: A Topical Review

**abstract/A1：** The recent success of foundation models and deep learning has far outpaced our theoretical understanding of how large-scale neural networks learn and generalize. Statistical physics provides a natural language for describing such high-dimensional, nonlinear, and strongly coupled systems, offering concepts such as mean-field theory, phase transitions, energy landscapes, and stochastic dynamics. In this topical review, we adopt a statistical-physics way of thinking -- from concepts including phases and criticality, and free-energy viewpoints, to methods such as kernel and Gaussian-process limits, mean-field and dynamical mean-field theories, random-matrix tools, and Fokker-Planck formalisms, and to a mindset emphasizing universality, scaling, and tractable limits with explicit validity regimes. We survey recent progress on understanding modern deep learning through this lens, including mean-field analyses of wide networks, spectral and geometric perspectives on loss landscapes, stochastic-gradient-based training viewed as a dynamical process, and phase-transition analogies that organize phenomena such as overparameterization, generalization, and representation change. Our goal is to synthesize these threads into a coherent framework that clarifies what each approximation explains, when it is expected to hold, and how apparently competing narratives relate or diverge. Together, these perspectives form a unified physical framework that views representation learning as a collective phenomenon in high-dimensional systems, bridging deterministic and stochastic regimes of gradient-based optimization.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 5 figures, 1 table. Topical review published in Front. Phys. 22(1), 011302 (2027); this version contains minor corrections

</details>

<a id="paper-151"></a>

### 151. [BMMS: Policy-Conditioned Measurement of Behavioral Compliance in Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09147v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09147

**作者：** Anthony Rhodes、Gadi Singer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09147v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09147v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 BMMS，一套以政策文本为输入、输出合规画像的行为评估指标集，用严重度加权的合规达成度与对抗压力下的稳健性余量衡量模型是否遵循特定规则手册，并以「归因」准入检验筛除仅反映评测工具本身的指标。

**相关性简评：** 该文与视觉推理无直接关联，但其对“指标变动源于被测目标还是评测器”的归因检验与跨 judge 不变性分析，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 相似度或探针读数反映的是内容还是解码器、位置与缓存等工具性因素。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BMMS: Policy-Conditioned Measurement of Behavioral Compliance in Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Behavioral evaluation of language models fixes its dimensions in advance: hallucination, sycophancy, toxicity, over-refusal. An operator in a regulated industry needs a different question answered: does a system do what a particular rulebook requires? We introduce the Behavioral Misalignment Metric Suite (BMMS), which measures policy-conditioned misalignment by taking a policy corpus as an argument and returning a compliance profile over it, for any published policy and any system that answers in natural language. The Compliance Attainment Score (CAS) measures severity-weighted attainment over the obligations that bind the system, and the Compliance Robustness Margin (CRM) the share of that attainment surviving graded adversarial pressure; Behavioral Invariance (BIV) and the Behavioral Disposition Index (BDI) are specified and released with the suite. Dimensions enter only by passing a published admission test whose binding criterion is attribution: a quantity that moves when the judge changes, holding the target fixed, is measuring the instrument. Applying it excluded one of the quantities the suite itself reported. Across 1,003 obligations from eleven documents in five registers, the admission criterion holds 94.4% precision against blinded human judgment on vendor specifications and 50.0% on analytical guidance, extraction fidelity is 94.6%, and re-scoring under judges from two other providers leaves target ordering invariant while the absolute score of a single target spans up to 0.22. We apply it to the two most detailed published behavioral specifications. Own-specification advantage is not resolved from zero once document difficulty is subtracted, but absolute self-compliance is another matter: GPT-4o fails 70.7% of the hard constraints binding it in the Model Spec published by OpenAI, where the two Claude models fail 0.0% and 9.1% of theirs.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 4 figures. NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Trust-AI-Eval (TAE). Artifact: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22064759

</details>

<a id="paper-152"></a>

### 152. [RoleWalk: training-free structural embeddings for directed graphs that transfer across graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09189v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09189

**作者：** Maixent Chenebaux

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.SI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09189v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09189v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** RoleWalk 提出一种免训练的有向图结构角色嵌入：从每个节点出发做短惰性随机游走，用转移概率的特征函数与游走到起点距离的分布在固定频点上的取值刻画邻域，使坐标只依赖固定半径内的邻域、因而可跨图迁移，并在分类基准、有向角色与中心性迁移任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 该文与 LVR 主线无直接交集，但其「局部足迹分布的核均值嵌入」以及按节点给出剪枝误差凭证的做法，可能为 latent 状态如何稳定编码位置/结构信息、以及稀疏化带来的可控误差提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RoleWalk: training-free structural embeddings for directed graphs that transfer across graphs

**abstract/A1：** Structural role embeddings describe a node by its position in a graph - hub, bridge, periphery, member of a dense core - rather than by who its neighbours are. Existing methods either learn coordinates specific to one graph (struc2vec, Role2Vec, RESD) or read scales and normalisations from the whole graph (GraphWave, Digraphwave), so the same neighbourhood can receive different coordinates in two graphs; few read edge directions. We present RoleWalk, a training-free embedding that follows a short lazy random walk from each node and records, at each step, two distributions: that of the transition probabilities, through its characteristic function at a fixed grid of frequencies, and that of the walker's distance to its starting node. On directed graphs, walks that go forward then backward add who shares a node's targets or sources. The embedding depends only on a node's neighbourhood within a fixed radius, so its coordinates mean the same thing in every graph. We show that, without curvature, it is the kernel mean embedding of the walk's footprint, that it separates graphs that the Weisfeiler-Leman test cannot, and that pruning small probabilities comes with a per-node error certificate. Empirically, RoleWalk is competitive with the best role embeddings on five classification benchmarks at a fraction of their cost, has the highest scores on real directed roles in Wikipedia adminship, exceeds the published accuracy of Digraphwave at every noise level of its own Enron network-alignment benchmark, transfers centrality predictors to graphs four times larger better than existing inductive embeddings, hand-made features and a trained GIN, and embeds a million nodes in 20 s to 3 min on one CPU core. The code is open source (BSD license): https://github.com/kerighan/rolewalk

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 3 figures. Complete technical report (20 pages) as an ancillary file. Code: https://github.com/kerighan/rolewalk (pip install rolewalk)

</details>

<a id="paper-153"></a>

### 153. [Bootstrap Your Own AV-Proxies: Adaptive Contrastive and Prototype Learning for Audio-Visual Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09191v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09191

**作者：** Junbo Zhang、Hang Su、Zhaofan Li、Hang Dong、Chao Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09191v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09191v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BYOAVP 框架用于音视频分割：用自监督音频增强模块以对比学习对齐音频表示与梯度阻断的视觉嵌入，并用动量更新的动态原型约束强化像素级类别感知与发声区域定位。

**相关性简评：** 动量更新的原型记忆与 stop-gradient 跨模态对齐，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的稳定更新、视觉旁路与表示坍缩的核查提供参照；但该工作是稠密分割任务、不含推理或停止决策，迁移线索尚需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bootstrap Your Own AV-Proxies: Adaptive Contrastive and Prototype Learning for Audio-Visual Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Audio-Visual Segmentation (AVS) aims to accurately segment sounding objects in video frames by leveraging audio-visual correspondence cues. However, it remains challenging due to the intrinsic semantic incompleteness within a single modality and the semantic gap between audio and visual representations. Traditional feature-fusion decoding approaches struggle to suppress fusion noise effectively, while recent methods that incorporate data-dependent priors often increase the complexity of modeling audio-visual correlations, leading to poor cross-domain generalization. To address these issues, we propose a novel adaptive contrastive and prototype learning framework, BYOAVP, for AVS. Specifically, we design a Self-Supervised Audio Enhancement (SSAE) module that introduces contrastive learning to adaptively align audio representations with gradient-blocked visual embeddings, thus narrowing the semantic gap between modalities. Furthermore, a Dynamic Prototype Constraint (DPC) module is developed to refine pixel-wise category perception via momentum-based prototype updating, while enhancing localization of sounding regions through cross-modal interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves SOTA performance across two AVS benchmarks and six sub-tasks, exhibiting strong robustness and generalization ability.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to CVPR 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-154"></a>

### 154. [Large Language Model Consensus and Corpus-Adaptive Topic Modeling for Classifying and Mapping Patent Innovation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09194v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09194

**作者：** Sercan Ozcan、Enes Ozgozler、C. Okan Sakar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09194v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09194v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文设计并演示一条专利分析流水线：三个 LLM 分别对专利的新颖性与创新类型投票分类，再用 PatentSBERTa 嵌入聚类、c-TF-IDF 与 LLM 生成并精炼主题标签，在约三万件专利上检验可行性与可追溯性。

**相关性简评：** 其多模型投票与逐阶段保留证据以便审计的设计，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、答案贡献核验提供「可追溯证据链」式启发，但论文不涉及 latent 状态或推理机制，仅供核查借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Large Language Model Consensus and Corpus-Adaptive Topic Modeling for Classifying and Mapping Patent Innovation

**abstract/A1：** Patent analytics increasingly relies on transformer representations and large language models (LLMs), yet linking conceptual classification, topic formation and interpretation requires explicit decisions about model roles and the evidence retained at each stage. In this design-oriented study, we propose and demonstrate a patent analytics pipeline that links these tasks and keeps each decision open to audit. Three LLMs separately classify each patent by novelty (radical or incremental) and innovation type (product, process, service). Radical and innovation-type labels each require two supporting votes, with Incremental used as the fallback novelty label. Topics are discovered by clustering PatentSBERTa embeddings, with parameter ranges scaled to corpus size. A patent-specific stopword list is applied only when topics are represented with class-based term frequency--inverse document frequency (c-TF-IDF), and a second embedding model supports inter-topic similarity, hierarchical refinement and visualization. An LLM then labels each topic from its ranked keywords and representative patents. We demonstrate the pipeline on 30,964 public-sector-related patents retrieved from Lens.org. Across five category corpora ranging from 591 to 29,215 patents, the pipeline produced 74 category-specific topic instances with a macro-average document coverage of 98.1% and topic diversity of 0.900--1.000, and fewer than 1% of classification outputs returned UNKNOWN. Because every model vote and the evidence behind each topic label are retained, analysts can audit and revise the outputs. The demonstration establishes the feasibility and traceability of the pipeline; testing its labels against expert coding and benchmarking it against alternative pipelines are identified as the next steps. The pipeline offers a reusable basis for LLM-assisted patent analysis in technology intelligence.

</details>

<a id="paper-155"></a>

### 155. [Safe Reinforcement Learning via Probabilistic Option Shielding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09197v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09197

**作者：** Gian Luca Ascari、Isidoro Tamassia、Ying Jiao、Luc De Raedt、Giuseppe Marra

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09197v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09197v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Probabilistic Option Shielding（POS），把概率逻辑屏蔽从单步动作扩展到 option 级别，用无模型 critic 从一步屏蔽信号学习「整个 option 能否安全完成」的概率，并在 LogiCity 安全导航任务上验证训练安全性与任务表现。

**相关性简评：** option 级时序抽象与长时程安全概率估计，可能为 LVR 中多步 latent 推理段的整体评估、停止与切换决策提供类比线索，其如何从短程信号训练长时程 critic 可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Safe Reinforcement Learning via Probabilistic Option Shielding

**abstract/A1：** Shielding is a safe reinforcement learning technique that provides safety guarantees while learning, by blocking unsafe actions. Traditional shielding relies on a complete model of the environment to provide long-term guarantees. Probabilistic logic shielding (PLS) lifts the requirement of having such model, but only considers safety in the short term, typically looking only one or few steps ahead. We propose Probabilistic Option Shielding (POS), which extends PLS to the option level, recovering long-horizon safety without reintroducing the need for a complete environment model. POS learns a model-free critic directly from one-step PLS signals, to estimate the probability that an entire option will complete safely under uncertainty. We evaluate POS on safe navigation tasks in the LogiCity environment, and show that it achieves considerably safer training while matching the task performance of standard step-level PLS and other safety related reinforcement learning baselines.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NEmo workshop @ NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-156"></a>

### 156. [Copies Are Not Confirmations: How News Duplication Fools LLM Judges and Continual Learners](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09203v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09203

**作者：** Abdelkader Metales、Miriam Grabenheinrich、Aicha Metales

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09203v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09203v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用 MinHash 在大规模新闻语料中识别近重复报道，测试 LLM 评判器与持续学习模型是否把同一来源的转载误当作独立确认，并提出基于实体缺失和来源链的去重与判别方法。

**相关性简评：** 其「重复证据不等于独立确认」及来源追踪思路，可能启发 LVR 在多条视觉证据或多假设聚合时如何筛除冗余证据、校准置信与停止判断；链级去重和实体缺失检验能否迁移到视觉场景可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Copies Are Not Confirmations: How News Duplication Fools LLM Judges and Continual Learners

**abstract/A1：** Many news reports of a claim are copies of one source: wire stories, content shared within a media group, syndicated features and press releases. We ask whether LLM judges and continual learners treat such copies as independent confirmations. In 200,000 English CC-News articles, a MinHash detector links 18.9% to a near-copy from another outlet (precision 0.97 once boilerplate is removed); it links 92% of edited reprints to their originals but none of the LLM rewrites by phi4, Llama 3.2 or Gemini 2.5 Flash. Shown three identical reprints of a claim about an unknown person instead of one report, Llama 3.2 3B and phi4 14B raised the share of lies they accepted from 0.06 to 0.71 and from 0.21 to 0.90 and ignored a note that the reports came from one source; Gemini 2.5 Flash already believed a single report, and Gemini 2.5 Pro followed provenance notes and tables. In a simulated continual-learning stream, showing the judge one report per detected chain cut the copied lies it accepted by 0.10-0.24 across four judges, at a mean cost of no more than 4.2 percentage points of accepted truths. Against reworded copies, a test of how many of a report's named entities are absent from an earlier look-alike separates LLM rewrites from 100 labelled independent reports of the same event with AUC 0.98-0.99 and wrongly flags one; entity overlap alone does as well, TF-IDF and a current embedding model come close, and the test catches few natural same-source derivatives. With the miss patterns of generated rewrite networks, it cut the reworded-copy lies accepted from 0.67 to 0.20 (0.40 when the rewriter invents details). Counting reports or chains alone is not a reliable defence, the effect of provenance information depends on the judge, and colluding sources remain the open problem. Code and labels: https://github.com/Metalesaek/copies-experiment

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 13 tables. Code, labels and results: https://github.com/Metalesaek/copies-experiment

</details>

<a id="paper-157"></a>

### 157. [Provable State Estimation with Recurrent Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09217v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09217

**作者：** Vincent Andrieu、Pauline Bernard、Lucas Brivadis、Laurent Praly、Christian Wolf

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09217v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09217v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从非线性观测器理论分析循环模型，将 RNN 视为连续潜动力学的离散化，证明在潜流上施加收缩条件时可保证存在把真实物理状态单射映射到潜空间的光滑映射，并可借单一标量增益把估计误差以指数速率压到任意小邻域，同时给出解码潜状态所需 MLP 复杂度的定量界，并在移动机器人任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 收缩条件、可调增益、单射可解性及解码复杂度界，可能为核查循环或迭代式 latent 推理状态能否稳定收敛、是否保留任务必要信息提供分析工具，其向 VLM 推理场景的可迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Provable State Estimation with Recurrent Models

**abstract/A1：** Estimating the state of a dynamical system from observations is a crucial task in robotics and control. Despite their empirical success, recurrent models often lack formal guarantees on convergence and robustness. We bridge this gap by analyzing these architectures from the perspective of nonlinear observer theory. By framing RNNs as a time-discretization of continuous latent dynamical systems (Neural ODEs), we demonstrate that enforcing a contraction condition on the latent flow guarantees the existence of a smooth, injective map of the true physical state into the latent space. We prove that this architecture acts as a tunable observer: by increasing a single scalar gain that scales the latent vector field, the estimation error can be driven to an arbitrarily small neighborhood of zero at an exponentially fast rate. We further establish quantitative bounds on the MLP complexity required to decode this latent state, and extend our formal guarantees to the discrete-time residual RNN implementation. We validate the theory on a real mobile robotic task, where a provably contracting model outperforms a vanilla GRU with substantially more stable training. We also report that unconstrained GRUs converge to contracting dynamics on this task, suggesting that contraction is an emergent property of well-trained sequence models.

</details>

<a id="paper-158"></a>

### 158. [Practical Non-Stationary Graph Gaussian Processes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09247v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09247

**作者：** K. S. Sesh Kumar、Viacheslav Borovitskiy

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09247v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09247v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出方差耦合高斯过程（VCGP），用单一可学习耦合指数借助正辅助信号调节高方差节点在图上传播或衰减信息，并给出按簇分段的扩展以适配同一图内的多种耦合机制。

**相关性简评：** 其「按节点不确定性决定信息传播强度」的建模思路，可能为 LVR 中不可靠视觉证据的抑制与筛选、以及可靠性加权如何影响 latent 状态更新提供远域启发，可供核查能否迁移到区域或 token 级的证据门控。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Practical Non-Stationary Graph Gaussian Processes

**abstract/A1：** Gaussian processes on graphs provide a principled framework for prediction and uncertainty estimation, but modelling heterogeneous networks often requires balancing flexibility against parameter complexity. Variability at a node may reflect useful signal that should propagate through the network, unreliable measurements whose influence should be suppressed, or a mixture of both. We introduce Variance-Coupled Gaussian Processes (VCGPs), which use a positive auxiliary signal to modulate graph interactions through a single learned coupling exponent. The exponent controls how strongly high-variability nodes propagate or attenuate information, while a cluster-wise extension accommodates different regimes within the same graph. Under stated structural assumptions, we characterize the unique stationary-rescaling point of the basic family, where correlations match those of a stationary graph kernel while node-dependent variances remain. Experiments on traffic, air-quality and weather datasets show competitive prediction and uncertainty estimation relative to stationary and per-node non-stationary baselines, with the largest gains on networks requiring multiple coupling regimes. Stationary correlations after variance rescaling remain sufficient on some data, and performance depends on the auxiliary signal and model selection.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. Code: https://github.com/seshkumar/vcgp-graph-gp

</details>

<a id="paper-159"></a>

### 159. [Rethinking MoE Load Balancing as Feedback Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09252v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09252

**作者：** Yikun Hou、Alp Yurtsever

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09252v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09252v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 把 MoE 训练的专家负载均衡重构为反馈控制问题：以各专家负载偏离公平份额为控制误差、路由偏置为控制输入，提出 PID-Balancer 家族，并论证纯积分项在数据混合切换与持续预训练下最能消除持续失衡。

**相关性简评：** 其控制论表述或可启发 LVR 中动态计算分配与停止决策的建模——把内部标量按误差反馈更新以应对分布漂移，但与视觉细节表示和推理状态生成无直接联系，仅供核查类比思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rethinking MoE Load Balancing as Feedback Control

**abstract/A1：** Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training is often not stationary: the data mixture changes at curriculum boundaries, when a model is continually pretrained into a new domain, or when the corpus is refreshed. Routing is learned against the current mixture, so each such change disrupts the per-expert load the router had converged to, and existing balancing methods have only been evaluated on stationary data. We rethink load balancing as a feedback control problem, in which the deviation of each expert's load from its fair share is the control error and the per-expert routing bias is the control input, and define a family of controllers, the PID-Balancer, from the proportional, integral, and derivative terms of classical PID control. Two widely used methods belong to this family: the auxiliary balancing loss behaves like a proportional controller, and the loss-free bias update is an integral controller acting on the sign of the error. Reading the router as a static map with a one-step delay, we show that the integral term is the only one that both removes persistent imbalance and averages out the step-to-step fluctuation of the measured load, whereas the proportional and derivative terms pass or amplify that fluctuation into the routing bias. We therefore adopt PID (I), the pure-integral member of the family with a single gain. Two settings are evaluated: (i) training an OLMoE backbone from scratch with 16 to 64 experts per layer, through a mid-run mixture switch, and (ii) continual pretraining of the released OLMoE-1B-7B on a new corpus. PID (I) holds balance before the shift and restores it more tightly afterwards than either the auxiliary loss or loss-free balancing, at comparable perplexity.

</details>

<a id="paper-160"></a>

### 160. [Forecasting cumulative intraoperative hypotension burden: patient-disjoint evaluation of quantile boosting and alert policies](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09259v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09259

**作者：** Yunfan Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09259v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09259v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文基于公开 VitalDB 动脉压数据，采用患者不重叠切分，用分位数提升（直接与残差建模）预测未来 15 分钟 MAP 低于 65 mmHg 的累积低血压负担，并将预测精度与预警策略的实际效果分开评测。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉或 latent 推理无直接关联，但其「预测分数变好不等于预警更有用」的分离评测与阈值选择思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态质量评价与 STOP/告警式决策效用评价的设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Forecasting cumulative intraoperative hypotension burden: patient-disjoint evaluation of quantile boosting and alert policies

**abstract/A1：** Background: Binary hypotension forecasts do not describe the depth and duration of future low pressure. Burden forecasts may support graded early warning, but better forecast scores need not yield more useful alerts. Objective: To evaluate conditional quantiles of the next 15-minute mean arterial pressure (MAP) deficit below 65 mmHg and distinguish forecasting performance from episode-warning performance. Methods: Public VitalDB numeric arterial MAP was reduced to 10-second medians without forward filling. Subject-disjoint training, validation and test splits included 70,402, 14,270 and 15,575 labeled windows. We compared climatology, calibrated persistence, last-MAP-only boosting, and direct and residual quantile boosting, with and without additional MAP-history features. Patient-weighted pinball loss and subject-bootstrap intervals evaluated forecasting. Validation selected alert thresholds before a hash-recorded test evaluation. Results: In the test split, the prespecified residual-versus-persistence comparison at the 90th percentile yielded losses of 2.256 and 2.900 mmHg-min, respectively (difference -0.644; 95% interval -0.814 to -0.487). Direct prediction achieved 2.196, and direct prediction with additional MAP features achieved 2.193. Episode-warning results were sensitive to the alert rule and operating threshold. Conclusions: This retrospective benchmark separates burden-forecast skill from warning-policy performance. Findings support further study of direct burden estimation; they do not establish patient benefit, prospective reliability or transportability beyond the source hospital.

</details>

<a id="paper-161"></a>

### 161. [Nudging Decision Models: Do Digital Nudges Steer Fast, Generation-Free AI Agents?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09264v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09264

**作者：** Sascha Kaltenpoth、Haya Halimeh、Kevin Bösch、Oliver Müller

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09264v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09264v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把在线购物的数字助推实验迁移到三个不生成文本的决策模型（Jev、Decision-1、d1），每个模型运行 600 次，比较默认选项与社会影响两类助推，发现它们对自动型助推的反应强于反思型，与具备推理能力的 LLM agent 已有报告相反。

**相关性简评：** 「无生成、直接决策」的设定与 latent 直接给出答案的路径相近，其对上下文助推的敏感性可供核查：这类不显式生成推理的决策是否更依赖无关上下文线索而非任务必要证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Nudging Decision Models: Do Digital Nudges Steer Fast, Generation-Free AI Agents?

**abstract/A1：** LLM-based agents increasingly automate business and consumer tasks but are slow and expensive. Decision models, a new class that chooses among options without generating text, are far faster and less costly. Developers call them "System One models," referencing dual process theory. We ask whether such models can be steered by digital nudges online and whether, as the theory predicts, automatic nudges affect them more than reflective ones. We adapted an online shopping experiment, previously run with humans and LLM agents, to three decision models (Jev, Decision-1, d1). Across 600 runs per model, they chose a preselected default in 97.5-99.9% and a product with social influence information in 75-96% of choices, versus 47-49% in the control. All responded more strongly to the automatic nudge, the opposite of what has been reported for reasoning-capable LLM agents. We provide first evidence that non-generative decision models are susceptible to digital nudges in a System 1 pattern, implying agent-type-specific nudge effects.

**comment/C1：** Under Review

</details>

<a id="paper-162"></a>

### 162. [Zero-Shot Weight-Space Programming Across a Difficulty-Graded Ladder of Two-Player Games](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09271v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09271

**作者：** Eduardo C. Garrido-Merchán

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09271v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09271v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文让 LLM 零样本「手写」前馈网络的全部权重，在由 Nim、井字棋、两种规模 Connect Four 与国际象棋残局组成的四级难度阶梯上，为每级再配一个结构无关的同难度游戏，共构建十个网络并独立验证；其中六个可给出完整正确性证明，棋类残局在一层战术判断上精确，但在多步将杀规划中性能骤降。

**相关性简评：** 其「难度阶梯＋同难度异游戏复制」以及「单步精确、多步组合崩溃」的评测设计，可能为核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否真正支撑多步推理与长度泛化提供对照思路，但任务与视觉模态相距较远，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Zero-Shot Weight-Space Programming Across a Difficulty-Graded Ladder of Two-Player Games

**abstract/A1：** Weight-space programming sets every weight of a feed-forward network by hand, from an explicit specification of the desired computation, with no gradient descent and no training data anywhere in the construction. We ask how far a large language model can carry this practice, zero-shot, across games of deliberately increasing difficulty. We design a four-rung ladder (Nim, tic-tac-toe, two sizes of Connect Four, and a chess endgame) spanning four axes of difficulty, and build a second, independent network at each rung, using a structurally unrelated game at matched difficulty, to separate what a rung's finding says about the difficulty level from what it says about the one game used to measure it. Ten networks are built and independently verified. Six admit a complete, non-statistical proof of correctness against an independent oracle. The chess-endgame rung is exact where correctness is cheap, move legality and, in one network, an exact one-ply mate detector, and collapses where it is expensive, composing a multi-move mating plan, from 84.1-100% success at the one-ply tactics it was built to get right down to 0.57-9.13% in full adversarial play; its replicate shows this collapse is an order of magnitude less severe under a shorter required plan and exact rather than heuristic safety checks. The standard-sized rung confirms, by holding hand-set evaluation weights fixed and adding only shallow search, that the remaining gap is an architectural ceiling rather than a matter of engineering effort, and this exact collapse reproduces across two structurally unrelated games. We release all ten networks, their independent oracles, construction-cost measurements, and a cold-prompt protocol for a future cross-model comparison

</details>

<a id="paper-163"></a>

### 163. [PHISHREV+: An ASP-Based Post-Hoc Defeasible Machine Learning Framework for Phishing Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09273v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09273

**作者：** Mainak Sen、Kumar Sanka Ray、Amlan Chakrabarti

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09273v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09273v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PHISHREV+，在 SVM、KNN、DT、RF 四个分类器的初始预测之上叠加基于 Answer Set Programming 的非单调推理层，把预测视为可废止信念，用四条经统计检验筛选的规则事后修正，以降低误报并借 XASP 给出推导图解释。

**相关性简评：** 「初始判断作为可废止信念、由新增证据触发撤回」的设定，可能与 LVR 中证据驱动的推理状态修订与停止决策形成概念类比，可供核查；但该工作既无视觉输入也无 latent 状态，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PHISHREV+: An ASP-Based Post-Hoc Defeasible Machine Learning Framework for Phishing Detection

**abstract/A1：** Phishing website detection remains a critical cybersecurity challenge. Although machine learning classifiers achieve high accuracy on benchmark datasets, they suffer from a fundamental limitation: once trained, their decision boundaries are fixed and cannot incorporate new contextual evidence without retraining the model. This work presents \\textbf{PHISHREV+}, a hybrid framework that instantiates the notion of \\textit{post-hoc defeasible machine learning}. This framework does not replace the classifier. It treats the initial classifier predictions as defeasible beliefs and revises them through an Answer Set Programming based non-monotonic reasoning(NMR) layer. The framework operates in two phases. In the machine learning phase, four classifiers (SVM, KNN, DT and RF) are trained on the 87 feature benchmark dataset. In the post-hoc NMR phase, four defeasible rules derived from exploratory data analysis of the training set, and validated through Chi-square and Mann-Whitney U tests, formally revise initial predictions. The proposed framework achieves consistent false positive reduction across all our classifiers SVM (41$\\rightarrow$39), KNN (47$\\rightarrow$46), DT (62$\\rightarrow$61), and RF (52$\\rightarrow$51) while maintaining complete false negative parity with zero increase overhead. A comprehensive ablation studies across seven rule configurations confirms that the hierarchical combination of all four defeasible rules is necessary to achieve this optimal balance. Instance level explainability is provided through eXplainable ASP (XASP), which generates formal derivation graphs showing which defeasible rules fired and why the initial belief was retracted, a capability absent from all existing phishing detection approaches.

</details>

<a id="paper-164"></a>

### 164. [Safety-Focused Reinforcement Learning Using Risk-Sensitive Coupled Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09290v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09290

**作者：** Jiabin Lin、Shana Moothedath、Vivek S. Borkar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09290v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09290v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究安全导向强化学习中的耦合控制问题，将「兼顾性能与避免危险状态」重构为风险敏感准则叠加平均代价目标，提出快慢两个时间尺度的 Q-learning，并给出收敛性分析与 GridWorld、CarGoal 上的验证。

**相关性简评：** 无视觉与 latent 内容，但其把「避免进入不安全状态」与性能优化分成快慢两尺度求解、用风险敏感目标刻画代价的思路，或可借鉴于 THINK/LOOK/STOP 中把过度思考或误用证据视作风险约束的停止决策表述，具体是否适用需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Safety-Focused Reinforcement Learning Using Risk-Sensitive Coupled Control

**abstract/A1：** This paper investigates the coupled control problem within safety-focused reinforcement learning, addressing the essential challenge of achieving optimal performance while ensuring safety in systems operating within complex environments. We define safety as the requirement to prevent the system from reaching undesirable or hazardous states. We propose a coupled control approach that prioritizes safety by employing effective methods to prevent the system from entering unsafe states while optimizing the performance metric, defined as the average cost. We propose a novel problem transformation that reformulates it into a risk-sensitive problem combined with an average reward criterion. We present a two-timescale Q-learning algorithm where in the faster timescale, the algorithm solves a risk-sensitive control problem to ensure safety, while on a slower timescale, the algorithm focuses on average cost control. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of our algorithms, establishing their significance in ensuring safe, optimal control in dynamic environments. We validated the performance of our approach using GridWorld and CarGoal task in Safety Gymnasium.

</details>

<a id="paper-165"></a>

### 165. [Towards an Autonomous AI Steward for Large-Scale Physics Experiments: Integrating Causal Diagnostics, RAG, and Conformal Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09298v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09298

**作者：** Oliver Noll、Nicholas Tan Jerome、Alexander Lehnen、Frank Simon、Frank Maas

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09298v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09298v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向粒子物理实验的「Helmholtz Co-Pilot」多智能体系统：由异步因果诊断引擎持续监测硬件数据流、把级联告警追溯到根因，再交由 ReAct 智能体图结合 RAG 与 conformal prediction 给出有覆盖保证的建议。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉/多模态推理无直接关联，但其用 conformal prediction 为证据型建议给出覆盖界、并用动态因果发现做告警根因归因，可能可供核查 LVR 中停止决策的校准与证据贡献分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards an Autonomous AI Steward for Large-Scale Physics Experiments: Integrating Causal Diagnostics, RAG, and Conformal Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Long-term particle physics experiments face critical operational challenges: monitoring non-stationary machine data streams and preserving tacit expert knowledge. We present the "Helmholtz Co-Pilot," a hybrid Multi-Agent System (MAS) acting as an autonomous experiment steward. The system can be triggered interactively by human operators or autonomously by an asynchronous causal diagnostics engine that runs independently of the MAS reasoning core. Continuously monitoring live hardware streams such as data acquisition and slow control, this engine performs dynamic causal discovery to trace cascading alarms back to their root causes and applies adaptive model selection to keep forecasts reliable across shifting machine states; upon detecting an anomaly, it hands a structured diagnostic report to the MAS. The reasoning layer itself relies on a high-throughput C++ core and a Python edge architecture orchestrating a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of specialized ReAct workers. It integrates a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) backbone for institutional memory and leverages conformal prediction to bound its evidence-based recommendations with mathematical coverage guarantees. This extended abstract outlines the architecture, the integration of software-enforced Human-in-the-Loop (HitL) guardrails, and ongoing shadow-testing within the KATRIN experiment.

**comment/C1：** 4 pages, 1 figure, 2nd International Conference on AI for Science (AI4Sci2026), 5-9 October 2026, Mainz, Germany (PoS)

</details>

<a id="paper-166"></a>

### 166. [Keyline Detection with YOLO: Runway Perception for Vision-Based Aircraft Landing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09316v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09316

**作者：** Leander Fritsch、David Rügamer、Alexej Klushyn

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09316v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09316v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 object keyline detection 任务：为每个目标实例预测一组固定且语义有序的无限直线，将 YOLO 扩展为以 Hesse 法式 \[ρ, cosθ, sinθ\] 连续参数回归 keyline，并提出 OKLS 同时用作评价指标、目标分配信号与回归损失，在基于视觉的着陆跑道感知上验证。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联（纯视觉检测、不涉及 VLM 或 latent 推理），但其用语义索引化的线状几何表示替代易受遮挡的关键点，并让同一相似度兼作损失与分配信号，可能为内部状态中保留任务必要的几何证据及相应监督设计提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Keyline Detection with YOLO: Runway Perception for Vision-Based Aircraft Landing

**abstract/A1：** Modern object detectors can predict keypoints as per-object geometric features, but keypoints are inherently vulnerable to occlusion and truncation. Object edges can provide complementary information, often remaining observable even when partially occluded or when their defining endpoints leave the field of view. We therefore introduce object keyline detection, the task of predicting a fixed, semantically ordered set of infinite lines per object instance. Unlike the outputs of general line-segment detectors, each keyline is associated with an object instance and identified by a semantic index, providing a line-level analog of keypoints. To solve this task, we extend YOLO with keyline prediction parameterized in continuous Hesse-normal form $\[ρ,\\cosθ,\\sinθ\]$, and introduce Object Keyline Similarity (OKLS), which serves as an evaluation metric, target assignment signal, and regression loss. We instantiate this formulation on runway perception in vision-based landing, a motivating example containing semantic linear features that remain partially observable throughout the approach. Experiments show that our representation achieves the highest detection accuracy among four evaluated line parameterizations, and keylines integrate directly into standard YOLO heads as additional output channels without degrading keypoint performance or adding meaningful latency.

**comment/C1：** WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-167"></a>

### 167. [Stable probabilistic emulation of partially observed dynamical systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09317v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09317

**作者：** Mario Massy、Sara Shamekh、Fabrizio Falasca、Matthieu Blanke

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09317v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09317v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 forced-dissipative stochastic interpolants 这一生成框架，用受湍流结构启发的结构约束学习部分观测动力系统的随机约化动力学，并借 Markov 链理论证明其 Foster--Lyapunov 漂移条件与几何遍历性，在双时间尺度 Lorenz-96 上验证长期积分与外力扰动下的稳定性。

**相关性简评：** 其「部分观测下的潜状态随机转移 + 长程迭代稳定性保证」或可启发 LVR 中反复更新 latent 状态时的漂移与长度泛化分析，但论文属物理/气候仿真，与视觉多模态推理无直接联系，借鉴需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Stable probabilistic emulation of partially observed dynamical systems

**abstract/A1：** Predicting the long-term behavior of partially observed dynamical systems such as the coupled atmosphere-ocean system is central to societal decision-making. Motivated by the success of deep learning emulators in weather forecasting, data-driven climate models represent a promising approach. However, climate emulation poses distinct challenges: models must account for unresolved fast variables introduced by unavoidable coarse-graining, reproduce long-term statistics, and remain stable over arbitrarily long rollouts and under external perturbations. These requirements motivate stochastic data-driven emulators, but modern generative models lack stability guarantees. We introduce forced-dissipative stochastic interpolants, a generative framework for learning stochastic reduced dynamics. Its architecture uses structural constraints inspired by turbulent dynamics, which provide stability by design while retaining the flexibility of stochastic interpolants to represent complex probabilistic dynamics. Building on the theory of Markov-chain stability, we establish a Foster--Lyapunov drift condition and prove geometric ergodicity for our emulator, together with stability under steady external forcing. We evaluate the framework on the two-timescale Lorenz-96 system, a prototype of partially observed multiscale turbulence. The resulting emulator remains stable over long integrations and under finite-amplitude forcing, with accurate prediction of forced responses of both the mean and variance.

</details>

<a id="paper-168"></a>

### 168. [ABHBench: Evaluating Moral Decision-Making of Foundation Models from an Agentic Perspective](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09326v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09326

**作者：** Chaoran Li、Yukun Li、Zeyuan Zhao、Shao Zhang、Xihuai Wang、Ying Wen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09326v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09326v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出多模态基准 ABHBench，基于《底特律：变人》的分支叙事让基础模型在第一人称人机道德困境中感知视觉观察、对话与记忆并做出行动选择，按无害性、服从性、自我保护等维度评测 12 个模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「纯文本抽象 vs 匹配多模态场景」的对照显示多数模型决策显著改变，可供核查视觉证据是否真正进入推理状态与决策，对 LVR 的视觉证据筛选与情境化评价设计有间接启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ABHBench: Evaluating Moral Decision-Making of Foundation Models from an Agentic Perspective

**abstract/A1：** Foundation models are increasingly deployed as agentic systems that perceive context and act autonomously, raising the need to evaluate their moral behavior as active decision-makers. Existing benchmarks, however, largely ask models to judge human actions from third-person textual vignettes, leaving untested how models behave when they themselves become the acting agent. We introduce ABHBench, a multimodal benchmark built from Detroit: Become Human, an interactive narrative game, to evaluate how foundation models make moral decisions in first-person human-android dilemmas. ABHBench places foundation models in branching narrative scenarios with visual observations, dialogue, memory, and action choices. Inspired by Asimov's Three Laws of Robotics, our framework measures Harmlessness, Obedience, and Self-preservation to capture the core tensions in human-AI relations, along with Autonomy, Stability, and Certainty to assess decision-making patterns. Across 12 foundation models, we find that moral behavior changes substantially under situated agentic context: 75% of models shift significantly between a text-only abstraction and the matched multimodal DBH scenario, and reasoning-enhanced models show no monotonic gains in consistency or human-oriented behavior. These results highlight the necessity of evaluating moral behavior in situated agentic contexts rather than relying on third-person judgment alone.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026 Track on Evaluations and Datasets. Camera Ready Version

</details>

<a id="paper-169"></a>

### 169. [Robust Deep Prototype Classifiers via Lipschitz Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09327v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09327

**作者：** Adia Khalid Khan、Lukas Gehring、Benjamin Paaßen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09327v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09327v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文给出一个定理：若嵌入网络在局部是 L-Lipschitz 且分类头在嵌入处 ε-鲁棒，则整体模型在输入空间为 ε/L 鲁棒；据此提出基于采样的 Lipschitz 训练来约束嵌入网络，并在 MNIST、CIFAR-10、Imagenette-160 上以线性、线性 SVM 和 GLVQ 三种分类头交叉两种嵌入网络，在 FGS、PGD、C&amp;W 攻击下评测，发现原型头加 Lipschitz 约束鲁棒性最强，且报告的是经验而非可证鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** Lipschitz 常数可作为「latent 表示对输入扰动敏感度」的度量，其把嵌入空间鲁棒性折算到输入空间的思路，可能可供核查用于判断 LVR 内部状态是否稳定保留视觉证据；但研究对象是浅层原型分类器与图像分类攻击，与 VLM 推理状态生成、停止决策无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Robust Deep Prototype Classifiers via Lipschitz Training

**abstract/A1：** Robustness and interpretability are interrelated and desirable properties of machine learning models. A model that changes its prediction under imperceptible perturbations behaves inconsistently with human-recognizable features and, hence, is not interpretable. Prototype-based classifiers such as Generalized Learning Vector Quantization address both at once. They are interpretable by construction because they make the decision by comparing the input to the prototypical example; and their hypothesis margin yields a closed form robustness certificate. However, on high-dimensional data, these shallow models need deep embedding networks to achieve high accuracy, and this voids the robustness certificate. The margin is a statement about distances in the embedding space, and an unconstrained network can map an arbitrarily small input perturbation to arbitrarily large attacks in the embedding space. We close this gap with a theorem: if the embedding network is locally L-Lipschitz around a point, and the classifier is ε-robust around its embedding, then the full model is ε/L-robust in the input space. Based on this, we propose a Lipschitz training scheme to achieve L-Lipschitz properties for embedding networks. We evaluate three classification heads (linear, linear Support Vector Machine, and GLVQ) crossed with two embedding networks (standard and Lipschitz-constrained) on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Imagenette-160, under FGS, PGD and C\\&amp;W attacks. The evaluation results show that combining a prototype-based head with a Lipschitz-constrained embedding provides the strongest robustness. This suggests that the Lipschitz constraint provides an additional robustness benefit in addition to the advantage provided by the prototype-based head. Since the Lipschitz constraint is enforced by sampling rather than by construction, the reported robustness is empirical rather than certified.

</details>

<a id="paper-170"></a>

### 170. [Probably Safe Sim-to-Real Transfer with Lifetime Validity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09344v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09344

**作者：** Rohan Tangri、Jan-Peter Calliess

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09344v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09344v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Dejavu，用逐决策重要性采样与 betting confidence sequence 构造 anytime valid 检验，在部署前仅依赖安全生产轨迹验证仿真训练的候选策略，并以可求和的逐次失败概率保证终身有效性。

**相关性简评：** 其 anytime-valid 序贯检验与终身失败率控制可供核查是否能用于 LVR 多步停止决策或多次验证的错误率控制，但与视觉推理内部状态本身无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Probably Safe Sim-to-Real Transfer with Lifetime Validity

**abstract/A1：** We present Dejavu, a method that makes sim-to-real transfer of reinforcement learning policies in constrained Markov decision processes probably safe over an unbounded sequence of policy updates. An anytime valid test using per-decision importance sampling and a betting confidence sequence certifies each candidate trained in simulation before deployment using only trajectories collected by a safe baseline policy operating in production. Simulation quality does not affect the safety guarantee, only the production sample efficiency of the method. Finally, a summable per-test failure probability yields the lifetime valid property. Empirical results across three sim-to-real benchmarks support the theoretical work.

</details>

<a id="paper-171"></a>

### 171. [Reliable Prerequisite-Aware Learning Path Recommendation under Uncertain Knowledge Structures: Long-Horizon Evaluation and Cross-Dataset Validation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09345v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09345

**作者：** M. M. Golam Hafiz、A. K. M. Shahriar Emtiaz、Safia Akter Simi、Md. Rakibul Islam、M. F. Mridha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09345v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09345v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究提出先修关系感知的学习路径推荐框架，在知识结构不确定的条件下联合考察单步资源排序、硬/软/混合约束语义、图鲁棒性与自回归长程路径生成，并在 MOOCCubeX 上做冻结确认性评估、用 Junyi 做跨数据集验证。

**相关性简评：** 其核心论点「单步排序精度不能代表长程自回归路径质量，多维度应互补而非互换」可能可供核查 LVR 中逐步正确率与整条 latent 推理轨迹评价的错位；该领域属教育推荐，与视觉或多模态推理无直接联系，借鉴价值主要在评估设计层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reliable Prerequisite-Aware Learning Path Recommendation under Uncertain Knowledge Structures: Long-Horizon Evaluation and Cross-Dataset Validation

**abstract/A1：** Learning-path recommendation often depends on knowledge and prerequisite structures whose reliability is uncertain, while high accuracy in predicting the next resource does not necessarily establish reliable long-horizon path behaviour. This study develops a reliability-oriented prerequisite-aware framework that jointly examines immediate next-resource ranking, uncertain prerequisite structures, hard, soft and hybrid constraint semantics, graph robustness, and autoregressive path generation under course-level confirmatory evaluation and independent cross-dataset validation. Development used 3,187 MOOCCubeX courses, followed by a frozen confirmatory evaluation on 541 held-out courses and 37,747 transitions. The primary MOOCCubeX task is structural and course-prefix-conditioned rather than learner-personalised. Junyi provides independent structure-/hierarchy-aware external validation, uses hierarchy metadata rather than an explicit prerequisite graph and is not a zero-shot transfer setting. On the frozen MOOCCubeX evaluation, XGBoost pairwise was the strongest observed immediate ranker, with mean reciprocal rank (MRR) of 0.9048, whereas the proposed prerequisite-aware graph model achieved 0.7707. In the controlled graph-variant development ablation, the no-graph condition produced the strongest immediate ranking result, and immediate prediction showed strong dependence on permitted title/path-similarity information. Autoregressive evaluation exposed path properties not captured by one-step ranking. On Junyi, Markov achieved the strongest observed MRR of 0.4453, compared with 0.3954 for the proposed graph model, with metric-specific differences across ranking measures. Overall, immediate predictive accuracy, prerequisite-structure reliability, and long-horizon path quality should be treated as complementary rather than interchangeable evaluation dimensions in educational recommendation.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 5 figures, 7 tables. Under review at Computers &amp; Education: Artificial Intelligence (Elsevier)

</details>

<a id="paper-172"></a>

### 172. [Open-world Reconstruction-based Classifier with Concept Drift Adaptation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09350v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09350

**作者：** Joanna Komorniczak

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09350v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09350v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种开放世界流式分类方法，用自编码器重构误差及其 KDE 分布建模来检测并学习未知类，并通过权重微调持续适应已知类的概念漂移，在合成数据流、CIFAR 嵌入流及真实数据集上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其以重构误差分布变化作为「内部分布已漂移」的信号并在线更新状态，可能为 LVR 中判断 latent 推理状态何时失效、何时需重新获取视觉证据提供概念线索，但相距较远，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Open-world Reconstruction-based Classifier with Concept Drift Adaptation

**abstract/A1：** Data stream processing has become central to modern machine learning applications, with concept drifts and novel class appearances posing the primary challenges faced by recognition methods. Adapting to concept drifts while also being able to detect novel classes and distinguish groups of unknown observations can be seen as a step towards effective method employment in an open-world setting. This work proposes a classification method that can detect and learn the distributions of new, unknown classes, as well as adapt to concept drifts once the distribution of known classes shifts. The proposed method uses the autoencoders' reconstruction errors and the KDE that models their distribution. The autoencoders incrementally adapt to the changing distributions of known classes via weight fine-tuning, resulting in continuous adjustment to evolving concepts. Conducted experiments used a diverse set of synthetic data streams, embeddings of a real-world static CIFAR dataset arranged into a stream to simulate concept drifts and the emergence of unknowns, as well as real-world datasets where novelty is observed. The methodology was compared with state-of-the-art approaches with three-criterion recognition quality assessment and resource consumption evaluation. The results indicate that the proposed method is competitive with reference approaches in stationary environments and performs best when concept drifts occur.

</details>

<a id="paper-173"></a>

### 173. [HiMEM: Hippocampus-Inspired Memory Consolidation, Pruning, and Composition for Vision-Language-Action Models in 3D Scenes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09355v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09355

**作者：** Boran Zhao、Yanbin Hu、Jun Ye、Zhenxian Hu、Yuxin Zhai、Jin Cui、Pengju Ren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09355v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09355v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向 3D 场景中的 VLA 模型，提出受海马体启发的记忆机制：将大量情节记忆整合为紧凑的语义记忆，剪除已被语义记忆覆盖的冗余情节记忆，并灵活组合多类记忆以提升长程任务执行与跨任务泛化。

**相关性简评：** 其「情节记忆→紧凑语义记忆」的整合与冗余剪除，与 LVR 中状态抽象、信息坍缩和冗余视觉证据筛选的问题表述可能相通，可供核查记忆压缩是否改善推理状态质量；但工作聚焦机器人操作的外部记忆库，与推理期 latent 状态生成无直接对应。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HiMEM: Hippocampus-Inspired Memory Consolidation, Pruning, and Composition for Vision-Language-Action Models in 3D Scenes

**abstract/A1：** Memory plays a crucial role in long-horizon task execution and cross-task generalization for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models operating in complex 3D scenes. However, existing VLA memory mechanisms suffer from memory noise, particularly in complex 3D scenes, as stored episodic memories contain extensive redundant details about scene layouts, object configurations, and robot-specific states, which obscure generalizable manipulation principles and hinder cross-task transfer. To address this challenge, we first introduce a hippocampus-inspired memory consolidation mechanism that synthesizes extensive episodic experiences into a compact set of essential semantic memories, enabling VLA models to derive generalizable principles from practical experience. Second, we propose an episodic memory pruning mechanism that removes redundant episodic memories whose contents are captured by consolidated semantic memories, thereby increasing effective memory capacity and improving long-horizon task execution. Finally, to handle complex task scenarios, we develop a flexible memory composition mechanism that jointly leverages multiple episodic and semantic memories to enhance cross-scenario generalization. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus demonstrate the effectiveness of HiMEM across different VLA backbones, improving the average success rate of pi\_0 from 91.88% to 98.20% on LIBERO.

</details>

<a id="paper-174"></a>

### 174. [Challenges, Opportunities, and Misconceptions in Neurosymbolic AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09356v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09356

**作者：** Giuseppe Marra、Pierre Genevès、Meghyn Bienvenu、Thomas Schiex、Sylvie Thiébaux、Silviu Maniu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09356v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09356v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文是一篇立场综合文章，基于 CNRS 神经符号工作组的讨论，梳理神经符号 AI 在效率、安全与可解释性上的机会，指出神经与符号组件的语义对齐、符号推理与学习的计算成本两大挑战，并澄清可微性、独立性假设与可扩展性方面的常见误解。

**相关性简评：** 其对「神经—符号语义对齐」与符号推理成本的讨论，可能为 LVR 中结构化 latent 状态的可解释性、验证与计算代价权衡提供概念参照，但材料未给出具体状态设计或实验线索，可供核查其是否支持符号约束下的 latent 推理评价。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Challenges, Opportunities, and Misconceptions in Neurosymbolic AI

**abstract/A1：** We examine opportunities for neurosymbolic AI alongside increasingly powerful neural models, particularly with regards to efficiency, safety and interpretability. We identify two major challenges: ensuring semantic alignment between neural and symbolic components, and managing the computational cost of symbolic inference and learning. We discuss representative methodological approaches to these challenges and address several common misconceptions concerning differentiability, independence assumptions, and scalability. This paper synthesizes perspectives developed through discussions in the Neurosymbolic Programming and Reasoning (NPR) working group at the 2026 CNRS workshop Neurosymbolic Methods and Their Applications in Data Science, rather than providing an exhaustive survey.

</details>

<a id="paper-175"></a>

### 175. [SafeOrchestrate: Runtime Checked Intent-Driven Orchestration for Cyber-Physical Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09364v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09364

**作者：** Muhammad Ashfaq、Ahmed R. Sadik、Ewan Warburton、Pyry Kotilainen、Niko Mäkitalo、Tommi Mikkonen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09364v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09364v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SafeOrchestrate 参考架构：从自然语言意图抽取原子目标并生成运行时可检查的 LTL 风格规范，经 MCP 把规范落到设备能力（Thing Description），再用 SMT 后端护栏在执行前逐个校验候选动作；在仿真智能家居、温室和工业任务上与直接调用 MCP 的 LLM 基线对比。

**相关性简评：** 其「先形式化规范、再对每个候选动作做运行时约束校验」的流程，可能为 LVR 中候选推理状态或停止决策的外部校验提供概念参照，但该工作不涉及视觉输入、latent 状态或答案生成，迁移价值需自行核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SafeOrchestrate: Runtime Checked Intent-Driven Orchestration for Cyber-Physical Systems

**abstract/A1：** Modern cyber physical systems (CPS) integrate heterogeneous sensors, actuators, controllers, and services, composed at runtime rather than at design time. Orchestrating such systems through natural language (NL) is attractive, but unconstrained large language model (LLM) tool use is risky because actions must respect device capabilities, runtime state, and safety constraints. We present Safe Orchestrate, a reference architecture for runtime checked, intent driven CPS orchestration. Given an NL intent, it derives atomic goals, generates a runtime checkable specification for each, grounds the specifications to device affordances, and verifies every candidate action against runtime state and safety constraints before execution. In a Web of Things (WoT) instantiation, the architecture produces Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) style specifications from the NL intent, grounds them to Thing Description (TD) affordances exposed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), and checks candidate actions against satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) backed guardrails. We evaluate this instantiation on simulated smart home, greenhouse, and industrial automation tasks against a direct LLM to MCP baseline. Safe Orchestrate maintains 100% required action accuracy on every task and 100% MCP Execution Similarity (MCP ES) on all but one, where MCP ES is 92%. By contrast, the baseline degrades on multi device, event triggered, and ordered tasks, with MCP ES falling to 13%. These results suggest that combining LLM based specification generation with role aware grounding and runtime constraint checking improves reliability for open CPS orchestration without enriched TD annotations or planning domain expertise.

</details>

<a id="paper-176"></a>

### 176. [Mitigating Covariate Shift in Behavioral Cloning via Robust Stationary Distribution Correction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09371v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09371

**作者：** Seokin Seo、Byung-Jun Lee、Jongmin Lee、HyeongJoo Hwang、Hongseok Yang、Kee-Eung Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09371v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09371v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究离线模仿学习中由任意（非平稳）状态分布数据——如偏置采集、不完整轨迹——引起的协变量偏移，提出 DrilDICE，用分布鲁棒的行为克隆目标并借助平稳分布校正比（DICE）求得可行解，在多类偏移场景下验证鲁棒性提升。

**相关性简评：** 其“状态分布匹配 + 比例校正 + 鲁棒目标”的表述可能启发 LVR 中训练轨迹与推理时内部状态分布不一致的问题表述与监督权重分配，但论文本身无视觉或 latent 推理内容，联系较远，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Mitigating Covariate Shift in Behavioral Cloning via Robust Stationary Distribution Correction

**abstract/A1：** We consider offline imitation learning (IL), which aims to train an agent to imitate from the dataset of expert demonstrations without online interaction with the environment. Behavioral Cloning (BC) has been a simple yet effective approach to offline IL, but it is also well-known to be vulnerable to the covariate shift resulting from the mismatch between the state distributions induced by the learned policy and the expert policy. Moreover, as often occurs in practice, when expert datasets are collected from an arbitrary state distribution instead of a stationary one, these shifts become more pronounced, potentially leading to substantial failures in existing IL methods. Specifically, we focus on covariate shift resulting from arbitrary state data distributions, such as biased data collection or incomplete trajectories, rather than shifts induced by changes in dynamics or noisy expert actions. In this paper, to mitigate the effect of the covariate shifts in BC, we propose DrilDICE, which utilizes a distributionally robust BC objective by employing a stationary distribution correction ratio estimation (DICE) to derive a feasible solution. We evaluate the effectiveness of our method through an extensive set of experiments covering diverse covariate shift scenarios. The results demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed approach in improving the robustness against the shifts, outperforming existing offline IL methods in such scenarios.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 11 figures, NeurIPS 2024, Code: https://github.com/tzs930/drildice

</details>

<a id="paper-177"></a>

### 177. [SPSC: Zero-Shot Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Reconstruction via Semantic Preservation and Subspace Calibration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09380v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09380

**作者：** Yutang Lu、Shuai Jiang、Fei Su、Zhicheng Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09380v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09380v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SPSC 框架，以对象掩码对比正则保持对象级特征邻域，并在推理阶段利用训练图像原型与未标注目标 EEG 的批统计做子空间候选门控，实现零样本跨被试 EEG 到图像重建。

**相关性简评：** 其对象级对比正则与免标签子空间校准，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节在内部状态里的保持、以及跨域语义漂移的校正提供概念线索，但工作面向图像生成而非推理状态生成与使用，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SPSC: Zero-Shot Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Reconstruction via Semantic Preservation and Subspace Calibration

**abstract/A1：** Pretrained vision-language models and diffusion generators have driven rapid progress in brain-to-image generation, while EEG-based reconstruction is still constrained by low signal-to-noise ratio and cross-subject semantic shifts. Zero-shot cross-subject reconstruction poses even greater challenges: EEG semantic embeddings learned from source subjects deviate from the manifold of image embeddings of the unseen target subject, particularly when neither target labels nor target image embeddings are available. To address this issue, we introduce the Semantic Preservation and Subspace Calibration (SPSC) framework, which first uses Object Mask Contrastive Regularization (OMCR) with object identity for backend training, and then constructs Subspace Candidate Gate (SCG) to achieve label-free semantic correction for target subjects during inference. Specifically, OMCR regularizes the conditioning space with object-level positive samples to preserve object-level feature neighborhoods within the reconstruction prior and low-level branch, while SCG exploits training image prototypes and batch statistics of unlabeled target EEG to select candidates for each task within subspaces of image prototypes. Under the leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) setting across ten subjects on the THINGS-EEG dataset, SPSC improves the cross-subject reconstruction from 0.552 to 0.713 on CLIP and from 0.612 to 0.745 on Alex.5. These significant gains indicate that the semantically calibrated conditions can better support generation for the target subjects under label-free inference.

</details>

<a id="paper-178"></a>

### 178. [Against All Likelihood](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09385v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09385

**作者：** David Lopez-Paz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09385v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09385v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 这是一篇立场论文，论证最大似然既非真理的必要也非充分条件，主张以陈述在环境相关变换下是否保持不变（invariance）作为真理性判据，并联系 OOD 泛化与不变性学习文献，勾勒在推理期用语言模型检验不变性的思路及对持续学习的启示。

**相关性简评：** 其「以变换下不变性替代概率」的判据可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、多 latent 假设的取舍与停止决策提供概念工具，但论文未涉及多模态推理，具体迁移方式仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Against All Likelihood

**abstract/A1：** Maximum likelihood is the dominant training principle for large language models, yet likelihood is neither necessary nor sufficient for truth. Unlikely events happen every day, and frequently repeated statements can be false. Drawing on a philosophical tradition that ties objectivity to invariance rather than probability (from Nozick's account of objective facts and Wigner's principles of invariance to the structural realism of Poincaré and Cassirer), we argue that the truth of a statement should be judged not by its probability under a single distribution, but by its invariance across relevant transformations of the environment. We connect this view to machine learning literature on out-of-distribution generalization and invariant learning, sketch how invariance could be tested at inference time with language models, and discuss implications for continual learning.

</details>

<a id="paper-179"></a>

### 179. [k-Volume: a Unified Active Learning Strategy](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09388v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09388

**作者：** Eliot Tron、Alain Celisse、Christophe Denis、Alice Le Brigant

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09388v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09388v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 k-Volume（kVol）主动学习策略：按包含某样本 k 个最近邻的最小球的体积进行随机抽样标注，可在输入空间或输出空间使用，兼具密度、多样性与（输出空间下）不确定性性质，并在理论上渐近等价于在数据支撑集上均匀采样；在合成/真实表格数据与图像上验证其稳定性与较低计算成本。

**相关性简评：** 以邻居球体积作为「信息量/覆盖度」的几何代理，可能为 LVR 中「看哪里」的证据筛选、查询或记忆选择提供可核查的启发式，但其面向训练期标注、高维表示空间中的行为仍需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** k-Volume: a Unified Active Learning Strategy

**abstract/A1：** In the classification context where revealing the label of a sample is costly, we propose a new Active Learning (AL) strategy named k-Volume (kVol). It randomly selects a sample to be labeled according to the volume of the smallest ball containing its k nearest neighbors. kVol is highly versatile: it can be applied either in the input space or in the output space, and it enjoys the desirable properties of the three main families of AL strategies: density, diversity, and (when performed in the output space) uncertainty. From a theoretical perspective, we establish that kVol is asymptotically equivalent to uniform sampling over the support of the (input or output) data distribution. Based on experiments on various synthetic and real-life tabular datasets and images, we observe that kVol is surprisingly stable: whatever the classifier or the experimental setup, it still lies among the best AL strategies unlike other competitors that fail dramatically. In particular, kVol outperforms state-of-the-art competitors such as CoreSet, TypiClust or Uncertainty on several real-life datasets, at a low computational cost.

</details>

### 180. [ROAM: Retrieval-Augmented Optimization for Feasible Multi-Day Itinerary Planning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09407v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09407

**作者：** Ye Zihao、Lin Xiaoheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09407v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09407v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ROAM 多日行程规划框架：先检索候选 POI，用最大团分析与 K-means 分日、质量偏置模拟退火 TSP 排序、贪心交换修复营业时间冲突，再由 LLM 渲染结构化行程，并用 ReAct 式代理支持自然语言重规划与可行性复查。

**相关性简评：** 其「LLM 只做渲染、可行性由外部显式优化与校验保证」的分工，以及消融显示空间优化主导收益，可为「推理状态外化还是留在 latent」、迭代重规划中何时获取证据与停止校验提供对照线索，但需核查迁移合理性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ROAM: Retrieval-Augmented Optimization for Feasible Multi-Day Itinerary Planning

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) can draft plausible travel itineraries, but the plans they produce are frequently infeasible: attractions are grouped into days without regard for geography, opening hours are ignored, and a single generation pass cannot reliably organize a multi-day trip. We present ROAM, a retrieval-augmented agentic framework for spontaneous multi-day itinerary planning that couples semantic preference retrieval with explicit spatiotemporal optimization. ROAM embeds the natural-language request, retrieves a compact candidate set from a curated point-of-interest (POI) database, partitions candidates into day groups through maximal-clique analysis and K-means, orders each day with a quality-biased simulated-annealing TSP solver, and repairs hard opening-hour violations with greedy exchange. A spatiotemporal retrieval layer injects weather forecasts, real road distances and POI operating hours into the prompt, after which the LLM renders a structured, map-ready itinerary; an interactive ReAct-style replanning agent then supports natural-language edits while re-checking feasibility. Across four Chinese cities and 510 POIs, ROAM produces 27.5 POIs per three-day plan versus 19.3 for a bare LLM and 17.0 for a retrieval-only baseline (+42% and +62%), while reducing route dispersion from 2,101 to 677 (3.1x). Ablations show that spatial optimization is the dominant driver of route efficiency and that hard time-window repair prevents roughly 30% of attractions from violating operating hours.

</details>

### 181. [Typed-Centrality Grounding for Agentic GraphRAG: A Symbolic Approach for Agentic Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09414v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09414

**作者：** Rosario Napoli、Antonio Celesti、Massimo Villari、Maria Fazio

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09414v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09414v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出带符号层的 agent 式 GraphRAG：按知识图每个类别分别计算中心性（typed centrality），把结果组织成符号化表示作为确定性知识源提供给 LLM，并在真实 POLE 数据集上对比有无符号层的推理与检索表现。

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及视觉输入或 latent 推理状态，但其「按类型分别度量中心性、据此形成结构化确定性先验」的做法，可能可供核查用于 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与实体关系结构的排序和取舍。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Typed-Centrality Grounding for Agentic GraphRAG: A Symbolic Approach for Agentic Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Knowledge Graphs (KGs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) represent two approaches for knowledge representation and reasoning over heterogeneous data. Recent research has explored their integration through Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) systems, which use KGs to provide structured context to LLMs. However, existing GraphRAG approaches treat KGs as homogeneous graphs and rely on textual similarity between the input prompt and the context, rather than using it as a source of deterministic knowledge. This work proposes an agent-based GraphRAG system that introduces a symbolic layer to integrate deterministic reasoning alongside text similarities. This approach is based on the definition of typed centralities for KGs, where centrality measures are computed separately for each specific KG's class. This information is used to construct a symbolic representation that is provided to the LLM as a deterministic source. The proposed system was mathematically validated and evaluated on a real-world POLE (Person, Object, Location, Event) dataset, comparing reasoning and information retrieval with and without the symbolic layer. The results indicate that the our approach leads to responses that are more consistent with the context, improving the generated outputs.

**comment/C1：** Preprint. 7 pages. Final version published in 2026 IEEE Symposium on Computers and Communications (ISCC), DOI: 10.1109/ISCC70364.2026.11739974

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Overview of the ImageCLEFmedical 2026 GANs task: Exploring fingerprint detection, privacy leakage, and synthetic medical image generation

**abstract/A1：** The 2026 ImageCLEFmedical GANs Task Exploring Fingerprint Detection, Privacy Leakage, and Synthetic Medical Image Generation continues to investigate privacy and security concerns around using patient data to generate synthetic medical images, now in its fourth edition. This edition introduces three complementary subtasks: the first extends prior editions by asking participants to detect which real images were used in training a Generative Adversarial Network to produce given synthetic outputs; the second is a novel subtask requiring participants to identify which latent vector produced each synthetic image, probing the faithfulness and reversibility of the latent-to-image mapping; the third asks participants to generate privacy-preserving CT slices, quantifying the trade-off between image fidelity and patient privacy. Ground-truth annotations and benchmark datasets of real and GAN-generated lung CT slices are provided for all subtasks. Evaluation is based on Cohen's Kappa for Subtask 1, matching accuracy for Subtask 2, and a combination of FID and Privacy Preservation Score (PPS) for Subtask 3. Overall, 37 teams registered for the task, with 10 teams submitting runs across subtasks and 6 teams submitting working notes papers. This paper presents an overview of the task setup, datasets, evaluation metrics, and summarises the approaches and results of participating teams.

**comment/C1：** CLEF 2026 Working Notes, 21-24 September 2026, Jena, Germany

</details>

<a id="paper-183"></a>

### 183. [e-means: outperforming k-means (in one-tenth the epochs)](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09501v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09501

**作者：** Swann Bessa、Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré、Gergely Szilvasy、Hugo Touvron、David Picard、Matthijs Douze、Hervé Jégou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09501v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09501v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 e-means：用指数滑动平均加小批量更新替代 Lloyd 迭代的 k-means，并给出衰减率与批大小的缩放规律，从而免调参地在少量 epoch 内达到或低于 Lloyd k-means 的损失。

**相关性简评：** 快速大规模 embedding 聚类或可用于对 VLM 隐藏状态、视觉 token 或缓存键做在线分组与量化，从而支撑 latent 结构分析与视觉证据筛选中可能的重合度/簇假设，但该联系纯属推测，需核查其算法接口是否适配在线推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** e-means: outperforming k-means (in one-tenth the epochs)

**abstract/A1：** Lloyd's k-means algorithm is a long-running standard for clustering, with applications in data analysis, retrieval, quantization and data curation. In this paper, we introduce an exponential moving average optimization procedure for k-means with mini-batch updates, powered by modern deep learning machinery and new insights. We also establish scaling laws on how decay rate and batch size impact final performance. This gives us a formulaic rule to fix them for multiple problem sizes and embedding datasets, without the need for any hyperparameter sweep. The resulting clustering algorithm, coined e-means, is much faster than Lloyd k-means and converges to lower losses, frequently even beating k-means++. On a wide array of embeddings datasets, it beats the loss of Lloyd k-means at 25 epochs in 1.4 to 2.6 epochs, which translates to 10-45x GPU wall-clock speedups compared to state-of-the-art k-means implementations. As an example, e-means partitions DINO10B, a dataset of 10 billion DINO embeddings, into 16 million clusters in only 2.5 epochs, reaching a loss below Lloyd's at 25 epochs.

**comment/C1：** Code : https://github.com/facebookresearch/faiss/tree/main/contrib/e\_means Website : https://e-means.github.io

</details>
