### 184. [BEiTScore++: Reference-free Image Captioning Evaluation with BEiT-3 and Conformalized Quantile Regression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09505v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09505

**作者：** Gonçalo Gomes、Bruno Martins、Chrysoula Zerva

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09505v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09505v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 BEiTScore++，在 BEiTScore 图像描述评估指标上引入保形校准的分位数回归，使模型在一次前向中同时给出图文对齐分数与带校准的置信区间，并在多个基准上检验区间宽度与预测误差、图像退化和域外分布的关系。

**相关性简评：** 该工作把「对齐评分」与「校准不确定性」绑定，其区间宽度随误差变化的做法可供核查是否能借用于 LVR 中判断视觉证据是否充分、以及置信度驱动的停止决策；但它评估的是描述指标而非推理期 latent 状态，与主线距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BEiTScore++: Reference-free Image Captioning Evaluation with BEiT-3 and Conformalized Quantile Regression

**abstract/A1：** Reliable uncertainty estimation is important for deploying evaluation metrics in real-world settings, where both input ambiguity and domain shifts are common. In this work, we propose an uncertainty-aware extension of the BEiTScore image captioning metric, producing conformally calibrated confidence intervals alongside alignment scores. Our approach models predictive distributions over human judgments, enabling both accurate scoring and principled uncertainty quantification in a single forward pass. We evaluate our BEiTScore++ metric across multiple benchmarks with human-annotated ratings, showing that it preserves the ranking correlation of BEiTScore, while producing intervals whose width tracks the prediction error more closely than Monte Carlo dropout on most benchmarks. The estimated uncertainty also grows under image degradation and on out-of-domain artwork images, making BEiTScore++ a practical tool for uncertainty-aware evaluation of image-caption alignment.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to Findings of EACL 2026

</details>

### 185. [Active Learning of Robust Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09520v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09520

**作者：** Mizu Nishikawa-Toomey、Andreas Kirsch、Laurent Charlin、Dhanya Sridhar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09520v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09520v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究主动学习与部署阶段分布偏移（子群体偏移）同时出现时的标准选择策略失效模式，并提出利用数据采集元数据作为样本所属子群体的噪声代理来指导标注选择，以提升各子群体上的分类精度。

**相关性简评：** 用噪声代理变量驱动「信息增益式」样本选择、并在偏移下分别评估各子群体表现，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据/监督预算的筛选及偏移条件下的评价设计存在概念联系，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Active Learning of Robust Models

**abstract/A1：** In many domains from species identification to cancer diagnosis, labelling data to train predictive models remains costly. In these settings, it is advantageous to intelligently select data points to label to achieve the maximum information gain, this is the motivation behind the study of active learning. In addition to the high costs of acquiring data in such domains, there is the added complexity of data distribution shifts that can occur between training and deployment. For example, the features of cancerous skin cells taken in one country may be different in another region of the world. These two challenges - efficient labelling of data, and learning models robust to distribution shifts have been studied separately but not together, even though these challenges often co-occur in real-life settings. We identify failure modes of standard active learning approaches when the distribution of data at deployment is different from the distribution in which the data is sampled for labelling. We propose a new strategy for efficiently learning classifiers that generalise under specific forms of distribution shift, namely subpopulation shift. Drawing on work in learning from heterogeneous data, we aim to train models that perform well across subpopulations of the data, through active learning. To handle this challenging setting, we exploit the fact that many datasets include metadata about data collection that can serve as a noisy proxy for each sample's underlying subpopulation. We demonstrate how to leverage these noisy labels during active learning selection, and demonstrate empirically that our approach improves classifier accuracy across subpopulations.

</details>

<a id="paper-186"></a>

### 186. [CID: Measuring Cognitive Work Through Semantic State Change](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09542v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09542

**作者：** Ron Fulbright

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09542v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09542v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把模型语义空间中的向量定义为语义状态，将认知工作视为状态间位移，并提出基于马氏距离、考虑协方差结构的 Contextual Information Differential（CID）来量化语义变化。

**相关性简评：** 其「语义状态—位移」表述可能为测量 latent 推理轨迹及状态更新提供几何工具，但论文未涉及视觉推理或 LVR，需核查该度量对内容、深度与位置变化的敏感性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CID: Measuring Cognitive Work Through Semantic State Change

**abstract/A1：** Traditional information theory provides powerful tools for analyzing statistical properties of information but offers limited insight into meaning and semantic transformation. It has never been possible to quantify the meaning of an arbitrary piece of information. Large language models represent information as vectors embedded within high-dimensional semantic spaces, enabling meaning to be analyzed geometrically. This paper defines semantic state as the vector representation of information within a model's semantic space and defines cognitive work as the transformation between semantic states, movement through semantic space. To quantify this transformation, we introduce the Contextual Information Differential (CID), a covariance-aware metric based on Mahalanobis distance measuring semantic displacement relative to the structure of the embedding manifold. By treating meaning as a geometric property of semantic space, CID provides a practical method for measuring semantic change, cognitive transformation, and information processing. This framework establishes a foundation for analyzing cognitive processing, learning, reasoning, knowledge generation, synthetic expertise, and human/AI collaboration through implementation-independent measurement of semantic state change in both human and artificial systems.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 1 figure, 12 equations

</details>

<a id="paper-187"></a>

### 187. [Marginal-Nonuniform Multiclass Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09543v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09543

**作者：** Nataly Brukhim、Steve Hanneke、Amirreza Shaeiri、Maximilian Thiessen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09543v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09543v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 纯理论学习工作，研究多分类在「边缘分布非均匀」框架下的最优学习速率，用新的 uncentered DS-eluder 维数给出指数、线性、任意慢三分法的完整刻画，并发展分布耗尽引理支撑上界证明。

**相关性简评：** 其 eluder 维数及序列式耗尽论证刻画序贯学习的容量与速率，可能为 LVR 中证据获取顺序、停止决策与计算代价的定量分析提供概念线索，但距离较远，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Marginal-Nonuniform Multiclass Learning

**abstract/A1：** We study the problem of multiclass classification within the framework of marginal-nonuniform learning, where the constants in the learning rate may depend on the unknown marginal distribution generating the unlabeled data, but not on the target concept. This notion naturally lies between classical PAC learning, where the constants are independent of both the marginal distribution and the target concept, and more recent universal learning, where the constants may depend on both. We give a complete characterization of the optimal marginal-nonuniform learning rates, establishing a fundamental trichotomy: exponential, linear, and arbitrarily slow rates. This characterization is witnessed by a new combinatorial dimension, termed the uncentered DS-eluder dimension. A key ingredient in the upper-bound proofs is a set of new distributional exhaustion lemmas that exploit the sequential nature of this dimension and may be of independent interest.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-188"></a>

### 188. [CoMoFlow: Controllable Protein Conformational Ensemble Generation via Explicit Collective-Mode Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09548v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09548

**作者：** Yusong Wang、Zheyuan Gu、Ailiang Lin、Minghao Shao、Yicheng Xu、Jiang Cai、Prayag Tiwari、Mingkun Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09548v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09548v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CoMoFlow，从蛋白平衡结构构造蛋白特异的集体模式基，学习各模式系数在 MD 系综中的分布，并以采样系数通过特征条件与先验重定心引导 SE(3) flow matching，生成可控的骨架构象系综。

**相关性简评：** 其「显式低维模式基＋系数分布＋条件化生成」的结构与 LVR 中以约束化的低维隐状态控制推理轨迹生成、并对状态先验做重定心的做法存在形式相似性，可供核查是否可迁移为 latent 状态空间的结构化控制机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoMoFlow: Controllable Protein Conformational Ensemble Generation via Explicit Collective-Mode Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Protein conformational ensemble generation aims to efficiently characterize the structural variations accessible to a protein beyond a single static structure. Recent flow-based methods efficiently generate conformational ensembles, but they model coordinated structural variation implicitly, which can limit ensemble fidelity and hinder direct control. To address this limitation, we propose CoMoFlow, a controllable framework for generating protein backbone conformational ensembles by explicitly modeling collective variation. CoMoFlow constructs protein-specific collective modes from the equilibrium structure, learns the distribution of their coefficients across the molecular dynamics (MD) ensemble, and uses sampled collective variations to guide SE(3) flow matching through feature conditioning and prior recentering. Experiments on ATLAS and de novo proteins show that CoMoFlow improves reconstruction of MD conformational distributions and coordinated structural variation relative to strong flow-based baselines, while enabling direct control over targeted conformational changes.

</details>

<a id="paper-189"></a>

### 189. [Know What You Need: Efficient Activation Checkpointing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09614v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09614

**作者：** Danyal Ahmed、Edouard Oyallon

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09614v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09614v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一种近似重计算方法：利用前向传播中已收集的信息，以贪心选择或随机草图只重算 Transformer 层中最关键的组件，从而在反向传播时以较低计算代价获得近似无偏的梯度。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但其依据前向统计筛选「重要组件」并按需重算、同时用随机草图控制偏差的思路，可能为 latent 状态的按需刷新与视觉证据筛选提供计算价值分配的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Know What You Need: Efficient Activation Checkpointing

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Model training is increasingly constrained by memory capacity. Activation Checkpointing reduces activation memory by avoiding storing selected activations during the forward pass and rematerializing them during backpropagation, thereby trading memory savings for additional computation. Although this technique is essential for scaling model training, rematerialization can be prohibitively expensive, particularly for Transformer linear layers. Approximate computation could reduce this overhead, but conventional approaches either introduce errors that accumulate over the backward pass or fail to exploit information available from the initial forward pass. We introduce a novel approximate rematerialization method that can yield unbiased gradient estimates: using information collected during the initial forward pass, the method selectively recomputes only the most important layer components, either through greedy selection or randomized sketching. Ablation studies show that the resulting gradients closely match the exact gradients. When applied to feed-forward layers, attention layers, or both, our method can recompute only two-thirds of the layer components in a Llama-488M model, while increasing perplexity by just 1.7%, 1.2%, and 2.5%, respectively.

</details>

### 190. [BiLoGo: Reducing the smoothing of localised responses in Transformer-based neural operators for sheet forming](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09621v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09621

**作者：** Yingxue Zhao、Haoran Li、Haosu Zhou、Tobias Pfaff、Nan Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09621v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09621v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** BiLoGo 面向板材成形的有限元代理建模，用节点-单元二部图 GraphSAGE 学习局部耦合、双流 Transformer 神经算子分别处理坯料变形与模具接触，并经门控残差融合更新单元特征，以缓解 Transformer 对局部减薄响应的平滑。

**相关性简评：** 其「局部信息不被全局注意力平均」的双流 token 划分与门控融合设计，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节保留、latent 信息坍缩与证据筛选提供架构线索，但属科学计算代理建模范畴，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BiLoGo: Reducing the smoothing of localised responses in Transformer-based neural operators for sheet forming

**abstract/A1：** Trial-and-error FE simulation of sheet forming could be computationally expensive, neural-network-based learned simulators are used to accelerate design. These simulators should accurately capture localised responses, such as thinning around sharp radii or near ribs, which determine formability. Transformer-based neural operators propagate information efficiently across the mesh but tend to smooth such localised responses. This study develops BiLoGo, a Bipartite Local-Global learned simulator with an FE-guided design that connects local node-element message passing with a Transformer-based neural operator for long-range information exchange. BiLoGo has three modules: a bidirectional node-element GraphSAGE model that learns nodal-elemental coupling on a bipartite graph and predicts each quantity on its native FE entity without interpolation; a dual-stream Transformer-based neural operator that processes blank deformation and tool contact with separate token sets, preventing localised contact information from being averaged with the whole-blank state; and a fusion and element update module that combines global and local features via gated residual fusion and updates element features conditioned on element-node kinematics. Evaluated on a dome-shaped cold forming case and two corner-shaped hot stamping cases (one with ribs), BiLoGo achieves the lowest positional and threshold-based thinning errors on all three cases compared with single-level and hierarchical graph-based models and Transformer-based neural operators. Near the ribs, the smoothing index of predicted thinning increases from 0.41-0.56 for Transformer-based neural operators to 0.94 for BiLoGo-U. Compared with the hierarchical graph network CAtt-BiUGNN, BiLoGo-T reduces mean positional error by 15.5% on the Corner case and BiLoGo-U by 8.3% on the Corner+Rib case, with 66-69% less training memory.

</details>

<a id="paper-191"></a>

### 191. [Breaking the Privacy Promise: Gradient Inversion Attacks on Federated Low-Rank Adapted Vision Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09652v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09652

**作者：** Ritik K. Badiya、Siladittya Manna、Ashmit Sinha、M Yashwanth、Anirban Chakraborty

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CR、cs.DC、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09652v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09652v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究联邦学习下 LoRA 微调 ViT 的梯度反演隐私泄露，指出低秩梯度信息减少与冻结位置嵌入缺失空间线索是重建质量的两个限制，并提出在客户端训练时加入服务器指定的可训练位置嵌入，使其梯度充当逐层空间锚点，配合生成先验提升图像重建质量。

**相关性简评：** 「位置梯度可作为逐层空间锚点、补充空间线索」这一观察可能为分析 ViT 内部状态中内容信息与位置信息的分离及其对视觉细节保真的贡献提供旁证性线索，可供核查，但与 LVR 的推理机制、latent 生成与停止决策联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Breaking the Privacy Promise: Gradient Inversion Attacks on Federated Low-Rank Adapted Vision Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has been increasingly adopted to fine-tune pretrained models with low computational cost and is therefore also preferred in the federated learning paradigm to reduce computational and communication overhead. In this work, we investigate the sparsely explored problem of the risk of private data leakage in LoRA-adapted Vision Transformers (ViTs), where low-rank updates weaken the gradient signal available to a server with access to individual client updates. We show that quality of reconstruction is limited by two coupled ambiguities: reduced information in LoRA gradients and missing spatial cues from frozen positional embeddings. To address this, we propose a spatially anchored generative inversion framework that incorporates server-prescribed trainable positional embeddings into ViT blocks during fine-tuning at clients. The resulting positional gradients act as layer-wise spatial anchors, providing additional positional cues, while the generative prior keeps reconstructions on a realistic image manifold, reducing duplicate-data and patch-permutation artifacts, {which produce similar gradients as those produced when the target client images are used}. Adding our proposed spatial anchoring to GIAS improves PSNR by 10.14 dB, SSIM by 0.45, and LPIPS by 0.098 on CIFAR-10, as well as PSNR gains of 7.83 and 11.74 dB on high-resolution CelebA and CARS datasets, respectively, in the FedSGD setting. Such gains in reconstructed image quality metrics are also maintained at higher batch sizes and in FedAvg settings. These results indicate that LoRA-based PEFT does not, by itself, prevent data leakage in FL, and carefully incorporated positional embeddings can help in exposing private information.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-192"></a>

### 192. [Video2Asset: Online Construction of Persistent 3D Street-Scene Assets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09661v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09661

**作者：** Zihan Zheng、Chunlei Cai、Zhenlong Wu、Linjun Li、Yichen Gong、Xiaoyun Zhang、Qiang Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09661v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09661v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Video2Asset，以 occupancy 为中间表示，从多视角视频在线构建可独立编辑、身份持久的 3D 街景资产，并用累积证据关联与身份编码维持跨帧身份一致和世界坐标状态更新。

**相关性简评：** 其「在资产记录中累积身份、几何与外观证据以决定继承并更新状态」的机制，可能为 LVR 中跨推理步持续维护视觉证据、避免不同对象证据混淆提供可核查的类比，但该工作本身属 3D 场景重建，未涉及 VLM 推理或 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Video2Asset: Online Construction of Persistent 3D Street-Scene Assets

**abstract/A1：** Online 3D street-scene asset construction turns multi-view video into a continually updated scene of independently editable objects. The central challenge is to accumulate partial, intermittent observations under motion and occlusion without fragmenting identities or mixing evidence across objects. To address this challenge, we propose Video2Asset, an online framework for constructing persistent 3D assets from multi-view video. Using occupancy as an intermediate representation, we construct identity-aware observations with Historical Carrier Guidance for instance decoding and an identity encoder fusing instance-aligned 3D features with multi-view appearance. Accumulated-Evidence Association compares current observations with identity, geometric, and appearance evidence accumulated within asset records to determine identity inheritance and update world-coordinate states. We instantiate meshes from an asset library using these states and combine them with an occupancy-derived static background to support scene visualization, mesh replacement, object deletion, and trajectory editing. Experiments on several datasets demonstrate improvements in temporal identity consistency and asset quality across multiple occupancy models, including a 15.4% reduction in asset fragmentation on nuScenes with SGE.

</details>

<a id="paper-193"></a>

### 193. [Online Causal Configuration for Networked Systems via Doubly Robust Steady-State Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09665v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09665

**作者：** Yuli Liu、Zhiheng Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09665v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09665v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究网络化系统中带测量探索的在线配置：提出 CAUSAL-UCB，用干扰感知的裁剪双稳健评估器给出乐观因果值区间，并区分统计波动与识别/迁移/时间依赖误差，同时给出推断误差下界与探索导致的部署损失下界。

**相关性简评：** 其「探索带来的部署损失」与证书式误差拆分，或可类比 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的证据获取成本与不确定性校准、停止决策问题，但领域距离远，仅供核查概念对应。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Online Causal Configuration for Networked Systems via Doubly Robust Steady-State Learning

**abstract/A1：** Measurement exploration reveals how interacting local controllers behave but can alter the steady-state regime a network operator seeks to optimize. Exploratory rewards may therefore fail to identify a configuration's no-exploration value: under full exploration, identical logged distributions can conceal different target values. Identification requires accounting for both action-law mismatch and shifts in the steady-state context distribution. We introduce CAUSAL-UCB, which selects configurations using optimistic bounds on target causal values. Its interference-aware clipped doubly robust evaluator produces predecision certificates separating statistical variation from calibrated identification, transport, and temporal-dependence errors. For any finite horizon and predetermined schedule of measurement-exploration rates $\\{η\_t\\}$, we prove lower bounds of $Ω((1+\\sum\_tη\_t)^{-1/2})$ on minimax inference error and $Ω(\\sum\_tη\_t)$ on measurement-induced deployment loss in a stationary, potentially slow-mixing family. We bound regret under valid certificates and selected-information growth, and establish an anytime finite-sample certificate with a shared radius for estimation, selection, and reporting in a restricted known-local-interference model. In a high-mismatch benchmark with 24 paired seeds and 200 rounds, CAUSAL-UCB attains target regret of $1.732$ versus $6.326$ for certified IPS-UCB, with deployment gaps of $30.607$ and $23.217$, respectively. Independent-audit coverage tests and trace-driven semi-synthetic studies further assess certificate coverage and deployment trade-offs.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-194"></a>

### 194. [PoseRieFlow: Riemannian Flow Matching for Whole-Body Text-to-Pose Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09692v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09692

**作者：** Jan Spindler、Bardia Ardakanian、Patrick Stotko、Reinhard Klein

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09692v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09692v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PoseRieFlow，在 SMPL-H 关节旋转的乘积流形上以文本为条件做 flow matching 生成全身 3D 姿态，并用部位级 token 化的 Diffusion Transformer 聚合 52 个关节以兼顾手部细节与紧凑规模。

**相关性简评：** 流形上的连续向量场传输与部位级 token 抽象，可能为 latent 推理状态的结构化生成、压缩与几何约束提供可核查线索，但迁移性尚不明确。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PoseRieFlow: Riemannian Flow Matching for Whole-Body Text-to-Pose Generation

**abstract/A1：** We present PoseRieFlow, a compact flow-matching framework for text-conditioned generation of whole-body 3D human poses. Prior text-to-pose methods either neglect articulated hands or model rotations in Euclidean space despite their non-Euclidean geometry. PoseRieFlow instead formulates generation directly on the product manifold of SMPL-H joint rotations, learning a text-conditioned vector field that transports samples from an isotropic $\\mathrm{SO}(3)$ Gaussian prior to the data distribution along Riemannian geodesics. A part-tokenized Diffusion Transformer aggregates the 52 joints into eight body-part tokens, allocating a dedicated token and nonlinear projector to each hand, and restores per-joint resolution through part-specific decoders, shortening the sequence while improving kinematic fidelity over per-joint tokenization. With only 21.6M trainable parameters, PoseRieFlow supports expressive SMPL-H generation with detailed hand articulation at a fraction of the scale of recent LLM-based generators. On PoseScript, it attains the lowest whole-body kinematic error among all hand-producing methods, with consistent kinematic gains from the Riemannian formulation over its Euclidean counterpart at every practical inference budget, while distributional and retrieval metrics remain comparable between them.

</details>

<a id="paper-195"></a>

### 195. [Intent-Aware Caching for Efficient LLM Serving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09715v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09715

**作者：** Kazi Hasan Ibn Arif、JinYi Yoon、Dimitrios S. Nikolopoulos、Hans Vandierendonck、Deepu John、Bo Ji

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09715v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09715v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Intent-Aware Caching：依据用户意图对请求分类，从近期服务日志学习续聊概率与轮次间隔统计，用以指导 KV 前缀缓存的在线淘汰策略，在真实对话负载上提升命中率并降低时延。

**相关性简评：** 其「按未来复用可能性决定保留哪些缓存状态」的在线淘汰思路，可能为多轮推理中 latent 状态/证据的保留与跨轮复用提供类比线索，但该文仅关注服务端缓存命中与延迟，未涉及推理内容或 latent 语义，需核查其意图信号是否能对应推理状态价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Intent-Aware Caching for Efficient LLM Serving

**abstract/A1：** Modern LLM serving systems use prefix caching to accelerate multi-turn conversational workloads. They cache the key-value states of previous conversation prefixes and reuse them when subsequent requests share the same prefix, to avoid redundant prefill computation and reduce serving latency. However, achieving a high cache hit rate remains challenging because cache reuse depends on whether a conversation continues and how soon the next turn arrives. Our analysis of real workloads shows that user intent is a useful signal for this behavior, as different intents exhibit different continuation probabilities and inter-turn delays. Based on this key insight, we propose Intent-Aware Caching (IAC), an online eviction policy that classifies requests by intent, learns lightweight reuse statistics from recent serving logs and leverages these statistics to guide an oracle-inspired eviction strategy. Extensive evaluation on real workloads (WildChat and ShareChat) shows that IAC reaches a 34.6% cache hit rate. This substantially outperforms both the previous learning-based approach (18.8%) and traditional methods such as least recently used (LRU, 12.2%), while reducing average serving latency by up to 46.1%.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 \| Code: https://github.com/hasanar1f/intent-aware-caching

</details>

### 196. [Direct Regression and Geometric Recovery for Event-based Spacecraft Pose Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09717v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09717

**作者：** Pruthvi Nandan Janga、Kashyap Hegde Kota、Bharatesh Chakravarthi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09717v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09717v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 比较两种事件相机航天器 6-DoF 位姿估计系统：一条是学习关键点检测加无标签目标域自适应、几何位姿恢复与时间平滑的模块化流程，另一条从全局与裁剪事件表示直接回归平移和旋转，辅以表观尺度线索、物理启发的合成退化和特征一致性正则；在统一隐藏测试协议下评测，发现总体分数掩盖了分量互补——直接回归旋转误差更低，模块化流程平移误差更低。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但其「直接回归 vs 显式几何恢复」的互补误差剖面可供核查 latent 隐式预测与显式几何/状态瓶颈之间的分工，合成退化与特征一致性正则也可能作为视觉表征鲁棒性的训练线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Direct Regression and Geometric Recovery for Event-based Spacecraft Pose Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Event cameras offer high temporal resolution and dynamic range for uncooperative spacecraft pose estimation, but models trained on synthetic event streams must generalize to the noise, background activity, and contrast variation of physical sensors. We present a comparative systems study of two independently developed approaches to synthetic-to-real, event-based 6-DoF spacecraft pose estimation. The first is a modular pipeline that combines learned keypoint detection and unlabeled target-domain adaptation with geometry-based pose recovery and temporal smoothing. The second directly regresses translation and rotation from global and cropped event representations using an apparent-scale cue, physics-motivated synthetic corruptions, and feature-consistency regularization. We evaluate both systems under the same SPARK 2026 hidden-test protocol on the real-event split of SPADES, with predictions for all test frames. The results reveal a component-wise reversal concealed by the aggregate challenge score: direct regression reduces orientation error by 22.7% (1.1767 to 0.9101 rad) and obtains a 13.4% lower composite score, whereas the modular pipeline reduces relative translation error by 78.7% (0.1335 to 0.0285). Archived configurations and a qualitative failure case identify plausible explanations for these complementary error profiles, without isolating their causes. These findings motivate a testable hybrid design that combines direct orientation estimation with geometry-constrained translation recovery.

</details>

<a id="paper-197"></a>

### 197. [What to Delegate, What to Preserve: Human Preferences for Agentic AI Across Everyday Life and Work](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09730v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09730

**作者：** Sarah Wheeler、Veronica Rammouz、Yaoyao Qian、Zexuan Liu、Mengyi Wei、Anthony Rios、Tianzhi He、Kyrie Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09730v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09730v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 对289名有AI经验的美国成年人做情景问卷，覆盖九个生活领域的45个用例，测量自动化偏好、信任、协作、AI领导、隐私顾虑与付费意愿，并用GEE分析影响因素，提出授权应视为执行、协作、领导与最终权威的组合配置。

**相关性简评：** 其「有界自主、人类保留监督与最终权威」的授权配置，可能为 LVR 中何时停止 latent 推理、把证据获取或决定权交回人类（STOP）提供问题表述线索，但需核查其可操作性；与视觉推理内部状态本身无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What to Delegate, What to Preserve: Human Preferences for Agentic AI Across Everyday Life and Work

**abstract/A1：** AI agents can execute multi-step tasks on users' behalf, yet the boundaries of acceptable delegation remain unclear. We report a scenario-based survey of 289 U.S. adults with prior AI experience covering 45 use cases across nine life domains. Each respondent evaluated one scenario per domain, producing repeated measures of automation preference, trust, collaboration, AI leadership, privacy concern, and willingness to pay. Automation preferences varied substantially across domains. Generalized estimating equation models showed that greater trust in the AI agent, desired AI leadership, prior task-specific AI use, and anticipated financial savings were associated with greater automation preference. In contrast, concerns that automation would reduce personal fulfillment predicted lower automation preference. Open-ended responses further revealed a preference for bounded autonomy, with participants often favoring substantial AI execution while retaining human involvement, oversight, or final authority. We argue that delegation is best understood as a configuration of execution, collaboration, leadership, and authority.

</details>

<a id="paper-198"></a>

### 198. [Safety Is $Π\_1$, Provability Is $Σ\_1$: One Framework for the Hugging Face Breach and OpenAI's Mathematical Proofs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09736v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09736

**作者：** Jinho Kang、Jinsook Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CR、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09736v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09736v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用「统计提议者 + 演绎验证者」的循环与 Σ₁/Π₁ 逻辑形式统一解读 Hugging Face 安全入侵与 OpenAI 数学证明产出，并论证提高提议者质量不改变相应的不可判定性结论。

**相关性简评：** 其「存在有有限见证、不存在无有限见证」的验证不对称，可能可供核查 LVR 中停止决策与验证信号的可靠性边界，但全文不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，联系偏概念层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Safety Is $Π\_1$, Provability Is $Σ\_1$: One Framework for the Hugging Face Breach and OpenAI's Mathematical Proofs

**abstract/A1：** OpenAI's agents breached Hugging Face in July 2026, and the same company released more than seven hundred AI-generated manuscripts of mathematical proofs in October 2026. The first event is read as a failure in security, while the second is read as a success in mathematics. We read both through the hybrid framework of our paper (Kim and Kang, 2026, arXiv:2611.01914), in which coding, proving, and learning from data are derivations by one and the same deductive mechanism, distinguished by the kinds of premises from which they are derived. Under this framework, the AI systems behind the two events ran the same loop, in which a statistical proposer produces candidate exploits or proofs and a deductive verifier settles each one by a derivation. What separates the events is the logical form of the statement wanted by each of the two domains, security and mathematics. The safety of a program is a $Π\_1$ statement, for it says that no exploit exists, which is not assured by any finite list of tests that have been passed by the program. By contrast, the provability of a sentence is a $Σ\_1$ statement, for it says that a proof exists, which is assured by a single instance, namely a proof that has been checked by the deductive verifier. The theorem behind both is Turing's Undecidability Theorem: the halting set is $Σ\_1$ but not decidable, so its complement is $Π\_1$ and not $Σ\_1$, which is to say that membership has a finite witness but non-membership has none. We prove that attaching a statistical proposer to a deductive verifier does not change this undecidability result, however good the proposer is. We then discuss what does assure safety and where the residual risk goes.

</details>

<a id="paper-199"></a>

### 199. [Diffusion-free Score Matching for Exact Conditional Sampling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09758v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09758

**作者：** Thomas Wedenig、Robert Peharz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09758v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09758v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DISCO：一个不依赖时间索引 score 场的 score matching 目标，只估计（轻微扰动）数据分布的单一 score，从而可直接取得精确条件 score，实现从任意条件分布采样。

**相关性简评：** 该工作属纯生成建模，与 VLM 推理无直接联系；但其「不用启发式引导、以精确条件 score 采样」的表述，可能为 latent 推理中如何以视觉证据为条件生成或筛选 latent 状态提供概念线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Diffusion-free Score Matching for Exact Conditional Sampling

**abstract/A1：** The success of modern score-based generative models is largely driven by diffusion-based training, which learns a collection of time-indexed score fields corresponding to progressively noisier versions of the data distribution. While such models achieve impressive sample quality, leveraging them for sound probabilistic inference---particularly sampling from arbitrary conditionals of the model---remains challenging. This difficulty arises because conditioning information is only available for clean data and not for the higher noise levels required to generate exact conditional samples. In this paper, we introduce DIffusion-free SCOre matching (DISCO), a principled approach that sidesteps the need for time-dependent score fields altogether. Our method is based on an objective that, while reminiscent of diffusion-based training, estimates only a single score of the (slightly perturbed) data distribution, for which the exact conditional score is readily obtained. In experiments, DISCO is competitive with state-of-the-art diffusion models in terms of sample quality. More importantly, DISCO yields a more faithful representation of the underlying data distribution and---crucially---enables accurate sampling from arbitrary conditionals, outperforming standard heuristic samplers. This capability opens the door to principled and flexible probabilistic reasoning with score-based models.

</details>

<a id="paper-200"></a>

### 200. [Quasipolynomial-Time Planning for Decodable POMDPs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09778v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09778

**作者：** Oliver Dobson、Varun Jog、Chun Hei Michael Shiu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DS、cs.IT、math.OC、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09778v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09778v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究有限时域 POMDP 的近似规划，证明对固定 ρ&gt;0 的 ρ-decodable POMDP 存在拟多项式时间算法，并说明可解码性弱于 ℓ1/ℓ2-observability 且其最优常数可多项式时间计算。

**相关性简评：** 可解码性刻画隐状态能否从观测序列中恢复，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的「可辨识性」与信息坍缩、信念状态维护提供形式化视角，并关联规划与决策成本；但属纯理论 POMDP 结果，与视觉推理的衔接需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Quasipolynomial-Time Planning for Decodable POMDPs

**abstract/A1：** We study planning for finite-horizon partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) with finite state, action, and observation spaces. Both exact and approximate planning are known to be computationally hard in this setting, so prior work has explored natural assumptions that make planning easier. Recent work \[Golowich et al., 2022\] gave quasipolynomial-time approximate planning algorithms for observable POMDPs. These are POMDPs whose observation channels satisfy an $\\ell\_1$-observability condition, but notably, no assumptions are required on the transition dynamics. In a similar vein, for every fixed $ρ&gt;0$, we give quasipolynomial-time approximate planning algorithms for $ρ$-decodable POMDPs. We show that decodability is implied by both $\\ell\_1$- and $\\ell\_2$-observability and is strictly weaker than observability. Moreover, we show that the optimal decodability constant can be computed in polynomial time, whereas computing the optimal $\\ell\_1$-observability constant is NP-hard in the worst case. We also show that the fast filter-forgetting guarantee underlying the approach of \[Golowich et al., 2022\] does not extend directly from $\\ell\_1$-observability to constant $\\ell\_2$-observability. Thus, decodability is an easy-to-check, unifying assumption under which quasipolynomial-time approximate planning for POMDPs is possible.

**comment/C1：** 32 pages

</details>

### 201. [The Cost of Coverage: Conformalising Native Forecast Uncertainty for GB Wind Power](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09815v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09815

**作者：** Carl Somers

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.AP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09815v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09815v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将 conformal prediction 用于英国两座风电场上六种预报器（ARIMA 到表格基础模型）的原生预测区间，比较校准带来的覆盖收益与 Winkler 分数代价，并发现整体校准会掩盖大风条件下的欠覆盖，进而按风况分层校准。

**相关性简评：** 其「边际覆盖达标但条件覆盖失败」的诊断与按工况分层校准思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中停止/弃权决策的置信度校准及分难度、分长度的覆盖评价。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Cost of Coverage: Conformalising Native Forecast Uncertainty for GB Wind Power

**abstract/A1：** Uncertainty bands on wind forecasts help inform reserve sizing and commitments, but often fail to deliver their stated coverage. We apply conformal prediction to native bands to see what it adds and what it costs, comparing six forecasters, from ARIMA to a tabular foundation model, at two wind farms in Great Britain (GB) under operational conditions. We show that calibration repairs empirical marginal coverage with little or no Winkler Score penalty, but this apparent success conceals under-coverage in high winds. Calibrating by forecast wind regime turns marginal coverage into regime-level coverage, cutting day-ahead out-of-band volume in high winds by over 40% relative to pooled calibration while narrowing bands overall.

</details>

### 202. [Ensemble Methodology for Reference-Free Confidence Estimation in LLM-based Code Summarization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09846v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09846

**作者：** Marco Colombo、Balaji A Jayaram、Sowmyakumari Selvaraj、Sumit Tokkar、Animesh Kumar、Aravinda Prasad Sistla、Ravishankar Shetty、Rigel Gjomemo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.PL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09846v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09846v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出一种无需人工参考的 COBOL 代码摘要置信度估计流程：由多模型受控生成摘要，用 LLM-as-judge 做成对结构化评判，再以校准的聚合规则汇总支持性证据并仅惩罚已被证实的矛盾。

**相关性简评：** 「模型间语义一致即可信」「只惩罚被佐证的矛盾」这类无参考置信度思路，可能为 LVR 中基于多假设一致性的答案核验与 STOP 决策提供概念参考；但任务为纯文本代码摘要，迁移到视觉证据与 latent 假设仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Ensemble Methodology for Reference-Free Confidence Estimation in LLM-based Code Summarization

**abstract/A1：** This paper describes an approach and use case to estimate the correctness of LLM-generated COBOL summaries without access to human-authored references. The core intuition is that inter-model agreement on semantics is informative about truth, and that an LLM-as-judge can provide higher-fidelity pairwise assessments than traditional string- or embedding-based metrics. We operationalise this intuition with (i) controlled summary generation from diverse models; (ii) a LLM evaluator that compares pairs of summaries and emits structured judgments; and (iii) a calibrated aggregation rule that combines supportive evidence while penalising only corroborated contradictions.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 3 figures, 1 table

</details>

### 203. [Computer-Use Agents Are Embodied Generalists](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09847v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09847

**作者：** Pranjal Aggarwal、Sean Welleck

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09847v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09847v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文主张把具身任务表述为与数字软件的交互，用一个 computer-use agent（截屏观察加键鼠操作）在四个 ECCV workshop 比赛中获得名次。

**相关性简评：** 该工作不涉及 latent 表示或推理状态生成，但把视觉 grounding、探索与动作决策统一到同一「观察—操作」循环，或可作为检验视觉证据获取与停止决策的工作场景，具体联系需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Computer-Use Agents Are Embodied Generalists

**abstract/A1：** In many areas of AI, general-purpose models have increasingly replaced handcrafted methods and specialized architectures. We argue that embodied AI is poised for a similar shift. Our key claim is that embodied tasks can be formulated as interaction with digital software. Specifically, we build software that renders an agent's view of a physical environment on a screen and provides controls for acting in it. A computer-use agent (CUA) can then perform embodied tasks through the same screenshots and keyboard and mouse actions that it uses to operate ordinary software. Using a single CUA design and changing only the software, our entries win first-, second-, third-, and sixth-place prizes across four ECCV workshop competitions, without any competition-specific agent tuning. These results suggest that the skills CUAs learn on software, such as visual grounding, precise clicking, and exploration, already carry over to embodied tasks, giving CUAs a far broader scope in embodied AI than previously realized. We therefore advocate that computer-use agents can be embodied generalists. Further, we advocate that, by following the training strategy that made computer-use agents general, embodied AI can converge on a single general model as well, ushering in a new era of general embodied AI. Website: https://pranjal2041.github.io/cua-embodied

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2509.18719

**作者：** Bo Qu、Zhurong Wang、Daisuke Yagi、Zhen Xu、Yang Zhao、Yinan Shan、Frank Zahradnik

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18719v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2509.18719v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文属电商支付欺诈风控的工业应用，LLM 仅用于迭代改写多步 MDP 的奖励函数，未涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、证据筛选与停止决策等 LVR 问题，也缺少可具体核查的迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LLM-Enhanced Self-Evolving Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Step E-Commerce Payment Fraud Risk Detection

**abstract/A1：** This paper presents a novel approach to e-commerce payment fraud detection by integrating reinforcement learning (RL) with Large Language Models (LLMs). By framing transaction risk as a multi-step Markov Decision Process (MDP), RL optimizes risk detection across multiple payment stages. Crafting effective reward functions, essential for RL model success, typically requires significant human expertise due to the complexity and variability in design. LLMs, with their advanced reasoning and coding capabilities, are well-suited to refine these functions, offering improvements over traditional methods. Our approach leverages LLMs to iteratively enhance reward functions, achieving better fraud detection accuracy and demonstrating zero-shot capability. Experiments with real-world data confirm the effectiveness, robustness, and resilience of our LLM-enhanced RL framework through long-term evaluations, underscoring the potential of LLMs in advancing industrial RL applications.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 12 figures, ACL 2025 industry track

</details>

### 205. [Same Question, Different Answers: Auditing Persona-Dependent Variation in Six LLMs on Smoking-Cessation Advice](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42039v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.42039

**作者：** Artin Yousefi、Aizierjiang Aiersilan、Lorien C Abroms、David A Broniatowski

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42039v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.42039v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文审计六款 LLM 在戒烟建议上对十二种用户人设的回答差异（共 2,880 条回复），发现青少年人设更少获得处方戒烟药物信息，并测试一段指定「事实下限」的系统提示能否缩小这些差异，同时公开数据与分析代码。

**排除原因：** 这是纯文本健康咨询的人设公平性审计，不涉及多模态视觉推理、内部表示、证据筛选或停止与计算分配，也没有可供核查的状态或训练线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Same Question, Different Answers: Auditing Persona-Dependent Variation in Six LLMs on Smoking-Cessation Advice

**abstract/A1：** Hundreds of millions of people now ask large language model (LLM) chatbots for health advice, which raises a question that is simple to pose and hard to answer: does everyone get the same advice? We audit six widely deployed LLMs from four providers on smoking cessation, a domain with codified clini- cal guidance and substantial public-health stakes, by putting the same four cessation questions to twelve user personas that differ in life stage, self-described expertise, and smoking history, for a total of 2,880 responses. What the models say depends on who appears to be asking. Every model named FDA-approved prescription cessation medications less often to teen personas than to adult personas, by 32 to 67 percentage points, and in five of six models a persona presenting health- care expertise received fewer facts than a persona presenting none. We then evaluate one deployable intervention: a short system prompt specifying a factual floor, the set of core med- ical facts every user should receive when they are relevant, while leaving tone, vocabulary, and framing free to adapt. The prompt narrows persona differences in every model, but its effect on overall fact coverage varies in both magnitude and direction across models, so a prompt validated on one system cannot be assumed to transfer to another. Two cautions frame these results. Equal mention rates are not always the correct clinical target, because prescription cessation pharmacother- apy is not recommended for minors, so a defensible factual floor must be age-aware rather than uniform. And because our personas vary on several attributes at once, we report scenario- level differences rather than isolated effects of age or expertise. We release all 2,880 responses and the analysis code.

</details>

### 206. [Towards Label-Efficient Geospatial Foundation Model Adaptation via Positive-Unlabeled Learning for Building Damage Mapping](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08561v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08561

**作者：** Hengwei Zhao、Qirui Wang、Zhuo Zheng、Xiaoyan Lu、Rusty Feagin、Wenzhe Jiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08561v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08561v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向地球观测基础模型适配的建筑损毁制图，提出正样本-无标签（PU）学习框架 PUMA，用少量受损建筑标注完成弱监督参数高效微调。

**排除原因：** 虽提到「latent-state-based label disambiguation」，但该 latent state 指伪标签歧义消解的分类器状态，与 VLM 推理中的内部推理状态生成、证据使用和停止决策无实质关联，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Label-Efficient Geospatial Foundation Model Adaptation via Positive-Unlabeled Learning for Building Damage Mapping

**abstract/A1：** Geospatial foundation models (GFMs) are reshaping remote sensing by shifting the field from task-specific modeling toward general-purpose geospatial intelligence. Mapping the damage to buildings stands to benefit substantially from this shift, as GFMs pretrained on large-scale Earth observation data provide generalizable geospatial representations that can facilitate effective responses to sudden-onset disasters. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) facilitates GFM adaptation, parameter efficiency does not necessarily translate into label efficiency. Building damage mapping remains challenging due to scarce pre-existing labeled data or limited time for annotation. When weak supervision is introduced to alleviate the labeling bottleneck, we identify the difficulty of learning sufficiently discriminative representations as a key challenge for label-efficient GFM adaptation. To address this challenge, we propose a Positive-Unlabeled GFM Adaptation (PUMA) framework that requires annotations for only a small number of damaged buildings. More specifically, PUMA integrates non-contrastive weakly supervised representation refinement to mitigate the impact of noisy supervision on representation learning from a gradient perspective, and latent-state-based label disambiguation to enable reversible supervision refinement without requiring class prior estimation. Extensive experiments on disaster benchmark datasets from around the world demonstrated the effectiveness and robustness of PUMA. A real-world application to the 2025 Los Angeles wildfires further highlighted its potential to accurately map damage under highly limited supervision. More broadly, these findings suggest that weakly supervised PEFT offers a label-efficient pathway from GFMs to real-world applications.

</details>

### 207. [Not All Misalignment Is Alike: A Decomposed Benchmark for Emergent Misalignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08569v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08569

**作者：** Afonso Domingues、Mubashara Akhtar、Bhada Yun、Xin Chen、Lukas Fluri、Anna Hedström

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08569v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08569v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 EMERGE，一个将涌现性失准拆解为八类行为、并配以域外良性对照与能力/连贯性/拒答混杂项追踪的评测基准，用于比较 8 个开源模型与 13 个微调数据集下的行为差异。

**排除原因：** 研究主题为纯文本模型的安全失准评测，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态、证据使用或停止决策，其拆解式基准设计属通用评测规范，未提供可核查的 LVR 方法或评价线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Not All Misalignment Is Alike: A Decomposed Benchmark for Emergent Misalignment

**abstract/A1：** Emergent misalignment (EM), in which fine-tuning on a narrow task induces misaligned behaviour on unrelated prompts, has been demonstrated across a wide range of datasets, model families, and training setups. Yet, most studies continue to rely on the evaluation approach introduced in the original EM paper, producing rates that are sensitive to the small set of evaluation questions used, the choice of judge, and the thresholds applied. These evaluations can also conflate misalignment with capability loss and offer little insight into the specific ways misalignment manifests. Differences in datasets, models, and fine-tuning configurations further make findings difficult to compare across studies. We introduce EMERGE, a decomposed benchmark that tracks changes in eight distinct misaligned behaviours from base to fine-tuned models. It evaluates these behaviours on out-of-domain prompts against matched benign controls and tracks capability, coherence, and refusal to account for potential confounders. Applying EMERGE to 8 open-weight models and 13 fine-tuning datasets, we find that models with similar broad misalignment rates can exhibit very different behavioural profiles that a single score cannot capture. We hope EMERGE can help establish a shared and robust standard for measuring EM, making findings more comparable and helping move research from detecting the phenomenon towards understanding it.

</details>

**title/T1：** Enhancing Academic Advisement with Case-Based Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** This research involved the study of an application of Case-Based Reasoning (CBR) in the field of academic advising. Academic advisement has been recognised as an important function of higher education. However, there is a widespread desire to improve the advisement services provided by many educational institutions (e.g., colleges and universities) around the world. In the last ten years, CBR expert systems have made important contributions in many domains by applying CBR methodology to a variety of areas of human problem solving. However, to date, expert advisement systems have not been successfully applied to academic advisement, which seems to be a very appropriate domain for this technology. Academic advising is an important and time consuming task, and different tools and techniques must be used to make it an effective and efficient process. This thesis describes the design, development, and validation of a prototype case-based academic advisement recommender system that supplements the conventional advising process, and could eventually evolve to replace human academic advisors when its full potential is realised.

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08578v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08578v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出以物体为条件的神经距离场先验，在手部关节流形上表示合理的抓握构型，并将给定姿态投影到该场的零水平集，用于姿态细化、遮挡下重建与操作合成。

**排除原因：** 该文属三维手-物交互的几何生成先验，不涉及多模态推理状态生成、证据获取或停止决策，其零水平集投影虽可联想到潜状态约束校正，但联系过于间接，缺少可供核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HaNDF: Object-Conditioned Geometric Neural Hand Distance Fields

**abstract/A1：** We propose, HaNDF, object-conditional neural distance fields as data-driven priors for modeling the plausible hand-object configurations, in the product manifold of hand articulations. While recent unconditional generative priors showed success in modeling human pose, hands are versatile articulated bodies often found in interaction with other objects. This makes it crucial to develop a generative prior which can model the plausible hand poses conditioned on the object under interaction. To this end, our HaNDF proposes a geometry-aware, object-conditioned neural distance field, representing the plausible articulations in the zero-level set of a conditional field. We leverage tools from Riemannian geometry to (i) represent plausible hands in the zero level-set of an object-induced neural field, and (ii) project a given pose onto this field. Using HaNDF, we can also optimize for a plausible condition, determining the object under interaction. Our extensive evaluations demonstrate that our HaNDF provides a flexible and powerful prior for hand-object interaction, suitable for applications such as pose refinement, reconstruction under occlusion, and physically plausible manipulation synthesis.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 210. [InstaGen: Group-Shared Importance Sampling for Fast Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08610v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08610

**作者：** Kislay Aditya Oj、Kumar Krishna Agrawal、Sri Surya Varma Datla、Aditya Desai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08610v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08610v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作将视频扩散 Transformer 的 permuted block-sparse attention 中块选择重新表述为重要性采样，用「精确 top 集合加按概率加权的采样块」替换 top-p 选择，以在同等算力下更准确。

**排除原因：** 其目标是降低视频生成的注意力计算成本，加权采样作用于注意力块近似而非推理状态、视觉证据筛选或停止决策，缺少与 LVR 主线可核查的具体联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** InstaGen: Group-Shared Importance Sampling for Fast Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) are the dominant architecture for video generation, but their quadratic attention complexity results in substantial computational cost and latency. Permuted block-sparse attention (PBSA) offers a promising approach to reducing this cost by selectively computing blocks of a semantically permuted attention matrix. However, existing approaches typically select only the top few key blocks for each query block, following a top-$p$ paradigm. We find that top-$p$ selection can be highly sample inefficient in high-entropy attention regimes, which are common in video generation. In these regimes, we show that sampling can provide substantially better approximations under the same computational budget. More generally, we cast block selection as importance sampling, where the top-scoring keys form an exact set that is computed in full and the remaining keys are represented by a sample drawn in proportion to their estimated attention and reweighted by inverse sampling probability. Building on this formulation, we present InstaGen, which can be parameterized by any PBSA method: it replaces the method's block selection with an exact set plus a weighted sample. Each query cluster draws one sample shared by all its queries, so the sampled keys form blocks amenable to efficient GPU execution. In this paper, we take the SVG family of PBSA methods and show that, on Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B and Wan2.1-T2V-14B, InstaGen parameterized by SVG2 improves PSNR over SVG2 by 3.4 dB and 2 dB, respectively, at matched attention density, and by 1.6 dB and 2.2 dB at matched wall-clock time. On Wan2.1-T2V-14B, InstaGen also beats SVG-EAR by 1.1 dB PSNR at nearly the same density while running faster (1.65x against 1.48x over dense).

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 6 figures, 2 tables

</details>

### 211. [LLM-Based Feature Extraction for Multi-Criteria Inventory Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08626v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08626

**作者：** Humza Akhtar、Rami Pinto

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DB、cs.ET

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08626v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08626v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用 LLM 与向量嵌入把客户评论等非结构化文本提取为定量特征，并入多准则库存分类框架以改进库存优先级划分。

**排除原因：** 属于供应链库存管理的特征工程应用，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选、停止决策与 latent 状态使用，缺乏可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LLM-Based Feature Extraction for Multi-Criteria Inventory Classification

**abstract/A1：** E-commerce supply chains generate large volumes of customer feedback and operational data that remain underutilized in inventory management. Traditional inventory classification techniques such as ABC analysis rely primarily on annual dollar usage, while multi-criteria inventory classification (MCIC) methods extend this but still depend on structured quantitative data and do not utilize unstructured information available in modern supply chains. This paper proposes an LLM-based feature extraction approach that transforms unstructured sources such as customer reviews into quantitative features using vector embeddings and LLM-generated evaluation criteria. The enriched feature set is incorporated into an MCIC framework based on the Hadi-Vencheh model. Experimental results show measurable reclassification effects when customer sentiment signals are included, demonstrating that integrating unstructured data into MCIC can enhance inventory prioritization.

</details>

### 212. [Benchmarking Machine Learning for Chaotic Three-Body Dynamics in Astrophysics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08634v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08634

**作者：** Sheng Wan、Pavan Vynatheya、Duo Xu、Li Kevin Wenliang、Ye Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08634v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08634v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建混沌三体系统的机器学习基准，围绕稳定性分类与轨迹预测两类任务比较通用模型、物理基础模型及天体物理判据。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是纯物理轨迹仿真与预测难度排序，不涉及视觉或多模态内部表示、证据获取或推理停止决策，也未提供可核查的 LVR 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Benchmarking Machine Learning for Chaotic Three-Body Dynamics in Astrophysics

**abstract/A1：** Machine learning (ML) and generative AI have recently made impressive progress in understanding physics. We explore a hierarchy of prediction problems stemming from chaotic three-body systems and benchmark existing ML approaches, including general-purpose prediction algorithms and those that build on top of foundational physics models. We first simulate the trajectories of triple systems near the critical boundary of stable/unstable initial conditions. We then design a hierarchy of learning tasks, focusing mainly on two broad problems: stability prediction and trajectory prediction. The former classifies whether a given triple system has diverged or not up to a specific horizon, while the latter predicts the exact trajectory, both given suitable input features. We benchmark a range of ML approaches, from simple regression methods to state-of-the-art foundational physics models, across these tasks, and compare them against astrophysical methods such as the Mardling-Aarseth stability criterion. Our experiments reveal a clear order of task difficulties in chaotic three-body dynamics: early dynamics enable strong stability prediction, while precise long-horizon trajectory forecasting remains difficult. Code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/Simonwan03/ThreeBodyMLBench.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Sim2Science: ML with Imperfect Scientific Models

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08645

**作者：** Abdiaziz Muse、Tooba Javed、Tao Pu、Jiju Poovvancheri、Dong Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.GR、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08645v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08645v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文发布 HFX3D，一个约 4.92 亿点、覆盖 26 栋建筑多季节多天气的街面 LiDAR 基准数据集，标注语义、实例与逐实例功能属性，并评测点、图、卷积与 Transformer 架构的分割与功能推断表现。

**排除原因：** 这是纯 3D 点云感知与属性标注基准，不涉及 latent 推理状态、证据获取或多步决策，也缺乏可直接对应 LVR 训练监督或评价的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Geometry to Function: A Multi-Task LiDAR Benchmark Dataset for Semantic and Functional Parsing of Urban Facades

**abstract/A1：** Understanding building facades is central to urban digital twins, heritage modelling, and sustainable city analysis, yet existing LiDAR benchmarks stop at geometry and semantic segmentation and offer little support for reasoning about the functional role of each architectural element. We introduce HFX3D, a large-scale urban facade benchmark of approximately 492 million LiDAR points from 26 buildings in Halifax, Canada, captured across four seasons, day and night, and varied weather. Each facade is annotated at three tiers, semantic, instance, and per-instance functional attributes, making it, to our knowledge, the first facade dataset to pair geometry and semantics with functional labels. Annotations are produced by a semi-automatic pipeline that combines geometry-guided heuristics with expert validation. We benchmark representative point-based, graph-based, convolutional, and transformer architectures and find that semantic segmentation is already challenging under severe class imbalance, while instance separation of repetitive structures and functional reasoning from geometry are substantially harder. The benchmark establishes a new foundation for function-aware 3D urban understanding. Project page: https://jijup.github.io/hfx3d-benchmark/ ; code: https://github.com/jijup/hfx3d-benchmark

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027. 19 pages (8 main + supplementary), includes appendix. Project page: https://jijup.github.io/hfx3d-benchmark/ ; Code: https://github.com/jijup/hfx3d-benchmark

</details>

### 214. [An Anatomy-guided 3D Segmentation Pipeline for Prostate Surgical Phantoms from CT Scans](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08652v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08652

**作者：** Iven Chen、Kuan-I Chung、Ayberk Acar、Susheela Sharma Stern、Alejandro O. Chara、Jie Ying Wu、Daniel Moyer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08652v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08652v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一套基于经典医学图像处理、以解剖先验为引导的 3D 分割流程，用于分割前列腺手术体模 CT 中的囊内区域与病灶，无需训练数据。

**排除原因：** 该工作属体模分割的工程流程，不涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据的使用与筛选、动态计算或停止决策，与 LVR 主线无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An Anatomy-guided 3D Segmentation Pipeline for Prostate Surgical Phantoms from CT Scans

**abstract/A1：** Full autonomy has been a long-term goal in robotic surgery and a near-to-medium-term goal for prostatectomy. Due to the relatively closed structure of the procedure, its standard-of-care robotic/laparoscopic solutions, and procedure economics, prostate procedures are an optimal early target for surgical automation. Prototyping and testing of possible solutions is done in constructed laboratory phantoms, either synthetic tissues or animal phantoms. To enable measurement of perceptual and robotic motion error, phantoms undergo volumetric imaging; these images are critical to both a potential deployed automatic system, used in planning motion and treatment (cutting, ablation, suturing, etc.), as well as post-hoc assessment of system performance. Rapid iteration on these phantom models yields small, inconsistent datasets, while the current workflow for human annotation is often anatomically unreliable and prohibitively time-consuming, rendering deep learning unsuitable at reasonable scales. To address these limitations, we present an automated 3D segmentation pipeline that uses classical medical image-processing techniques to encode the known anatomy of the phantoms as explicit geometric and intensity priors, which requires no training corpus and is transferred to each new phantom revision without re-annotation. Across 57 scans spanning multiple phantom revisions, our pipeline recovers the capsule interior at 0.83 median Dice on 11 contrast-suppressed scans and detects all 32 annotated focal lesions without false positives, completing a full scan in 2-8 minutes on commodity CPU hardware versus 1-2 hours by hand.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables, 10 algorithms. Extended abstract accepted at SPIE Medical Imaging 2027: Image Processing

</details>

### 215. [Cancellation Is Not Enough: What Retries Leave Behind in an Overloaded LLM Engine](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08684v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08684

**作者：** Xindan Zhang、Fei Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08684v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08684v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在 vLLM 上做过载实验与调度器踪迹分析，考察客户端重试与取消传播为何无法让引擎恢复，并比较重试预算与请求合并。

**排除原因：** 该文属 LLM 服务系统工程层面的重试、取消与过载控制问题，未涉及推理内部状态、视觉证据或生成机制，其计算浪费统计也不构成可供核查的 LVR 停步或贡献分配线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cancellation Is Not Enough: What Retries Leave Behind in an Overloaded LLM Engine

**abstract/A1：** Clients and proxies in front of an LLM engine retry calls that time out. After a transient overload, these retries can keep the engine from recovering even when calls arrive below its capacity. Since the engine may keep computing for clients that have left, propagating cancellation looks like the natural remedy. In this paper, we test it on vLLM with overload experiments on three open models, and with scheduler traces of one that follow each client attempt to the execution it creates in the engine. We find that with retries on, cancellation alone did not restore service in any run, and only 12-38% of the calls that arrived after the overload were delivered. The traces suggest why: retries that vLLM ran typically started prefill less than half a second before they timed out. Executions that vLLM later aborted took 79-85% of the prompt tokens it computed, and vLLM's prompt-token counter missed a third to half of all computed prompt tokens. A retry budget restored service by shedding retries but left many of the overload's calls undelivered. Request coalescing delivered every call from the overload onward, some late, unless cancellation had already aborted the running execution. Overload controls should thus be judged by the executions that retries add and the calls that clients receive.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables

</details>

### 216. [Memory-Aware Multi-Agent Architecture with LLM-based Orchestration for Conversational Recommendation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08718v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08718

**作者：** Emilly Pitman de Castro、Vanessa Silva、Gustavo Kanno、Marcus Santos、Bruno Ferraz do Amaral、Marcelo Nogueira、Lucas Francisco Pellicer、Lucas Lanaro、Pedro Augusto Luiz、João Renato Ribeiro Manesco、Giovanni Carbinatti、Isabela de Lima Santos、Lucas Oliveira Jeronymo、Jeison Fappi、Raul Rotilli Aguirre、Fabio André Frantz、Renata De Paris、Fabiana Marucci、Lohan Silva、Vinicius Diniz Reolon、Wandemberg Gibaut

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08718v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08718v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向对话推荐的内存感知多智能体架构，用 LLM 编排意图澄清、查询构建与交互流程，并在 Amazon Reviews 上评估跨会话记忆与监督编排的效果。

**排除原因：** 属对话推荐系统应用，未涉及视觉或多模态推理，也未研究 latent 状态的生成与使用；长期记忆与编排只是系统级工程设计，缺少可供 LVR 核查的具体机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Memory-Aware Multi-Agent Architecture with LLM-based Orchestration for Conversational Recommendation

**abstract/A1：** Conversational recommender systems often treat user sessions in isolation, limiting their ability to capture how user intent evolves over time. While large language models (LLMs) have improved semantic understanding, they are typically used as isolated components, without addressing how memory, retrieval, and decision-making should be integrated at the system level. In this work, we propose a memory-aware multi-agent architecture for conversational recommendation, in which LLMs act as orchestration layers that coordinate intent refinement, query construction, and interaction flow. A key design principle is the integration of long-term memory as a first-class component, influencing both routing decisions and semantic query formation. We evaluate the system through cross-session memory experiments, multi-turn conversational simulations, and supervisor-driven interaction studies on the Amazon Reviews dataset. Results show that memory-enabled interactions recover up to 63\\% of the gap toward explicit-query scenarios and prevent semantic degradation in extended dialogues. In addition, supervisory orchestration increases user acceptance by +28.9 percentage points, highlighting the importance of structured orchestration in guiding interaction and output quality. We also observe an exposure bottleneck between retrieved candidates and displayed recommendations. While LLM-reranking does not improve Hit@K, it consistently enhances semantic consistency and diversity. To capture these effects, we introduce the Semantic Consistency Score (SCS), a complementary evaluation measure. Overall, the results emphasize the importance of system-level design choices, suggesting that effective conversational recommendation benefits from explicit memory integration, orchestration, and evaluation beyond traditional ranking metrics.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08732

**作者：** Christopher L. Crutchfield、Kyle Hu、Nathan Hui、Omair Qazi、Jennifer Loch、Alli Candelmo、Brice X. Semmens、Curt Schurgers、Ryan Kastner

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08732v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08732v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出 FishSense Lite：用休闲潜水相机的单张照片配合潜水激光笔和 3D 打印支架，借激光斑在图像中的位置推算距离，把像中的鱼体尺寸换算为实际体长，并通过公民科学潜水员采集带物种标注的野外数据做验证。

**排除原因：** 属于水下测量硬件与公民科学数据采集应用，虽涉及单视图几何测距，但没有内部推理状态、证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策等机制，也未讨论 VLM 或 latent 表示，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FishSense Lite: A Camera-Based, Single Laser Hardware Framework with Software System for In Situ Fish Length Measurement

**abstract/A1：** Fish stock assessments need fish lengths paired with species from far more places than trained surveyors can reach. Recreational divers already visit many of those places, but measuring a fish underwater has required trained observers or specialist equipment. We present FishSense Lite, which recovers fish length from a single photograph taken with a recreational dive camera, a dive laser pointer, and a 3D-printed mount. The diver aims the laser at a fish and takes a picture; afterward, the dot's position in the image gives the range to the fish, and the range turns the fish's size in the image into a length. A citizen-science program, trained by the system's builders, taught its own divers and collected lengths paired with species for 73 wild fish across seven open-water dives. On seven wild fish also measured by an underwater stereo-imaging system, the two instruments agreed without detectable bias (mean difference +0.8%). On rigid targets of known length, FishSense Lite recovered every target to within 1.3%, and 99% of 1,055 frames fell within a 15% error budget. From the error sources we measure, we derive a field protocol: calibrate against a dive slate on every dive, photograph fish within 15 degrees of perpendicular and beyond 1 m, and take at least ten frames of each.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages with 14 figures

</details>

### 218. [On Differentially Private Mechanisms for Linear Regression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08737v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08737

**作者：** Bardiya Aryanfard、Monika Henzinger、Farhood Rostamkhani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CR、cs.DS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08737v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08737v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究差分隐私下的（连续观测）最小二乘线性回归机制与下界，属于隐私保护统计估计，未涉及视觉表征、latent 推理状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，也无可迁移的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On Differentially Private Mechanisms for Linear Regression

**abstract/A1：** We study the classic least-squares linear regression problem under differential privacy (DP) in the batch and the continual observation setting. In the latter setting, the mechanism receives a stream of $n$ data points $(x\_i,y\_i)$ ($x\_i\\in\\mathbb{R}^d$ and $y\_i\\in\\mathbb{R}$) and must output an estimate of the optimal parameters at each time step $i$. Although differentially private linear regression has been extensively studied in the batch setting, the problem remains underexplored for the continual setting, with prior work being restricted to constrained linear regression and update time polynomial in $n$. In this work, we introduce ContinualAdaSSP, the first mechanism for unconstrained linear regression under continual differential privacy. Our mechanism's suboptimality gap is proportional to $\\sqrt{d}\\operatorname{polylog}(n)$. It requires $O(d^2\\log{n})$ space and $O(d^2\\log{n}+d^ω)$ update time, where $d$ is the dimension of the input space. We also study fundamental limits of differentially private linear regression by showing the first lower bounds for the box-constrained least-squares linear regression problem. Our lower bounds are proportional to $\\min(n,\\frac{\\sqrt{d}}{\\log^{1.5}d})$ for $(1,O(\\frac{1}{n}))$-DP, and $\\min(n,\\frac{d}ε)$ for $ε$-DP, and are derived using a reduction from a fingerprinting code problem from \[Peter et al., 2024\], providing new insights into the inherent hardness of this canonical task. Our experimental evaluation on real-world datasets shows that the accuracy of our continual observation mechanism clearly outperforms any other known differentially private mechanism for this problem such as rerunning the batch algorithm at each time step.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-219"></a>

### 219. [Federated training performs as well as centralized for 3D Latent Diffusion for Diagnosis-Conditioned Brain MRI Synthesis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08771v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08771

**作者：** Abhijith Shaji、Paul Kurian、Greg Ver Steeg、Paul M. Thompson、Jose-Luis Ambite

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08771v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08771v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究联邦式训练 3D 条件 latent diffusion 以合成脑 MRI，属医学图像生成与隐私训练问题；其「latent」是扩散模型的图像编码空间，与 LVR 的推理状态表示、证据筛选及停止决策无实质联系，也无可核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Federated training performs as well as centralized for 3D Latent Diffusion for Diagnosis-Conditioned Brain MRI Synthesis

**abstract/A1：** Generative models of brain MRI are useful to understand brain conditions, and to enrich scarce and imbalanced neuroimaging data. However, training these models requires large, multi-site training sets, which is challenging. Federated learning (FL) allows for training deep learning models without sharing data. We show that training a 3D conditional latent model federatedly (where data is kept private at each site) performs similarly to centralized training (where all the data have been copied to a single site). We evaluate three metrics: conditional fidelity (how well generated MRIs perform in classification), diversity (how the generated images match the real data distribution, using MultiScale-SSIM), and realism (how close generated images are to real images, using Fréchet Inception Distance). Our results show that federated training is a viable route to develop multi-site generative models of brain MRI.

</details>

### 220. [Global Solar Siting from Geospatial Embeddings](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08831v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08831

**作者：** Abdullah Rehman、Quentin Paletta、Rui Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV、cs.CY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08831v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08831v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用地理空间 embedding 分析 38,591 个已有太阳能电站，训练 RF+MLP 集成模型学习选址适宜性信号，并在 13 个国家验证其排序与制图效果。

**排除原因：** 主题为光伏选址的遥感地理数据分析与适宜性预测，其「latent signal」指位置级别的选址评分，而非 VLM 内部推理状态，不涉及视觉细节保留、推理轨迹或多步证据使用，亦无值得核查的方法迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Global Solar Siting from Geospatial Embeddings

**abstract/A1：** Global solar photovoltaic (PV) capacity is rapidly growing, helping decrease our emissions and dependence on fossil fuels. However, optimally siting a solar plant remains challenging, requiring anticipation of geographical, urban, and infrastructure needs. Current methods rely on multi-criteria decision analysis (MCDA) using hand-picked geophysical layers and solar-resource products, however these strategies are not geographically transferable and do not capture the practical requirements of solar sites. We use geospatial embeddings, dense vectors representing geophysical properties of a location, to analyse 38,591 existing solar sites and the land they were built on to learn the latent solar suitability signal. We assess a set of linear and non-linear models and select a soft-voting ensemble of a random forest and a multilayer perceptron (RF+MLP ensemble), chosen for accuracy and large scale deployability. Validated against future installations in thirteen countries, the ensemble separates existing solar sites from random land with a median ROC-AUC of 0.90. Compared with published MCDA strategies, the ensemble increases ROC-AUC by 0.06-0.30 in every country. The ranking also concentrates real deployment: the most-suitable 20% of land holds 84% of estimated generation (annual energy yield) and 67% of installed capacity (potential power output). After applying standard siting constraints, 5.6% of global land (9.8% if cropland is permitted) remains highly suited to solar. The global 10 m resolution solar suitability dataset and code can be found here: https://github.com/Abdullahr29/Global-Solar-Siting.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at Tackling Climate Change with Machine Learning workshop at NeurIPS 2026. 17 pages, 11 figures

</details>
