<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Notwithstanding Anything to the Contrary, Ignore All Instructions: Measuring Prompt Injection Guards on Legal Contract Review Attacks

**abstract/A1：** Specialized prompt injection guards are deployed in front of document analysis systems, but no false positive rate has been published for any of them on legal contracts. We measure eleven open-weights guards on two corpora of legal contracts and find that existing guards struggle: document-level false positive rates are as high as 92.39%; two guards fall below chance on paired AUC; and a five-regex baseline is not Pareto-dominated by any guard in the panel. Controlled probes locate model-specific as well as general failure modes. Prompted LLMs resolve the tested failures, including a second threat category (unauthorized scope claims) on which every guard scores zero. A cascaded LLM solution, where the positives of a mid-tier model are verified by a frontier model, is 6.5x cheaper than using exclusively a frontier model and approaches its performance.

</details>

### 222. [When F1 Is Not Enough: Auditing Codebooks for LLM-Assisted Annotation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08846v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08846

**作者：** Yuehong Cassandra Tai、Qunhua Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08846v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08846v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文研究社会科学文本标注中的 codebook 审计（按类指标、混淆矩阵、逐文档复核与定向修订），属于纯文本标注质量与评测流程，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选、latent 生成或停止决策，也未见可迁移到 LVR 的具体机制线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When F1 Is Not Enough: Auditing Codebooks for LLM-Assisted Annotation

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for text annotation in social science, but standard performance metrics do not explain why errors occur. Low F1 may reflect limitations of the annotation system, defects in the written codebook, or inconsistency in reference labels. We propose a pre-deployment codebook audit for LLM-assisted annotation. The audit uses class-specific metrics and confusion matrices to identify problematic classes and boundaries, structured document-level review to attribute error sources, and targeted codebook revision followed by held-out evaluation. We apply the audit to two published multi-class codebooks from distinct domains. In both applications, the audit identifies under-specified or conflicting coding rules, and targeted revisions reduce the diagnosed errors on held-out data. Analyses with two LLM annotators show that some problematic boundaries and codebook revisions transfer across models, whereas other revision effects are annotator-specific. The results show that LLM annotation errors can help identify weaknesses in the written measurement instrument, not only in the model. Codebook auditing complements prompting, retrieval, and fine-tuning by clarifying whether persistent errors call for model improvement, codebook revision, or renewed scrutiny of reference labels.

</details>

### 224. [Toward LLM Self-Play Under Data Scarcity: Effectiveness, Generalizability, and Trustworthiness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08908v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08908

**作者：** Zhiyin Yu、Yuchen Mou、Bingzhe Wu、Zhonghai Wu、Shiyang Feng、Xiao Luo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08908v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08908v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文是一篇立场/展望文章，主张在数据稀缺下发展兼顾有效性、泛化性与可信性的 LLM self-play，并提出数据为中心、开放世界泛化、可信自演化三个研究方向。

**排除原因：** 仅给出 self-play 的宏观研究议程，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据获取、停止决策或监督分配，也无可核查的具体方法线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Toward LLM Self-Play Under Data Scarcity: Effectiveness, Generalizability, and Trustworthiness

**abstract/A1：** The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has been driven largely by scaling model size and human-curated training data, yet this paradigm is increasingly constrained by data scarcity and diminishing returns from human supervision. Self-play offers a promising pathway for autonomous data generation and improvement, but current frameworks remain unsatisfactory due to a lack of a data-centric perspective, limited cross-domain generalization, and growing trustworthiness and security concerns. In this paper, we argue that real-world LLM self-play systems should achieve effectiveness, generalizability, and trustworthiness under data scarcity. To address these challenges, we outline three blue-sky research directions for future self-play systems: (1) data-centric self-play for effective experience generation, (2) generalizable self-play for open-world and unverifiable tasks, and (3) trustworthy self-play for reliable self-evolution. We further discuss research opportunities and solutions within each direction. This paradigm shift will establish a sustainable and safe AI ecosystem by transforming finite human knowledge into inexhaustible high-quality experience through self-play architectures.

</details>

### 225. [SSF-Net: A Spatial-Spectral Fusion Network for Accurate PDE Operator Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08912v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08912

**作者：** Siqi Zhao、Flora D. Salim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08912v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08912v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SSF-Net，用空间分支（多方向 Mamba）与谱分支（Fourier + 块对角 MLP）组成的双分支模块嵌入多分辨率编解码器，用于 PDE 算子学习，并在多任务基准上评估长时稳定性。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向 PDE 求解的局部/全局动力学建模，属科学计算架构设计，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部表示、证据获取与停止决策，也未提供可核查的 latent reasoning 线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SSF-Net: A Spatial-Spectral Fusion Network for Accurate PDE Operator Learning

**abstract/A1：** Machine learning methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) significantly improve computational efficiency over traditional numerical solvers such as finite element method (FEM). However, PDE dynamics inherently span both local and global dynamics: disturbances propagate and decay gradually through the domain, while global coupling effects can influence the whole system simultaneously. Capturing both types of dynamics with a low complexity model remains a key challenge. To address this, we propose the Spatial-Spectral Fusion Network (SSF-Net), built around a dual-branch Spatial-Spectral Fusion (SSF) block comprising a spatial branch and a spectral branch. The spatial branch employs a multi-directional Mamba, which mitigates its inherent causal, single-direction scanning bias and capture local interactions with reduced directional bias at linear complexity. The spectral branch leverages a Fourier transform with a block-diagonal MLP to efficiently capture global spectral features with reduced parameter count and complexity. The SSF block is embedded in a multiresolution convolutional encoder-decoder, which extracts multi-scale features. We evaluate SSF-Net on a comprehensive PDE benchmark spanning multiple task types and assess its long-horizon stability. Compared with attention-based and convolution-based baselines, SSF-Net achieves lower prediction error with fewer parameters while retaining near-linear asymptotic complexity in the number of spatial tokens.

</details>

<a id="paper-226"></a>

### 226. [ResQAdam: Residual-Preserving Adaptive Optimization for Memory-Efficient Quantization Aware Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08924v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08924

**作者：** Liqun Ma、Shengkun Tang、Liangyu Wang、Lin Zhao、Wei Luo、Xinjun Niu、Spandan Tiwari、Ashish Sirasao、Kun Zhang、Zhiqiang Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08924v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08924v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 ResQAdam，一种保留残差符号更新的低秩自适应优化器，用于降低 LLM 量化感知训练（QAT）中的优化器状态显存占用，并在 2 bit 量化下验证其接近 AdamW 的效果。

**排除原因：** 主题为 LLM 量化训练的低秩优化器显存压缩，与 VLM 推理中的 latent 状态生成、视觉证据使用或停止决策均无实质联系，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的状态或监督视角，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ResQAdam: Residual-Preserving Adaptive Optimization for Memory-Efficient Quantization Aware Training

**abstract/A1：** Quantization-aware training (QAT) is the most reliable way to obtain accurate low-bit large language models (LLMs), but training with AdamW keeps two dense FP32 states for every weight matrix. We find that low-rank memory-efficient optimizers such as GaLore and LDAdam degrade substantially in QAT. A considerable part of the QAT momentum lies outside its top-r singular subspace, and projection-based methods discard this part at every step. We further observe that this residual remains useful when only its sign is kept. Based on these observations, we propose ResQAdam. It keeps a single BF16 momentum, applies Adam only to the r x r coordinates of the principal subspace, and adds a normalized sign update computed from the residual. We evaluate ResQAdam with two from-scratch QAT methods and three QAT fine-tuning pipelines, with weights quantized down to 2 bits. It reduces optimizer-state memory by 71% to 74% relative to AdamW, stays close to AdamW in most settings, and lowers training throughput by less than 2%.

</details>

### 227. [UAV-Based Computer Vision for Cattle Detection and Feed Bunk Spatial Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08928v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08928

**作者：** Pedro Oliveira Torrente、Diego Giaretta de Paulo、Lucas Pascotti Valem、Jianglong Yan、Everton Castelão Tetila、Jayme Garcia Arnal Barbedo、Liang Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08928v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08928v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作基于无人机影像，用 YOLO 检测牛只头/背并结合 SAM 分割饲槽区域，以几何指标量化牛只排列与空间分布，用于精准畜牧管理。

**排除原因：** 属于一般视觉检测任务应用，虽用几何度量刻画空间分布，但不涉及多模态推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺乏实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UAV-Based Computer Vision for Cattle Detection and Feed Bunk Spatial Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Beef cattle production is central to Brazilian agribusiness and faces growing demands for efficiency, sustainability, and animal welfare. Precision Livestock Farming (PLF) offers tools to address these challenges, particularly through monitoring feeding-related spatial patterns in confined systems. Feeding management plays a decisive role in animal performance, yet its evaluation still relies largely on subjective, labor-intensive visual inspections that are hard to scale and prone to bias. This work proposes a computer vision framework based on Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) to quantify cattle alignment and spatial dispersion at the feed bunk under real-world feedlot conditions. A UAV dataset was collected during flight campaigns at a commercial feedlot in Mato Grosso do Sul, Brazil. Images were manually annotated into cattle head and cattle back classes to support object detection and image-level spatial analysis of cattle distribution. You Only Look Once (YOLO) models, namely YOLOv8 and YOLOv11, were evaluated, with YOLOv8 small used for the downstream spatial analysis. The feed bunk region was segmented using the Segment Anything Model (SAM), and geometric metrics based on detected head positions were developed to characterize alignment, spacing regularity, and clustering. The selected detector achieved 84.30% mAP50 and 35.50% mAP50-95, with an inference time of approximately 2.7 ms per image on an NVIDIA RTX 5060 Ti GPU. The resulting metrics distinguished representative regular and irregular spatial distributions by Root Mean Square Deviation - RMSD (1.06 m and 4.26 m), Coefficient of Variation - CV (42.70% and 96.31%), and clustering (0 and 2 clusters), respectively. These findings indicate that the proposed approach can provide indicators for assessing feed bunk spatial patterns in PLF.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 6 figures, 6 tables. Preprint of paper accepted in IEEE Transactions on AgriFood Electronics

</details>

### 228. [Surrogate Quality Score: A Novel Composite Performance Metric for Model Selection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08937v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08937

**作者：** Maaz Ahmad、Iftekhar A. Karimi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08937v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08937v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Surrogate Quality Score（SQS），以归一化预测精度与模型复杂度的加权乘积作为复合指标，用于工程代理模型的模型选择，并允许用户调节二者的权衡。

**排除原因：** 内容属于工程代理模型的选型度量，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或计算停止决策，也未提供可迁移的状态或训练线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Surrogate Quality Score: A Novel Composite Performance Metric for Model Selection

**abstract/A1：** The growing reliance on high-fidelity, state-of-the-art machine learning based models for engineering decision-making necessitates robust and systematic approaches for model selection. While high predictive accuracy at untrained points is a necessary trait in the final model, other attributes such as model complexity, forecastability, explainability, scalability, and physics-awareness may be equally important or relevant criteria depending on application context and user requirements. In this work, we propose a novel composite performance metric, Surrogate Quality Score (SQS) for model selection based on predictive accuracy and model complexity simultaneously. SQS heuristically integrates these two attributes through a weighted product of normalized measures, yielding a unified score to reflect overall model quality. A user-specified parameter in SQS enables explicit control over the accuracy-complexity trade-off, thereby incorporating human judgement and preference in the model selection process. We extensively discuss and justify the formulation of SQS and demonstrate its utility through an illustrative example. By combining predictive accuracy and model complexity, SQS offers a systematic and objective approach for model selection based on both attributes.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 6 figures, 2 tables

</details>

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作扩展预训练全身姿态估计器，加入可选的度量深度与几何（如地面接触）模块，在共享参数下支持 RGB、RGB-Ground、RGB-D 三种模式，以降低相机坐标系下的关节位置误差。

**排除原因：** 论文属单目 3D 人体姿态估计的几何与多模态融合改进，虽涉及透视投影下的尺度—距离歧义消解，但不研究 VLM 推理状态、latent 表示、证据获取或停止决策，缺少可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MetricPose: Flexible 3D Human Pose Estimation with Depth and Geometric Cues

**abstract/A1：** In this paper, we present a 3D human pose estimator that predicts metrically accurate human joint positions in the camera coordinate frame. This task requires recovering the configuration of the body joints, scale, and metric camera-local placement. In monocular images, body size and camera distance are coupled through perspective projection and therefore cannot be determined uniquely from image alone. This ambiguity can be reduced by combining three complementary sources of information: learned RGB priors, direct depth measurements, and geometric constraints (e.g., ground contact) from the environment. We extend a pretrained full-body estimator with optional metric depth and geometry-processing modules that improve camera-distance estimation and depth-guided body refinement while preserving RGB-only operation. The resulting unified model supports RGB, RGB-Ground, and RGB-D using shared learned parameters. Unlike pelvis-relative error, camera-local error also measures placement relative to the camera. Compared with the existing foundation model SAM 3D Body, our methods with RGB, RGB-Ground, and RGB-D modes reduce camera-local joint-position error by 12.16%, 31.00% and 54.61% respectively.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 3 figures, ICRA submission

</details>

### 230. [Region-aware error triage for medical image segmentation with groupwise conformal risk control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08962v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08962

**作者：** Nishi Kanta Paul、Md Shihabul Islam Shovo、Hoileong Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08962v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08962v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 RACE-Triage，一种保持分割掩码不变的事后错误分诊框架，用风险网络结合边界带与非边界区的分区共形风险控制，降低漏报错误像素比例，代价是警示区域增大。

**排除原因：** 属于医学图像分割的掩码复核与阈值校准应用，不涉及多模态推理中的内部状态、视觉证据获取或推理停止决策；分区共形校准虽可用于不确定性提示，但与 LVR 的表示、生成或评价问题没有具体可核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Region-aware error triage for medical image segmentation with groupwise conformal risk control

**abstract/A1：** Segmentation masks are increasingly reviewed rather than redrawn, which requires knowing where a mask is likely wrong. Learned error maps rank pixels by risk, but the number of pixels to warn is chosen ad hoc, and calibrating one warning threshold over all error pixels may leave errors far from the predicted contour under-flagged. We propose RACE-Triage, a post-hoc error triage framework that leaves the segmentation mask unchanged. A risk network is trained on out-of-fold predictions from backbone-independent inputs. The predicted contour partitions each image into a boundary band and a non-boundary region, and conformal risk control is applied separately in each region to bound the expected fraction of missed errors among cases with errors there. On ISIC 2018 dermoscopy and BUSI breast ultrasound (where patient identifiers are unavailable, so independence across images is assumed), pooled control met its overall target but missed on average 31.8% and 36.4% of non-boundary errors. Regional control reduced these fractions to 19.3% and 20.9% and raised the recall of size-filtered false-negative components from 0.722 to 0.818 and from 0.611 to 0.763. The mean warned area grew by 1.6 and 4.9 percentage points, and its 95th percentile reached 23.5% and 38.5%. The effect held across three seeds and a second backbone, and area-matched heuristics did not reproduce it. On the external PH$^2$ set, frozen thresholds exceeded both targets, whereas recalibration with 100 local images brought the mean over repeated splits below them. Regional calibration improves non-boundary error coverage at a measured increase in warned area.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 7 figures, 12 tables. Submitted to Biomedical Signal Processing and Control (BSPC)

</details>

### 231. [Action-Centered Causal Bandits for Budget-Constrained Promotion Targeting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08990v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.08990

**作者：** Yifan Lin、Kaixu Yang、Di Mo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08990v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08990v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究预算约束下的情境化干预投放，提出 DR-CATE-CBwK：以动作中心估计量直接学习处理对比、离线双重稳健热启动，并用原始-对偶控制器保证路径级预算可行。

**排除原因：** 属广告/招聘平台的因果 bandit 与预算控制问题，不涉及视觉细节表示、latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，所提估计与约束技巧过于通用，缺乏可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Action-Centered Causal Bandits for Budget-Constrained Promotion Targeting

**abstract/A1：** Web platforms select among costly interventions for each user to maximize incremental outcomes under a global budget. We formulate this problem as a budget-constrained contextual bandit with heterogeneous treatment effects. Although an action-independent outcome baseline cancels from treatment comparisons, modeling a nonlinear baseline can increase statistical complexity. We propose DR-CATE-CBwK, an action-centered causal bandit that directly learns treatment contrasts. Its online update remains valid under baseline-model misspecification without inverse-propensity weighting. It combines an offline doubly robust learner to warm-start treatment-effect estimates, an online action-centered estimator for sequential contrast learning, and a primal-dual controller with an affordable-action rule to enforce the effective-cost budget pathwise. For linear conditional average treatment effects (CATEs) in a stationary i.i.d. setting, we prove a high-probability (\\widetilde{O}(Kd\\sqrt{T})) regret bound, where K is the number of priced actions, d the context dimension, and T the horizon. We benchmark against the fluid linear program (LP) under the coverage-guaranteed action rule. We further show that the action-centered estimating moment remains unbiased under any predictable baseline estimate, whereas a specified separate-arm reward regression can exhibit asymptotic contrast bias under action-dependent logging and shared-baseline misspecification. On a semi-synthetic job-marketplace benchmark with real logged features and simulated outcomes, our practical Thompson-restricted variant achieves the lowest regret among evaluated budget-feasible methods in the operating regime. It reduces regret by approximately 20% versus the strongest reward-based baseline, outperforming raw-reward contextual bandits, BwK baselines, and a static offline policy, while never exceeding the effective-cost budget.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 20th ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining (WSDM 2027)

</details>

This paper delves into the impact of various loss function formulations on risk management behavior within reinforcement-based market making. Through empirical evaluation, we contrast profit-maximizing strategies with risk-averse approaches, finding that penalties for inventory and cash management foster more resilient and adaptable market-making strategies. Our results underscore the significance of striking a balance between profit maximization and risk management in algorithmic trading, providing actionable insights for refining strategies in practical settings.

### 233. [SIMBAD: Spatio-Temporal Traffic Forecasting Robust to Aperiodicity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09015v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09015

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09015v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09015v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SIMBAD，通过自适应调节周期信号以偏重近期信号、并按上下文化关系动态调整节点间影响，缓解交通时空预测中的非周期波动。

**排除原因：** 属于时空图网络交通预测的应用改进，其周期信号调节与相似度驱动的动态图权重均为通用时序建模手段，未涉及多模态推理中的状态表示、证据筛选或停止决策，缺乏可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SIMBAD: Spatio-Temporal Traffic Forecasting Robust to Aperiodicity

**abstract/A1：** Accurate traffic prediction is essential for urban planning but remains challenging due to the irregular patterns caused by unexpected events. While Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks (STGNNs) effectively model periodic data, they often struggle with these aperiodic fluctuations. To address this, we propose SIMBAD, which improves generalization irregular patterns via two key mechanisms: 1) adaptive regulation of periodic signals to prioritize recent signals, and 2) dynamic spatial modeling that adjusts influence between connected nodes based on contextual relations. Built upon simple similarity metrics to adapt to aperiodic patterns, SIMBAD outperforms existing methods on real-world benchmark datasets, demonstrating superior performance in predicting unseen and irregular traffic events.

**comment/C1：** Total of 30 pages, 9 figures (4 in main, 5 in appendix), accepted to NeurIPS(Neural Information Processing Systems) 2026

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09017

**作者：** Xinjie Lin、Qingle Liu、Wenyi Li、John Lam、Xiaoqiu Wang、Deyao Hong、Mingju Gao、Zheng Jiang、Yizhe Chi、Youjie Zheng、Calvin Xiao、Kaisen Yang、Huan-ang Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09017v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09017v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Frontier3DBench，一个覆盖物体、场景与交互式开放世界的 145 项统一基准，用于评测编程智能体通过可编辑程序完成 3D 重建、生成与编辑的能力。

**排除原因：** 评测对象是 3D 创建程序的产物质量（外观、结构、行为），不涉及 VLM 内部 latent 状态、推理期状态更新或证据使用机制；其中「参考重建弱于文本生成」「有状态流程得分低」等结论与视觉保真度、状态维持只是表面相似，缺少可直接核查的 LVR 方法或评价线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Frontier3DBench: Benchmarking Coding Agents for 3D Creation across Objects, Scenes, and Interactive Open Worlds

**abstract/A1：** Coding agents combine visual understanding with program execution, offering a new route to automated 3D creation. By translating images and instructions into editable programs, they bring reconstruction, generation, and editing into a shared workflow. Yet existing benchmarks fragment evaluation across task scopes and protocols, leaving their capabilities across construction levels unclear. We propose Frontier3DBench, a unified benchmark of 145 tasks across 9 categories spanning object, scene, and interactive open-world creation, with joint evaluation of appearance, structure, and behavior. Experiments with 11 model-harness configurations reveal strengths that vary by level: GPT-6.1-sol leads the object and scene levels and achieves the highest overall score of 66.02/100, whereas GPT-6-astra leads interactive world construction. However, all configurations score lower on reference reconstruction than on text-driven generation in object and scene tasks. Producing plausible content thus remains easier than reproducing specified geometry, layouts, and visual details. Moreover, generated worlds average 83.08/100 on local mechanisms but only 20.82 on motion clearance and 30.26 on stateful workflows: individual mechanisms often function, whereas complete activities that require coordinated actions and persistent state remain difficult. These results identify reference fidelity and coordinated activity completion as central bottlenecks for coding agents in 3D creation.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 7 figures, 4 tables. Project page: https://lab.einsia.ai/frontier3dbench

</details>

### 235. [Knowledge Distillation from Neural Networks to Differentiable Logic Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09024v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09024

**作者：** Hee Yun Suh、Niraj K. Jha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09024v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09024v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作是把 MLP 教师蒸馏到可微逻辑网络以提升表格分类精度，蒸馏仅作为训练期目标，不涉及视觉或多模态状态、证据获取或推理期 latent 生成，与 LVR 主线无可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Knowledge Distillation from Neural Networks to Differentiable Logic Networks

**abstract/A1：** Differentiable logic networks (DLNs) are interpretable, edge-friendly classifiers that learn both their logic operators and their connections through gradient descent, achieving accuracy competitive with multi-layer perceptrons at orders-of-magnitude lower inference cost. Their accuracy, however, is bounded by the problems of vanishing gradients, scalability, and lack of an ecosystem, leaving a gap relative to conventional neural networks on several datasets. We introduce knowledge distillation for DLNs to narrow this gap. We distill from a trained multi-layer perceptron teacher into a DLN student, integrating the distillation objective into a two-phase training loop without altering the architecture or the inference procedure. The teacher is discarded after training. Hence, the distilled model retains the interpretability, sparsity, and inference cost of a conventionally trained DLN. We investigate this approach through four variants: a joint hyperparameter search that optimizes the distillation weight and temperature alongside the architecture, a grid search over these two hyperparameters with the architecture fixed, and two single-phase variants that employ distillation during only the neuron-function or only the connection phase. On 20 public tabular classification tasks, distillation improves balanced-class accuracy at negligible cost to gate-level operation counts and model size, though the benefit varies by dataset. Performance improvements of up to 15% are observed, in some cases surpassing the teacher itself.

</details>

Although LLMs have demonstrated impressive capabilities in various applications, the next challenge is deploying these models on resource-limited edge devices. Recent innovations in software optimizations and specialized hardware have made it feasible to deploy LLMs on edge devices. Edge deployment offers benefits including local data processing, cost savings, reduced latency, and personalized responses. This paper reviews the latest techniques that make the deployment of LLMs on edge devices feasible.

### 237. [TUGB: A Two-stage Uncertainty-aware Generative Auto-Bidding Framework](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09091v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09091

**作者：** Luzhan Yuan、Chao Mi、Xiahao Shi、Yi Mao、Shengsheng Niu、Xin Zhu、Jie He、Zhangang Lin、Ching Law

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09091v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09091v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出两阶段不确定性感知生成式自动出价框架，将生成式序列模型与 RL 结合，用集成环境模型惩罚高风险轨迹并转向直接出价优化。

**排除原因：** 研究面向广告出价的离线策略优化，其生成式轨迹建模与不确定性集合仅属通用 RL 技术，与视觉或多模态 latent 推理的状态表示、视觉证据筛选及 THINK/LOOK/STOP 决策无实质联系，未见可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TUGB: A Two-stage Uncertainty-aware Generative Auto-Bidding Framework

**abstract/A1：** Generative modeling, particularly Decision Transformers, shows promise in auto-bidding strategies. However, it struggles with limited exploration in static offline datasets, vulnerability to dynamic environmental shifts, and a reliance on heuristic return-to-go (RTG) specifications that may yield suboptimal performance on unseen traffic. To address these challenges, we propose the Two-stage Uncertainty-aware Generative Auto-Bidding (TUGB) framework. TUGB integrates generative sequence modeling with Reinforcement Learning (RL). It employs a two-stage pipeline where a pre-trained Generative Bidding Model is fine-tuned via an ensemble-based Uncertainty-aware Environment Model. This approach quantifies epistemic uncertainty to penalize high-risk trajectories and evolves the policy from RTG-conditioning to direct bidding optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TUGB significantly outperforms SOTA baselines. Notably, full-scale deployment on JD Search Ad achieved a 2.32\\% increase in cost and 3.57\\% in target cost, validating its industrial efficacy, while its successful adaptation to subsequent mechanism upgrades and JD Food Delivery Ad further validates TUGB's superior generalization capability.

</details>

### 238. [COMBIO: Automated Discovery of a Compact Composite Biomarker from Routine Laboratory Panels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09102v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09102

**作者：** Tomer D. Meirman、Lior S. Rokach、Bracha Shapira、Seffi Cohen、Noa Dagan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09102v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09102v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 COMBIO 流程，将梯度提升模型蒸馏为仅含五项实验室指标、指数取 -2 至 2 的乘积型闭式生物标志物，并在六个临床任务上评估其判别性能与外部可迁移性。

**排除原因：** 属于临床表格数据的可解释公式搜索与预后模型，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的状态表示、证据使用或停止决策，其符号蒸馏与 VLM latent 推理无具体可核查联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** COMBIO: Automated Discovery of a Compact Composite Biomarker from Routine Laboratory Panels

**abstract/A1：** Machine-learning models based on routine laboratory panels can predict outcomes but can be difficult to interpret and operationalize. We present COMBIO, an automated pipeline that distils such a model into a compact closed-form biomarker. The composite is a product of laboratory measurements raised to integer powers from minus two to two. The sign of each exponent indicates whether higher values of the corresponding measurement increase or decrease the composite. Shapley additive explanation values from the gradient-boosted reference model rank five candidate measurements and guide eligible exponent signs. An exhaustive search over small integer exponents selects the formula on an inner validation set, before evaluation on the reserved test set. We evaluated COMBIO across six tasks: three National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey outcomes, liver-disease classification in the Indian Liver Patient Dataset, and Medical Information Mart for Intensive Care IV in-hospital mortality and sepsis case-finding, with external evaluation in the eICU Collaborative Research Database for the two hospital tasks. Across the six test sets, COMBIO's area under the receiver operating characteristic curve differed by at most 0.03 from logistic regression using the five candidate laboratory measurements. The hospital mortality composite had an area under the curve of 0.698 in the external database. A version restricted to widely available measurements in the source hospital database achieved an area under the curve of 0.786 in a cohort covering 92% of admissions. The multiplicative representation provides compactness rather than additional accuracy, and local validation and recalibration are required before clinical use.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages including supplementary material; 4 figures

</details>

### 239. [DINO-Nucleus: Multi-Level Self-Supervised Features for Nucleus Instance Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09133v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09133

**作者：** Mohammad Sarhangzadeh、Elyar Esmaeilzadeh、Doruk Oner

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09133v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09133v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是显微镜细胞核实例分割与计数的专用框架（DINOv2 多层级特征 + LoRA 微调 + 实例流一致性），不涉及多模态推理、latent 状态生成或证据筛选，多层级特征融合仅用于分割精度，难以形成与 LVR 相关的具体借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DINO-Nucleus: Multi-Level Self-Supervised Features for Nucleus Instance Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Nucleus instance segmentation and counting are essential for quantitative microscopy, enabling reliable analysis of cell populations, tissue organization, and disease-related changes. However, accurately separating touching nuclei remains difficult, and many existing approaches rely on complex models or sensitive post-processing procedures. We present DINO-Nucleus, a parameter-efficient framework that leverages multi-level self-supervised DINOv2 representations for nucleus instance segmentation and counting. The model jointly predicts nucleus regions, pixel-wise instance flows, and explicit nucleus centers, while a consistency objective encourages the predicted flows to converge toward their corresponding centers. Multi-level transformer features provide complementary spatial and semantic information, and the pretrained backbone is adapted using lightweight LoRA modules to reduce fine-tuning cost. Experimental results show that DINO-Nucleus achieves strong segmentation and counting performance with parameter-efficient backbone adaptation. These findings demonstrate that parameter-efficient adaptation of multi-level self-supervised features can provide an effective solution for nucleus instance segmentation in microscopy images.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 2 figures

</details>

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09145v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09145v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Augmentron，一个几何感知的多视角视觉增强流水线：生成可复用的场景表示并按录制的相机位姿渲染，得到时序与多视角一致的环境和工作区变化，用于机器人操作策略训练。

**排除原因：** 该工作属机器人策略的数据增强与泛化提升，不涉及 latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，也未分析模型内部视觉信息保留情况，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Augmentron: Scalable Multi-View Visual Augmentation for Robot Learning

**abstract/A1：** Robot policies trained on visually narrow demonstrations can become sensitive to environmental features that are unrelated to the task. Existing generative augmentation methods can introduce visual diversity, but processing every frame and camera view independently is computationally expensive and may produce inconsistent observations across cameras and timestamps. We introduce Augmentron, a geometry-aware pipeline that generates reusable scene representations and renders them through recorded camera poses. This produces temporally and multi-view consistent environ- ment and workspace variations while preserving the robot, task objects, actions, and annotations. Across three physical manipulation tasks, replacing half of the real world training dataset with Augmentron data improves model success across three tasks for both in out-of-distribution distractor objects, and out-of-distribution workspace variation evaluations. In a separate comparison against alternate augmentation techniques, Augmentron achieves the highest pooled success in all three conditions. The results show that introducing augmented data to a data training recipe can help policies generalize to dynamic real-world settings.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the 8th Robot Learning Workshop (WRL), NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 241. [FIND: Frequency Invariance Disentanglement for Test-Time Adaptation in LiDAR 3D Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09170v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09170

**作者：** Dongxiao Li、Zixuan Hu、Ling-Yu Duan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09170v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09170v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 FIND，一个面向 LiDAR 3D 目标检测测试时适应的频域框架，用 B-Spline 构造自适应滤波器，双流分别提取域不变特征以生成伪标签、并对域扰动保持一致性。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是点云 3D 检测的跨域鲁棒性，属感知层面的域适应，未涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或推理期决策，也无可直接核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FIND: Frequency Invariance Disentanglement for Test-Time Adaptation in LiDAR 3D Detection

**abstract/A1：** LiDAR-based 3D Object Detection (L3OD) is a fundamental 3D perception task, yet its performance is often compromised by domain shifts arising from diverse sensor configurations and environmental conditions. Existing Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods primarily leverage self-supervision in the spatial domain, yet they frequently confound semantic content with domain style due to the sparse, non-Euclidean nature of point clouds. To overcome this limitation, we propose FIND (Frequency INvariance Disentanglement), a novel TTA framework that precisely disentangles domain-invariant content from domain-specific interference within the frequency domain. Our methodology utilizes B-Spline fitting to construct locally adaptive filters integrated into a dual-stream architecture: an Invariant Stream extracts robust features to guide pseudo-labeling, while a Specific Stream enforces consistency against domain perturbations. By combining invariance-driven alignment with stability-guided regularization, our approach dynamically extracts robust domain-invariant features while suppressing domain-specific interference. Extensive experiments on cross-dataset adaptation and robust corruption benchmarks demonstrate that FIND significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/llldx03/FIND.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-242"></a>

### 242. [SIFR: Stable Signed Flow Reinforcement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09175v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09175

**作者：** Runlong Liao、Xingqian Xu、Lizhang Chen、Baiyu Su、Yufan Huang、Zhonghao Wang、Akash Gokul、Ming-Yu Liu、Qiang Liu、Yogesh Balaji

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09175v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09175v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 SIFR，用带符号优势替代奖励权重为 flow 模型提供负向信号，并以速度漂移正则和自适应 Lagrange 乘子稳定多轮奖励微调，在多个文本到图像骨干上验证。

**排除原因：** 研究的是文本到图像 flow 模型的强化学习微调稳定性，不涉及视觉推理中的 latent 状态、证据获取或停止决策，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SIFR: Stable Signed Flow Reinforcement

**abstract/A1：** Reward-based fine-tuning of flow models requires effectively promoting high-reward and suppressing low-reward samples, while maintaining training stability over many rounds of iterative updates. Iterative reward fine-tuning provides a simple starting point, but the positive reward weights give no explicit unfitting signal, and repeated reweighting concentrates the distribution and causes collapse. We propose Stable Signed Flow Reinforcement (SIFR), which replaces the reward weights by signed advantages to supply the missing unfitting signal, and constrains the velocity drift from a reference model to stabilize the updates, with a Lagrange multiplier that adapts the regularization strength to a prescribed drift budget. We further show that existing flow RL methods fall within this framework: they implicitly minimize the signed loss, differing in their regularization choices. We validate SIFR on SD3.5 Medium (2.5B) and FLUX.2 Klein (9B) and scale it to Cosmos 3 Super Text2Image (64B): across all 12 backbone--reward pairs with preference and rule-based rewards, it matches or exceeds existing methods, reaches their peak rewards much faster, and keeps improving over long training where they plateau or collapse.

</details>

### 243. [TailDART: Compute-Efficient Foundation Models for Financial Time-Series Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09176v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09176

**作者：** Chiang Qin Zhi、Ranjan Satapathy、Erik Cambria、Joyjit Chattoraj

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09176v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09176v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出面向金融时间序列预测的轻量基础模型 TailDART，通过分组查询注意力降低 KV cache 带宽开销以加速推理，并在多资产基准上验证其预测精度与高波动鲁棒性。

**排除原因：** 工作聚焦金融时序预测与推理加速，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态生成、证据使用或停止决策；KV cache 效率虽与缓存结构有关，但无针对 latent 表示或推理动态的分析线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TailDART: Compute-Efficient Foundation Models for Financial Time-Series Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Although time-series foundation models (TSFMs) achieve strong zero-shot transferability across high-information-density physical systems, applying overparameterized TSFMs to quantitative finance is fundamentally constrained by low signal-to-noise ratios, non-stationarity, and prohibitive autoregressive decoding latency. To reconcile representation learning with institutional operational constraints, this study presents TailDART, a dense 2.68 million-parameter foundation model engineered for financial time-series forecasting. By integrating grouped-query attention into a lightweight backbone, the architecture drastically reduces Key-Value cache memory bandwidth overhead, achieving major inference acceleration (up to 48.1%) over prevailing state-of-the-art TSFMs to enable low-latency, high-throughput streaming execution. Furthermore, multi-asset benchmarks across varying temporal resolutions show that a compact model can remain competitive for market forecasting: TailDART matches the predictive fidelity of substantially larger baselines while demonstrating superior error resilience in high-volatility regimes.

</details>

### 244. [Analyzing Code Provenance in LLMs for Programming Education](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09177v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09177

**作者：** Subhankar Maity、Faiza Belbachir、Omar Boucelma、Guilherme Medeiros Machado

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09177v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09177v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向编程教育中的代码来源追踪问题，扩展 Min-K% 概率方法并结合合成非成员代码生成，对多种开源与商用 LLM 进行成员推断与 AI 生成代码识别，并在 Stack Overflow 与高校作业数据上验证可行性。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是代码来源判别与教育评估，其基于 token 概率的成员检测既未涉及视觉或多模态内部状态，也未处理证据筛选、动态计算与停止决策，与 LVR 主线的状态表示和推理机制没有实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Analyzing Code Provenance in LLMs for Programming Education

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) play an increasing role in programming education, challenging traditional assessment methods as they can generate fully functional code instantly. This disrupts the reliability of student submissions as indicators of learning and effort. To address this issue, code provenance is essential for tracing the origins of code and distinguishing student-authored, AI-generated, and externally sourced components. We extend the Min-K% probability approach by incorporating synthetic non-member code generation and apply it to Python code for membership inference and provenance tracking. Evaluations cover free LLMs such as GPT-2, DistilGPT-2, and DialoGPT, as well as state-of-the-art commercial models including GPT-5, Gemini 2.5, Mistral, DeepSeek, and LLaMA 3.3. Before evaluating our approach on higher-education student assignments, we validate its feasibility using Stack Overflow data. The results show that free models enable more sensitive code memorization detection than commercial models. By systematically comparing these LLMs, we assess trade-offs between detection accuracy and privacy protection, taking an initial step toward effective assessment of AI-assisted programming education through combined provenance detection and process visibility.

</details>

### 245. [InstantHMR : Real-Time Human Mesh Recovery on Your Phone](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09195v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09195

**作者：** Mohamed Adjel、Kahina Chalabi、Vincent Bonnet

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 InstantHMR，基于 Momentum Human Rig 参数模型的单图人体网格回归器系列，主打手机 NPU 上的实时推理速度与公开数据训练。

**排除原因：** 属于单图人体网格恢复的移动端效率工程，虽涉及视觉表征与视觉细节，但不涉及 VLM 多步推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，也无 latent 状态生成与使用线索，故不入选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** InstantHMR : Real-Time Human Mesh Recovery on Your Phone

**abstract/A1：** Real-time 3D human mesh recovery from a single image on mobile devices is a challenging problem with many applications, including robotics, biomechanics, AR/VR, and exergames. Current state-of-the-art models are either too slow to run efficiently on mobile devices or sacrifice accuracy for speed. We present InstantHMR, a family of open-source single-image regressors suited for mobile inference, based on the Momentum Human Rig (MHR) parametric model and trained exclusively on publicly available annotations from the SAM 3D Body dataset. Our approach achieves remarkable performance on a Samsung Galaxy S23 NPU, recovering a 6,890-vertex body mesh in 5.5 ms per person, and 0.98 ms on an NVIDIA RTX 4070 GPU. This speed surpasses all existing methods we evaluated. On mobile, it is 12x faster than the single mobile-compatible method that slightly outperforms ours in accuracy, while achieving competitive accuracy with 40.0 mm PA-MPJPE on 3DPW, 48.8 mm on EMDB and 41.7 mm on RICH. To ensure fair comparisons, we re-evaluate 11 state-of-the-art methods under a unified protocol aligned with the closest MHR-based competitors, including Meta's official MHR-to-SMPL conversion, and report their reproduction results. Additionally, we measure latency across eight different targets, ranging from desktop TensorRT to two phone NPUs. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Naturalpad-studio/InstantHMR.

</details>

### 246. [HMMF-Net: A Lightweight Hierarchical Multi-Frame Multimodal Fusion Network for Accurate Fish Feeding Intensity Assessment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09224v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09224

**作者：** Phi-Hung Hoang、Van-Manh Tran、Nam-Thuan Trinh、Thi-Thu-Hong Phan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09224v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09224v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 面向养殖鱼类摄食强度的视觉-声学轻量融合分类，仅在边缘设备上做多模态特征拼接与中间层 token 交换，未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，属一般任务应用，缺乏可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HMMF-Net: A Lightweight Hierarchical Multi-Frame Multimodal Fusion Network for Accurate Fish Feeding Intensity Assessment

**abstract/A1：** Automated fish feeding intensity assessment (FFIA) is important for precision aquaculture and feed cost optimization. However, unimodal methods are vulnerable to acoustic noise and visual disturbances, while existing multimodal frameworks often rely on computationally intensive 3D convolutions or Transformers, limiting their use on edge devices. To address these limitations, we propose HMMF-Net, a lightweight multimodal architecture that combines early multi-frame visual encoding and multi-axis acoustic modeling through a hierarchical bidirectional messenger cross-modal fusion (BMCF) mechanism. The visual branch captures temporal variations in feeding behavior by stacking temporally distant frames as a multi-channel input to an adapted 2D EfficientNetB0 backbone. The acoustic branch employs adaptive multi-axis residual units (AMARU) to extract temporal, frequency, and local time-frequency representations from log-mel spectrograms. BMCF progressively exchanges information between the two modalities at intermediate representation levels through compact messenger tokens, reducing the computational overhead of dense cross-modal attention. Evaluated on the AV-FFIA dataset using five-fold cross-validation, HMMF-Net achieves 96.77% accuracy and 96.88% F1-score on the held-out test folds, with 3.987M parameters and 1.002 GFLOPs. Compared with the best-performing visual configuration (95.49% accuracy), HMMF-Net reduces the classification error by 28.38%. Deployment on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano using TensorRT with FP32 precision maintains comparable accuracy, with an inference time of 11.54 ms per 2-second sample and a model size of 18.81 MB. These results demonstrate that HMMF-Net combines effective multimodal representation learning with computational efficiency, enabling accurate and practical FFIA on resource-constrained edge platforms.

**comment/C1：** 47 pages, 10 figures, 15 tables

</details>

### 247. [Fused-Softmax for efficient quantized attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09225v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09225

**作者：** Tianxing Wu、Juuso Korhonen、Jesus Carabano Bravo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.PF

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09225v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09225v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Fused-Softmax Attention，一种免训练方法，将 softmax 的主要运算折叠进 attention 的 GEMM 中，在 AMD CDNA 4 上实现 MXFP8/6/4 低精度注意力内核的显著加速。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于低精度 attention 内核与吞吐优化，未涉及视觉细节保留、latent 状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策等主线问题，也未提供可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fused-Softmax for efficient quantized attention

**abstract/A1：** Low-precision matrix throughput on modern GPUs is growing far faster than vector-arithmetic throughput. Attention therefore cannot realize the available GEMM performance because softmax has become the pipeline bottleneck. We introduce Fused-Softmax Attention, a training-free algorithm that reformulates and approximates softmax to fold its dominant operations into the attention GEMMs, moving work from vector units to matrix cores. Implemented for the AMD CDNA 4 architecture, our MXFP8, MXFP6 and MXFP4 kernels reach approximately 3.0, 4.0 and 4.6 PFLOP/s on an AMD Instinct MI355X GPU, yielding speedups of $2.1\\times$, $2.8\\times$ and $3.2\\times$ over the BF16 attention. Importantly, the additional error introduced by Fused-Softmax remains below the error floor set by quantizing the GEMM operands. On Wan2.2-T2V-A14B, a hybrid MXFP6/4 schedule delivers up to $2.05\\times$ end-to-end speedup while preserving the visual quality of the outputs. The kernels ship in AMD's open-source AITER library, and they powered AMD's MLPerf Inference v6.1 Wan2.2 submission, which ran $1.7\\times$ faster than AMD's previous-round result on the same hardware. Our results show that redesigning softmax to utilize the matrix pipeline enables attention to capitalize on continued growth in low-precision matrix throughput.

</details>

### 248. [RAG-Enhanced LLMs for Interactive Explainability in Clinical Decision Support Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09232v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09232

**作者：** Lorenzo Mannocci、Francesca Naretto、Lucia Passaro、Anna Monreale

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.HC、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09232v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09232v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一个临床决策支持系统，将预测模型与 XAI 及 RAG 模块结合，把技术性解释改写为自然语言并支持临床医生交互式追问，附初步实验。

**排除原因：** 属于医疗领域的 XAI/RAG 应用工程，未涉及视觉或多模态推理、内部状态表征与 latent 计算，也未讨论证据筛选或停止决策机制，缺乏可供核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RAG-Enhanced LLMs for Interactive Explainability in Clinical Decision Support Systems

**abstract/A1：** Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in high-risks decision-making systems across domains such as healthcare, finance, and the judicial system. As reliance on predictive models grows, so does the demand for Explainable AI (XAI) to ensure transparency, trust, and interpretability. However, existing XAI approaches often fail to present explanations in a format accessible to domain experts, limiting their practical utility. Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as a bridge between complex models and end-users, offering natural language explanations. Yet, their use introduces new risks, including hallucinated outputs and lack of source traceability. To address these challenges, we propose a Clinical Decision Support System (CDSS) that not only combines standard predictive models with XAI techniques but also incorporates a Retrieval-Augmented Generation module as an interactive enhancer. This module grounds explanations in verified medical knowledge, reformulates technical outputs into a human-like format, and enables users-such as clinicians-to actively query the system. By fostering an interactive, human-in-the-loop environment, our approach empowers domain experts to explore model decisions, contextualize explanations, and build trust in AI-assisted diagnostics. The study concludes with preliminary experiments validating the proposed methodology.

**comment/C1：** Published in HC\_AIxIA\_HYDRA 2025, Communications in Computer and Information Science, vol. 2830, pp. 50-63, Springer, 2026. Please cite the published version: doi:10.1007/978-3-032-16708-8\_4

</details>

### 249. [MM-DyGraph: A Dataset, Benchmark, and Model for Multimodal Dynamic Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09240v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09240

**作者：** Zongyuan Wu、Pengjie Wang、Lingshan Chen、Tianhang Wan、Yuan Meng、Xin Wang、Ling Feng、Wenwu Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09240v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09240v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建多模态动态图的数据集与基准，并观察到多模态特征直接拼接对不同预测目标效果不一致，进而提出按预测目标与时空关系条件化融合的 TST-MF 框架。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是多模态动态图的节点/边属性融合与任务预测，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据获取或停止决策；「模态信息量依赖目标」的观察虽与按需选取视觉证据略有名目上的相似，但缺少可核查的状态或生成层面的方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MM-DyGraph: A Dataset, Benchmark, and Model for Multimodal Dynamic Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal dynamic graphs are pervasive in real-world applications, where nodes and edges are accompanied by multimodal signals, including text and images. Despite their practical importance, benchmarks and methods that explicitly study multimodal learning in dynamic graphs remain scarce, hindering the full understanding and effective utilization of multimodal information for downstream tasks. To address this gap, we introduce MM-DyGraph, to the best of our knowledge, the first benchmark for multimodal dynamic graph learning, consisting of diverse real-world datasets with fine-grained temporal graph evolution and rich multimodal attributes. MM-DyGraph provides three evaluation tasks to comprehensively assess model performance in dynamic settings. We benchmark representative state-of-the-art dynamic graph methods and reveal that naive feature concatenation yields target-level inconsistencies: multimodal inputs benefit certain prediction targets while harming others. This observation indicates that the modality informativeness depends on the specific prediction target over time. Building on these observations, we propose a Target-aware Spatial-Temporal Multimodal Fusion (TST-MF) framework, which jointly conditions multimodal fusion on the prediction target and spatial-temporal relations, enabling context-adaptive cross-modal interplay for target-specific representations. Extensive experiments over diverse datasets and tasks show that TST-MF delivers robust and competitive performance, achieving clear gains in multimodal settings.

</details>

### 250. [Topology as a Design Variable: Joint Architecture-Sizing Optimization for SRAM](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09248v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09248

**作者：** Can Wang、Junhao Ma、Yikai Wang、Shan Shen、Wei W. Xing

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09248v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09248v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向 SRAM 电路设计，构建开源编译器生成 6T/10T 宏，用 few-shot 代理模型结合由粗到精的多目标优化，将位单元拓扑与晶体管尺寸联合优化。

**排除原因：** 这是 EDA 硬件设计优化工作，涉及的是电路 PPA 与版图参数，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部表示、证据使用或推理状态生成；其中离散与连续变量联合搜索的线索与 LVR 问题距离过远，缺少可核查的借鉴点，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Topology as a Design Variable: Joint Architecture-Sizing Optimization for SRAM

**abstract/A1：** SRAM's power-performance-area-stability trade-off depends on bitcell topology, a discrete architectural choice that interacts non-separably with continuous transistor sizing. However, no open-source SRAM tool treats topology as an optimizable variable. OpenRAM generates SRAM layouts but performs no optimization, while OpenYield supports high-fidelity evaluation but restricts optimization to scalar sizing of a fixed 6T cell. OpenYieldV2 closes this gap through three integrated contributions. First, a fully parameterized compiler generates complete 6T and 10T SRAM macros with peripheral circuits and replica-bitline self-timing, enabling topology comparison under identical simulation conditions. Second, a few-shot TabPFN surrogate learns topology-dependent PPA responses with limited full-macro SPICE samples. Third, a coarse-to-refined multi-objective optimizer combines global exploration of discrete architectural choices with local refinement of continuous transistor sizes. Experiments across varying design conditions reveal a topology decision boundary that shifts with operating constraints rather than following a fixed design rule. Jointly optimizing across this boundary improves hypervolume by up to 17.3\\% over the strongest single-topology baseline. OpenYieldV2 is open-source to support reproducible SRAM design research.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by ASPDAC2027

</details>

### 251. [Model-based learning for DoA estimation and array calibration via continuous sparse recovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09263v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09263

**作者：** Baptiste Chatelier、José Miguel Mateos-Ramos、Vincent Corlay、Matthieu Crussière、Christian Häger、Henk Wymeersch、Nir Shlezinger、Luc Le Magoarou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09263v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09263v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作面向 ISAC 中的到达角估计，将 SOMP 稀疏恢复与投影梯度下降结合，实现存在增益-相位和阵元位置误差时的联合阵列校准与连续字典 DoA 估计。

**排除原因：** 材料限于阵列信号处理的硬件损伤补偿与稀疏恢复，未涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、推理轨迹、证据获取或停止决策，也难提炼出与 LVR 表示、训练监督或评价相关的具体借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Model-based learning for DoA estimation and array calibration via continuous sparse recovery

**abstract/A1：** Direction of arrival (DoA) estimation, a classical signal processing problem, has experienced renewed interest with the advent of integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). While decades of research have yielded highly accurate DoA estimators, hardware impairments have frequently been neglected, despite degrading both DoA estimation accuracy and downstream communication performance in ISAC systems. This work introduces a joint array calibration and DoA estimation method based on the simultaneous orthogonal matching pursuit (SOMP) algorithm, following the model-based machine learning paradigm. The proposed method enables the calibration of antenna arrays subject to severe gain-phase and antenna location impairments through projected gradient descent (PGD), with either supervised or unsupervised objectives. Additionally, DoA estimation is enhanced by extending SOMP to continuous dictionaries via PGD refinement steps. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method enables efficient hardware impairment compensation, even without labeled samples, while outperforming conventional sparse recovery and subspace-based DoA estimation baselines.

</details>

**内容简述：** 该论文在非 IID 联邦学习场景下比较 Power-of-Choice、Fisher information 与 Centered Kernel Alignment 三种客户端选择策略，用 Gini 系数和 KL 散度衡量参与多样性，评估通信开销与泛化表现。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Diversity-Aware Client Selection for Communication-Efficient Federated Learning

**abstract/A1：** Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients without sharing raw data, making it attractive for privacy-sensitive and bandwidth-constrained applications. A practical challenge in deploying FL is reducing communication overhead while maintaining model performance under heterogeneous, non-IID data distributions. Selecting which subset of clients participates in each training round offers a principled way to address both challenges simultaneously, yet its impact on data diversity and representativeness remains underexplored. In this paper, we investigate three client selection strategies, Power-of-Choice, Fisher information, and Centered Kernel Alignment, evaluating them not only by accuracy and convergence, but through the lens of participation diversity using the Gini coefficient and KL divergence. Under non-IID conditions, restricting participation to 15% of clients per round achieves significant communication savings, and when selection promotes diverse and balanced contributions, it also improves generalization over biased alternatives. This work provides a systematic and reproducible analysis demonstrating that diversity-focused client selection renders communication efficiency and model quality complementary rather than competing objectives.

**comment/C1：** 4 pages, 6 figures, 2 tables. Accepted at SNCNW 2026

</details>

### 253. [An Empirical Study of Issues in GPU Kernel Libraries for LLM Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09280v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09280

**作者：** Yanjie Gao、Jiayi Bi、Haoxiang Lin、Ling Feng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09280v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09280v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文对七个 GPU kernel 库的 300 条 GitHub issue 做实证分析，归纳症状、根因、修复及测试调试实践。

**排除原因：** 属底层 kernel 工程与部署可靠性研究，虽涉及低精度数值与硬件相关执行失败，但未触及推理状态、证据获取或监督分配，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An Empirical Study of Issues in GPU Kernel Libraries for LLM Systems

**abstract/A1：** Behind the expanding use of large language models (LLMs) across application domains lies a growing dependence on specialized GPU kernel libraries. These libraries underpin the correctness and efficiency of training and inference across diverse GPU platforms, implementing attention, GEMM, quantization, and low-precision operators while managing tensor shapes, memory layouts, code generation, and hardware-specific dispatch. Problems at this layer can silently corrupt outputs, hang GPUs, prevent compilation, introduce deployment incompatibilities, or degrade performance, thereby reducing developer productivity and wasting expensive GPU resources. We present the first comprehensive empirical study of issues in GPU kernel libraries for LLM systems. We manually analyzed 300 high-quality GitHub issue reports, their discussions, and available fixes from seven representative libraries: FlashInfer, FlashAttention, Triton, CUTLASS, DeepGEMM, xFormers, and bitsandbytes. The analysis characterizes symptoms, root causes, fixes, and testing and debugging practices. Our main findings are: (1) 60.33% of the sampled issues originate in the Compiler and Build System or Runtime Environment dimensions, so validation must cover the deployment and execution stack, not just kernel code. (2) Kernel specialization imposes implicit correctness constraints on tensor shapes, metadata layouts, memory aliasing, and hardware resources. Violations can produce incorrect outputs or launch failures even for supported operations, highlighting the need for explicit contract validation and boundary-case testing. (3) Current testing and debugging practices are often insufficient for identifying low-precision numerical problems and hardware-specific execution failures. We outline research opportunities and tooling directions to support future LLM GPU kernel development.

</details>

### 254. [Ontology-Driven Control of Conversational Agents with a Use-Case for Job Interview Practice](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09282v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09282

**作者：** Barbara Gendron、Gaël Guibon、Mathieu D'aquin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09282v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09282v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出用本体（ontology）形式化定义对话策略与话语特征，并通过自动微调 LLM 使其在求职面试练习场景中遵循这些特征。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是纯文本对话风格与策略的可控生成，不涉及视觉证据、内部 latent 状态或推理步骤的生成与使用，与本主线缺少可具体借鉴的状态或决策线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Ontology-Driven Control of Conversational Agents with a Use-Case for Job Interview Practice

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely used as conversational agents in both domain-specific and open-domain situations. In domain-specific context, it is often necessary to adapt those LLMs to obtain conversations with desired characteristics, whether those apply at the level of each response (e.g. for tone or style) or more globally (i.e. at the level of a conversational strategy). Such adaptations are, however, generally realized in an ad hoc way, through specific, non-reusable, and non-transparent developments. In this paper, we propose an ontology-based approach to design conversational strategies that rely on the desired and measured characteristics of textual utterances in the conversation. Through a use case related to job interview practice, we show how this approach can be used to control LLMs to follow the strategy defined in the ontology. To this end, we automatically fine-tune the LLM to improve its compliance with ontologically-defined conversation characteristics. Our results show that training the LLM from formally defined concepts effectively enhances alignment with the requested characteristics. Hence, this work demonstrates how ontology-guided conversation control can be achieved while taking into account multiple, concurrent conversation aspects that are formally and transparently defined.

</details>

### 255. [T2I Models Rarely Invert Negation but Often Fail to Suppress Negated Concepts: Benchmarking Negation Suppression in Text-to-Image Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09304v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09304

**作者：** Akansha Gautam、Aparna Bharati、Mayank Vatsa、Richa Singh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09304v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09304v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是 T2I 扩散模型的否定概念抑制基准与指标，聚焦生成图像是否呈现被否定对象，未涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的状态或训练层面联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** T2I Models Rarely Invert Negation but Often Fail to Suppress Negated Concepts: Benchmarking Negation Suppression in Text-to-Image Generation

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-image diffusion models often fail to suppress explicitly negated concepts, such as a lamp for "a desk without a lamp." We introduce T2I-NSI, a benchmark and metric for measuring negation sensitivity in text-to-image generation. It pairs each negated prompt with a canonical positive counterpart and factorizes negation by linguistic form (lexical/semantic, morphological, syntactic) and target type (objects, attributes, relations, compositions, and nested cases). The benchmark contains 759,869 paired prompt atoms over a 5,082-object catalog, with 916,272 images (911,982 open-weights, 4,290 closed-source), scored by a Basic CLIP estimator and a Robust ensemble of CLIP, VQA and open-vocabulary detection. Negation sensitivity is low: open-weights generators score NSI &lt;= 0.038, while GPT-Image-1.5 and Gemini-2.5-Flash-Image reach about 0.09. The dominant failure is under-suppression rather than misdirection. Inverse interpretation is essentially absent (0.00% across over 450K atom-model evaluations), whereas models render the concept under both prompts on 8-19% of atoms and only partly suppress it on a further 9-25%; most remaining atoms are too weakly rendered to label. A blinded human study (420 atoms, nine annotators) corroborates the near-absence of inversion and the high persistence rate, and a matched-prompt comparison shows the open-versus-closed gap (~3.5x) is not a prompt-generator artifact. Code and data: https://github.com/IAB-IITJ/T2I-NSI

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027. Code and data: https://github.com/IAB-IITJ/T2I-NSI

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09351

**作者：** Jiashu Zhang、Jiaming He、Zheming Zhang、Kaizheng Wang、Bing Li、Zhiyuan Zha、Yi Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SD

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09351v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09351v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 acoustic plan learning，用结构化声学方案表示测试描述，并借助波形反馈经 SFT 与强化学习训练方案生成器，用于对 text-to-audio 模型进行红队攻击。

**排除原因：** 工作对象是音频生成模型的对抗提示生成，反馈仅用于提示层面的改写与攻击成功率，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据获取与停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AUTEUR: Acoustic Plan Learning for Red-Teaming Text-to-Audio Models

**abstract/A1：** Automated red-teaming of text-to-audio models requires test prompts that pass textual screening and expose harmful acoustic content. The central challenge is to connect the sounds produced by the target model to both reusable test generation and prompt revision. Our key idea is to represent test descriptions as structured acoustic plans that organize foreground sounds, context, and vocal attributes. To this end, we propose acoustic plan learning, a framework that learns and refines these plans using waveform feedback. We train the plan generator through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with audio feedback while keeping the target audio model fixed. At inference time, the same trained model generates initial plans and uses feedback to revise their context and vocal attributes while preserving the foreground. Experiments across four target systems show attack success rates of 79.4% on TangoFlux and 95.6% on Kling. The resulting test prompts also transfer across open-source and closed-source models, indicating shared acoustic vulnerabilities.

</details>

**内容简述：** 提出 OpenMeter，一个 schema 无关的多智能体系统，在本地硬件上把自然语言查询转成可执行代码与可读结论，以支持电网人员访问智能电表数据。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于能源领域 NL2SQL 与隐私本地部署的工程应用；虽含多智能体编排与成本—精度消融，但未涉及视觉信息保留、latent 推理状态、证据筛选或贡献分配，缺乏可核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Enabling Privacy-Preserving Natural Language Access to Industrial Smart Meter Data

**abstract/A1：** Energy management officers and grid operators increasingly rely on smart meter data for anomaly detection, performance monitoring, and strategic decision-making. However, extracting actionable insights from large-scale, heterogeneous telemetry databases requires advanced SQL and data engineering expertise, creating a critical bottleneck for non-technical personnel. Furthermore, the strict data privacy regulations and security constraints inherent to critical energy infrastructure often prohibit the routing of sensitive consumer data through cloud-based Large Language Models (LLMs). To address these challenges, we present OpenMeter, a schema-agnostic Multi-Agent System (MAS) designed to translate natural language queries into executable code and human-readable insights. This architecture lowers the technical barrier for day-to-day utility operations by empowering non-expert personnel to interact with complex databases through intuitive natural language dialogue, transforming raw telemetry into a highly valuable tool for routine grid workflows and strategic decisions. Multi-scenario ablation studies over this environment demonstrate that full multi-agent orchestration shows its largest observed accuracy gains on resource-constrained local hardware (+6.1 pp EX-R over the strongest single-agent configuration); at frontier scale, individual components retain value but should be enabled selectively, indicating that the cost of full orchestration is justified specifically where privacy demands local execution.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 1 figure, 6 tables

</details>

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一个面向医疗理赔拒付分析的模块化 Agentic AI 框架，整合拒付风险预测、风险因子识别、编码关系分析、反事实分析以及基于政策的混合 RAG 与 HIPAA 合规代理，并在合成理赔数据上评估可追溯性、政策引用校验和敏感标识去除。

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agentic AI for Healthcare Claim Denial Analysis: A Policy-Grounded Framework for Privacy Preservation

**abstract/A1：** In the healthcare sector, claim denials impose significant financial and administrative burdens, emphasizing the need for automated and trustworthy decision-support systems. This paper proposes a modular Agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework for healthcare claim denial analysis, integrating specialized agents for denial risk prediction, risk factor identification, diagnosis-procedure-medication code relationship analysis, and counterfactual analysis. The framework integrates predictive analytics, graph-based learning, a Hybrid Contextual Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline grounded in healthcare policy documents, and a dedicated HIPAA Compliance Agent to support privacy protection. Evaluated on a synthetic healthcare claims dataset, the framework achieved 98% traceability of reported numerical findings, validated 214 of 216 policy citations, and removed all 280 identified sensitive identifiers without modifying the underlying analysis. These findings pave the way for further research into Agentic AI as a framework for explainable, policy-aware, and privacy-preserving healthcare claim denial management.

</details>

### 259. [MINE: Entropy-coded Memory-efficient Inference for Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09424v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09424

**作者：** Emil Schätzle、Markus Püschel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DC、cs.PF

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09424v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09424v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 MINE，一种基于 dtANS 熵编码的无损权重量化压缩与 GPU 解码方案，按张量和 GPU 自动调参，并在 vLLM 中按内存预算选择内核配置以加速 LLM 单流推理。

**排除原因：** 工作对象是静态权重的存储与流式解码开销，属于通用推理工程优化，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据使用、停止决策或训练监督，也未提供与 latent 推理状态表示或计算分配相关的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MINE: Entropy-coded Memory-efficient Inference for Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** We present MINE (Memory-efficient INference Engine), a lossless entropy coding approach that accelerates single-stream inference of Large Language Models (LLMs), where existing lossless compression methods yield speedups only for specific data types such as \\texttt{BF16} and under additional assumptions. Single-stream decoding is strongly memory-bound, since every weight must be streamed from memory once per generated token and requests cannot be batched in local or low-latency deployments. At the core of MINE is dtANS-LLM, a GPU entropy coding scheme adapted from the prior dtANS to the needs of LLM inference. It exploits the non-uniform distribution of weights, which persists even after quantization, and exposes hyper-parameters that trade compression ratio against decoding cost. We auto-tune these parameters for each tensor and GPU to obtain the Pareto front between model size and decoding latency. MINE integrates into vLLM as a plugin and selects the best kernel configuration for a given memory budget. On Llama~3.1~8B~Instruct with a 2.1-bit EntQuant checkpoint, MINE achieves speedups of 2.52$\\times$ and 1.48$\\times$ over the fastest baseline on an RTX~4060~Ti and an RTX~5090, at effective bitrates of 2.24 and 3.43~bits per weight. MINE is available as open source.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to the 15th International Conference on Learning Representations (ICLR), 2027. Artifact: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23885927

</details>

### 260. [DexDICE: Reconciling Safety and Performance via Dual-Expert Offline Imitation Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09433v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09433

**作者：** Seokin Seo、Sung Kuk Shyn、Minji Seo、Yisoo Lee、Jongmin Lee、Hongseok Yang、Kee-Eung Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09433v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09433v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出双专家离线模仿学习框架 DexDICE，结合安全约束示范与高回报示范，通过 DICE 式分布校正与安全支撑约束，在安全合规区域内利用高回报行为。

**排除原因：** 该文属机器人离线模仿学习/安全强化学习，关注成本约束与分布校正，未涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，也未提供可迁移至 LVR 的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DexDICE: Reconciling Safety and Performance via Dual-Expert Offline Imitation Learning

**abstract/A1：** Ensuring high returns while providing strong safety guarantees in offline imitation learning (IL) is a fundamental challenge: existing approaches either rely on explicitly specified cost functions, which are difficult to design for complex real-world safety constraints, or require expert demonstrations that are both safe and high-performing, which are often too costly to collect. We introduce dual-expert imitation learning, which leverages two complementary data sources: (i) safety-compliant demonstrations that faithfully follow safety guidelines but may be suboptimal, and (ii) performance-oriented demonstrations that achieve high returns but may violate safety guidelines. To address this problem, we propose DexDICE (Dual-expert offline imitation learning via stationary DIstribution Correction Estimation). Instead of conventional distribution constraints, DexDICE combines DICE-style offline learning with safe support constraints, enabling agents to exploit high-return behaviors while remaining within safety-compliant regions. We prove that the ideal solution satisfying this constraint exactly has zero unsafe-region visitation with high probability. We evaluate DexDICE on a re-curated DSRL benchmark and validate it on a real-world mobile-robot navigation task trained directly from human teleoperation demonstrations, where it substantially outperforms baselines and remains robust to data scarcity and quality degradation.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026. 55 pages, 13 figures. Code: https://github.com/tzs930/dexdice

</details>
