### 261. [Consensus-Constrained Dual-Federated Optimization with Client-Side Dynamic Weighting for Distributed Android Device Threat Defense](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09470v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09470

**作者：** Mohammad Mashreghi、Mohammad Hossein Badiei、Md. Jalil Piran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CR、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09470v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09470v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 DW-FedADMM，一种结合原始-对偶协调与按性能动态加权的联邦优化框架，用于跨客户端 Android 恶意软件检测，并在三个基准上验证其精度与稳定性。

**排除原因：** 这是联邦学习的优化与隐私训练问题，客户端权重与对偶变量调整服务于模型聚合，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺乏可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Consensus-Constrained Dual-Federated Optimization with Client-Side Dynamic Weighting for Distributed Android Device Threat Defense

**abstract/A1：** Smartphones, smart-home hubs, and wearables are increasingly exposed to sophisticated Android malware, while centralized threat-detection pipelines raise privacy and scalability concerns. Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without collecting raw client data, but partial participation and variation in local model quality can destabilize global optimization. In this article, we propose DW-FedADMM, a federated optimization framework that combines primal-dual coordination with performance-dependent client-side weighting. The dynamic coefficient modulates each participating client's local state before the augmented-Lagrangian update, while the dual variables regulate disagreement between local and global model states. We evaluate DW-FedADMM on three Android malware benchmarks, namely Drebin, Malgenome, and TUANDROMD, with 5, 10, and 15 clients holding IID data shards, partial client participation, and communication budgets of 5 and 10 rounds. Each configuration is repeated with ten random seeds. DW-FedADMM obtains the highest seed-averaged accuracy on Drebin (97.64% +/- 0.20%) and the best mean accuracy in 9 of the 18 dataset-client-round configurations, including 7 of the 9 configurations with a 10-round budget. Pooled over all configurations, it reaches 95.42% accuracy, 94.62% F1-score, 96.06% precision, and 99.45% AUC, compared with 95.32%, 94.51%, 95.56%, and 99.41% for FedADMM, while also giving the lowest mean false-positive rate and the smallest client update norm among the six compared methods. Both primal-dual methods are well ahead of the averaging- and proximal-based baselines on Malgenome and TUANDROMD, where the latter often remain at the majority-class solution when clients are fragmented. The gain of dynamic weighting over FedADMM is most pronounced on Drebin and with the longer communication budget, where it also reduces the variation across seeds.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 6 figures, 8 tables

</details>

**排除原因：** 该文面向腐蚀螺栓连接的剩余功能评估，核心是多模态退化观测对齐与 PDE 发现，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策；虽用到跨模态相似度对齐，但属工程退化建模，与 LVR 主线缺乏实质联系，也无可供核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Physics-Informed Learning Framework for Linking Microscopic Surface Degradation to Bolted Joint Functional Behavior

**abstract/A1：** Returned products in circular engineering often have heterogeneous degradation histories, making remaining function difficult to assess. For bolted joints, corrosion alters surface morphology, contact, friction, preload retention, and mechanical response. This paper presents a physics-informed multimodal framework for linking microscopic and camera-based degradation observations to functional indicators of corroded bolted joints. The workflow defines the corrosion failure mode, generates synchronized inspection and functional-response records, preprocesses image, roughness, exposure, and joint descriptors, aligns microscopy and camera observations through similarity learning, and constructs a calibrated corrosion-severity field. Partial differential equation (PDE) discovery is applied to this field to identify candidate effective mesoscale laws rather than complete electrochemical mechanisms. The corrosion-evolution model is coupled to a separate functional model, with friction coefficient as the primary target and preload loss included when clamp-force data are available. The contribution is a reproducible pathway for physically constrained assessment of degraded bolted-joint behavior.

**comment/C1：** 10, Proceedings of the ASME 2026 International Mechanical Engineering Congress and Exposition (IMECE 2026), November 8-12, 2026, Vancouver, Canada

</details>

### 263. [Atik: A Shared-Mesh Transformer Accelerator for RISC-V](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09522v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09522

**作者：** Ahmed Zeer、Erkan Uslu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.AR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09522v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09522v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Atik，一个面向 RocketChip 的开源 RoCC Transformer 加速器，用同一固定点 MAC mesh 执行矩阵乘与 dense attention，采用流式 online-softmax 避免物化完整注意力分数矩阵，并在 FireSim 上做周期级评测。

**排除原因：** 属于通用 Transformer 推理的硬件加速与物理设计工作，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选、停止决策或 latent 训练监督，缺乏可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Atik: A Shared-Mesh Transformer Accelerator for RISC-V

**abstract/A1：** Transformer inference repeatedly invokes dense matrix multiplication and attention, including its data-dependent softmax normalization. We present Atik, an open-source RoCC accelerator for RocketChip that executes matrix multiplication and dense attention through one shared fixed-point MAC mesh. Atik accepts and returns BF16 tensors, uses a streaming online-softmax schedule that never materializes the full score matrix, and is invoked through a small descriptor-based ABI. We evaluate three meshes cycle-by-cycle on FireSim and report standalone Sky130 physical-design area. Across 137 logged FireSim test records, all tests pass their benchmark tolerance with zero reported output mismatches. Relative to the same scalar Rocket BF16 reference, the 8x8 configuration reaches 31.5x peak matmul speedup and 225.8x peak attention speedup. Hybrid projections for the single-layer workload-derived C models, using directly measured accelerator-kernel cycles, give aggregate speedups of 44.2x, 77.1x, and 32.3x for ViT, TinyBERT, and GPT-2 prefill, respectively; these are not full-model hardware replays.

</details>

### 264. [StockASkill: A Local-First AgentSkill for Intelligent Stock Selection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09528v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09528

**作者：** Xiangjing An、Zhibin Jiao、Aiting Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09528v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09528v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 StockASkill，一个本地优先的量化选股与组合构建 AgentSkill，结合 DuckDB 列式数据库、因子 alpha 模型、CVaR 约束下的 SLSQP 优化与整手离散化，并为 LLM 工具调用提供 JSON 流式接口。

**排除原因：** 内容为金融量化选股与工程加速，虽面向 LLM 工具调用，但未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选、latent 表示或推理期决策停止，缺乏可迁移的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StockASkill: A Local-First AgentSkill for Intelligent Stock Selection

**abstract/A1：** Quantitative equity selection and portfolio construction in emerging and cross-border markets frequently suffer from computational bottlenecks, remote API fragility, survivorship bias, and rigid execution constraints such as discrete board lots and investor-mandated drawdown limits. In this paper, we present StockASkill, a local-first, full-data-driven AgentSkill engineered as a specialized Quant Co-Processor for autonomous AI agents and quantitative researchers. Tailored for agile portfolios (10^5 ~ 10^7 RMB) where discrete lot execution and capital protection are mandatory, StockASkill embeds a columnar DuckDB OLAP engine over 42.44 million historical price records and 186,432 financial disclosures spanning 26 years across China A-shares, Hong Kong, US equities, and ETFs. By eliminating ad-hoc network dependencies during analysis, StockASkill reduces cross-sectional screening latency across 5,400+ equities from over 25 seconds to sub-0.5 seconds (a 95%+ reduction). We formalize an end-to-end mathematical pipeline integrating a 7+1 factor alpha model, an SLSQP convex optimizer under smooth CVaR drawdown surrogates with exact path projection, dynamic 100-share board lot integer quantization, and an autonomous thesis lifecycle finite-state machine. Furthermore, we provide a zero-disk, strongly-typed JSON streaming interface designed for high-throughput LLM tool-calling. Open-sourced at https://github.com/axjing/stockaskill, comprehensive empirical benchmarks from 2018 to 2024 demonstrate that the architecture achieves annualized Sharpe ratios exceeding 1.48 and Calmar ratios up to 2.17, while reducing max drawdown by up to 85% relative to benchmark indices.

</details>

### 265. [PreForceVision: Physics-Guided Optical Estimation of Bolt Preload from Bolt-Head Deformation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09529v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09529

**作者：** Nehal Afifi、Klaus Rappenecker、Florian Peinhardt、Gerhard Kachel、Alfred Isele、Sven Matthiesen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09529v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09529v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 这是机械工程中的螺栓预紧力光学测量工作，用 DIC/光流从螺栓头表面形变反演力值；虽含图像与不确定性回归，但无推理状态生成、证据筛选或 VLM 推理机制方面的可借鉴线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PreForceVision: Physics-Guided Optical Estimation of Bolt Preload from Bolt-Head Deformation

**abstract/A1：** Bolt preload is a decisive functional quantity in bolted joints because it determines whether external loads are transmitted primarily through frictional clamping or directly through the bolt. Existing preload measurement techniques, including strain-gauge bolts, force washers, ultrasonic methods, and bolt-length measurements, are effective in certain applications but require additional hardware, coupling, calibration, or joint modifications. This limits their scalability for large numbers of standard fasteners and motivates a non-contact optical approach. This paper proposes PreForceVision, a physics-guided optical framework for estimating bolt preload from preload-induced surface deformation of the bolt-head front face. The method treats preload estimation as an inverse deformation-to-force problem, where deformation of the optically accessible bolt-head surface is extracted from reference-loaded image pairs and related to the internal preload state. Preliminary results for an M16 strength class 8.8 bolt show an approximately \\SI{12}{\\micro\\meter} simulated head-face depression at \\SI{90}{\\kilo\\newton}, strain-gauge measurements of comparable magnitude, and an optical 3D measured central depression of approximately \\SIrange{10}{20}{\\micro\\meter}. The contribution is a physics-guided optical methodology combining controlled image-pair acquisition, DIC- or optical-flow-based deformation extraction, multimodal validation, and uncertainty-aware regression for rough preload estimation under controlled conditions.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, Proceedings of the ASME 2026 International Mechanical Engineering Congress and Exposition (IMECE 2026), November 8-12, 2026, Vancouver, Canada. Accepted and under publication

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09536

**作者：** Xiao Tan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09536v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09536v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作面向弱监督视频异常检测，用文本提示与 LLM 构建任务先验、经字典学习压缩，并通过跨模态注意力融合先验与帧特征来提升检测性能与稳定性。

**排除原因：** 属于视频异常检测的应用型多模态特征增强，文本先验与跨模态注意力仅用于离线训练和检测打分，未涉及推理期 latent 状态、视觉证据获取或停止决策，缺少可迁移到 LVR 主线的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multimodal Training with Knowledge Traction for Video Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** The task of weakly supervised video anomaly detection is challenging. Existing approaches mainly model video frame features and determine whether anomalies are present based on their magnitudes. In this work, we propose a novel method that incorporates external knowledge into training to improve model performance, robustness, and generalization. We leverage textual prompts and a large language model (LLM) to establish prior information for the specific task, and employ dictionary learning to normalize and compress this information. Moreover, during experiments, we observed inconsistencies in the textual descriptions extracted from similar video frames by image caption models. To address this, we devise a method that leverages an LLM and auxiliary information to ensure consistency in the textual descriptions of similar frames within a video. Finally, we introduce a feature processing approach that incorporates prior knowledge and cross-modal attention mechanisms. This approach learns the relationships and characteristics among different modalities to construct more accurate and reliable feature representations, thereby improving the accuracy and stability of downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that our method surpasses other state-of-the-art approaches.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 10 figures, 8 tables

</details>

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 MViS，用轻量测试时优化框架为多台自由移动相机拍摄的同一场景联合预测逐视角、逐时刻的单应矩阵，实现兼顾多视角与时间一致性的视频几何对齐，并建立了三相机以上的新基准。

**排除原因：** 排除原因：该工作聚焦视频几何拼接与单应估计，虽含测试时优化和时序一致性，但既不涉及视觉推理状态、证据筛选，也未给出可供 LVR 借鉴的具体机制线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Video Stitching from Multiple Moving Cameras

**abstract/A1：** We address geometric video alignment for the same scene captured by multiple freely moving cameras. Existing methods, which are based on image stitching or video stabilization, are typically limited to two cameras or must assume the restrictive setting of a fixed linear ordering of the cameras. We propose MViS, a lightweight test-time optimization framework for joint multi-view alignment with temporal consistency. MViS uses a spatial-transformer-inspired net to jointly process frames from all views within a short temporal window and predict per-view, per-time homographies. Optimization proceeds in two stages: geometry-guided alignment followed by appearance-based refinement, with both stages enforcing multi-view and temporal consistency across the sequence. The method requires no scene-specific hyperparameter tuning, handles arbitrary and time-varying view-intersection graphs, and remains robust under rapid camera motion. On a standard two-view video benchmark, MViS matches or surpasses the state of the art. To facilitate evaluation in more complex scenarios, we introduce an annotated dataset with more than two freely moving cameras and establish the first benchmark for this setting. Project page: https://bgu-cs-vil.github.io/MViS-project/ .

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 268. [Coordinatewise Epsilon for Adam under Heavy-Tailed Noise](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09596v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09596

**作者：** Jeonseong Kim、Chenglin Fan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09596v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09596v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究 Adam 中逐坐标 ε 与共享标量 ε 对收敛性的影响：在逐坐标重尾噪声矩假设下给出可匹配最优时间指数的 ε\_i 选择与共享 ε 的下界。

**排除原因：** 属纯优化收敛理论分析，讨论的是优化器超参数与坐标异质性，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的状态表示、证据获取、停止决策或监督分配，也无可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Coordinatewise Epsilon for Adam under Heavy-Tailed Noise

**abstract/A1：** Adam estimates second moments coordinatewise, but typically adds the same scalar $\\varepsilon$ to every denominator. We study how this shared $\\varepsilon$ affects convergence when noise and curvature vary across coordinates. Under coordinatewise finite-$p$ noise moments, $p\\in(1,2\]$, choosing $\\varepsilon\_i$ from the noise and curvature bounds for each coordinate attains the heavy-tailed $\\ell\_1$-stationarity benchmark $O(T^{-\\frac{p-1}{3p-2}})$, matching the known optimal time exponent for smooth nonconvex stochastic optimization. For $1&lt;p&lt;2$, Adam with a shared $\\varepsilon\_t$ admits a lower bound of order $Ω(T^{-\\frac{p-1}{2p}})$. This lower bound holds for every predictable scalar choice of $η\_t$, $β\_{1,t}$, $β\_{2,t}$, and $\\varepsilon\_t$, precluding a dimension-uniform benchmark rate for this class.

**comment/C1：** 32 pages

</details>

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dual Architecture Fusion Vehicle Lidar 3D Target Detection

**abstract/A1：** In this paper, a 3D backbone network (SpconvTransformer) based on the fusion of convolutional architecture branch and Transformer architecture branch is proposed. The convolution branch uses 3D sparse convolution and submanifold convolution to learn local voxel features and semantic information, and the Transformer branch uses the self-attention mechanism to model the remote relationship between voxels, uses the broad receptive field to capture rich context information, and complementing the advantages of the two architectures by fusing the local features extracted from the two branches and the global features solves the problem of limited receptive fields of 3D convolutional neural networks. The experimental results show that the average accuracy of SpconvTransformer in 3D object detection and bird's-eye view detection tasks reaches 81.45% and 88.09%, respectively, and the average directional similarity reaches 91.10%, which is significantly better than other existing algorithms.

**内容简述：** 该论文用多层图卷积自编码器处理姿态估计数据，在智能手机上以无监督方式筛查运动员的前交叉韧带损伤风险，并在 Drop Vertical Jump 与 Single Leg Hop 两项测试上报告约 75%–78% 的敏感度与特异度。

**排除原因：** 属运动医学领域的姿态序列异常检测应用，虽然包含自编码器的隐表示，但未涉及 VLM 推理状态如何生成、使用或评价，也未涉及视觉证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，与本主线缺乏具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evaluating Anterior Cruciate Ligament Risk of Injury Using a Graph Convolutional Autoencoder

**abstract/A1：** Current solutions for assessing the risk of anterior cruciate ligament (ACL) injury typically rely on expensive motion-capture labs that require substantial resource investment or subjective evaluations of specific athletic stress tests by experts. The Drop Vertical Jump (DVJ) and the Single Leg Hop (SLH) are two examples of such tests typically used to track progress in ACL rehabilitation. In this paper, we develop an unsupervised learning model that runs entirely on a smartphone for two tasks, based on an autoencoder architecture with multiple layers of graph convolutional networks, which utilizes pose estimation data to determine a person's injury risk status. Our model accurately predicts athletes at high risk of injury for both athletic stress tests, with average sensitivities and specificities of 78% and 78% for the DVJ, and 75% and 74% for the SLH. Our findings support the development of a widely accessible smartphone solution for real-time screening of ACL injury risk.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 9 figures

</details>

**内容简述：** 论文首次对五个开源统一多模态大模型做零样本基准评测，考察其在浅水 UAV 图像中同时完成水体畸变评估与图像复原的能力，并附带人类标注与两类复原基线对比。

**排除原因：** 该工作是图像复原任务的通用 VLM 刷分式基准，未涉及视觉细节在内部状态中的保留与使用、latent 推理生成、证据筛选或停止决策；「诊断不等于复原」的结论虽可类比，但材料未提供状态或贡献分配层面的具体线索，故不入选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Zero-Shot Benchmarking of Unified Multimodal Models for Shallow-Water UAV Image Distortion Assessment and Restoration

**abstract/A1：** Shallow-water UAV imagery provides centimeter-scale observations of benthic environments, but water-induced distortions complicate interpretation. To our knowledge, we present the first systematic zero-shot benchmark of unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for both assessing water-induced distortions and restoring shallow-water imagery within the same model framework. We evaluate five open-weight unified MLLMs with frozen pretrained weights and without access to paired reference images or descriptions of the true seabed. The benchmark contains 1,000 image pairs: 600 semi-synthetic UAV scenes created by applying synthetic water distortions to real shallow-water imagery and 400 fully synthetic through-water scenes. Three independent human annotations cover five distortion types. We evaluate four assessment prompts (P0-P3), from direct instruction to structured task decomposition, on all 1,000 images and four diagnosis-guided restoration prompts (R0-R3) on a common 100-image subset. Assessment varies substantially by model and prompt, with systematic prediction biases and a best pooled balanced accuracy of 0.654 for distortion presence. The best pooled PSNR gain is +0.255 dB, no unified MLLM improves pooled LPIPS, and some outputs alter scene structure or hallucinate content. Two restoration-only baselines also show limited transfer: InstructIR achieves a pooled PSNR gain of +0.040 dB, while PromptFix degrades all four pooled metrics under every prompt. These results show that recognizing shallow-water degradation does not imply reliable physical restoration and provide a reproducible baseline for future work.

</details>

**title/T1：** Continuous wavelet-based feature extraction for laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy classification of human plasma samples

**abstract/A1：** Extracting discriminative features from laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy (LIBS) spectra is important for the classification of complex biological samples. This study evaluates continuous wavelet transform (CWT)-based spectral peak extraction for distinguishing human plasma samples labeled as SARS-CoV-2-positive or negative. Plasma specimens from 181 individuals, comprising 53 positive samples and 128 negative controls, were deposited as 5 μL droplets on silicon substrates and dried before analysis. Spectra were acquired using a nanosecond Nd:YAG laser operating at 1064 nm, with 100 single-shot measurements collected per specimen and averaged after intensity-based outlier rejection. CWT-based peak detection was coupled with a support vector machine (SVM) classifier. The reported leave-one-out evaluation yielded an accuracy of 93.00%, sensitivity of 94.67%, and specificity of 91.61%; these estimates remain provisional pending reconciliation with specimen-level predictions. Features identified by the CWT pipeline included emission lines assigned to Ca II, K I, and N I. The workflow provides a basis for examining spectral features associated with the studied plasma groups. Independent validation and assessment of specimen, substrate, and acquisition-related variability are required to establish generalizability and diagnostic relevance.

</details>

### 273. [A lightweight convolutional neural network for target detection in an edge environment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09756v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09756

**作者：** Hughes Perreault、Ola Ahmad、Daniel Czuboka

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09756v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09756v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种面向边缘设备的轻量卷积网络，用于热成像场景下的目标检测与分类，并配合实时热点检测算法提升推理速度。

**排除原因：** 属于边缘端目标检测的工程部署工作，不涉及视觉推理的内部状态、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的状态或训练线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A lightweight convolutional neural network for target detection in an edge environment

**abstract/A1：** This paper proposes a novel, lightweight convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for edge device deployment. The CNN is developed to recognize and classify threats or targets of interest. The model is equipped with a real-time hotspot detection algorithm, which enables it to process information quickly and accurately, resulting in improved inference time of up to 20 fps and a detection range of more than 400 meters with typical thermal situational awareness sensors. The proposed CNN has been rigorously tested and validated to demonstrate its high accuracy, sensitivity, and low false positive rate, providing a reliable solution for edge devices to detect potential targets. Additionally, the lightweight design of the CNN allows for easy deployment, making it an ideal solution for extending the security bubble for edge devices. The proposed model can be used for a variety of applications, including surveillance, security, and object recognition.

**comment/C1：** Presented at GVSETS 2023, Novi, MI. Distribution A: approved for public release

</details>

### 274. [From PAC bounds on the generalization error to PAC bounds on the parameter estimation error](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09767v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09767

**作者：** Mihaly Petreczky、John Leth、Emma Lukacs、Balint Daroczy

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09767v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09767v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文借助 PAC 泛化界导出的 oracle 不等式，为有界输入与噪声下的线性、双线性系统推导有限样本参数估计误差界，并分析收敛速率。

**排除原因：** 属于系统辨识的统计学习理论，讨论的是参数估计误差与泛化界，未涉及视觉证据、latent 推理状态或停止决策，也没有可直接核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From PAC bounds on the generalization error to PAC bounds on the parameter estimation error

**abstract/A1：** Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) bounds on the generalization error (the difference between the empirical prediction error computed on the training data and its expectation) are an established tool in statistical machine learning, and there have been several extensions of these bounds to dynamical systems. In parallel, in recent years, there has been growing interest in finite-sample bounds for parameter estimation error in system identification. The latter bounds tend to be algorithm-specific, relying on reformulating the identification algorithm as a least-squares regression problem. This is in contrast to classical PAC bounds, which are algorithm-agnostic and can be applied to any identification algorithm based on minimizing a cost function involving the empirical loss. In this paper, we show how oracle bounds derived from PAC bounds can be used to derive finite-sample bounds for dynamical systems. As an illustration of this approach, we present finite-sample parameter-estimation bounds for linear and bilinear systems with bounded inputs and noise. These bounds apply to identification algorithms based on minimizing a regularized empirical loss function. The key ingredient is to establish an oracle inequality, i.e., an inequality between the true prediction error of the learned model and the minimum achievable true prediction error, and then use either the convexity of the true loss or system-theoretic properties of the loss function to derive bounds on the parameter estimation error. We also analyze the rates of the obtained bounds and compare them with those of classical asymptotic results.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 0 figures, under submission to SysID2027

</details>

### 275. [ZeoBench: A Benchmark for Self-Supervised Learning on 3D Zeolite Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09880v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09880

**作者：** Aaron Sun、Yachan Liu、Ping Yang、Peng Bai、Subhransu Maji

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09880v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09880v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ZeoBench 面向全硅沸石的稠密 3D 表示，构建自监督预训练与六个吸附性质预测任务的评测基准，比较 3D SSL、图像预训练 2D 网络转 3D、域外 3D 卷积预训练及手工描述符在少标签条件下的表现。

**排除原因：** 该工作是材料科学领域的 3D 表示学习基准，考察的是低数据条件下性质预测的特征质量，不涉及视觉推理状态生成、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的具体机制线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ZeoBench: A Benchmark for Self-Supervised Learning on 3D Zeolite Representations

**abstract/A1：** Zeolites are an important class of porous crystalline materials with broad applications in gas storage, separations, and catalysis. Although hundreds of thousands of experimentally realized and hypothetical zeolite structures are known, identifying materials optimized for a target application remains challenging because property labels are expensive to obtain and available only for limited structure-molecule pairs. While prior work has demonstrated that 3D neural representations can predict zeolite properties effectively, it remains unclear which representation-learning strategies are most effective in the low-data regime relevant to materials discovery. We present ZeoBench, a benchmark for self-supervised learning on dense 3D representations of all-silica zeolites, and evaluate the resulting features on six existing downstream adsorption-property prediction tasks, across a range of training set sizes. Our study compares self-supervised 3D pretraining on volumetric zeolite data, adaptations of image-pretrained 2D ConvNets and vision transformers to 3D via multiview and channel-inflation strategies, and 3D ConvNets pretrained on out-of-domain volumetric datasets, alongside hand-crafted descriptors and supervised baselines. Using our pretraining and evaluation framework, we find self-supervised 3D representations outperform existing methods and achieve state-of-the-art performance across both in-domain label regimes and for the unseen domain of metal organic frameworks. Learned representations consistently outperform hand-crafted descriptors, and convolutional architectures are generally more effective than vision transformers in this setting. These results establish ZeoBench as a practical benchmark for 3D representation learning in zeolites and provide guidance for data-limited adsorption property prediction.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 Datasets and Benchmarks Track

</details>

### 276. [Video-Coder: Reinforcing Agentic Coding for Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09894v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.09894

**作者：** Chaoyang Wang、Sicheng Gao、Junan Hu、Kunyang Li、Dongyang Chen、Yiyang Zhou、Huaxiu Yao、Yong Jae Lee、Radu Timofte、Yuzhang Shang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09894v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09894v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Video-Coder：让编码 agent 自主编写并执行代码构建 3D 场景（Draft Video），为下游视频生成器提供结构与运动信号，并用数据管线、交互环境及 SFT 加双奖励的 agentic RL 训练该 agent。

**排除原因：** 该工作属视频生成应用，代码生成与执行、奖励退火均服务于视频质量提升，不涉及 VLM 推理期的 latent 状态生成、视觉证据使用或停止决策，缺少可供核查的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Video-Coder: Reinforcing Agentic Coding for Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Recent years have witnessed remarkable progress in video generation in terms of visual quality and temporal consistency. However, generating videos with precise spatial structure, coherent motion dynamics, and controllable scene composition remains challenging. In this paper, we introduce Video-Coder, the first attempt to train a code-driven video generation agent, which autonomously writes and executes code to construct a dynamic 3D scene (Draft Video) that provides structure and motion signals to downstream video generators. To support this paradigm, we design a dedicated data pipeline and curate two high-quality datasets, Video-Coder-SFT-6k and Video-Coder-RL-2k, which contain diverse prompts and ground-truth reference videos. We further develop the Video- Coder Harness, a unified interactive environment integrating code execution, 3D scene rendering, and execution feedback for agent training and inference. We then train Video-Coder with SFT fol- lowed by harness-native agentic reinforcement learning with dual reward feedback, which combines execution-based and video-based rewards. Additionally, we design an annealing training strategy that gradually reduces the execution reward over time, allowing the model to rely more on the video reward in the later training stages. Experiments show that Video-Coder achieves significant gains, improving Minimax-H3 by approximately 7 points on PhyGenBench and 24 points on VBench-2.0. We hope this work can serve as an open foundation for coding agents in video generation, and we fully open-source our data, models, and code

**comment/C1：** Project Page:https://cywang735.github.io/Video-Coder/ Code: https://github.com/CYWang735/Video-Coder

</details>
